===== FILE: components/pipelines/event_merger/live/Cargo.toml =====
[package]
name = "event_merger_live"
version = "0.1.0"
edition = "2021"

# Deliberately dependency-free.
#
# This crate is the live state-machine that turns typed market events into
# canonical rows. It must know NOTHING about:
#   * how events arrive (websockets, REST, files)      -> `uets` owns that
#   * Binance's JSON wire format                        -> `uets` owns that
#   * Arrow / Parquet / any historical storage          -> `merger/` owns that
#
# The payoff: the core is allocation-free, deterministically testable with no
# I/O, and the same code can later backtest a recorded feed.
[lib]
name = "event_merger_live"
path = "src/lib.rs"

[dependencies]


===== FILE: components/pipelines/event_merger/live/src/book.rs =====
//! `book.rs` â€” the live full-depth order book.
//!
//! Owned entirely by this crate. It is a deliberate, self-contained
//! reimplementation of the data structure and semantics used by the historical
//! engine (`rust/lob_builder/btree_ver/book.rs`), so live and offline agree on
//! what a book is â€” without importing or depending on that file, so the two
//! stay independently buildable.
//!
//! Why full depth rather than the top-20 partial snapshot:
//! `depth20@100ms` pushes a *complete top-20 snapshot* whose `lastUpdateId` is
//! an id for that snapshot object. The historical `lob__final_update_id` is
//! the id of the last diff event applied to a *full-depth* book. Those are
//! different objects, so the partial snapshot cannot stand in for it. The live
//! engine therefore consumes the diff stream (`@depth@100ms`) plus a REST
//! snapshot seed, which makes `lob__final_update_id` mean the same thing in
//! both engines by construction.
//!
//! Complexity: `BTreeMap` gives `O(log n)` per mutation and best-price lookup,
//! and keeps both sides sorted so emitting top-N is a bounded prefix walk
//! rather than a sort per row.

use std::collections::BTreeMap;

/// Fixed-point resolution used to key book levels as integers.
///
/// Prices are rounded to the nearest `1 / TICK_SCALE` and stored as `i64`, so
/// map keys compare exactly (no floating-point key comparisons) while still
/// resolving far finer than any real tick size.
pub const TICK_SCALE: f64 = 1e8;

/// Price represented as an integer tick.
pub type PriceTick = i64;

/// Convert a price into its integer tick representation.
///
/// Returns `None` for non-finite or negative prices.
pub fn price_to_tick(price: f64) -> Option<PriceTick> {
    if !price.is_finite() || price < 0.0 {
        return None;
    }
    let scaled = price * TICK_SCALE;
    if scaled > i64::MAX as f64 {
        return None;
    }
    Some(scaled.round() as PriceTick)
}

/// Convert an integer tick back to a price.
pub fn tick_to_price(tick: PriceTick) -> f64 {
    tick as f64 / TICK_SCALE
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default)]
pub struct Level {
    pub quantity: f64,
}

/// Full-depth L2 book.
#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct LobBook {
    /// Sorted ascending; best bid is the largest key.
    bids: BTreeMap<PriceTick, Level>,
    /// Sorted ascending; best ask is the smallest key.
    asks: BTreeMap<PriceTick, Level>,
}

impl LobBook {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    pub fn clear(&mut self) {
        self.bids.clear();
        self.asks.clear();
    }

    pub fn bid_levels(&self) -> usize {
        self.bids.len()
    }

    pub fn ask_levels(&self) -> usize {
        self.asks.len()
    }

    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.bids.is_empty() && self.asks.is_empty()
    }

    /// Replace the whole book with a trusted full snapshot.
    ///
    /// Non-positive quantities are dropped.
    pub fn apply_snapshot(&mut self, bids: &[(PriceTick, f64)], asks: &[(PriceTick, f64)]) {
        self.clear();
        for &(price, quantity) in bids {
            if quantity > 0.0 {
                self.bids.insert(price, Level { quantity });
            }
        }
        for &(price, quantity) in asks {
            if quantity > 0.0 {
                self.asks.insert(price, Level { quantity });
            }
        }
    }

    /// Apply one update.
    ///
    /// `quantity > 0` sets the absolute new quantity at that price;
    /// `quantity == 0` removes the level. Returns whether the book changed.
    ///
    /// The single-side invariant is enforced: a price may not rest on both
    /// sides at once.
    pub fn apply_update(&mut self, is_bid: bool, price: PriceTick, quantity: f64) -> bool {
        if quantity < 0.0 {
            return false;
        }
        if quantity == 0.0 {
            return if is_bid {
                self.bids.remove(&price).is_some()
            } else {
                self.asks.remove(&price).is_some()
            };
        }
        if is_bid {
            self.asks.remove(&price);
            self.bids
                .insert(price, Level { quantity })
                .map_or(true, |old| old.quantity != quantity)
        } else {
            self.bids.remove(&price);
            self.asks
                .insert(price, Level { quantity })
                .map_or(true, |old| old.quantity != quantity)
        }
    }

    pub fn best_bid(&self) -> Option<(PriceTick, f64)> {
        self.bids
            .iter()
            .next_back()
            .map(|(&price, level)| (price, level.quantity))
    }

    pub fn best_ask(&self) -> Option<(PriceTick, f64)> {
        self.asks
            .iter()
            .next()
            .map(|(&price, level)| (price, level.quantity))
    }

    /// True when best bid >= best ask.
    pub fn is_crossed(&self) -> bool {
        match (self.best_bid(), self.best_ask()) {
            (Some((bid, _)), Some((ask, _))) => bid >= ask,
            _ => false,
        }
    }

    /// Borrowing bid-side top-N: highest -> lowest, no allocation.
    pub fn top_n_bids(&self, levels: usize) -> impl Iterator<Item = (PriceTick, f64)> + '_ {
        self.bids
            .iter()
            .rev()
            .take(levels)
            .map(|(&price, level)| (price, level.quantity))
    }

    /// Borrowing ask-side top-N: lowest -> highest, no allocation.
    pub fn top_n_asks(&self, levels: usize) -> impl Iterator<Item = (PriceTick, f64)> + '_ {
        self.asks
            .iter()
            .take(levels)
            .map(|(&price, level)| (price, level.quantity))
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    /// (tick, quantity) pair, as `apply_snapshot` expects.
    fn p(price: f64) -> (PriceTick, f64) {
        (price_to_tick(price).expect("finite price"), 1.0)
    }

    fn tick(price: f64) -> PriceTick {
        price_to_tick(price).expect("finite price")
    }

    #[test]
    fn snapshot_then_best_prices() {
        let mut book = LobBook::new();
        book.apply_snapshot(&[p(100.0), p(99.0)], &[p(101.0), p(102.0)]);
        assert_eq!(book.best_bid().map(|(t, _)| tick_to_price(t)), Some(100.0));
        assert_eq!(book.best_ask().map(|(t, _)| tick_to_price(t)), Some(101.0));
        assert!(!book.is_crossed());
    }

    #[test]
    fn zero_quantity_removes_level() {
        let mut book = LobBook::new();
        book.apply_snapshot(&[p(100.0)], &[p(101.0)]);
        assert!(book.apply_update(true, tick(100.0), 0.0));
        assert_eq!(book.best_bid(), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn top_n_is_best_first_and_bounded() {
        let mut book = LobBook::new();
        let bids: Vec<_> = (1..=30).map(|i| p(100.0 - i as f64)).collect();
        book.apply_snapshot(&bids, &[]);
        let top: Vec<f64> = book.top_n_bids(5).map(|(t, _)| tick_to_price(t)).collect();
        assert_eq!(top, vec![99.0, 98.0, 97.0, 96.0, 95.0]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn update_enforces_single_side_invariant() {
        // apply_snapshot loads both sides verbatim, so a price CAN appear on
        // both. The invariant is enforced on update: re-quoting one side
        // evicts the other at the same price.
        let mut book = LobBook::new();
        book.apply_snapshot(&[p(100.0)], &[p(100.0)]);
        assert_eq!(book.bid_levels(), 1);
        assert_eq!(book.ask_levels(), 1);

        book.apply_update(true, tick(100.0), 2.0);
        assert_eq!(book.bid_levels(), 1, "bid remains");
        assert_eq!(book.ask_levels(), 0, "ask evicted at the same price");
    }

    #[test]
    fn tick_roundtrip_is_stable() {
        let price = 108663.2;
        let tick = price_to_tick(price).unwrap();
        assert!((tick_to_price(tick) - price).abs() < 1e-6);
    }

    #[test]
    fn non_finite_and_negative_prices_are_rejected() {
        assert!(price_to_tick(f64::NAN).is_none());
        assert!(price_to_tick(f64::INFINITY).is_none());
        assert!(price_to_tick(-1.0).is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn snapshot_drops_non_positive_quantities() {
        let mut book = LobBook::new();
        book.apply_snapshot(&[p(100.0), (tick(99.0), 0.0)], &[]);
        assert_eq!(book.bid_levels(), 1);
    }
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/event_merger/live/src/csv.rs =====
//! `csv.rs` — the live canonical-row CSV sink.
//!
//! The live capture is written as CSV because that is the interchange format
//! the feature layer and the parity harness already speak, and because a row
//! here is *sparse*: a TRADE row has no `lob__*` and a LOB row has no
//! `agg__*`, so most fields are empty.
//!
//! # Byte-for-byte schema compatibility
//!
//! The header is [`crate::row::schema`] and the field order is produced by
//! [`Merger::write_row`], the same emitter the historical pipeline uses. That
//! is the point: a live capture and a historical merge are readable by one
//! feature spec with no column remapping. Every row is checked against the
//! 128-column count before it is written, so a column can never silently
//! shift.
//!
//! # Missing vs zero
//!
//! Absent values are written as **empty fields**, never `0.0`. There is no
//! live source for `depth__*` / `depth_state__*` (Binance's `bookDepth` is a
//! downloaded dataset, not a stream), and a fabricated zero there would read
//! downstream as "no liquidity" rather than "not observed".
//!
//! # Durability
//!
//! Writes are line-buffered and flushed on [`CsvRecorder::flush`], on `Drop`,
//! and every [`CsvRecorder::flush_every`] rows, so a capture killed
//! mid-session still leaves a readable file rather than a zero-byte one.

use std::fs::{File, OpenOptions};
use std::io::{BufWriter, Write};
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use crate::merger::{CanonicalRow, Merger};
use crate::row::{schema, FieldWriter, FIELD_COUNT};

/// Why a row could not be written.
#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum CsvError {
    Io(std::io::Error),
    /// The row did not carry exactly one field per column. Writing it anyway
    /// would shift every downstream column, so it is refused instead.
    FieldCountMismatch {
        expected: usize,
        got: usize,
    },
}

impl std::fmt::Display for CsvError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::Io(e) => write!(f, "csv io error: {e}"),
            Self::FieldCountMismatch { expected, got } => write!(
                f,
                "row has {got} fields but the schema has {expected}; \
                 refusing to write a row that would shift columns"
            ),
        }
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for CsvError {}

impl From<std::io::Error> for CsvError {
    fn from(value: std::io::Error) -> Self {
        Self::Io(value)
    }
}

/// True when the file exists and holds at least one byte.
fn file_is_non_empty(path: &Path) -> Result<bool, CsvError> {
    Ok(std::fs::metadata(path)
        .map(|m| m.len() > 0)
        .unwrap_or(false))
}

/// Append-only CSV writer for the canonical 128-column row.
///
/// Owns the [`FieldWriter`], so steady-state encoding allocates nothing: the
/// buffer is reused and a null costs one separator.
pub struct CsvRecorder {
    out: BufWriter<File>,
    writer: FieldWriter,
    path: PathBuf,
    /// Guards against emitting a second header when reopening for append.
    header_written: bool,
    rows_written: u64,
    rows_since_flush: u64,
    flush_every: u64,
    /// True when the file already existed and rows were appended, which is
    /// legitimate for a resumed capture but worth reporting.
    appended: bool,
}

impl CsvRecorder {
    /// Create a capture file, truncating any previous one.
    ///
    /// The parent directory is created when missing, so a fresh
    /// `market_data/live_db` needs no manual `mkdir`.
    pub fn create(path: impl AsRef<Path>) -> Result<Self, CsvError> {
        Self::open(path, false)
    }

    /// Open a capture for appending, keeping the existing header.
    pub fn append(path: impl AsRef<Path>) -> Result<Self, CsvError> {
        Self::open(path, true)
    }

    fn open(path: impl AsRef<Path>, append: bool) -> Result<Self, CsvError> {
        let path = path.as_ref().to_path_buf();
        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
            if !parent.as_os_str().is_empty() {
                std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
            }
        }
        let existed = path.exists();
        let file = OpenOptions::new()
            .create(true)
            .write(true)
            .append(append)
            .truncate(!append)
            .open(&path)?;
        // An appended file already carries a header, so suppress writing a
        // second one. A truncated (fresh) file never does.
        let header_written = append && file_is_non_empty(&path)?;
        Ok(Self {
            out: BufWriter::new(file),
            writer: FieldWriter::new(FIELD_COUNT),
            path,
            header_written,
            rows_written: 0,
            rows_since_flush: 0,
            flush_every: 256,
            appended: append && existed,
        })
    }

    /// Flush to the OS every `n` rows. Lower means less data at risk on a
    /// crash; higher means fewer syscalls.
    pub fn flush_every(mut self, n: u64) -> Self {
        self.flush_every = n.max(1);
        self
    }

    pub fn path(&self) -> &Path {
        &self.path
    }

    pub fn rows_written(&self) -> u64 {
        self.rows_written
    }

    /// True when this capture appends to a pre-existing file.
    pub fn is_appending(&self) -> bool {
        self.appended
    }

    /// Write the header when it has not been written yet.
    pub fn write_header(&mut self) -> Result<(), CsvError> {
        if self.header_written {
            return Ok(());
        }
        self.writer.begin_header();
        let line = self.writer.take_row();
        self.out.write_all(line.as_bytes())?;
        self.header_written = true;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Encode one canonical row through the merger and append it.
    ///
    /// Returns the encoded line length in bytes. A row the merger could not
    /// encode at the full field width is refused rather than written, because
    /// a short row would shift every downstream column.
    pub fn write_row(
        &mut self,
        merger: &mut Merger,
        row: &CanonicalRow,
    ) -> Result<usize, CsvError> {
        self.write_header()?;
        // `Merger::write_csv` finishes the row itself (it calls
        // FieldWriter::finish_row, which appends the newline and resets the
        // field counter). So the line is taken afterwards, never re-finished,
        // and the field count can no longer be read here -- the None return
        // from write_csv is what reports a width mismatch.
        if merger.write_csv(row, &mut self.writer).is_none() {
            return Err(CsvError::FieldCountMismatch {
                expected: FIELD_COUNT,
                got: 0,
            });
        }
        let line = self.writer.take_row();
        self.out.write_all(line.as_bytes())?;
        self.rows_written += 1;
        self.rows_since_flush += 1;
        if self.rows_since_flush >= self.flush_every {
            self.out.flush()?;
            self.rows_since_flush = 0;
        }
        Ok(line.len())
    }

    /// Push buffered rows to the OS.
    pub fn flush(&mut self) -> Result<(), CsvError> {
        self.out.flush()?;
        self.rows_since_flush = 0;
        Ok(())
    }
}

impl Drop for CsvRecorder {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        // Best effort: a recorder dropped during a panic should still leave
        // every completed row on disk.
        let _ = self.out.flush();
    }
}

/// The 128-column header line, newline included.
pub fn header_line() -> String {
    let mut line = String::with_capacity(4096);
    for name in schema() {
        line.push_str(name);
        line.push(',');
    }
    line.pop();
    line.push('\n');
    line
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::event::{BookSnapshot, BookUpdate, DiffFrame, MarketEvent, Trade};
    use crate::DEFAULT_LEVELS;

    fn temp_path(name: &str) -> PathBuf {
        let mut p = std::env::temp_dir();
        p.push(format!("eml_csv_test_{name}"));
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&p);
        p
    }

    fn seed() -> MarketEvent {
        MarketEvent::Snapshot(BookSnapshot {
            last_update_id: 100,
            bids: vec![(99.0, 1.0), (98.0, 2.0)],
            asks: vec![(101.0, 1.5), (102.0, 2.5)],
        })
    }

    fn trade(ts: i64, id: u64) -> MarketEvent {
        MarketEvent::Trade(Trade {
            event_time_ms: ts,
            transact_time_ms: ts,
            agg_trade_id: id,
            first_trade_id: id,
            last_trade_id: id,
            price: 100.0,
            quantity: 0.5,
            is_buyer_maker: false,
        })
    }

    fn diff(ts: i64, first: u64, last: u64) -> MarketEvent {
        MarketEvent::Diff(DiffFrame {
            event_time_ms: ts,
            first_update_id: first,
            final_update_id: last,
            updates: vec![BookUpdate {
                is_bid: true,
                price: 99.5,
                quantity: 3.0,
            }],
        })
    }

    #[test]
    fn header_has_128_columns_in_schema_order() {
        let line = header_line();
        let names: Vec<&str> = line.trim_end().split(',').collect();
        assert_eq!(names.len(), FIELD_COUNT);
        assert_eq!(names[0], "timestamp");
        assert_eq!(names[1], "event_type");
        assert_eq!(names[2], "event_sequence");
        assert_eq!(names[85], "agg__agg_trade_id");
        assert_eq!(names[127], "source_depth__notional");
    }

    #[test]
    fn every_written_row_has_the_full_field_width() {
        let path = temp_path("width");
        let mut rec = CsvRecorder::create(&path).expect("create");
        let mut merger = Merger::new(DEFAULT_LEVELS);

        let (seed_row, _) = merger.merge_event(seed());
        rec.write_row(&mut merger, &seed_row).expect("seed row");
        let (lob_row, _) = merger.merge_event(diff(1000, 101, 105));
        rec.write_row(&mut merger, &lob_row).expect("lob row");
        let (trade_row, _) = merger.merge_event(trade(2000, 55));
        rec.write_row(&mut merger, &trade_row).expect("trade row");
        rec.flush().expect("flush");

        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).expect("read back");
        let lines: Vec<&str> = text.lines().collect();
        assert_eq!(lines.len(), 4, "header + 3 rows");
        for line in &lines {
            assert_eq!(
                line.matches(',').count(),
                FIELD_COUNT - 1,
                "row must carry {FIELD_COUNT} fields"
            );
        }
        assert_eq!(rec.rows_written(), 3);
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn trade_row_leaves_lob_identity_empty_not_zero() {
        let path = temp_path("sparse");
        let mut rec = CsvRecorder::create(&path).unwrap();
        let mut merger = Merger::new(DEFAULT_LEVELS);
        merger.merge_event(seed());
        let (row, _) = merger.merge_event(trade(1000, 7));
        rec.write_row(&mut merger, &row).unwrap();
        rec.flush().unwrap();

        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();
        let fields: Vec<&str> = text.lines().nth(1).unwrap().split(',').collect();
        assert_eq!(fields[1], "TRADE");
        // A fabricated 0.0 here would read as "LOB update at time 0".
        assert_eq!(fields[3], "", "lob__event_time must be absent");
        assert_eq!(fields[4], "", "lob__final_update_id must be absent");
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn depth_block_is_always_empty() {
        let path = temp_path("depth");
        let mut rec = CsvRecorder::create(&path).unwrap();
        let mut merger = Merger::new(DEFAULT_LEVELS);
        let (row, _) = merger.merge_event(seed());
        rec.write_row(&mut merger, &row).unwrap();
        rec.flush().unwrap();

        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();
        let fields: Vec<&str> = text.lines().nth(1).unwrap().split(',').collect();
        // depth__timestamp .. depth_state__notional_5 == fields 93..=116.
        for (offset, value) in fields[93..=116].iter().enumerate() {
            assert_eq!(*value, "", "depth field {offset} must be absent");
        }
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn appending_keeps_exactly_one_header() {
        let path = temp_path("append");
        {
            let mut rec = CsvRecorder::create(&path).unwrap();
            let mut merger = Merger::new(DEFAULT_LEVELS);
            let (row, _) = merger.merge_event(seed());
            rec.write_row(&mut merger, &row).unwrap();
            rec.flush().unwrap();
        }
        {
            let mut rec = CsvRecorder::append(&path).unwrap();
            assert!(rec.is_appending(), "reopening an existing file appends");
            let mut merger = Merger::new(DEFAULT_LEVELS);
            let (row, _) = merger.merge_event(trade(1000, 9));
            rec.write_row(&mut merger, &row).unwrap();
            rec.flush().unwrap();
        }
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();
        let lines: Vec<&str> = text.lines().collect();
        assert_eq!(lines.len(), 3, "one header + two rows");
        assert!(lines[0].starts_with("timestamp,"));
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn creates_missing_parent_directories() {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join("eml_csv_nested_test");
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&root);
        let path = root.join("inner").join("capture.csv");
        let mut rec = CsvRecorder::create(&path).expect("create nested");
        rec.write_header().expect("header");
        rec.flush().expect("flush");
        assert!(path.exists(), "parent directories are created on demand");
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&root);
    }
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/event_merger/live/src/event.rs =====
//! `event.rs` — the typed events this crate accepts.
//!
//! This is the crate's entire input surface. It is deliberately free of any
//! Binance wire-format knowledge: whoever feeds the engine (`uets` today)
//! parses JSON/websocket frames and hands over a `MarketEvent`. That keeps
//! this crate allocation-free (no `String`, no `serde_json::Value`) and
//! independently testable.

/// Which stream an event came from.
///
/// The discriminants deliberately match
/// `event_merger/merger/model.rs::Source` (`Lob = 0, Trade = 1, Depth = 2`)
/// so the deterministic tie-break below produces the *same ordering
/// semantics* as the historical merger.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord)]
pub enum Source {
    Lob = 0,
    Trade = 1,
    Depth = 2,
}

impl Source {
    /// The literal written to the `event_type` column.
    pub fn name(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::Lob => "LOB",
            Self::Trade => "TRADE",
            Self::Depth => "BOOKDEPTH",
        }
    }
}

/// One order-book mutation from the diff stream.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
pub struct BookUpdate {
    pub is_bid: bool,
    /// Price as received (caller converts to a tick via `book::price_to_tick`).
    pub price: f64,
    /// Absolute new quantity; `0.0` removes the level.
    pub quantity: f64,
}

/// A full-depth REST snapshot used to seed (or re-seed) the book.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct BookSnapshot {
    pub last_update_id: u64,
    pub bids: Vec<(f64, f64)>,
    pub asks: Vec<(f64, f64)>,
}

/// A diff-frame envelope: one websocket `depth` push carries many updates that
/// share an event time and an update-id range.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct DiffFrame {
    /// Exchange event time, epoch **milliseconds**.
    pub event_time_ms: i64,
    pub first_update_id: u64,
    /// Id of the last update applied by this frame.
    pub final_update_id: u64,
    pub updates: Vec<BookUpdate>,
}

/// One aggregated trade.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
pub struct Trade {
    pub event_time_ms: i64,
    /// Exchange trade time, epoch ms (`T` on the wire).
    pub transact_time_ms: i64,
    pub agg_trade_id: u64,
    pub first_trade_id: u64,
    pub last_trade_id: u64,
    pub price: f64,
    pub quantity: f64,
    /// `true` => the buyer was the maker, i.e. the taker sold.
    pub is_buyer_maker: bool,
}

/// Anything the engine can accept.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum MarketEvent {
    /// A diff frame from the depth stream.
    Diff(DiffFrame),
    /// A snapshot seed / re-seed.
    Snapshot(BookSnapshot),
    /// An aggregated trade.
    Trade(Trade),
}

impl MarketEvent {
    /// Source priority used as the secondary sort key.
    pub fn source(&self) -> Source {
        match self {
            Self::Diff(_) | Self::Snapshot(_) => Source::Lob,
            Self::Trade(_) => Source::Trade,
        }
    }

    /// Exchange event time in epoch ms, used as the primary sort key.
    pub fn event_time_ms(&self) -> i64 {
        match self {
            Self::Diff(frame) => frame.event_time_ms,
            Self::Snapshot(snapshot) => snapshot.last_update_id as i64,
            Self::Trade(trade) => trade.event_time_ms,
        }
    }

    /// Monotonic per-source counter, used as the tertiary sort key so that two
    /// events sharing a timestamp and a source still have a total order.
    pub fn source_sequence(&self) -> u64 {
        match self {
            Self::Diff(frame) => frame.final_update_id,
            Self::Snapshot(snapshot) => snapshot.last_update_id,
            Self::Trade(trade) => trade.agg_trade_id,
        }
    }
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/event_merger/live/src/lib.rs =====
//! `event_merger_live` — the live, stateful canonical-row engine.
//!
//! # Boundary
//!
//! ```text
//! Binance wire format  ──(uets)──►  MarketEvent  ──(this crate)──►  CanonicalRow
//! ```
//!
//! This crate is **dependency-free on purpose**: no `serde_json` (the caller
//! parses the wire format), no Arrow/Parquet (that belongs to the historical
//! `merger/`), and no networking (that belongs to the caller). What remains is
//! a pure state machine, so it is allocation-light, deterministic, and
//! testable with no I/O — and the same code can later replay a recorded feed.
//!
//! # Parity with the historical pipeline
//!
//! The emitted schema is byte-compatible with
//! `event_merger/merger/writer.rs`, and the ordering key matches
//! `merger/merge.rs`. Two things are deliberately *not* claimed:
//!
//! * `lob__final_update_id` is the id of the last diff applied to a
//!   **full-depth** book. Binance's `depth20@100ms` `lastUpdateId` is an id for
//!   a *partial top-20 snapshot* — a different object — so the live engine
//!   consumes the diff stream plus a REST seed instead of aliasing it.
//! * `depth__*` / `depth_state__*` have no live source (Binance's `bookDepth`
//!   is a downloaded dataset). They are emitted as **empty fields, never
//!   `0.0`**, so a consumer cannot mistake "absent" for "no liquidity".

pub mod book;
pub mod csv;
pub mod event;
pub mod merger;
pub mod reorder;
pub mod row;
pub mod state;

pub use book::{price_to_tick, tick_to_price, LobBook, PriceTick};
pub use csv::{header_line, CsvError, CsvRecorder};
pub use event::{BookSnapshot, BookUpdate, DiffFrame, MarketEvent, Source, Trade};
pub use merger::{CanonicalRow, Merger, Outcome};
pub use reorder::ReorderBuffer;
pub use row::{schema, FieldWriter};
pub use state::{BookHealth, Counters, State};

/// Default book depth emitted per side, matching the historical default.
pub const DEFAULT_LEVELS: usize = 20;

/// Default reorder window in milliseconds. Bounds how far the two sockets may
/// interleave before events are dropped rather than emitted mis-ordered.
pub const DEFAULT_LATENESS_MS: i64 = 200;

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn trade(ts: i64, id: u64, price: f64, qty: f64) -> MarketEvent {
        MarketEvent::Trade(Trade {
            event_time_ms: ts,
            transact_time_ms: ts,
            agg_trade_id: id,
            first_trade_id: id,
            last_trade_id: id,
            price,
            quantity: qty,
            is_buyer_maker: false,
        })
    }

    fn diff(ts: i64, first: u64, last: u64, updates: Vec<BookUpdate>) -> MarketEvent {
        MarketEvent::Diff(DiffFrame {
            event_time_ms: ts,
            first_update_id: first,
            final_update_id: last,
            updates,
        })
    }

    fn snapshot(last: u64, bids: &[(f64, f64)], asks: &[(f64, f64)]) -> MarketEvent {
        MarketEvent::Snapshot(BookSnapshot {
            last_update_id: last,
            bids: bids.to_vec(),
            asks: asks.to_vec(),
        })
    }

    #[test]
    fn schema_is_128_columns_and_ordered() {
        let names = schema();
        assert_eq!(names.len(), 128);
        assert_eq!(names[0], "timestamp");
        assert_eq!(names[2], "event_sequence");
        assert_eq!(names[5], "book_state__best_bid_price");
        assert_eq!(names[9], "book_state__bid_price_2");
        // 5 common + 4 best + 76 levels == 85, then agg__agg_trade_id.
        assert_eq!(names[85], "agg__agg_trade_id");
        assert_eq!(names[93], "depth__timestamp");
        assert_eq!(names[97], "depth_state__-5");
        assert_eq!(names[107], "depth_state__notional_-5");
        assert_eq!(names[127], "source_depth__notional");
        let unique: std::collections::HashSet<&&str> = names.iter().collect();
        assert_eq!(unique.len(), 128);
    }

    #[test]
    fn unseeded_book_is_flagged_invalid() {
        let mut merger = Merger::new(DEFAULT_LEVELS);
        let (row, outcome) = merger.merge_event(trade(1000, 1, 100.0, 1.0));
        assert!(!merger.state().book_valid());
        assert!(!outcome.needs_reseed);
        assert_eq!(row.event_sequence, 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn seed_then_diff_gives_valid_book_and_update_id() {
        let mut merger = Merger::new(DEFAULT_LEVELS);
        merger.merge_event(snapshot(100, &[(99.0, 1.0)], &[(101.0, 2.0)]));
        let (row, _) = merger.merge_event(diff(
            1000,
            101,
            105,
            vec![BookUpdate {
                is_bid: true,
                price: 100.0,
                quantity: 5.0,
            }],
        ));
        assert!(merger.state().book_valid());
        // The id is the last *diff* applied, not the snapshot id.
        assert_eq!(row.lob_final_update_id, Some(105));
        assert_eq!(row.source, Source::Lob);
    }

    #[test]
    fn sequence_gap_requests_reseed_and_freezes_book() {
        let mut merger = Merger::new(DEFAULT_LEVELS);
        merger.merge_event(snapshot(100, &[(99.0, 1.0)], &[(101.0, 2.0)]));
        merger.merge_event(diff(1000, 101, 105, vec![]));

        let (_, outcome) = merger.merge_event(diff(2000, 200, 205, vec![]));
        assert!(outcome.needs_reseed);
        assert!(merger.needs_reseed());
        assert!(!merger.state().book_valid());
        assert_eq!(merger.counters().gaps, 1);

        // While stale, further diffs are not applied.
        let (_, outcome2) = merger.merge_event(diff(3000, 206, 210, vec![]));
        assert!(!outcome2.applied);
    }

    #[test]
    fn reseed_restores_validity() {
        let mut merger = Merger::new(DEFAULT_LEVELS);
        merger.merge_event(snapshot(100, &[(99.0, 1.0)], &[(101.0, 2.0)]));
        merger.merge_event(diff(1000, 500, 505, vec![]));
        assert!(merger.needs_reseed());
        merger.merge_event(snapshot(600, &[(98.0, 1.0)], &[(102.0, 1.0)]));
        assert!(!merger.needs_reseed());
        assert!(merger.state().book_valid());
    }

    #[test]
    fn trade_rows_omit_lob_identity_but_carry_the_book() {
        let mut merger = Merger::new(DEFAULT_LEVELS);
        merger.merge_event(snapshot(100, &[(99.0, 1.0)], &[(101.0, 2.0)]));
        let (row, _) = merger.merge_event(trade(1000, 55, 100.5, 0.25));
        assert_eq!(row.source, Source::Trade);
        assert_eq!(row.lob_final_update_id, None);
        assert!(merger.state().book_valid());
    }

    #[test]
    fn emitted_row_has_128_fields() {
        let mut merger = Merger::new(DEFAULT_LEVELS);
        let mut writer = FieldWriter::new(schema().len());
        merger.merge_event(snapshot(100, &[(99.0, 1.0)], &[(101.0, 2.0)]));
        let (row, _) = merger.merge_event(diff(
            1000,
            101,
            105,
            vec![BookUpdate {
                is_bid: true,
                price: 100.0,
                quantity: 5.0,
            }],
        ));
        assert!(merger.write_csv(&row, &mut writer).is_some());
    }

    #[test]
    fn depth_columns_are_reported_absent() {
        assert!(!Merger::new(DEFAULT_LEVELS).state().depth_valid());
    }

    #[test]
    fn timestamp_is_nanoseconds() {
        let mut merger = Merger::new(DEFAULT_LEVELS);
        let (row, _) = merger.merge_event(trade(1_751_245_200_083, 1, 100.0, 1.0));
        assert_eq!(row.timestamp_ns, 1_751_245_200_083_000_000);
    }
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/event_merger/live/src/merger.rs =====
//! `merger.rs` — the state machine: one event in, one canonical row out.
//!
//! This is the point of the crate. It is deliberately free of I/O, of
//! Binance's wire format, and of Arrow/Parquet, so the same code path serves a
//! live capture today and a recorded-feed backtest later.

use crate::book::{price_to_tick, tick_to_price, PriceTick};
use crate::event::{BookSnapshot, MarketEvent, Source, Trade};
use crate::row::FieldWriter;
use crate::state::{BookHealth, Counters, State};

/// One canonical row.
///
/// `book_state__*` is written from live state at emit time — the as-of carry
/// the historical finalize stage reconstructs with a forward fill is *exact*
/// here, because the book is maintained rather than reconstructed.
pub struct CanonicalRow {
    /// Epoch **nanoseconds**, matching the historical `timestamp` column.
    pub timestamp_ns: i64,
    pub source: Source,
    pub event_sequence: u64,
    /// Id of the last diff applied to the book; null on non-LOB rows.
    pub lob_final_update_id: Option<i64>,
}

/// What the caller must do after `merge_event`.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Outcome {
    /// A sequence gap was hit: the book is stale and must be re-seeded from
    /// REST before it can be trusted again.
    pub needs_reseed: bool,
    /// False when a diff arrived while the book was already stale and was
    /// therefore not applied. The row is still emitted, still flagged.
    pub applied: bool,
}

/// The stateful canonical-row engine.
pub struct Merger {
    state: State,
    levels: usize,
    /// Trade payload of the row being emitted.
    last_trade: Option<Trade>,
    /// Reused price/quantity scratch so row emission allocates nothing.
    bids: Vec<f64>,
    bid_qty: Vec<f64>,
    asks: Vec<f64>,
    ask_qty: Vec<f64>,
}

impl Merger {
    pub fn new(levels: usize) -> Self {
        Self {
            state: State::new(),
            levels,
            last_trade: None,
            bids: Vec::with_capacity(levels),
            bid_qty: Vec::with_capacity(levels),
            asks: Vec::with_capacity(levels),
            ask_qty: Vec::with_capacity(levels),
        }
    }

    pub fn state(&self) -> &State {
        &self.state
    }

    pub fn counters(&self) -> Counters {
        self.state.counters
    }

    /// True while the book is unusable and a REST re-seed is required.
    pub fn needs_reseed(&self) -> bool {
        self.state.health == BookHealth::Stale
    }

    /// Apply a REST snapshot, clearing any stale marking.
    pub fn apply_seed(&mut self, snapshot: &BookSnapshot) {
        let bids = to_ticks(&snapshot.bids);
        let asks = to_ticks(&snapshot.asks);
        self.state.apply_seed(&bids, &asks, snapshot.last_update_id);
    }

    /// The core: apply one event to state and produce its canonical row.
    pub fn merge_event(&mut self, event: MarketEvent) -> (CanonicalRow, Outcome) {
        let mut needs_reseed = false;
        let mut applied = true;

        let source = event.source();
        let timestamp_ms = event.event_time_ms();
        self.last_trade = None;

        match &event {
            MarketEvent::Snapshot(snapshot) => self.apply_seed(snapshot),
            MarketEvent::Diff(frame) => {
                // While the book is already stale, do not pile further diffs
                // onto a book we know is wrong: freeze it and wait for the
                // re-seed. The row is still emitted, flagged invalid.
                if self.state.health == BookHealth::Stale {
                    applied = false;
                    needs_reseed = true;
                } else {
                    let gaps_before = self.state.counters.gaps;
                    self.state.apply_diff(frame);
                    needs_reseed = self.state.counters.gaps > gaps_before;
                }
            }
            MarketEvent::Trade(trade) => {
                self.state.apply_trade(trade);
                self.last_trade = Some(*trade);
            }
        }

        self.state.event_sequence += 1;

        let row = CanonicalRow {
            timestamp_ns: timestamp_ms.saturating_mul(1_000_000),
            source,
            event_sequence: self.state.event_sequence,
            lob_final_update_id: if source == Source::Lob {
                self.state.lob_final_update_id
            } else {
                None
            },
        };

        (
            row,
            Outcome {
                needs_reseed,
                applied,
            },
        )
    }

    /// Encode a row as one CSV line into the caller's reused buffer.
    ///
    /// Returns `None` if the field count does not match the schema, which
    /// would silently misalign every downstream column.
    pub fn write_csv(&mut self, row: &CanonicalRow, out: &mut FieldWriter) -> Option<()> {
        self.refresh_book();

        let ts_ms = row.timestamp_ns / 1_000_000;
        out.begin_row(row.timestamp_ns, row.source.name(), row.event_sequence);

        // LOB identity: present only on LOB rows.
        if row.source == Source::Lob {
            out.push_opt_i64(Some(ts_ms));
            out.push_opt_i64(row.lob_final_update_id);
        } else {
            out.push_null();
            out.push_null();
        }

        // Book: best 4, then levels 2..=20.
        out.push_opt_f64(self.bids.first().copied());
        out.push_opt_f64(self.bid_qty.first().copied());
        out.push_opt_f64(self.asks.first().copied());
        out.push_opt_f64(self.ask_qty.first().copied());
        for level in 1..self.levels {
            out.push_opt_f64(self.bids.get(level).copied());
            out.push_opt_f64(self.bid_qty.get(level).copied());
            out.push_opt_f64(self.asks.get(level).copied());
            out.push_opt_f64(self.ask_qty.get(level).copied());
        }

        self.write_trade_block(out, true);
        // depth__*, depth_state__* and their notional columns have NO live
        // source (Binance's bookDepth is a downloaded dataset, not a stream).
        // Always empty: never 0.0, which would read as "no liquidity".
        for _ in 0..(4 + 10 + 10) {
            out.push_null();
        }
        self.write_trade_block(out, false);
        for _ in 0..4 {
            out.push_null();
        }

        out.finish_row().map(|_| ())
    }

    /// Emit `agg__*` (8 cols) or `source_agg__*` (7 cols). Both blocks carry
    /// the same values; only the verbatim-source column set differs.
    fn write_trade_block(&self, out: &mut FieldWriter, with_qty_alias: bool) {
        let width = if with_qty_alias { 8 } else { 7 };
        let Some(trade) = self.last_trade else {
            for _ in 0..width {
                out.push_null();
            }
            return;
        };
        out.push_opt_u64(Some(trade.agg_trade_id));
        out.push_opt_f64(Some(trade.price));
        out.push_opt_f64(Some(trade.quantity));
        if with_qty_alias {
            // agg__qty is the raw Binance `q`; quantity is carried in
            // agg__quantity. The live feed gives one value, so the alias stays
            // empty rather than duplicating a value under two names.
            out.push_null();
        }
        out.push_opt_u64(Some(trade.first_trade_id));
        out.push_opt_u64(Some(trade.last_trade_id));
        out.push_opt_i64(Some(trade.transact_time_ms));
        out.push_opt_bool(Some(trade.is_buyer_maker));
    }

    /// Copy the top-N book into the reused scratch vectors. O(levels), and no
    /// allocation once the buffers have grown.
    fn refresh_book(&mut self) {
        self.bids.clear();
        self.bid_qty.clear();
        self.asks.clear();
        self.ask_qty.clear();
        for (tick, qty) in self.state.book.top_n_bids(self.levels) {
            self.bids.push(tick_to_price(tick));
            self.bid_qty.push(qty);
        }
        for (tick, qty) in self.state.book.top_n_asks(self.levels) {
            self.asks.push(tick_to_price(tick));
            self.ask_qty.push(qty);
        }
    }
}

fn to_ticks(raw: &[(f64, f64)]) -> Vec<(PriceTick, f64)> {
    raw.iter()
        .filter_map(|&(price, quantity)| {
            if quantity <= 0.0 || !quantity.is_finite() {
                return None;
            }
            price_to_tick(price).map(|tick| (tick, quantity))
        })
        .collect()
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/event_merger/live/src/reorder.rs =====
//! `reorder.rs` — deterministic total order over independently-arriving streams.
//!
//! Two sockets deliver LOB and TRADE events independently, so arrival order is
//! not event order. This buffer restores a total order using the same key the
//! historical merger uses:
//!
//! ```text
//! primary    event timestamp (exchange ms)
//! secondary  source priority     (LOB < TRADE, mirroring merge.rs Source)
//! tertiary   source-local sequence (update id / agg trade id)
//! ```
//!
//! **Same ordering semantics, not byte-identical output.** The historical
//! merger sorts a complete input set; a live feed only knows what has arrived.
//! Anything later than the watermark cannot be placed in order, so it is
//! *dropped and counted*. Dropping beats emitting a mis-ordered row: a
//! mis-ordered row silently corrupts the as-of book state of every downstream
//! consumer, whereas a drop is visible in `late_dropped`.

use std::cmp::Reverse;
use std::collections::BinaryHeap;

use crate::event::MarketEvent;

/// Total order over events.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord)]
struct OrderKey {
    timestamp_ms: i64,
    source: u8,
    source_sequence: u64,
}

impl OrderKey {
    fn of(event: &MarketEvent) -> Self {
        Self {
            timestamp_ms: event.event_time_ms(),
            source: event.source() as u8,
            source_sequence: event.source_sequence(),
        }
    }
}

/// A buffered event plus its ordering key and arrival counter.
///
/// The counter makes the order a strict *total* order even when two events
/// share timestamp, source and source-local sequence, so emission is
/// deterministic rather than dependent on heap internals.
///
/// The event is kept out of the heap's tuple so `MarketEvent` (which holds
/// `f64`) does not need to implement `Ord`.
struct Entry {
    key: OrderKey,
    arrival: u64,
    event: MarketEvent,
}

/// Bounded, watermark-driven reorder buffer.
pub struct ReorderBuffer {
    /// Min-heap over (key, arrival); yields the smallest key first.
    heap: BinaryHeap<Reverse<(OrderKey, u64)>>,
    /// Events awaiting emission, in no particular order.
    pending: Vec<Entry>,
    /// Highest timestamp seen so far; the watermark trails it.
    watermark_ms: i64,
    lateness_ms: i64,
    late_dropped: u64,
    capacity: usize,
    overflow_dropped: u64,
    arrival: u64,
}

impl ReorderBuffer {
    /// `lateness_ms` is the reorder window: how far out-of-order the two
    /// sockets may interleave before events are dropped rather than emitted
    /// mis-ordered. It also bounds memory to a few thousand events.
    pub fn new(lateness_ms: i64, capacity: usize) -> Self {
        Self {
            heap: BinaryHeap::new(),
            pending: Vec::with_capacity(capacity.min(1024)),
            watermark_ms: i64::MIN,
            lateness_ms,
            late_dropped: 0,
            capacity,
            overflow_dropped: 0,
            arrival: 0,
        }
    }

    /// Offer an event; returns the events that are now safe to emit.
    pub fn push(&mut self, event: MarketEvent) -> Vec<MarketEvent> {
        let key = OrderKey::of(&event);

        // Late-event policy: an event older than the emission cutoff can no
        // longer be placed in order, so it is dropped and counted. The
        // comparison is against the *cutoff* (watermark minus the lateness
        // window), not the watermark itself -- otherwise every out-of-order
        // arrival would be discarded and the window would have no effect.
        if self.watermark_ms != i64::MIN && key.timestamp_ms < self.watermark_ms - self.lateness_ms
        {
            self.late_dropped += 1;
            return Vec::new();
        }

        // Backpressure guard: a stalled writer must not grow memory forever.
        if self.pending.len() >= self.capacity {
            self.overflow_dropped += 1;
            return Vec::new();
        }

        if key.timestamp_ms > self.watermark_ms {
            self.watermark_ms = key.timestamp_ms;
        }
        let arrival = self.arrival;
        self.arrival += 1;
        self.heap.push(Reverse((key, arrival)));
        self.pending.push(Entry {
            key,
            arrival,
            event,
        });

        self.drain_ready()
    }

    /// Flush everything still buffered, e.g. on shutdown.
    pub fn flush(&mut self) -> Vec<MarketEvent> {
        let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(self.pending.len());
        self.pending.sort_by_key(|entry| (entry.key, entry.arrival));
        out.extend(self.pending.drain(..).map(|entry| entry.event));
        self.heap.clear();
        out
    }

    pub fn late_dropped(&self) -> u64 {
        self.late_dropped
    }

    pub fn overflow_dropped(&self) -> u64 {
        self.overflow_dropped
    }

    pub fn len(&self) -> usize {
        self.pending.len()
    }

    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.pending.is_empty()
    }

    /// Pop every event safely behind the watermark. O(k log n).
    fn drain_ready(&mut self) -> Vec<MarketEvent> {
        if self.watermark_ms == i64::MIN {
            return Vec::new();
        }
        let cutoff = self.watermark_ms - self.lateness_ms;
        let mut ready: Vec<Entry> = Vec::new();
        // The heap top is the smallest key, so stop as soon as it is not safe.
        // Copy the key out before popping: peeking borrows `self.heap`
        // immutably, which cannot overlap the `pop` below.
        loop {
            let Some(Reverse((key, arrival))) = self.heap.peek().copied() else {
                break;
            };
            if key.timestamp_ms > cutoff {
                break;
            }
            self.heap.pop();
            if let Some(position) = self.pending.iter().position(|e| e.arrival == arrival) {
                ready.push(self.pending.remove(position));
            }
        }
        ready.sort_by_key(|entry| (entry.key, entry.arrival));
        ready.into_iter().map(|entry| entry.event).collect()
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::event::{DiffFrame, Source, Trade};

    fn trade(ts: i64, id: u64) -> MarketEvent {
        MarketEvent::Trade(Trade {
            event_time_ms: ts,
            transact_time_ms: ts,
            agg_trade_id: id,
            first_trade_id: id,
            last_trade_id: id,
            price: 100.0,
            quantity: 1.0,
            is_buyer_maker: false,
        })
    }

    fn lob(ts: i64, last_id: u64) -> MarketEvent {
        MarketEvent::Diff(DiffFrame {
            event_time_ms: ts,
            first_update_id: last_id,
            final_update_id: last_id,
            updates: Vec::new(),
        })
    }

    #[test]
    fn orders_out_of_order_arrivals() {
        // Window (250ms) must exceed the 200ms spread being reordered, or
        // the stragglers are legitimately late and dropped instead.
        let mut buffer = ReorderBuffer::new(250, 1024);
        let mut all = Vec::new();
        // Arrive backwards; the watermark holds them until ordering is safe.
        all.extend(buffer.push(trade(300, 3)));
        all.extend(buffer.push(trade(100, 1)));
        all.extend(buffer.push(trade(200, 2)));
        all.extend(buffer.push(trade(500, 5)));
        all.extend(buffer.flush());
        let times: Vec<i64> = all.iter().map(|e| e.event_time_ms()).collect();
        assert_eq!(times, vec![100, 200, 300, 500], "reordered, not dropped");
    }

    #[test]
    fn drops_events_later_than_the_watermark() {
        let mut buffer = ReorderBuffer::new(50, 1024);
        buffer.push(trade(1000, 1));
        let _ = buffer.push(trade(200, 2));
        assert_eq!(buffer.late_dropped(), 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn capacity_bounds_memory() {
        let mut buffer = ReorderBuffer::new(10_000, 8);
        for i in 0..20u64 {
            buffer.push(trade((i * 10) as i64, i));
        }
        assert!(buffer.overflow_dropped() > 0);
        assert!(buffer.len() <= 8);
    }

    #[test]
    fn lob_sorts_before_trade_at_equal_timestamp() {
        let mut buffer = ReorderBuffer::new(1, 1024);
        let mut all = Vec::new();
        all.extend(buffer.push(trade(500, 1)));
        all.extend(buffer.push(lob(500, 7)));
        all.extend(buffer.flush());
        assert_eq!(all[0].source(), Source::Lob);
    }

    #[test]
    fn flush_emits_in_total_order() {
        let mut buffer = ReorderBuffer::new(1_000_000, 1024);
        buffer.push(trade(300, 3));
        buffer.push(trade(100, 1));
        buffer.push(trade(200, 2));
        let times: Vec<i64> = buffer.flush().iter().map(|e| e.event_time_ms()).collect();
        assert_eq!(times, vec![100, 200, 300]);
    }
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/event_merger/live/src/row.rs =====
//! `row.rs` â€” the canonical 128-column row and its sparse CSV encoding.
//!
//! Column order and names are byte-compatible with the historical output
//! (`event_merger/merger/writer.rs::state_fields`), so a live capture and a
//! historical merge can be read by the same feature code.
//!
//! Sparsity is the point: 100 of the 128 columns are unchanged between book
//! updates, but a fixed-width format still has to emit them. The win therefore
//! comes from *not allocating* them â€” one buffer is reused for every row, and
//! a null costs nothing but a separator.

use std::fmt::Write as _;
use std::sync::OnceLock;

/// Band labels in emission order: -5..-1 then 1..5 (0 is excluded).
const BAND_LABELS: [&str; 10] = ["-5", "-4", "-3", "-2", "-1", "1", "2", "3", "4", "5"];

/// Number of columns per row. Guards against silently misaligned output.
pub const FIELD_COUNT: usize = 128;

/// The 128 column names, in emission order. Built once; not a hot path.
pub fn schema() -> &'static [&'static str] {
    static LEAKED: OnceLock<&'static [&'static str]> = OnceLock::new();
    *LEAKED.get_or_init(|| Box::leak(build_schema().into_boxed_slice()))
}

fn intern(value: String) -> &'static str {
    Box::leak(value.into_boxed_str())
}

fn build_schema() -> Vec<&'static str> {
    let mut names: Vec<&'static str> = Vec::with_capacity(FIELD_COUNT);
    names.extend([
        "timestamp",
        "event_type",
        "event_sequence",
        "lob__event_time",
        "lob__final_update_id",
        "book_state__best_bid_price",
        "book_state__best_bid_qty",
        "book_state__best_ask_price",
        "book_state__best_ask_qty",
    ]);
    // Levels 2..=20, four columns each = 76.
    for level in 2..=20 {
        names.push(intern(format!("book_state__bid_price_{level}")));
        names.push(intern(format!("book_state__bid_qty_{level}")));
        names.push(intern(format!("book_state__ask_price_{level}")));
        names.push(intern(format!("book_state__ask_qty_{level}")));
    }
    names.extend([
        "agg__agg_trade_id",
        "agg__price",
        "agg__quantity",
        "agg__qty",
        "agg__first_trade_id",
        "agg__last_trade_id",
        "agg__transact_time",
        "agg__is_buyer_maker",
        "depth__timestamp",
        "depth__percentage",
        "depth__depth",
        "depth__notional",
    ]);
    for band in BAND_LABELS {
        names.push(intern(format!("depth_state__{band}")));
    }
    for band in BAND_LABELS {
        names.push(intern(format!("depth_state__notional_{band}")));
    }
    names.extend([
        "source_agg__agg_trade_id",
        "source_agg__price",
        "source_agg__quantity",
        "source_agg__first_trade_id",
        "source_agg__last_trade_id",
        "source_agg__transact_time",
        "source_agg__is_buyer_maker",
        "source_depth__timestamp",
        "source_depth__percentage",
        "source_depth__depth",
        "source_depth__notional",
    ]);
    names
}

/// Reusable, allocation-free-per-row CSV field writer.
///
/// The buffer is owned here and cleared per row, so steady-state emission does
/// no allocator work: a null field costs one separator, a populated field is
/// formatted in place.
pub struct FieldWriter {
    buf: String,
    fields: usize,
    expected: usize,
}

impl FieldWriter {
    pub fn new(expected_fields: usize) -> Self {
        Self {
            // One merged row is ~2 KB; reserving it once avoids regrowth.
            buf: String::with_capacity(4096),
            fields: 0,
            expected: expected_fields,
        }
    }

    /// Start a new row and write the header line.
    pub fn begin_header(&mut self) {
        self.buf.clear();
        self.fields = 0;
        for name in schema() {
            self.push_str(name);
        }
        self.newline();
    }

    /// Begin a row, writing the common leading fields.
    ///
    /// `timestamp` is epoch **nanoseconds**, matching the historical column.
    pub fn begin_row(&mut self, timestamp_ns: i64, event_type: &str, sequence: u64) {
        self.buf.clear();
        self.fields = 0;
        self.push_i64(timestamp_ns);
        self.push_str(event_type);
        self.push_u64(sequence);
    }

    /// Emit an explicitly empty field.
    pub fn push_null(&mut self) {
        self.fields += 1;
        if self.fields > 1 {
            self.buf.push(',');
        }
    }

    pub fn push_opt_i64(&mut self, value: Option<i64>) {
        match value {
            Some(v) => self.push_i64(v),
            None => self.push_null(),
        }
    }

    pub fn push_opt_u64(&mut self, value: Option<u64>) {
        match value {
            Some(v) => self.push_u64(v),
            None => self.push_null(),
        }
    }

    pub fn push_opt_f64(&mut self, value: Option<f64>) {
        match value {
            // Non-finite would serialize as `NaN`/`inf`, which is not valid
            // CSV for the downstream reader. Treat it as missing.
            Some(v) if v.is_finite() => self.push_f64(v),
            _ => self.push_null(),
        }
    }

    pub fn push_opt_bool(&mut self, value: Option<bool>) {
        match value {
            Some(v) => self.push_str(if v { "True" } else { "False" }),
            None => self.push_null(),
        }
    }

    /// Number of fields written to the row currently in the buffer.
    pub fn field_count(&self) -> usize {
        self.fields
    }

    /// Take the line currently buffered (header or row) and clear the buffer.
    ///
    /// Lets a caller assemble several encoded lines before writing them out,
    /// which keeps the syscall count low without exposing the buffer.
    pub fn take_row(&mut self) -> String {
        self.fields = 0;
        std::mem::take(&mut self.buf)
    }

    /// Finish the row and return the encoded line (newline included).
    ///
    /// Returns `None` when the field count does not match the schema, which
    /// would silently shift every downstream column.
    pub fn finish_row(&mut self) -> Option<&str> {
        if self.fields != self.expected {
            return None;
        }
        self.newline();
        Some(&self.buf)
    }

    fn push_i64(&mut self, value: i64) {
        self.fields += 1;
        if self.fields > 1 {
            self.buf.push(',');
        }
        let _ = write!(self.buf, "{value}");
    }

    fn push_u64(&mut self, value: u64) {
        self.fields += 1;
        if self.fields > 1 {
            self.buf.push(',');
        }
        let _ = write!(self.buf, "{value}");
    }

    fn push_f64(&mut self, value: f64) {
        self.fields += 1;
        if self.fields > 1 {
            self.buf.push(',');
        }
        let _ = write!(self.buf, "{value}");
    }

    fn push_str(&mut self, value: &str) {
        self.fields += 1;
        if self.fields > 1 {
            self.buf.push(',');
        }
        self.buf.push_str(value);
    }

    fn newline(&mut self) {
        self.buf.push('\n');
        self.fields = 0;
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn schema_has_128_unique_columns_in_order() {
        let names = schema();
        assert_eq!(names.len(), FIELD_COUNT);
        assert_eq!(names[0], "timestamp");
        assert_eq!(names[1], "event_type");
        assert_eq!(names[2], "event_sequence");
        assert_eq!(names[3], "lob__event_time");
        assert_eq!(names[4], "lob__final_update_id");
        assert_eq!(names[5], "book_state__best_bid_price");
        assert_eq!(names[9], "book_state__bid_price_2");
        // 5 common + 4 best-of-book + 19 levels x 4 == 85.
        assert_eq!(names[85], "agg__agg_trade_id");
        assert_eq!(names[93], "depth__timestamp");
        assert_eq!(names[97], "depth_state__-5");
        assert_eq!(names[107], "depth_state__notional_-5");
        assert_eq!(names[117], "source_agg__agg_trade_id");
        assert_eq!(names[127], "source_depth__notional");
        let unique: std::collections::HashSet<&&str> = names.iter().collect();
        assert_eq!(unique.len(), FIELD_COUNT);
    }

    #[test]
    fn writer_emits_one_field_per_column() {
        let mut w = FieldWriter::new(FIELD_COUNT);
        w.begin_row(1_751_245_200_083_000_000, "LOB", 1);
        while w.field_count() < FIELD_COUNT {
            w.push_null();
        }
        let line = w.finish_row().expect("field count matches schema");
        assert_eq!(line.matches(',').count(), FIELD_COUNT - 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn wrong_field_count_is_rejected() {
        let mut w = FieldWriter::new(FIELD_COUNT);
        w.begin_row(0, "LOB", 0);
        w.push_null();
        assert!(w.finish_row().is_none(), "short row must not be emitted");
    }

    #[test]
    fn non_finite_numbers_become_empty_fields() {
        // begin_row emits 3 fields (timestamp, event_type, sequence).
        let mut w = FieldWriter::new(6);
        w.begin_row(0, "LOB", 0);
        w.push_opt_f64(Some(f64::NAN));
        w.push_opt_f64(Some(1.5));
        w.push_opt_f64(Some(f64::INFINITY));
        let line = w.finish_row().expect("field count matches");
        assert_eq!(line, "0,LOB,0,,1.5,\n");
    }

    #[test]
    fn nulls_are_empty_not_zero() {
        // begin_row emits 3 fields; 3 more nulls makes 6.
        let mut w = FieldWriter::new(6);
        w.begin_row(0, "TRADE", 0);
        w.push_null();
        w.push_null();
        w.push_null();
        let line = w.finish_row().expect("ok");
        // The guard that keeps a fabricated 0.0 out of absent liquidity.
        assert_eq!(line, "0,TRADE,0,,,\n");
    }

    #[test]
    fn header_matches_schema() {
        let mut w = FieldWriter::new(FIELD_COUNT);
        w.begin_header();
        assert_eq!(w.field_count(), 0, "header resets the row counter");
    }
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/event_merger/live/src/state.rs =====
//! `state.rs` — the live state carried between events.
//!
//! Holds the full-depth book, the sequence bookkeeping used to detect diff
//! gaps, and the validity flags that make a compromised book *visible* rather
//! than silently wrong.

use crate::book::{LobBook, PriceTick};
use crate::event::{DiffFrame, Source, Trade};

/// Why the book cannot currently be trusted.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum BookHealth {
    /// Book reflects every diff event received so far.
    Valid,
    /// A sequence gap was detected; the book is stale until a re-seed lands.
    Stale,
    /// No snapshot has been applied yet.
    Unseeded,
}

impl BookHealth {
    pub fn is_valid(self) -> bool {
        matches!(self, Self::Valid)
    }
}

/// Counters for operational visibility. Cheap to update, always surfaced.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default)]
pub struct Counters {
    pub events_applied: u64,
    pub diff_frames: u64,
    pub trades: u64,
    /// Frames rejected because they arrived after the watermark.
    pub late_dropped: u64,
    /// Sequence gaps detected on the diff stream.
    pub gaps: u64,
    /// REST snapshot seeds applied (initial + re-seeds).
    pub seeds_applied: u64,
    /// Updates dropped because their price could not be represented as a tick.
    pub bad_price_dropped: u64,
}

/// Everything the merger needs to emit one canonical row.
pub struct State {
    pub book: LobBook,
    /// Last `final_update_id` applied to the book, if the book is seeded.
    pub last_update_id: Option<u64>,
    /// As-of LOB identity for the next emitted row.
    pub lob_event_time_ms: Option<i64>,
    pub lob_final_update_id: Option<i64>,
    /// Monotonic emission counter.
    pub event_sequence: u64,
    pub health: BookHealth,
    pub counters: Counters,
}

impl Default for State {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            book: LobBook::new(),
            last_update_id: None,
            lob_event_time_ms: None,
            lob_final_update_id: None,
            event_sequence: 0,
            health: BookHealth::Unseeded,
            counters: Counters::default(),
        }
    }
}

impl State {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    /// Number of price levels the book considers valid to emit.
    pub fn book_valid(&self) -> bool {
        self.health.is_valid() && !self.book.is_empty()
    }

    /// `depth__*` / `depth_state__*` have no live source (Binance's bookDepth
    /// is a downloaded dataset, not a stream), so they are always absent.
    /// Consumers must treat these as *missing*, never as zero.
    pub fn depth_valid(&self) -> bool {
        false
    }

    /// Mark the book stale. Called on a detected sequence gap; the caller is
    /// responsible for triggering a re-seed.
    pub fn mark_gap(&mut self) {
        self.health = BookHealth::Stale;
        self.counters.gaps += 1;
    }

    /// Apply a trusted full snapshot, clearing any stale marking.
    pub fn apply_seed(
        &mut self,
        bids: &[(PriceTick, f64)],
        asks: &[(PriceTick, f64)],
        last_update_id: u64,
    ) {
        self.book.apply_snapshot(bids, asks);
        self.last_update_id = Some(last_update_id);
        self.health = if self.book.is_empty() {
            BookHealth::Unseeded
        } else {
            BookHealth::Valid
        };
        self.counters.seeds_applied += 1;
    }

    /// Apply one diff frame, detecting sequence gaps.
    ///
    /// Binance's diff stream is ordered, so continuity is
    /// `first_update_id <= last_applied + 1`. Anything else is a gap: the book
    /// is marked stale and the frame is *still applied* (best effort) while the
    /// caller re-seeds. Historical replay treats the same condition as
    /// `first_update_id > last_final_update_id + 1`.
    pub fn apply_diff(&mut self, frame: &DiffFrame) -> bool {
        self.counters.diff_frames += 1;

        if let Some(last) = self.last_update_id {
            if frame.first_update_id > last.saturating_add(1) {
                self.mark_gap();
            }
        }

        for update in &frame.updates {
            let Some(tick) = crate::book::price_to_tick(update.price) else {
                self.counters.bad_price_dropped += 1;
                continue;
            };
            self.book.apply_update(update.is_bid, tick, update.quantity);
        }

        self.last_update_id = Some(frame.final_update_id);
        self.lob_event_time_ms = Some(frame.event_time_ms);
        self.lob_final_update_id = Some(frame.final_update_id as i64);
        self.counters.events_applied += 1;
        true
    }

    /// Record a trade. The book is untouched: the as-of snapshot is read at
    /// emit time, not captured here.
    pub fn apply_trade(&mut self, _trade: &Trade) {
        self.counters.trades += 1;
        self.counters.events_applied += 1;
    }

    /// Source priority of the last applied event, for row labelling.
    pub fn last_source(&self) -> Source {
        if self.lob_event_time_ms.is_some() {
            Source::Lob
        } else {
            Source::Trade
        }
    }
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/event_merger/merger/args.rs =====
//! CLI args for `event_merger`.

use std::path::PathBuf;

pub struct MergeArgs {
    pub root: PathBuf,
    pub symbol: String,
    pub source: String,    // "ingest_parquet" | "matched"
    pub date: String,      // YYYY-MM-DD
    pub hour: Option<u32>, // None means the whole UTC date (all 24 outputs)
    pub matched_lob_root: Option<PathBuf>,
    pub lob_levels: usize,
}

pub type BoxError = Box<dyn std::error::Error>;

fn next_value<'a>(args: &'a [String], i: &mut usize, flag: &str) -> Result<&'a str, BoxError> {
    let value = args
        .get(*i + 1)
        .ok_or_else(|| format!("{flag} requires a value"))?;
    *i += 2;
    Ok(value.as_str())
}

pub fn parse_args(args: &[String]) -> Result<MergeArgs, BoxError> {
    let mut root = None;
    let mut symbol = String::from("BTCUSDT");
    let mut source = String::from("ingest_parquet");
    let mut date = None;
    let mut hour = None;
    let mut matched_lob_root = None;
    let mut lob_levels = 20usize;

    let mut i = 0;
    while i < args.len() {
        match args[i].as_str() {
            "--root" => root = Some(PathBuf::from(next_value(args, &mut i, "--root")?)),
            "--symbol" => {
                symbol = next_value(args, &mut i, "--symbol")?.to_uppercase();
            }
            "--source" => {
                source = next_value(args, &mut i, "--source")?.to_string();
            }
            "--date" => {
                date = Some(next_value(args, &mut i, "--date")?.to_string());
            }
            "--hour" => {
                let raw = next_value(args, &mut i, "--hour")?;
                if raw.eq_ignore_ascii_case("all") || raw == "-1" {
                    hour = None;
                } else {
                    let value = raw.parse::<u32>()?;
                    if value > 23 {
                        return Err("--hour must be 0..23, all, or -1".into());
                    }
                    hour = Some(value);
                }
            }
            "--matched-lob-root" => {
                matched_lob_root = Some(PathBuf::from(next_value(
                    args,
                    &mut i,
                    "--matched-lob-root",
                )?));
            }
            "--lob-levels" => {
                lob_levels = next_value(args, &mut i, "--lob-levels")?.parse()?;
            }
            "--keep-inputs" | "--delete-inputs" => {
                i += 1;
            }
            other => return Err(format!("unknown flag `{other}`").into()),
        }
    }

    let date = date.ok_or("--date YYYY-MM-DD is required")?;
    if date.len() != 10 {
        return Err(format!("bad --date `{date}`, expected YYYY-MM-DD").into());
    }
    if lob_levels == 0 {
        return Err("--lob-levels must be greater than zero".into());
    }
    if source != "ingest_parquet" && source != "matched" {
        return Err(format!("bad --source `{source}`, expected ingest_parquet|matched").into());
    }

    Ok(MergeArgs {
        root: root.ok_or("--root market_data dir is required")?,
        symbol,
        source,
        date,
        hour,
        matched_lob_root,
        lob_levels,
    })
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/event_merger/merger/layout.rs =====
//! Input/output layout + UTC hour-window math.

use std::path::PathBuf;

use crate::args::MergeArgs;

pub type BoxError = Box<dyn std::error::Error>;

/// Millis of UTC midnight for a YYYY-MM-DD (days-from-civil, Hinnant).
pub fn midnight_ms(date: &str) -> Result<i64, BoxError> {
    let y: i64 = date[0..4].parse()?;
    let m: i64 = date[5..7].parse()?;
    let d: i64 = date[8..10].parse()?;
    let y_adj = if m <= 2 { y - 1 } else { y };
    let era = if y_adj >= 0 { y_adj } else { y_adj - 399 } / 400;
    let yoe = y_adj - era * 400;
    let mp = (m + 9) % 12;
    let doy = (153 * mp + 2) / 5 + d - 1;
    let doe = yoe * 365 + yoe / 4 - yoe / 100 + doy;
    let days = era * 146097 + doe - 719468;
    Ok(days * 86_400_000)
}

pub struct HourInputs {
    pub lob_paths: Vec<PathBuf>,
    pub agg_path: PathBuf,
    pub depth_path: PathBuf,
    pub output_dir: PathBuf,
    pub day_start_ns: i64,
    pub day_end_ns: i64,
}

fn first_matching_file(root: &std::path::Path, date: &str, hour: u32) -> Option<std::path::PathBuf> {
    if root.is_file() {
        return Some(root.to_path_buf());
    }
    let hh = format!("{hour:02}");
    let mut stack = vec![root.to_path_buf()];
    while let Some(dir) = stack.pop() {
        let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(dir) else { continue };
        for entry in entries.flatten() {
            let path = entry.path();
            if path.is_dir() {
                stack.push(path);
                continue;
            }
            if path.extension().and_then(|value| value.to_str()) != Some("parquet") {
                continue;
            }
            let name = path.file_name().and_then(|value| value.to_str()).unwrap_or_default();
            if name == format!("{hh}.parquet")
                || name == format!("{hh}_every_event.parquet")
                || name.ends_with("_every_event.parquet")
                || name.contains(&format!("{date}_{hh}"))
                || name.contains(&format!("{date}-{hh}"))
                || name.contains(&format!("{date}_{date}_{hh}-{hh}"))
            {
                return Some(path);
            }
        }
    }
    None
}

fn matched_lob_path(
    work: &std::path::Path,
    ticker_root: &std::path::Path,
    ingest_root: &std::path::Path,
    symbol: &str,
    date: &str,
    hour: u32,
) -> std::path::PathBuf {
    let hh = format!("{hour:02}");
    let candidates = [
        work.join("orderflow")
            .join(date)
            .join(&hh)
            .join(format!("{hh}_every_event.parquet")),
        work.join("orderflow")
            .join(date)
            .join(&hh)
            .join(format!("{hh}.parquet")),
        work.join(date)
            .join(&hh)
            .join(format!("{hh}_every_event.parquet")),
        work.join(date)
            .join(&hh)
            .join(format!("{hh}.parquet")),
        work.join(&hh).join(format!("{hh}_every_event.parquet")),
        work.join(&hh).join(format!("{hh}.parquet")),
        work.join(format!("{hh}_every_event.parquet")),
        work.join("lob_state")
            .join(date)
            .join(format!("{hh}.parquet")),
        work.join(date).join(format!("{hh}.parquet")),
        work.join(format!("{hh}.parquet")),
        work.join("source")
            .join("orderbook")
            .join(date)
            .join(format!("{hh}.parquet")),
        work.join("source").join("orderbook").join(format!("{hh}.parquet")),
        ingest_root
            .join("orderbook")
            .join(date)
            .join(format!("{hh}.parquet")),
        ticker_root.join(format!("{symbol}_{date}_{date}_{hh}-{hh}.parquet")),
        ticker_root
            .join("snapshots")
            .join(format!("{date}_{hh}.parquet")),
        ticker_root
            .join("snapshots")
            .join(date)
            .join(format!("{hh}.parquet")),
    ];
    candidates
        .iter()
        .find(|path| path.exists())
        .cloned()
        .or_else(|| first_matching_file(work, date, hour))
        .or_else(|| first_matching_file(ticker_root, date, hour))
        .unwrap_or_else(|| candidates[0].clone())
}

pub fn hour_inputs(args: &MergeArgs) -> Result<HourInputs, BoxError> {
    let day_start_ms = midnight_ms(&args.date)?;
    let day_start_ns = day_start_ms.saturating_mul(1_000_000);
    let day_end_ns = day_start_ns.saturating_add(86_400_000i64.saturating_mul(1_000_000));
    let ticker_root = args.root.join("matched").join(&args.symbol);
    let work = ticker_root.join("_pipeline");
    let ingest_root = args.root.join("ingest_parquet").join(&args.symbol);
    let work_agg = work
        .join("source")
        .join("aggTrades")
        .join(format!("{}.parquet", args.date));
    let work_depth = work
        .join("source")
        .join("bookDepth")
        .join(format!("{}.parquet", args.date));
    let (agg_path, depth_path) = if work_agg.exists() && work_depth.exists() {
        (work_agg, work_depth)
    } else {
        (
            ingest_root
                .join("aggTrades")
                .join(format!("{}.parquet", args.date)),
            ingest_root
                .join("bookDepth")
                .join(format!("{}.parquet", args.date)),
        )
    };
    let lob_work = args
        .matched_lob_root
        .as_deref()
        .map(std::path::Path::to_path_buf)
        .unwrap_or_else(|| work.clone());
    let lob_paths = if lob_work.is_file() {
        vec![lob_work]
    } else {
        match args.hour {
            Some(hour) => {
                let path = matched_lob_path(
                    &lob_work,
                    &ticker_root,
                    &ingest_root,
                    &args.symbol,
                    &args.date,
                    hour,
                );
                if path.exists() { vec![path] } else { Vec::new() }
            }
            None => (0..24)
                .map(|hour| {
                    matched_lob_path(
                        &lob_work,
                        &ticker_root,
                        &ingest_root,
                        &args.symbol,
                        &args.date,
                        hour,
                    )
                })
                .filter(|path| path.exists())
                .collect(),
        }
    };
    let output_dir = args
        .root
        .join("matched")
        .join(&args.symbol)
        .join("sparse");

    Ok(HourInputs {
        lob_paths,
        agg_path,
        depth_path,
        output_dir,
        day_start_ns,
        day_end_ns,
    })
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/event_merger/merger/main.rs =====
//! `event_merger` — unified event timeline over LOB book-state + aggTrades + bookDepth.
//!
//! LOB replay is deliberately outside this binary. The merger reads materialized
//! LOB rows, keeps every aggTrades/bookDepth source row, and writes a sparse,
//! hourly chronological stream. Source-specific fields stay null on other event
//! types; Python may later forward-fill persistent state explicitly.
//!
//! Timeline uses received_time when present, otherwise event_time, for LOB rows;
//! aggTrades use transact_time and bookDepth uses timestamp. Source-specific
//! state stays null until its source is triggered; this binary never mutates
//! or deletes `ingest_parquet` or existing matched samples.

pub mod args;
pub mod layout;
pub mod merge;
pub mod model;
pub mod stream;
pub mod writer;

use std::process::ExitCode;

fn print_usage() {
    eprintln!(
        "event_merger --root <market_data> --symbol <SYM> \
         --source <ingest_parquet|matched> --date <YYYY-MM-DD> \
         [--hour <0..23|all>] [--lob-levels <N>]"
    );
}

fn main() -> ExitCode {
    let raw: Vec<String> = std::env::args().skip(1).collect();
    if raw.iter().any(|arg| arg == "-h" || arg == "--help") {
        print_usage();
        return ExitCode::SUCCESS;
    }
    match args::parse_args(&raw).and_then(|a| merge::run_merge(&a)) {
        Ok(()) => ExitCode::SUCCESS,
        Err(err) => {
            eprintln!("error: {err}");
            print_usage();
            ExitCode::FAILURE
        }
    }
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/event_merger/merger/merge.rs =====
//! Bounded three-way chronological merge driver.

use std::cmp::Reverse;
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BinaryHeap};

use crate::args::{BoxError, MergeArgs};
use crate::layout::hour_inputs;
use crate::model::{Cursor, Event, Source};
use crate::stream::{next_depth, next_lob, next_trade};
use crate::writer::{self, HourWriter, SourceSchema};

pub fn run_merge(args: &MergeArgs) -> Result<(), BoxError> {
    let inputs = hour_inputs(args)?;
    if !inputs.agg_path.exists() {
        return Err(format!("aggTrades input not found: {}", inputs.agg_path.display()).into());
    }
    if !inputs.depth_path.exists() {
        return Err(format!("bookDepth input not found: {}", inputs.depth_path.display()).into());
    }
    let mut lob = Vec::new();
    for path in inputs.lob_paths.iter().filter(|p| p.exists()) {
        lob.push(Cursor::open(path)?);
    }
    if lob.is_empty() {
        return Err("no LOB book-state input found".into());
    }
    let mut trade = Cursor::open(&inputs.agg_path)?;
    let mut depth = Cursor::open(&inputs.depth_path)?;
    let mut depth_state = BTreeMap::new();
    let mut sequence = 0u64;
    let mut pending: Vec<Option<Event>> = (0..lob.len() + 2).map(|_| None).collect();
    let mut heap: BinaryHeap<Reverse<(i64, u8, usize)>> = BinaryHeap::new();

    let selected_start = args
        .hour
        .map(|hour| inputs.day_start_ns + hour as i64 * 3_600_000_000_000);
    let selected_end = selected_start.map(|start| start + 3_600_000_000_000);
    let in_selected_window = |timestamp: i64| {
        selected_start.is_none_or(|start| {
            timestamp >= start && timestamp < selected_end.expect("selected end")
        })
    };

    for stream in 0..lob.len() {
        loop {
            let Some(event) = next_lob(&mut lob[stream], args.lob_levels, 0)? else {
                break;
            };
            if !in_selected_window(event.timestamp) {
                if selected_end.is_some_and(|end| event.timestamp >= end) {
                    break;
                }
                continue;
            }
            heap.push(Reverse((event.timestamp, 0, stream)));
            pending[stream] = Some(event);
            break;
        }
    }
    loop {
        let Some(event) = next_trade(&mut trade, 0)? else {
            break;
