        };
        if !in_selected_window(event.timestamp) {
            if selected_end.is_some_and(|end| event.timestamp >= end) {
                break;
            }
            continue;
        }
        heap.push(Reverse((event.timestamp, 1, lob.len())));
        pending[lob.len()] = Some(event);
        break;
    }
    loop {
        let Some(event) = next_depth(&mut depth, 0)? else {
            break;
        };
        if !in_selected_window(event.timestamp) {
            if selected_end.is_some_and(|end| event.timestamp >= end) {
                break;
            }
            continue;
        }
        heap.push(Reverse((event.timestamp, 2, lob.len() + 1)));
        pending[lob.len() + 1] = Some(event);
        break;
    }

    let hours: Vec<u32> = args
        .hour
        .map(|h| vec![h])
        .unwrap_or_else(|| (0..24).collect());
    let mut source_fields = lob
        .iter()
        .map(|cursor| SourceSchema {
            source: Source::Lob,
            schema: cursor.schema(),
        })
        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
    source_fields.push(SourceSchema {
        source: Source::Trade,
        schema: trade.schema(),
    });
    source_fields.push(SourceSchema {
        source: Source::Depth,
        schema: depth.schema(),
    });
    let (merged_schema, source_outputs) = writer::source_schema(args.lob_levels, &source_fields)?;
    let mut writers: BTreeMap<u32, HourWriter> = BTreeMap::new();
    while let Some(Reverse((timestamp, _kind, stream))) = heap.pop() {
        if !in_selected_window(timestamp) {
            if selected_end.is_some_and(|end| timestamp >= end) {
                break;
            }
            continue;
        }
        let hour =
            (((timestamp - inputs.day_start_ns) / 3_600_000_000_000) as i32).clamp(0, 23) as u32;
        let Some(mut event) = pending[stream].take() else {
            continue;
        };
        event.sequence = sequence;
        sequence += 1;
        if event.source == Source::Depth {
            if let Some(value) = event.depth.as_ref() {
                if let Some(percentage) = value.percentage {
                    depth_state.insert(percentage, (value.depth, value.notional));
                }
            }
            event.depth_state = Some(depth_state.clone());
        }
        if hours.contains(&hour) {
            // Replace: format!("{hour:02}.parquet")
            let output = inputs.output_dir.join(format!("{}_{hour:02}.parquet", args.date));
            if !writers.contains_key(&hour) {
                writers.insert(
                    hour,
                    HourWriter::new(
                        &output,
                        args.lob_levels,
                        merged_schema.clone(),
                        source_outputs.clone(),
                    )?,
                );
            }
            writers
                .get_mut(&hour)
                .expect("writer exists")
                .push(event.clone())?;
        }
        let source = event.source;
        loop {
            let next = match source {
                Source::Lob => next_lob(&mut lob[stream], args.lob_levels, 0)?,
                Source::Trade => next_trade(&mut trade, 0)?,
                Source::Depth => next_depth(&mut depth, 0)?,
            };
            let Some(next) = next else {
                break;
            };
            if !in_selected_window(next.timestamp) {
                if selected_end.is_some_and(|end| next.timestamp >= end) {
                    break;
                }
                continue;
            }
            let next_kind = match next.source {
                Source::Lob => 0,
                Source::Trade => 1,
                Source::Depth => 2,
            };
            heap.push(Reverse((next.timestamp, next_kind, stream)));
            pending[stream] = Some(next);
            break;
        }
    }
    for (_, writer) in writers {
        writer.finish()?;
    }
    println!(
        "MERGED source={} date={} hours={} output={}",
        args.source,
        args.date,
        hours.len(),
        inputs.output_dir.display()
    );
    Ok(())
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/event_merger/merger/model.rs =====
//! Shared event model for the streaming event merger.

use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::fs::File;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Arc;

use arrow::array::{
    Array, ArrayRef, BooleanArray, Float64Array, Int64Array, LargeStringArray, RecordBatchReader,
    StringArray, UInt64Array,
};
use arrow::datatypes::{Field, Schema};
use arrow::record_batch::RecordBatch;
use parquet_rs::arrow::arrow_reader::{ParquetRecordBatchReader, ParquetRecordBatchReaderBuilder};

pub type BoxError = Box<dyn std::error::Error>;

pub fn timestamp_ns(value: i64) -> i64 {
    let magnitude = value.unsigned_abs();
    if magnitude >= 100_000_000_000_000_000 {
        value
    } else if magnitude >= 100_000_000_000_000 {
        value.saturating_mul(1_000)
    } else if magnitude >= 100_000_000_000 {
        value.saturating_mul(1_000_000)
    } else {
        value.saturating_mul(1_000_000_000)
    }
}

fn index(batch: &RecordBatch, name: &str) -> Option<usize> {
    batch.schema().index_of(name).ok()
}

pub fn i64_at(batch: &RecordBatch, name: &str, row: usize) -> Result<Option<i64>, BoxError> {
    let Some(i) = index(batch, name) else {
        return Ok(None);
    };
    let a = batch
        .column(i)
        .as_any()
        .downcast_ref::<Int64Array>()
        .ok_or_else(|| format!("{name} is not int64"))?;
    Ok((!a.is_null(row)).then(|| a.value(row)))
}

pub fn u64_at(batch: &RecordBatch, name: &str, row: usize) -> Result<Option<u64>, BoxError> {
    let Some(i) = index(batch, name) else {
        return Ok(None);
    };
    let a = batch
        .column(i)
        .as_any()
        .downcast_ref::<UInt64Array>()
        .ok_or_else(|| format!("{name} is not uint64"))?;
    Ok((!a.is_null(row)).then(|| a.value(row)))
}

pub fn integer_at(batch: &RecordBatch, name: &str, i: usize) -> Result<Option<i64>, BoxError> {
    if let Ok(value) = i64_at(batch, name, i) {
        return Ok(value);
    }
    if let Ok(value) = u64_at(batch, name, i) {
        return Ok(value.and_then(|v| i64::try_from(v).ok()));
    }
    Ok(None)
}

pub fn f64_at(batch: &RecordBatch, name: &str, row: usize) -> Result<Option<f64>, BoxError> {
    let Some(i) = index(batch, name) else {
        return Ok(None);
    };
    let a = batch
        .column(i)
        .as_any()
        .downcast_ref::<Float64Array>()
        .ok_or_else(|| format!("{name} is not float64"))?;
    Ok((!a.is_null(row)).then(|| a.value(row)))
}

pub fn bool_at(batch: &RecordBatch, name: &str, row: usize) -> Result<Option<bool>, BoxError> {
    let Some(i) = index(batch, name) else {
        return Ok(None);
    };
    let a = batch
        .column(i)
        .as_any()
        .downcast_ref::<BooleanArray>()
        .ok_or_else(|| format!("{name} is not bool"))?;
    Ok((!a.is_null(row)).then(|| a.value(row)))
}

pub fn string_at(batch: &RecordBatch, name: &str, row: usize) -> Result<Option<String>, BoxError> {
    let Some(i) = index(batch, name) else {
        return Ok(None);
    };
    let column = batch.column(i);
    if let Some(a) = column.as_any().downcast_ref::<StringArray>() {
        return Ok((!a.is_null(row)).then(|| a.value(row).to_string()));
    }
    if let Some(a) = column.as_any().downcast_ref::<LargeStringArray>() {
        return Ok((!a.is_null(row)).then(|| a.value(row).to_string()));
    }
    Err(format!("{name} is not string or large_string").into())
}

pub struct Cursor {
    pub reader: ParquetRecordBatchReader,
    pub batch: Option<RecordBatch>,
    pub row: usize,
    pub path: PathBuf,
    /// One-row pushback used when a long-format LOB event spans Arrow batches.
    pub pushed: Option<RecordBatch>,
    pub pushed_row: usize,
}

impl Cursor {
    pub fn open(path: &Path) -> Result<Self, BoxError> {
        let reader = ParquetRecordBatchReaderBuilder::try_new(File::open(path)?)?.build()?;
        Ok(Self {
            reader,
            batch: None,
            row: 0,
            path: path.to_path_buf(),
            pushed: None,
            pushed_row: 0,
        })
    }

    pub fn schema(&self) -> Arc<Schema> {
        self.reader.schema()
    }

    pub fn next_row(&mut self) -> Result<bool, BoxError> {
        if self.pushed.is_some() {
            self.batch = self.pushed.take();
            self.row = self.pushed_row;
            return Ok(true);
        }
        loop {
            if self.batch.as_ref().is_some_and(|b| self.row < b.num_rows()) {
                return Ok(true);
            }
            match self.reader.next() {
                Some(Ok(batch)) => {
                    self.batch = Some(batch);
                    self.row = 0;
                }
                Some(Err(e)) => return Err(e.into()),
                None => {
                    self.batch = None;
                    return Ok(false);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /// Push the current physical row back so the next `next_row()` sees it.
    pub fn push_back(&mut self) {
        if let Some(batch) = self.batch.clone() {
            self.pushed = Some(batch);
            self.pushed_row = self.row;
        }
    }

    /// Consume the current physical row and count it.
    pub fn consume_row(&mut self) {
        self.row += 1;
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash)]
pub enum Source {
    Lob = 0,
    Trade = 1,
    Depth = 2,
}

impl Source {
    pub fn name(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::Lob => "LOB",
            Self::Trade => "TRADE",
            Self::Depth => "BOOKDEPTH",
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct LobState {
    pub levels: Vec<Option<f64>>,
    pub event_time: Option<i64>,
    pub final_update_id: Option<i64>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct TradeState {
    pub agg_trade_id: Option<i64>,
    pub price: Option<f64>,
    pub quantity: Option<f64>,
    pub source_qty: Option<f64>,
    pub source_quantity: Option<f64>,
    pub source_fields: Arc<Vec<Field>>,
    pub source_values: Vec<ArrayRef>,
    pub first_trade_id: Option<i64>,
    pub last_trade_id: Option<i64>,
    pub transact_time: Option<i64>,
    pub is_buyer_maker: Option<bool>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct DepthState {
    pub timestamp: Option<i64>,
    pub percentage: Option<i64>,
    pub depth: Option<f64>,
    pub notional: Option<f64>,
    pub source_fields: Arc<Vec<Field>>,
    pub source_values: Vec<ArrayRef>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Event {
    pub timestamp: i64,
    pub source: Source,
    pub sequence: u64,
    pub lob: Option<LobState>,
    pub trade: Option<TradeState>,
    pub depth: Option<DepthState>,
    pub depth_state: Option<BTreeMap<i64, (Option<f64>, Option<f64>)>>,
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/event_merger/merger/stream.rs =====
//! Streaming readers for the three source streams.
//!
//! LOB inputs are long-format LOB-builder checkpoints:
//! one physical row per resting level with `(event_time, final_update_id,
//! is_bid, price, quantity)`. `next_lob` groups every physical row sharing the
//! same `(event_time, final_update_id)` into one aggregated book-state event
//! and emits `bid_level_1..N` / `ask_level_1..N` in the canonical order.

use std::sync::Arc;

use arrow::array::Array;
use arrow::record_batch::RecordBatch;

use crate::args::BoxError;
use crate::model::{
    bool_at, f64_at, i64_at, integer_at, timestamp_ns, Cursor, DepthState, Event, LobState, Source,
    TradeState,
};

fn source_values(batch: &RecordBatch, row: usize) -> Vec<arrow::array::ArrayRef> {
    (0..batch.num_columns())
        .map(|index| batch.column(index).slice(row, 1))
        .collect()
}

fn source_fields(batch: &RecordBatch) -> Arc<Vec<arrow::datatypes::Field>> {
    Arc::new(batch.schema().fields().iter().map(|field| field.as_ref().clone()).collect())
}

pub fn next_lob(
    cursor: &mut Cursor,
    levels: usize,
    sequence: u64,
) -> Result<Option<Event>, BoxError> {
    if !cursor.next_row()? {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    let batch = cursor.batch.clone().expect("cursor row available");
    let row = cursor.row;
    cursor.consume_row();
    let event_time = i64_at(&batch, "event_time", row)?.ok_or("LOB event_time is null")?;
    let final_update_id = integer_at(&batch, "final_update_id", row)?;
    let first_is_bid = bool_at(&batch, "is_bid", row)?.ok_or("LOB is_bid is null")?;
    let first_price = f64_at(&batch, "price", row)?.ok_or("LOB price is null")?;
    let first_quantity = f64_at(&batch, "quantity", row)?.ok_or("LOB quantity is null")?;

    // Aggregate every physical level row belonging to this exchange event.
    let mut bids: Vec<(f64, f64)> = Vec::new();
    let mut asks: Vec<(f64, f64)> = Vec::new();
    if first_is_bid {
        bids.push((first_price, first_quantity));
    } else {
        asks.push((first_price, first_quantity));
    }
    loop {
        if !cursor.next_row()? {
            break;
        }
        let batch = cursor.batch.clone().expect("cursor row available");
        let row = cursor.row;
        let row_time = i64_at(&batch, "event_time", row)?.ok_or("LOB event_time is null")?;
        let row_final = integer_at(&batch, "final_update_id", row)?;
        if row_time != event_time || row_final != final_update_id {
            cursor.push_back();
            break;
        }
        let is_bid = bool_at(&batch, "is_bid", row)?.ok_or("LOB is_bid is null")?;
        let price = f64_at(&batch, "price", row)?.ok_or("LOB price is null")?;
        let quantity = f64_at(&batch, "quantity", row)?.ok_or("LOB quantity is null")?;
        if is_bid {
            bids.push((price, quantity));
        } else {
            asks.push((price, quantity));
        }
        cursor.consume_row();
    }

    // Canonical book order: bids high to low, asks low to high.
    bids.sort_by(|a, b| b.0.partial_cmp(&a.0).unwrap_or(std::cmp::Ordering::Equal));
    asks.sort_by(|a, b| a.0.partial_cmp(&b.0).unwrap_or(std::cmp::Ordering::Equal));
    let mut values = vec![None; 4 * levels];
    if let Some((price, quantity)) = bids.first() {
        values[0] = Some(*price);
        values[1] = Some(*quantity);
    }
    if let Some((price, quantity)) = asks.first() {
        values[2] = Some(*price);
        values[3] = Some(*quantity);
    }
    for level in 2..=levels {
        let base = 4 * (level - 1);
        if let Some((price, quantity)) = bids.get(level - 1) {
            values[base] = Some(*price);
            values[base + 1] = Some(*quantity);
        }
        if let Some((price, quantity)) = asks.get(level - 1) {
            values[base + 2] = Some(*price);
            values[base + 3] = Some(*quantity);
        }
    }

    Ok(Some(Event {
        timestamp: timestamp_ns(event_time),
        source: Source::Lob,
        sequence,
        lob: Some(LobState {
            levels: values,
            event_time: Some(event_time),
            final_update_id,
        }),
        trade: None,
        depth: None,
        depth_state: None,
    }))
}

pub fn next_trade(cursor: &mut Cursor, sequence: u64) -> Result<Option<Event>, BoxError> {
    if !cursor.next_row()? {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    let batch = cursor.batch.clone().expect("cursor row available");
    let row = cursor.row;
    cursor.consume_row();
    let transact_time =
        i64_at(&batch, "transact_time", row)?.ok_or("aggTrades transact_time is null")?;
    let source_qty = f64_at(&batch, "qty", row)?;
    let source_quantity = f64_at(&batch, "quantity", row)?;
    let quantity = source_quantity.or(source_qty);
    Ok(Some(Event {
        timestamp: timestamp_ns(transact_time),
        source: Source::Trade,
        sequence,
        lob: None,
        trade: Some(TradeState {
            agg_trade_id: i64_at(&batch, "agg_trade_id", row)?,
            price: f64_at(&batch, "price", row)?,
            quantity,
            source_qty,
            source_quantity,
            first_trade_id: i64_at(&batch, "first_trade_id", row)?,
            last_trade_id: i64_at(&batch, "last_trade_id", row)?,
            transact_time: Some(transact_time),
            is_buyer_maker: bool_at(&batch, "is_buyer_maker", row)?,
            source_fields: source_fields(&batch),
            source_values: source_values(&batch, row),
        }),
        depth: None,
        depth_state: None,
    }))
}

pub fn next_depth(cursor: &mut Cursor, sequence: u64) -> Result<Option<Event>, BoxError> {
    if !cursor.next_row()? {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    let batch = cursor.batch.clone().expect("cursor row available");
    let row = cursor.row;
    cursor.consume_row();
    let timestamp = i64_at(&batch, "timestamp", row)?.ok_or("bookDepth timestamp is null")?;
    let percentage = i64_at(&batch, "percentage", row)?;
    let depth = f64_at(&batch, "depth", row)?;
    let notional = f64_at(&batch, "notional", row)?;
    Ok(Some(Event {
        timestamp: timestamp_ns(timestamp),
        source: Source::Depth,
        sequence,
        lob: None,
        trade: None,
        depth: Some(DepthState {
            timestamp: Some(timestamp),
            percentage,
            depth,
            notional,
            source_fields: source_fields(&batch),
            source_values: source_values(&batch, row),
        }),
        depth_state: None,
    }))
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/event_merger/merger/writer.rs =====
//! Bounded Parquet writer for merged events.

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::fs::File;
use std::path::Path;
use std::sync::Arc;

use arrow::array::{
    new_null_array, Array, ArrayRef, BooleanArray, Float64Array, Int64Array, StringArray,
    UInt64Array,
};
use arrow::compute::concat;
use arrow::datatypes::{DataType, Field, Schema};
use arrow::record_batch::RecordBatch;
use parquet_rs::arrow::ArrowWriter;
use parquet_rs::file::properties::WriterProperties;

use crate::args::BoxError;
use crate::model::{Event, Source};

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct SourceSchema {
    pub source: Source,
    pub schema: Arc<Schema>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct SourceOutput {
    source: Source,
    source_name: String,
    data_type: DataType,
}

pub fn schema(levels: usize) -> Arc<Schema> {
    Arc::new(Schema::new(state_fields(levels)))
}

fn state_fields(levels: usize) -> Vec<Field> {
    let mut fields = vec![
        Field::new("timestamp", DataType::Int64, false),
        Field::new("event_type", DataType::Utf8, false),
        Field::new("event_sequence", DataType::UInt64, false),
        Field::new("lob__event_time", DataType::Int64, true),
        Field::new("lob__final_update_id", DataType::Int64, true),
    ];
    for name in [
        "book_state__best_bid_price",
        "book_state__best_bid_qty",
        "book_state__best_ask_price",
        "book_state__best_ask_qty",
    ] {
        fields.push(Field::new(name, DataType::Float64, true));
    }
    for level in 2..=levels {
        for name in [
            format!("book_state__bid_price_{level}"),
            format!("book_state__bid_qty_{level}"),
            format!("book_state__ask_price_{level}"),
            format!("book_state__ask_qty_{level}"),
        ] {
            fields.push(Field::new(name, DataType::Float64, true));
        }
    }
    for name in [
        "agg__agg_trade_id",
        "agg__price",
        "agg__quantity",
        "agg__qty",
        "agg__first_trade_id",
        "agg__last_trade_id",
        "agg__transact_time",
        "agg__is_buyer_maker",
    ] {
        let kind = if name == "agg__price" || name == "agg__quantity" || name == "agg__qty" {
            DataType::Float64
        } else if name == "agg__is_buyer_maker" {
            DataType::Boolean
        } else {
            DataType::Int64
        };
        fields.push(Field::new(name, kind, true));
    }
    for name in [
        "depth__timestamp",
        "depth__percentage",
        "depth__depth",
        "depth__notional",
    ] {
        let kind = if name == "depth__depth" || name == "depth__notional" {
            DataType::Float64
        } else {
            DataType::Int64
        };
        fields.push(Field::new(name, kind, true));
    }
    for band in -5..=5 {
        if band != 0 {
            fields.push(Field::new(
                format!("depth_state__{band}"),
                DataType::Float64,
                true,
            ));
        }
    }
    for band in -5..=5 {
        if band != 0 {
            fields.push(Field::new(
                format!("depth_state__notional_{band}"),
                DataType::Float64,
                true,
            ));
        }
    }
    fields
}

pub fn source_schema(
    levels: usize,
    sources: &[SourceSchema],
) -> Result<(Arc<Schema>, Vec<SourceOutput>), BoxError> {
    let mut fields = state_fields(levels);
    let mut outputs = Vec::new();
    let mut seen: HashMap<(Source, String), DataType> = HashMap::new();
    for source in sources {
        // LOB levels are emitted only through the aggregated canonical
        // book-state fields. Keeping a second verbatim `source_lob__*` copy
        // would duplicate the same resting levels, so skip the LOB source
        // schema here.
        if source.source == Source::Lob {
            continue;
        }
        for field in source.schema.fields() {
            let output_name = format!("source_{}__{}", source_prefix(source.source), field.name());
            let key = (source.source, field.name().to_string());
            if let Some(existing) = seen.get(&key) {
                if existing != field.data_type() {
                    return Err(format!(
                        "source field {}::{} has incompatible types {} and {}",
                        source_prefix(source.source), field.name(), existing, field.data_type()
                    )
                    .into());
                }
                continue;
            }
            seen.insert(key, field.data_type().clone());
            fields.push(Field::new(&output_name, field.data_type().clone(), true));
            outputs.push(SourceOutput {
                source: source.source,
                source_name: field.name().to_string(),
                data_type: field.data_type().clone(),
            });
        }
    }
    Ok((Arc::new(Schema::new(fields)), outputs))
}

fn source_prefix(source: Source) -> &'static str {
    match source { Source::Lob => "lob", Source::Trade => "agg", Source::Depth => "depth" }
}

fn i64s(values: &[Option<i64>]) -> ArrayRef {
    Arc::new(Int64Array::from(values.to_vec()))
}
fn f64s(values: &[Option<f64>]) -> ArrayRef {
    Arc::new(Float64Array::from(values.to_vec()))
}
fn bools(values: &[Option<bool>]) -> ArrayRef {
    Arc::new(BooleanArray::from(values.to_vec()))
}

fn source_arrays(
    events: &[Event],
    source_outputs: &[SourceOutput],
) -> Result<Vec<ArrayRef>, BoxError> {
    let mut output = Vec::with_capacity(source_outputs.len());
    for source in source_outputs {
        let mut pieces = Vec::with_capacity(events.len());
        for event in events {
            if event.source == source.source {
                let (fields, values) = match event.source {
                    Source::Lob => {
                        return Err("LOB source output is disabled; LOB levels use book_state__*".into())
                    }
                    Source::Trade => event.trade.as_ref().map(|x| (x.source_fields.as_ref(), x.source_values.as_slice())),
                    Source::Depth => event.depth.as_ref().map(|x| (x.source_fields.as_ref(), x.source_values.as_slice())),
                }
                .ok_or_else(|| "source state missing from event".to_string())?;
                let Some(position) = fields
                    .iter()
                    .position(|field| field.name() == source.source_name.as_str())
                else {
                    pieces.push(new_null_array(&source.data_type, 1));
                    continue;
                };
                pieces.push(values[position].slice(0, 1));
            } else {
                pieces.push(new_null_array(&source.data_type, 1));
            }
        }
        let refs: Vec<&dyn Array> = pieces.iter().map(|array| array.as_ref()).collect();
        output.push(concat(&refs)?);
    }
    Ok(output)
}

pub struct HourWriter {
    path: std::path::PathBuf,
    temp: std::path::PathBuf,
    writer: Option<ArrowWriter<File>>,
    buffer: Vec<Event>,
    rows: usize,
    levels: usize,
    schema: Arc<Schema>,
    source_outputs: Vec<SourceOutput>,
}

impl HourWriter {
    pub fn new(
        path: &Path,
        levels: usize,
        schema: Arc<Schema>,
        source_outputs: Vec<SourceOutput>,
    ) -> Result<Self, BoxError> {
        let temp = path.with_extension("tmp.parquet");
        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
            std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
        }
        let writer = ArrowWriter::try_new(
            File::create(&temp)?,
            schema.clone(),
            Some(WriterProperties::builder().build()),
        )?;
        Ok(Self {
            path: path.to_path_buf(),
            temp,
            writer: Some(writer),
            schema: schema.clone(),
            source_outputs,
            buffer: Vec::new(),
            rows: 0,
            levels,
        })
    }

    pub fn push(&mut self, event: Event) -> Result<(), BoxError> {
        self.buffer.push(event);
        self.rows += 1;
        if self.buffer.len() >= 8192 {
            self.flush()?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn flush(&mut self) -> Result<(), BoxError> {
        if self.buffer.is_empty() {
            return Ok(());
        }
        let batch = build_batch(
            &self.buffer,
            self.levels,
            self.schema.clone(),
            &self.source_outputs,
        )?;
        self.writer.as_mut().expect("writer open").write(&batch)?;
        self.buffer.clear();
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn finish(mut self) -> Result<(), BoxError> {
        self.flush()?;
        self.writer.take().expect("writer open").close()?;
        if self.rows == 0 {
            std::fs::remove_file(&self.temp)?;
            if self.path.exists() {
                std::fs::remove_file(&self.path)?;
            }
            return Ok(());
        }
        if let Some(parent) = self.path.parent() {
            std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
        }
        std::fs::rename(self.temp, &self.path)?;
        Ok(())
    }
}

fn build_batch(
    events: &[Event],
    levels: usize,
    output_schema: Arc<Schema>,
    source_outputs: &[SourceOutput],
) -> Result<RecordBatch, BoxError> {
    let mut arrays: Vec<ArrayRef> = vec![
        Arc::new(Int64Array::from(
            events.iter().map(|e| e.timestamp).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
        )),
        Arc::new(StringArray::from(
            events.iter().map(|e| e.source.name()).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
        )),
        Arc::new(UInt64Array::from(
            events.iter().map(|e| e.sequence).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
        )),
    ];
    macro_rules! li {
        ($field:ident) => {{
            let v: Vec<Option<i64>> = events
                .iter()
                .map(|e| e.lob.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.$field))
                .collect();
            arrays.push(i64s(&v));
        }};
    }
    li!(event_time);
    li!(final_update_id);
    for c in 0..4 * levels {
        let v: Vec<Option<f64>> = events
            .iter()
            .map(|e| {
                e.lob
                    .as_ref()
                    .and_then(|x| x.levels.get(c).copied().flatten())
            })
            .collect();
        arrays.push(f64s(&v));
    }
    let v: Vec<Option<i64>> = events
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.trade.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.agg_trade_id))
        .collect();
    arrays.push(i64s(&v));
    let v: Vec<Option<f64>> = events
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.trade.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.price))
        .collect();
    arrays.push(f64s(&v));
    let v: Vec<Option<f64>> = events
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.trade.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.quantity))
        .collect();
    arrays.push(f64s(&v));
    let v: Vec<Option<f64>> = events
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.trade.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.source_qty))
        .collect();
    arrays.push(f64s(&v));
    let v: Vec<Option<i64>> = events
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.trade.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.first_trade_id))
        .collect();
    arrays.push(i64s(&v));
    let v: Vec<Option<i64>> = events
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.trade.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.last_trade_id))
        .collect();
    arrays.push(i64s(&v));
    let v: Vec<Option<i64>> = events
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.trade.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.transact_time))
        .collect();
    arrays.push(i64s(&v));
    let v: Vec<Option<bool>> = events
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.trade.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.is_buyer_maker))
        .collect();
    arrays.push(bools(&v));
    let v: Vec<Option<i64>> = events
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.depth.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.timestamp))
        .collect();
    arrays.push(i64s(&v));
    let v: Vec<Option<i64>> = events
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.depth.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.percentage))
        .collect();
    arrays.push(i64s(&v));
    let v: Vec<Option<f64>> = events
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.depth.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.depth))
        .collect();
    arrays.push(f64s(&v));
    let v: Vec<Option<f64>> = events
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.depth.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.notional))
        .collect();
    arrays.push(f64s(&v));
    for band in -5..=5 {
        if band == 0 {
            continue;
        }
        let v: Vec<Option<f64>> = events
            .iter()
            .map(|e| {
                e.depth_state
                    .as_ref()
                    .and_then(|s| s.get(&band).and_then(|x| x.0))
            })
            .collect();
        arrays.push(f64s(&v));
    }
    for band in -5..=5 {
        if band == 0 {
            continue;
        }
        let v: Vec<Option<f64>> = events
            .iter()
            .map(|e| {
                e.depth_state
                    .as_ref()
                    .and_then(|s| s.get(&band).and_then(|x| x.1))
            })
            .collect();
        arrays.push(f64s(&v));
    }
    let base_len = arrays.len();
    let source_arrays = source_arrays(events, source_outputs)?;
    debug_assert_eq!(base_len + source_arrays.len(), output_schema.fields().len());
    arrays.extend(source_arrays);
    Ok(RecordBatch::try_new(output_schema, arrays)?)
}

pub fn write(path: &Path, events: &[Event], levels: usize) -> Result<(), BoxError> {
    let schema = schema(levels);
    let mut arrays: Vec<ArrayRef> = vec![
        Arc::new(Int64Array::from(
            events.iter().map(|e| e.timestamp).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
        )),
        Arc::new(StringArray::from(
            events.iter().map(|e| e.source.name()).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
        )),
        Arc::new(UInt64Array::from(
            events.iter().map(|e| e.sequence).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
        )),
    ];
    macro_rules! li {
        ($field:ident) => {{
            let v: Vec<Option<i64>> = events
                .iter()
                .map(|e| e.lob.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.$field))
                .collect();
            arrays.push(i64s(&v));
        }};
    }
    li!(event_time);
    li!(final_update_id);
    for c in 0..4 * levels {
        let v: Vec<Option<f64>> = events
            .iter()
            .map(|e| {
                e.lob
                    .as_ref()
                    .and_then(|x| x.levels.get(c).copied().flatten())
            })
            .collect();
        arrays.push(f64s(&v));
    }
    let v: Vec<Option<i64>> = events
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.trade.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.agg_trade_id))
        .collect();
    arrays.push(i64s(&v));
    let v: Vec<Option<f64>> = events
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.trade.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.price))
        .collect();
    arrays.push(f64s(&v));
    let v: Vec<Option<f64>> = events
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.trade.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.quantity))
        .collect();
    arrays.push(f64s(&v));
    let v: Vec<Option<i64>> = events
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.trade.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.first_trade_id))
        .collect();
    arrays.push(i64s(&v));
    let v: Vec<Option<i64>> = events
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.trade.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.last_trade_id))
        .collect();
    arrays.push(i64s(&v));
    let v: Vec<Option<i64>> = events
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.trade.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.transact_time))
        .collect();
    arrays.push(i64s(&v));
    let v: Vec<Option<bool>> = events
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.trade.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.is_buyer_maker))
        .collect();
    arrays.push(bools(&v));
    let v: Vec<Option<i64>> = events
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.depth.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.timestamp))
        .collect();
    arrays.push(i64s(&v));
    let v: Vec<Option<i64>> = events
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.depth.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.percentage))
        .collect();
    arrays.push(i64s(&v));
    let v: Vec<Option<f64>> = events
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.depth.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.depth))
        .collect();
    arrays.push(f64s(&v));
    let v: Vec<Option<f64>> = events
        .iter()
        .map(|e| e.depth.as_ref().and_then(|x| x.notional))
        .collect();
    arrays.push(f64s(&v));
    for band in -5..=5 {
        if band == 0 {
            continue;
        }
        let v: Vec<Option<f64>> = events
            .iter()
            .map(|e| {
                e.depth_state
                    .as_ref()
                    .and_then(|s| s.get(&band).and_then(|x| x.0))
            })
            .collect();
        arrays.push(f64s(&v));
    }
    for band in -5..=5 {
        if band == 0 {
            continue;
        }
        let v: Vec<Option<f64>> = events
            .iter()
            .map(|e| {
                e.depth_state
                    .as_ref()
                    .and_then(|s| s.get(&band).and_then(|x| x.1))
            })
            .collect();
        arrays.push(f64s(&v));
    }
    let batch = RecordBatch::try_new(schema.clone(), arrays)?;
    if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
        std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
    }
    let tmp = path.with_extension("tmp.parquet");
    let file = File::create(&tmp)?;
    let mut writer = ArrowWriter::try_new(file, schema, Some(WriterProperties::builder().build()))?;
    writer.write(&batch)?;
    writer.close()?;
    std::fs::rename(tmp, path)?;
    Ok(())
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/lob_builder/btree_ver/book.rs =====
//! `book.rs` — what the book is.
//!
//! This module owns the L2 order-book data structure and nothing else: given
//! book updates, it maintains the current bids/asks. It knows nothing about
//! parquet, the CLI, ingest mode, or matched mode — those live in
//! `parquet.rs` and `main.rs`. Whether a given update stream is *trustworthy*
//! (gaps, sequencing, warm start) is `replay.rs`'s job, not this one.

use std::collections::BTreeMap;

/// Fixed-point resolution used to key book levels as integers.
///
/// Every price the book sees — regardless of the instrument's own exchange
/// tick size — is rounded to the nearest `1 / TICK_SCALE` and stored as that
/// integer. This keeps `BTreeMap<PriceTick, Level>` lookups exact (no
/// floating-point key comparisons) while resolving prices far finer than any
/// real tick size.
///
/// Example: price `42100.10` at `TICK_SCALE = 1e8` -> tick `4_210_010_000_000`.
pub const TICK_SCALE: f64 = 1e8;

/// Price represented as an integer tick (see [`TICK_SCALE`]).
pub type PriceTick = i64;

/// Convert a price into its integer tick representation.
///
/// Returns `None` for non-finite, negative, or out-of-range prices.
pub fn price_to_tick(price: f64) -> Option<PriceTick> {
    if !price.is_finite() || price < 0.0 {
        return None;
    }

    let scaled = price * TICK_SCALE;

    if scaled > i64::MAX as f64 {
        return None;
    }

    Some(scaled.round() as PriceTick)
}

/// Convert an integer tick back into a price.
pub fn tick_to_price(tick: PriceTick) -> f64 {
    tick as f64 / TICK_SCALE
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
pub struct Level {
    pub quantity: f64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct LobBook {
    // Sorted ascending.
    // Best bid = largest key.
    bids: BTreeMap<PriceTick, Level>,

    // Sorted ascending.
    // Best ask = smallest key.
    asks: BTreeMap<PriceTick, Level>,
}

impl LobBook {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    pub fn clear(&mut self) {
        self.bids.clear();
        self.asks.clear();
    }

    pub fn bid_levels(&self) -> usize {
        self.bids.len()
    }

    pub fn ask_levels(&self) -> usize {
        self.asks.len()
    }

    /// Replace the entire book with a trusted full snapshot.
    ///
    /// IMPORTANT:
    /// We retain the FULL book internally.
    /// `levels` is only applied when emitting top-N output.
    pub fn apply_snapshot(
        &mut self,
        bids: &[(PriceTick, f64)],
        asks: &[(PriceTick, f64)],
    ) {
        self.clear();

        for &(price, quantity) in bids {
            if quantity > 0.0 {
                self.bids.insert(
                    price,
                    Level { quantity },
                );
            }
        }

        for &(price, quantity) in asks {
            if quantity > 0.0 {
                self.asks.insert(
                    price,
                    Level { quantity },
                );
            }
        }
    }

    /// Apply one update.
    ///
    /// qty > 0:
    ///     absolute new quantity at this price.
    ///
    /// qty == 0:
    ///     remove this price level.
    ///
    /// Returns whether the book changed.
    pub fn apply_update(
        &mut self,
        is_bid: bool,
        price: PriceTick,
        quantity: f64,
    ) -> bool {
        if quantity < 0.0 {
            return false;
        }

        if quantity == 0.0 {
            // IMPORTANT:
            // Delete ONLY from the specified side.
            //
            // Do not remove the opposite side here.
            return if is_bid {
                self.bids.remove(&price).is_some()
            } else {
                self.asks.remove(&price).is_some()
            };
        }

        // Positive quantity:
        // enforce the single-side invariant.
        if is_bid {
            self.asks.remove(&price);

            let old = self.bids.insert(
                price,
                Level { quantity },
            );

            old.map_or(true, |x| x.quantity != quantity)
        } else {
            self.bids.remove(&price);

            let old = self.asks.insert(
                price,
                Level { quantity },
            );

            old.map_or(true, |x| x.quantity != quantity)
        }
    }

    /// Best bid.
    pub fn best_bid(&self) -> Option<(PriceTick, f64)> {
        self.bids
            .iter()
            .next_back()
            .map(|(&price, level)| (price, level.quantity))
    }

    /// Best ask.
    pub fn best_ask(&self) -> Option<(PriceTick, f64)> {
        self.asks
            .iter()
            .next()
            .map(|(&price, level)| (price, level.quantity))
    }

    /// Returns true if best bid >= best ask.
    pub fn is_crossed(&self) -> bool {
        match (self.best_bid(), self.best_ask()) {
            (Some((bid, _)), Some((ask, _))) => bid >= ask,
            _ => false,
        }
    }

    /// Extract top-N levels.
    ///
    /// Bids: highest -> lowest
    /// Asks: lowest -> highest
    pub fn top_n(
        &self,
        levels: usize,
    ) -> (
        Vec<(PriceTick, f64)>,
        Vec<(PriceTick, f64)>,
    ) {
        let bids = self
            .bids
            .iter()
            .rev()
            .take(levels)
            .map(|(&price, level)| {
                (price, level.quantity)
            })
            .collect();

        let asks = self
            .asks
            .iter()
            .take(levels)
            .map(|(&price, level)| {
                (price, level.quantity)
            })
            .collect();

        (bids, asks)
    }

    /// Every resting level on both sides (no `levels` cap).
    ///
    /// Bids: highest -> lowest
    /// Asks: lowest -> highest
    ///
    /// This is what `replay.rs` uses to materialize a full-book checkpoint —
    /// `top_n` is for bounded display/output, `full_snapshot` is for storage.
    pub fn full_snapshot(
        &self,
    ) -> (
        Vec<(PriceTick, f64)>,
        Vec<(PriceTick, f64)>,
    ) {
        let bids = self
            .bids
            .iter()
            .rev()
            .map(|(&price, level)| (price, level.quantity))
            .collect();

        let asks = self
            .asks
            .iter()
            .map(|(&price, level)| (price, level.quantity))
            .collect();

        (bids, asks)
    }
}

===== FILE: components/pipelines/lob_builder/btree_ver/columnar.rs =====
//! `columnar.rs` — columnar replay + emission.
//!
//! `parquet.rs` converts a file into `Vec<LobRow>` up front and only ever
//! writes full-book checkpoint *images* (`ingest_parquet`, `process_matched`).
//! Neither mode emits the thing `live.py::fetch_source_data` actually joins
//! trades against: one top-N-per-side book-state row per `event_time` batch.
//! This module is that missing piece, and it stays columnar the whole way:
//! the file is read as flat `Vec<i64>`/`Vec<f64>`/`Vec<bool>` columns (SoA),
//! and only a small per-batch slice (bounded by that event's row count, not
//! the file's) is ever turned into `LobRow`s to drive `ReplayEngine`.
//!
//! Two invocations (wired in `main.rs` as `columnar --snapshot-policy ...`):
//!
//!   * `--snapshot-policy day_start`      — one full-book image per UTC day
//!     (hour 00), written wide-schema per day: `<dir>/<YYYY-MM-DD>.parquet`.
//!     Same file layout `live.py::load_day_snapshot` already reads/writes,
//!     so a Python-seeded day and a Rust-seeded day are interchangeable.
//!   * `--snapshot-policy every_snapshot` — one full-book image per VALID
//!     EVENT (literally per row, not just exchange snapshot frames) —
//!     storage-heavy, meant to run only when building the `matched` dataset,
//!     not for routine day-start ingestion.
//!
//! Both invocations always emit the top-N book-state file; the policy only
//! controls which checkpoint images additionally get written to disk.

use crate::book::{price_to_tick, tick_to_price, PriceTick};
use crate::replay::{
    day_index, BookCheckpoint, CheckpointPolicy, EventType, LobRow, ReplayEngine, ReplayError,
    ReplayStats,
};

use std::fs::File;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Arc;

use arrow::array::{
    Array, ArrayRef, BooleanArray, Float64Array, Int64Array, UInt32Array, UInt64Array,
};
use arrow::datatypes::{DataType, Field, Schema};
use arrow::record_batch::RecordBatch;
use parquet_rs::arrow::arrow_reader::ParquetRecordBatchReaderBuilder;
use parquet_rs::arrow::ArrowWriter;
use parquet_rs::file::properties::WriterProperties;

type BoxError = Box<dyn std::error::Error>;

/// Sentinel for "no id" in the columnar id columns (mirrors `live.py::NULL_ID`
/// and `parquet.rs::id_or_none`'s wire convention).
pub const NULL_ID: i64 = -1;

// ===========================================================
// COLUMNAR INPUT
// ===========================================================

/// Flat columnar view of the LOB depth-diff stream — the same 8 columns
/// `live.py::_LOB_REPLAY_SELECT` sends across the boundary — held as
/// contiguous `Vec`s instead of `Vec<LobRow>`.
pub struct LobColumns {
    pub event_time: Vec<i64>,
    pub is_bid: Vec<bool>,
    pub is_snapshot: Vec<bool>,
    pub price: Vec<f64>,
    pub quantity: Vec<f64>,
    pub first_update_id: Vec<i64>,
    pub final_update_id: Vec<i64>,
    pub prev_final_update_id: Vec<i64>,
}

impl LobColumns {
    pub fn len(&self) -> usize {
        self.event_time.len()
    }

    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.event_time.is_empty()
    }
}

pub fn read_lob_columns(path: &Path) -> Result<LobColumns, BoxError> {
    let file = File::open(path)?;
    let builder = ParquetRecordBatchReaderBuilder::try_new(file)?;
    let reader = builder.build()?;

    let mut out = LobColumns {
        event_time: Vec::new(),
        is_bid: Vec::new(),
        is_snapshot: Vec::new(),
        price: Vec::new(),
        quantity: Vec::new(),
        first_update_id: Vec::new(),
        final_update_id: Vec::new(),
        prev_final_update_id: Vec::new(),
    };

    for batch in reader {
        append_columns(&batch?, &mut out)?;
    }

    Ok(out)
}

fn append_columns(batch: &RecordBatch, out: &mut LobColumns) -> Result<(), BoxError> {
    let event_time = get_i64(batch, "event_time")?;
    let is_bid = get_bool(batch, "is_bid")?;
    let is_snapshot = get_bool(batch, "is_snapshot")?;
    let price = get_f64(batch, "price")?;
    let quantity = get_f64(batch, "quantity")?;
    let first_update_id = get_i64(batch, "first_update_id")?;
    let final_update_id = get_i64(batch, "final_update_id")?;
    let prev_final_update_id = get_i64(batch, "prev_final_update_id")?;

    let n = batch.num_rows();
    for v in [
        &mut out.event_time.capacity(),
    ] {
        let _ = v; // no-op: silence unused warning if reserve calls are trimmed below
    }
    out.event_time.reserve(n);
    out.is_bid.reserve(n);
    out.is_snapshot.reserve(n);
    out.price.reserve(n);
    out.quantity.reserve(n);
    out.first_update_id.reserve(n);
    out.final_update_id.reserve(n);
    out.prev_final_update_id.reserve(n);

    for i in 0..n {
        out.event_time.push(event_time.value(i));
        out.is_bid.push(is_bid.value(i));
        out.is_snapshot.push(is_snapshot.value(i));
        out.price.push(price.value(i));
        out.quantity.push(quantity.value(i));
        out.first_update_id.push(id_or_sentinel(first_update_id, i));
        out.final_update_id.push(id_or_sentinel(final_update_id, i));
        out.prev_final_update_id
            .push(id_or_sentinel(prev_final_update_id, i));
    }

    Ok(())
}

fn id_or_sentinel(col: &Int64Array, i: usize) -> i64 {
    if col.is_null(i) {
        return NULL_ID;
    }
    let v = col.value(i);
    if v < 0 {
        NULL_ID
    } else {
        v
    }
}

fn opt_id(v: i64) -> Option<u64> {
    if v < 0 {
        None
    } else {
        Some(v as u64)
    }
}

fn get_i64<'a>(batch: &'a RecordBatch, name: &str) -> Result<&'a Int64Array, BoxError> {
    batch
        .column_by_name(name)
        .ok_or_else(|| format!("missing column `{name}`"))?
        .as_any()
        .downcast_ref::<Int64Array>()
        .ok_or_else(|| format!("column `{name}` is not int64").into())
}

fn get_f64<'a>(batch: &'a RecordBatch, name: &str) -> Result<&'a Float64Array, BoxError> {
    batch
        .column_by_name(name)
        .ok_or_else(|| format!("missing column `{name}`"))?
        .as_any()
        .downcast_ref::<Float64Array>()
        .ok_or_else(|| format!("column `{name}` is not float64").into())
}

fn get_u64<'a>(batch: &'a RecordBatch, name: &str) -> Result<&'a UInt64Array, BoxError> {
    batch
        .column_by_name(name)
        .ok_or_else(|| format!("missing column `{name}`"))?
        .as_any()
        .downcast_ref::<UInt64Array>()
        .ok_or_else(|| format!("column `{name}` is not uint64").into())
}

fn get_bool<'a>(batch: &'a RecordBatch, name: &str) -> Result<&'a BooleanArray, BoxError> {
    batch
        .column_by_name(name)
        .ok_or_else(|| format!("missing column `{name}`"))?
        .as_any()
        .downcast_ref::<BooleanArray>()
        .ok_or_else(|| format!("column `{name}` is not bool").into())
}

/// Start index of every maximal `event_time` run (mirrors `live.py`'s
/// `_batch_bounds`).
fn batch_starts(event_time: &[i64]) -> Vec<usize> {
    let mut starts = Vec::new();
    if event_time.is_empty() {
        return starts;
    }
    starts.push(0);
    for i in 1..event_time.len() {
        if event_time[i] != event_time[i - 1] {
            starts.push(i);
        }
    }
    starts
}

// ===========================================================
// COLUMNAR REPLAY
// ===========================================================

/// Top-N-per-side book state, one row per published batch, flattened
/// `[bid_px, bid_qty, ask_px, ask_qty]` per level (NaN = untouched level) —
/// the layout `live.py::_book_frame` used to build in Python.
pub struct BookStateRows {
    pub event_time: Vec<i64>,
    pub levels: Vec<f64>, // len == event_time.len() * 4 * lob_levels
    pub lob_levels: usize,
}

pub struct ColumnarOutput {
    pub rows: BookStateRows,
    pub checkpoints: Vec<BookCheckpoint>,
    pub stats: ReplayStats,
}

/// Replay `cols` end to end. `skip_until`: drop rows at/before this
/// `event_time` (a resumed day-start seed already covers them — re-feeding
/// them would look like an update-ID gap).
pub fn replay_columnar(
    engine: &mut ReplayEngine,
    cols: &LobColumns,
    lob_levels: usize,
    skip_until: i64,
) -> Result<ColumnarOutput, ReplayError> {
    let starts = batch_starts(&cols.event_time);
    let n = cols.event_time.len();
    let stride = 4 * lob_levels;

    let mut out_times = Vec::new();
    let mut out_levels = Vec::new();
    let mut checkpoints = Vec::new();

    for (bi, &start) in starts.iter().enumerate() {
        let end = starts.get(bi + 1).copied().unwrap_or(n);
        let event_time = cols.event_time[start];

        if event_time <= skip_until {
            continue;
        }

        engine.stats.batches += 1;

        let rows: Vec<LobRow> = (start..end)
            .map(|i| LobRow {
                event_time: cols.event_time[i],
                event_type: if cols.is_snapshot[i] {
                    EventType::Snapshot
                } else {
                    EventType::Update
                },
                is_bid: cols.is_bid[i],
                price: cols.price[i],
                quantity: cols.quantity[i],
                first_update_id: opt_id(cols.first_update_id[i]),
                final_update_id: opt_id(cols.final_update_id[i]),
                prev_final_update_id: opt_id(cols.prev_final_update_id[i]),
            })
            .collect();

        match engine.process_event(&rows) {
            Ok(()) => {}
            Err(ReplayError::PartialSnapshot) => {
                // `process_snapshot` already invalidated; count it (Rust's
                // own path doesn't, live.py's mirror does — keep parity).
                engine.stats.malformed_events += 1;
            }
            Err(ReplayError::InconsistentBatchIds) => {
                // Already counted + invalidated inside `process_event`.
            }
            Err(ReplayError::NonMonotonicEventTime) => {
                engine.stats.malformed_events += 1;
            }
            Err(other) => return Err(other),
        }

        // Checkpoint attempt happens every batch under either policy; the
        // engine's own `checkpoint()` applies the DayStart hour/day gate
        // internally, and applies no gate at all under EverySnapshot —
        // "full reconstruction snapshots per row" is literal.
        if let Some(ckpt) = engine.checkpoint(event_time) {
            checkpoints.push(ckpt);
        }

        if !engine.is_valid() {
            engine.stats.skipped_state_rows += 1;
            continue;
        }

        let (bids, asks) = engine.book().top_n(lob_levels);

        if bids.is_empty() || asks.is_empty() {
            engine.stats.skipped_state_rows += 1;
            continue;
        }

        if bids[0].0 >= asks[0].0 {
            engine.stats.crossed_books += 1;
            engine.stats.skipped_state_rows += 1;
            continue;
        }

        let mut row = vec![f64::NAN; stride];
        for (idx, &(tick, qty)) in bids.iter().enumerate() {
            row[4 * idx] = tick_to_price(tick);
            row[4 * idx + 1] = qty;
        }
        for (idx, &(tick, qty)) in asks.iter().enumerate() {
            row[4 * idx + 2] = tick_to_price(tick);
            row[4 * idx + 3] = qty;
        }

        out_times.push(event_time);
        out_levels.extend_from_slice(&row);
        engine.stats.emitted_rows += 1;
    }

    Ok(ColumnarOutput {
        rows: BookStateRows {
            event_time: out_times,
            levels: out_levels,
            lob_levels,
        },
        checkpoints,
        stats: engine.stats,
    })
}

// ===========================================================
// OUTPUT: top-N book-state parquet
// ===========================================================

fn book_state_level_names(lob_levels: usize) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut names = vec![
        "best_bid_price".to_string(),
        "best_bid_qty".to_string(),
        "best_ask_price".to_string(),
        "best_ask_qty".to_string(),
    ];
    for level in 2..=lob_levels {
        names.push(format!("bid_price_{level}"));
        names.push(format!("bid_qty_{level}"));
        names.push(format!("ask_price_{level}"));
        names.push(format!("ask_qty_{level}"));
    }
    names
}

fn write_book_state_parquet(path: &Path, rows: &BookStateRows) -> Result<(), BoxError> {
    let stride = 4 * rows.lob_levels;
    let names = book_state_level_names(rows.lob_levels);
    let n = rows.event_time.len();

    let mut fields = vec![Field::new("event_time", DataType::Int64, false)];
    for name in &names {
        fields.push(Field::new(name, DataType::Float64, true));
    }
    let schema = Arc::new(Schema::new(fields));

    let mut arrays: Vec<ArrayRef> = vec![Arc::new(Int64Array::from(rows.event_time.clone()))];
    for c in 0..stride {
        let col: Vec<Option<f64>> = (0..n)
            .map(|r| {
                let v = rows.levels[r * stride + c];
                if v.is_nan() {
                    None
                } else {
                    Some(v)
                }
            })
            .collect();
        arrays.push(Arc::new(Float64Array::from(col)));
    }

    let batch = RecordBatch::try_new(schema.clone(), arrays)?;
    let tmp = path.with_extension("tmp.parquet");
    {
        let file = File::create(&tmp)?;
        let props = WriterProperties::builder().build();
        let mut writer = ArrowWriter::try_new(file, schema, Some(props))?;
        writer.write(&batch)?;
        writer.close()?;
    }
    std::fs::rename(&tmp, path)?;

    Ok(())
}

// ===========================================================
// OUTPUT: wide per-day checkpoint images
// ===========================================================
//
// Same schema `live.py::_write_day_snapshot` / `load_day_snapshot` already
// use: event_time, final_update_id, n_bid_levels, n_ask_levels, then
// bid_price_1..M / bid_qty_1..M / ask_price_1..M / ask_qty_1..M.
//
// DayStart: one checkpoint per day -> one row, M = that day's own level
// count (no cap). EverySnapshot: many checkpoints per day -> one row per
// event, M = `--snapshot-levels` (default 50), shorter books null-padded.

fn write_wide_snapshot_file(
    path: &Path,
    checkpoints: &[BookCheckpoint],
    cap_levels: Option<usize>,
) -> Result<(), BoxError> {
    let m = cap_levels.unwrap_or_else(|| {
        checkpoints
            .iter()
            .map(|c| c.bid_levels().max(c.ask_levels()))
            .max()
            .unwrap_or(0)
    });

    let mut fields = vec![
        Field::new("event_time", DataType::Int64, false),
        Field::new("final_update_id", DataType::UInt64, false),
        Field::new("n_bid_levels", DataType::UInt32, false),
        Field::new("n_ask_levels", DataType::UInt32, false),
    ];
    for level in 1..=m {
        fields.push(Field::new(format!("bid_price_{level}"), DataType::Float64, true));
        fields.push(Field::new(format!("bid_qty_{level}"), DataType::Float64, true));
    }
    for level in 1..=m {
        fields.push(Field::new(format!("ask_price_{level}"), DataType::Float64, true));
        fields.push(Field::new(format!("ask_qty_{level}"), DataType::Float64, true));
    }
    let schema = Arc::new(Schema::new(fields));

    let event_time: Vec<i64> = checkpoints.iter().map(|c| c.event_time).collect();
    let final_update_id: Vec<u64> = checkpoints.iter().map(|c| c.final_update_id).collect();
    let n_bid: Vec<u32> = checkpoints.iter().map(|c| c.bid_levels() as u32).collect();
    let n_ask: Vec<u32> = checkpoints.iter().map(|c| c.ask_levels() as u32).collect();

    let mut arrays: Vec<ArrayRef> = vec![
        Arc::new(Int64Array::from(event_time)),
        Arc::new(UInt64Array::from(final_update_id)),
        Arc::new(UInt32Array::from(n_bid)),
        Arc::new(UInt32Array::from(n_ask)),
    ];

    for level in 1..=m {
        let px: Vec<Option<f64>> = checkpoints
            .iter()
            .map(|c| c.bid_prices.get(level - 1).copied())
            .collect();
        let qty: Vec<Option<f64>> = checkpoints
            .iter()
            .map(|c| c.bids.get(level - 1).map(|&(_, q)| q))
            .collect();
        arrays.push(Arc::new(Float64Array::from(px)));
        arrays.push(Arc::new(Float64Array::from(qty)));
    }
    for level in 1..=m {
        let px: Vec<Option<f64>> = checkpoints
            .iter()
            .map(|c| c.ask_prices.get(level - 1).copied())
            .collect();
        let qty: Vec<Option<f64>> = checkpoints
            .iter()
            .map(|c| c.asks.get(level - 1).map(|&(_, q)| q))
            .collect();
        arrays.push(Arc::new(Float64Array::from(px)));
        arrays.push(Arc::new(Float64Array::from(qty)));
    }

    let batch = RecordBatch::try_new(schema.clone(), arrays)?;
    let tmp = path.with_extension("tmp.parquet");
    {
        let file = File::create(&tmp)?;
        let props = WriterProperties::builder().build();
        let mut writer = ArrowWriter::try_new(file, schema, Some(props))?;
        writer.write(&batch)?;
        writer.close()?;
    }
    std::fs::rename(&tmp, path)?;

    Ok(())
}

fn write_day_start_snapshots(checkpoints: &[BookCheckpoint], dir: &Path) -> Result<(), BoxError> {
    for ckpt in checkpoints {
        let path = dir.join(format!("{}.parquet", day_string(day_index(ckpt.event_time))));
        write_wide_snapshot_file(&path, std::slice::from_ref(ckpt), None)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn write_every_snapshot_images(
    checkpoints: &[BookCheckpoint],
    dir: &Path,
    cap: usize,
) -> Result<(), BoxError> {
    use std::collections::BTreeMap;

    let mut by_day: BTreeMap<i64, Vec<BookCheckpoint>> = BTreeMap::new();
    for c in checkpoints {
        by_day
            .entry(day_index(c.event_time))
            .or_default()
            .push(c.clone());
    }

    for (day, group) in by_day {
        let path = dir.join(format!("{}.parquet", day_string(day)));
        write_wide_snapshot_file(&path, &group, Some(cap))?;
    }

    Ok(())
}

/// `YYYY-MM-DD` for a UTC day index (see `replay::day_index`). Duplicated
/// from `parquet.rs::civil_from_days` (Howard Hinnant's algorithm) so this
/// file has no cross-module dependency beyond `book`/`replay`.
fn day_string(day: i64) -> String {
    let z = day + 719_468;
    let era = if z >= 0 { z } else { z - 146_096 } / 146_097;
    let doe = (z - era * 146_097) as u64;
    let yoe = (doe - doe / 1460 + doe / 36_524 - doe / 146_096) / 365;
    let y = yoe as i64 + era * 400;
    let doy = doe - (365 * yoe + yoe / 4 - yoe / 100);
    let mp = (5 * doy + 2) / 153;
    let d = (doy - (153 * mp + 2) / 5 + 1) as u32;
    let m = if mp < 10 { mp + 3 } else { mp - 9 } as u32;
    let year = if m <= 2 { y + 1 } else { y };
    format!("{year:04}-{m:02}-{d:02}")
}

// ===========================================================
// SEED (resume from a stored day-start image)
// ===========================================================

fn read_seed_snapshot(
    path: &Path,
) -> Result<(i64, u64, Vec<(PriceTick, f64)>, Vec<(PriceTick, f64)>), BoxError> {
    let file = File::open(path)?;
    let builder = ParquetRecordBatchReaderBuilder::try_new(file)?;
    let schema = builder.schema().clone();
    let mut reader = builder.build()?;
    let batch = reader
        .next()
        .ok_or("seed snapshot file is empty")??;

    let event_time = get_i64(&batch, "event_time")?.value(0);
    let final_update_id = get_u64(&batch, "final_update_id")?.value(0);

    let mut bid_levels: Vec<usize> = schema
        .fields()
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|f| f.name().strip_prefix("bid_price_").and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()))
        .collect();
    bid_levels.sort_unstable();

    let mut ask_levels: Vec<usize> = schema
        .fields()
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|f| f.name().strip_prefix("ask_price_").and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()))
        .collect();
    ask_levels.sort_unstable();

    let mut bids = Vec::new();
    for lvl in &bid_levels {
        let px = get_f64(&batch, &format!("bid_price_{lvl}"))?;
        if px.is_null(0) {
            continue;
        }
        let qty = get_f64(&batch, &format!("bid_qty_{lvl}"))?;
        let tick = price_to_tick(px.value(0)).ok_or("invalid seed bid price")?;
        bids.push((tick, qty.value(0)));
    }

    let mut asks = Vec::new();
    for lvl in &ask_levels {
        let px = get_f64(&batch, &format!("ask_price_{lvl}"))?;
        if px.is_null(0) {
            continue;
        }
        let qty = get_f64(&batch, &format!("ask_qty_{lvl}"))?;
        let tick = price_to_tick(px.value(0)).ok_or("invalid seed ask price")?;
        asks.push((tick, qty.value(0)));
    }

    Ok((event_time, final_update_id, bids, asks))
}

// ===========================================================
// CLI
// ===========================================================

struct ColumnarArgs {
    input: PathBuf,
    output: PathBuf,
    tick_size: f64,
    warm_start: bool,
    levels: usize,
    policy: CheckpointPolicy,
    snapshot_dir: Option<PathBuf>,
    snapshot_levels: usize,
    seed_snapshot: Option<PathBuf>,
    skip_until: i64,
}

fn next_value<'a>(args: &'a [String], i: &mut usize, flag: &str) -> Result<&'a str, BoxError> {
    let value = args
        .get(*i + 1)
        .ok_or_else(|| format!("{flag} requires a value"))?;
    *i += 2;
    Ok(value.as_str())
}

fn parse_args(args: &[String]) -> Result<ColumnarArgs, BoxError> {
    let mut input = None;
    let mut output = None;
    let mut tick_size = None;
    let mut warm_start = false;
    let mut levels = 20usize;
    let mut policy = None;
    let mut snapshot_dir = None;
    let mut snapshot_levels = 50usize;
    let mut seed_snapshot = None;
    let mut skip_until = 0i64;

    let mut i = 0;
    while i < args.len() {
        match args[i].as_str() {
            "--input" => input = Some(PathBuf::from(next_value(args, &mut i, "--input")?)),
            "--output" => output = Some(PathBuf::from(next_value(args, &mut i, "--output")?)),
            "--tick-size" => {
                tick_size = Some(next_value(args, &mut i, "--tick-size")?.parse::<f64>()?)
            }
            "--warm-start" => {
                warm_start = true;
                i += 1;
            }
            "--levels" => levels = next_value(args, &mut i, "--levels")?.parse()?,
            "--snapshot-policy" => {
                let name = next_value(args, &mut i, "--snapshot-policy")?;
                policy = Some(
                    CheckpointPolicy::parse(name)
                        .ok_or_else(|| format!("unknown snapshot policy `{name}`"))?,
                );
            }
            "--snapshot-dir" => {
                snapshot_dir = Some(PathBuf::from(next_value(args, &mut i, "--snapshot-dir")?))
            }
            "--snapshot-levels" => {
                snapshot_levels = next_value(args, &mut i, "--snapshot-levels")?.parse()?
            }
            "--seed-snapshot" => {
                seed_snapshot = Some(PathBuf::from(next_value(args, &mut i, "--seed-snapshot")?))
            }
            "--skip-until" => skip_until = next_value(args, &mut i, "--skip-until")?.parse()?,
            other => return Err(format!("unknown flag `{other}`").into()),
        }
    }

    Ok(ColumnarArgs {
        input: input.ok_or("--input is required")?,
        output: output.ok_or("--output is required")?,
        tick_size: tick_size.ok_or("--tick-size is required")?,
        warm_start,
        levels,
        policy: policy.ok_or("--snapshot-policy is required (day_start | every_snapshot)")?,
        snapshot_dir,
        snapshot_levels,
        seed_snapshot,
        skip_until,
    })
}

fn print_stats(stats: &ReplayStats) {
    println!(
        "STATS snapshot_events={} update_events={} gaps={} malformed_events={} \
crossed_books={} rows_before_initial_snapshot={} snapshots={} skipped_checkpoints={} \
batches={} emitted_rows={} skipped_state_rows={} warm_start_batches={}",
        stats.snapshot_events,
        stats.update_events,
        stats.gaps,
        stats.malformed_events,
        stats.crossed_books,
        stats.rows_before_initial_snapshot,
        stats.snapshots,
        stats.skipped_checkpoints,
        stats.batches,
        stats.emitted_rows,
        stats.skipped_state_rows,
        stats.warm_start_batches,
    );
}

/// `lob_replay columnar --input <FILE> --output <FILE> --tick-size <F>
/// --levels <N> --snapshot-policy <day_start|every_snapshot>
/// [--snapshot-dir <DIR>] [--snapshot-levels <N>] [--warm-start]
/// [--seed-snapshot <FILE>] [--skip-until <MS>]`
pub fn run(args: &[String]) -> Result<(), BoxError> {
    let parsed = parse_args(args)?;

    let mut engine = ReplayEngine::with_options(parsed.tick_size, parsed.warm_start)?;
    engine.set_checkpoint_policy(parsed.policy);

    let mut skip_until = parsed.skip_until;

    if let Some(seed_path) = &parsed.seed_snapshot {
        let (event_time, final_update_id, bids, asks) = read_seed_snapshot(seed_path)?;
        engine.seed_snapshot(event_time, final_update_id, &bids, &asks)?;
        skip_until = skip_until.max(event_time);
    }

    let cols = read_lob_columns(&parsed.input)?;
    let output = replay_columnar(&mut engine, &cols, parsed.levels, skip_until)?;

    write_book_state_parquet(&parsed.output, &output.rows)?;

    if let Some(dir) = &parsed.snapshot_dir {
        std::fs::create_dir_all(dir)?;
        match parsed.policy {
            CheckpointPolicy::DayStart => write_day_start_snapshots(&output.checkpoints, dir)?,
            CheckpointPolicy::EverySnapshot => {
                write_every_snapshot_images(&output.checkpoints, dir, parsed.snapshot_levels)?
            }
        }
    }

    print_stats(&output.stats);

    Ok(())
}

===== FILE: components/pipelines/lob_builder/btree_ver/main.rs =====

//! `main.rs` — CLI entry point for the LOB replay component.
//!
//! The entry point parses CLI arguments and dispatches to:
//!
//!     columnar
//!     ingest-parquet
//!     matched
//!
//! Replay/book mechanics remain in `replay.rs` and `book.rs`.

mod book;
mod columnar;
mod parquet;
mod replay;

use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::process::ExitCode;

type BoxError = Box<dyn std::error::Error>;

fn main() -> ExitCode {
    let args: Vec<String> =
        std::env::args().collect();

    if args.len() < 2 {
        print_usage();
        return ExitCode::FAILURE;
    }

    let mode = args[1].as_str();
    let rest = &args[2..];

    let result = match mode {
        "columnar" => columnar::run(rest),

        "ingest-parquet" =>
            run_ingest(rest),

        "matched" =>
            run_matched(rest),

        "-h" | "--help" | "help" => {
            print_usage();
            return ExitCode::SUCCESS;
        }

        other => Err(
            format!("unknown mode `{other}`").into()
        ),
    };

    match result {
        Ok(()) => ExitCode::SUCCESS,

        Err(err) => {
            eprintln!("error: {err}");
            ExitCode::FAILURE
        }
    }
}

fn print_usage() {
    eprintln!(
        "\
lob_replay — L2 order-book replay CLI

USAGE:

    lob_replay ingest-parquet \
        --input <FILE|DIR> \
        --output-dir <DIR> \
        --tick-size <F> \
        [--snapshot-policy <day_start|every_event>] \
        [--warm-start]

    lob_replay matched \
        --input <FILE> \
        --output <FILE> \
        --tick-size <F> \
        [--warm-start]

    lob_replay columnar \
        --input <FILE> \
        --output <FILE> \
        --tick-size <F> \
        --levels <N> \
        --snapshot-policy <day_start|every_event> \
        [--snapshot-dir <DIR>] \
        [--snapshot-levels <N>] \
        [--seed-snapshot <FILE>] \
        [--skip-until <MS>] \
        [--warm-start]


MODES:

    ingest-parquet
        Replay one parquet file OR every parquet file in a directory.

        day_start:
            write one full-book checkpoint per UTC day,
            using the first valid exchange event during UTC hour 00.

        every_event:
            write one full-book checkpoint after every valid
            exchange event.

        Directory mode uses one shared ReplayEngine across
        all parquet files.

    matched
        Replay one parquet file and write a full-book checkpoint
        for every valid exchange event.

    columnar
        Replay one parquet file while preserving every original
        source row and attaching the reconstructed top-N book state.


OPTIONS:

    --input <FILE|DIR>
        Input parquet file or directory.

    --output <FILE>
        Output parquet file.

    --output-dir <DIR>
        Output directory.

    --tick-size <F>
        Instrument tick size, e.g. 0.01.

    --snapshot-policy <day_start|every_event>
        Checkpoint policy for ingest/columnar.

        `every_snapshot` is accepted as a legacy alias
        for `every_event`.

    --levels <N>
        Number of book levels emitted by columnar mode.

    --snapshot-dir <DIR>
        Optional directory for additional checkpoint images
        in columnar mode.

    --snapshot-levels <N>
        Maximum levels retained in columnar checkpoint images.

    --seed-snapshot <FILE>
        Full-book seed snapshot for columnar replay.

    --skip-until <MS>
        Skip source events at or before this market timestamp.

    --warm-start
        Accept diff-only input and rebase after gaps.
"
    );
}

struct IngestArgs {
    input: PathBuf,
    output_dir: PathBuf,
    tick_size: f64,
    warm_start: bool,
    policy: replay::CheckpointPolicy,
}

// ============================================================================
// MATCHED ARGUMENTS

    input: PathBuf,
    output: PathBuf,
    tick_size: f64,
    warm_start: bool,
}

// ============================================================================
// INGEST
// ============================================================================

fn run_ingest(args: &[String]) -> Result<(), BoxError> {
    let parsed = parse_ingest_args(args)?;

    let stats =
        parquet::ingest_parquet_with_policy(
            &parsed.input,
            &parsed.output_dir,
            parsed.tick_size,
            parsed.warm_start,
            parsed.policy,
        )?;

    println!("{stats:#?}");

    Ok(())
}

// ============================================================================
// MATCHED
// ============================================================================

fn run_matched(
    args: &[String],
) -> Result<(), BoxError> {
    let parsed =
        parse_matched_args(args)?;

    let stats =
        parquet::process_matched(
            &parsed.input,
            &parsed.output,
            parsed.tick_size,
            parsed.warm_start,
        )?;

    println!("{stats:#?}");

    Ok(())
}

// ============================================================================
// PARSE INGEST ARGUMENTS
// ============================================================================

fn parse_ingest_args(
    args: &[String],
) -> Result<IngestArgs, BoxError> {
    let mut input = None;
    let mut output_dir = None;
    let mut tick_size = None;
    let mut warm_start = false;

    let mut policy =
        replay::CheckpointPolicy::DayStart;

    let mut i = 0usize;

    while i < args.len() {
        match args[i].as_str() {
            "--input" => {
                input = Some(PathBuf::from(
                    next_value(
                        args,
                        &mut i,
                        "--input",
                    )?,
                ));
            }

            "--output-dir" => {
                output_dir = Some(
                    PathBuf::from(
                        next_value(
                            args,
                            &mut i,
                            "--output-dir",
                        )?,
                    ),
                );
            }

            "--tick-size" => {
                let value =
                    next_value(
                        args,
                        &mut i,
                        "--tick-size",
                    )?;

                tick_size =
                    Some(value.parse::<f64>()?);
            }

            "--warm-start" => {
                warm_start = true;
                i += 1;
            }

            "--snapshot-policy" => {
                let value =
                    next_value(
                        args,
                        &mut i,
                        "--snapshot-policy",
                    )?;

                policy =
                    replay::CheckpointPolicy::parse(
                        value,
                    )
                    .ok_or_else(|| {
                        format!(
                            "unknown snapshot policy `{value}`; \
                             expected `day_start` or `every_event`"
                        )
                    })?;
            }

            other => {
                return Err(
                    format!(
                        "unknown flag `{other}`"
                    )
                    .into()
                );
            }
        }
    }

    Ok(IngestArgs {
        input: input.ok_or(
            "--input is required"
        )?,

        output_dir: output_dir.ok_or(
            "--output-dir is required"
        )?,

        tick_size: tick_size.ok_or(
            "--tick-size is required"
        )?,

        warm_start,
        policy,
    })
}

// ============================================================================

fn parse_matched_args(
