) -> Result<MatchedArgs, BoxError> {
    let mut input = None;
    let mut output = None;
    let mut tick_size = None;
    let mut warm_start = false;

    let mut i = 0usize;

    while i < args.len() {
        match args[i].as_str() {
            "--input" => {
                input = Some(PathBuf::from(
                    next_value(
                        args,
                        &mut i,
                        "--input",
                    )?,
                ));
            }

            "--output" => {
                output = Some(PathBuf::from(
                    next_value(
                        args,
                        &mut i,
                        "--output",
                    )?,
                ));
            }

            "--tick-size" => {
                let value =
                    next_value(
                        args,
                        &mut i,
                        "--tick-size",
                    )?;

                tick_size =
                    Some(value.parse::<f64>()?);
            }

            "--warm-start" => {
                warm_start = true;
                i += 1;
            }

            other => {
                return Err(
                    format!(
                        "unknown flag `{other}`"
                    )
                    .into()
                );
            }
        }
    }

    Ok(MatchedArgs {
        input: input.ok_or(
            "--input is required"
        )?,

        output: output.ok_or(
            "--output is required"
        )?,

        tick_size: tick_size.ok_or(
            "--tick-size is required"
        )?,

        warm_start,
    })
}

// ============================================================================
// COMMON CLI HELPER
// ============================================================================

/// Consume `--flag <value>` and advance `i` past both tokens.
fn next_value<'a>(
    args: &'a [String],
    i: &mut usize,
    flag: &str,
) -> Result<&'a str, BoxError> {
    let value =
        args.get(*i + 1)
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                format!(
                    "{flag} requires a value"
                )
            })?;

    *i += 2;

    Ok(value.as_str())
}

===== FILE: components/pipelines/lob_builder/btree_ver/parquet.rs =====
//! parquet.rs
//!
//! Raw input:
//!   received_time
//!   event_time
//!   transaction_time
//!   symbol
//!   event_type
//!   first_update_id
//!   final_update_id
//!   prev_final_update_id
//!   last_update_id
//!   side
//!   price
//!   quantity
//!
//! `is_bid` is derived from `side`.
//! Snapshot/update classification is derived from `event_type`.

use crate::book::tick_to_price;
use crate::replay::{
    BookCheckpoint,
    CheckpointPolicy,
    EventType,
    LobRow,
    ReplayEngine,
    ReplayError,
    ReplayStats,
};

use arrow::array::{
    Array,
    ArrayRef,
    BooleanArray,
    Float64Array,
    Int64Array,
    StringArray,
    UInt64Array,
};
use arrow::datatypes::{
    DataType,
    Field,
    Schema,
};
use arrow::record_batch::RecordBatch;

use parquet_rs::arrow::arrow_reader::ParquetRecordBatchReaderBuilder;
use parquet_rs::arrow::ArrowWriter;
use parquet_rs::file::properties::WriterProperties;

use std::fs::File;
use std::path::{
    Path,
    PathBuf,
};
use std::sync::Arc;

type BoxError = Box<dyn std::error::Error>;


// ============================================================================
// INPUT FILE DISCOVERY
// ============================================================================

fn discover_input_files(
    input: &Path,
) -> Result<Vec<PathBuf>, BoxError> {
    if input.is_file() {
        if input.extension()
            .and_then(|x| x.to_str())
            .map(|x| x.eq_ignore_ascii_case("parquet"))
            != Some(true)
        {
            return Err(
                format!(
                    "input file is not parquet: {}",
                    input.display()
                )
                .into()
            );
        }

        return Ok(vec![input.to_path_buf()]);
    }

    if !input.is_dir() {
        return Err(
            format!(
                "input path does not exist: {}",
                input.display()
            )
            .into()
        );
    }

    fn visit(
        dir: &Path,
        files: &mut Vec<PathBuf>,
    ) -> Result<(), BoxError> {
        for entry in std::fs::read_dir(dir)? {
            let path = entry?.path();

            if path.is_dir() {
                visit(&path, files)?;
                continue;
            }

            if path.is_file()
                && path
                    .extension()
                    .and_then(|x| x.to_str())
                    .map(|x| x.eq_ignore_ascii_case("parquet"))
                    .unwrap_or(false)
            {
                files.push(path);
            }
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    let mut files = Vec::new();

    visit(input, &mut files)?;

    files.sort();

    if files.is_empty() {
        return Err(
            format!(
                "no parquet files found under {}",
                input.display()
            )
            .into()
        );
    }

    Ok(files)
}


// ============================================================================
// ARROW COLUMN HELPERS
// ============================================================================

fn get_i64<'a>(
    batch: &'a RecordBatch,
    name: &str,
) -> Result<&'a Int64Array, BoxError> {
    let column = batch
        .column_by_name(name)
        .ok_or_else(|| {
            format!("missing column `{name}`")
        })?;

    column
        .as_any()
        .downcast_ref::<Int64Array>()
        .ok_or_else(|| {
            format!(
                "column `{name}` is not int64"
            )
            .into()
        })
}


fn get_string<'a>(
    batch: &'a RecordBatch,
    name: &str,
) -> Result<&'a StringArray, BoxError> {
    let column = batch
        .column_by_name(name)
        .ok_or_else(|| {
            format!("missing column `{name}`")
        })?;

    column
        .as_any()
        .downcast_ref::<StringArray>()
        .ok_or_else(|| {
            format!(
                "column `{name}` is not string/utf8"
            )
            .into()
        })
}


fn optional_update_id(
    column: &Int64Array,
    row: usize,
) -> Option<u64> {
    if column.is_null(row) {
        return None;
    }

    let value = column.value(row);

    if value < 0 {
        return None;
    }

    Some(value as u64)
}


// ============================================================================
// RAW PARQUET -> LobRow
// ============================================================================

fn append_rows(
    batch: &RecordBatch,
    rows: &mut Vec<LobRow>,
) -> Result<(), BoxError> {
    let event_time =
        get_i64(batch, "event_time")?;

    let event_type =
        get_string(batch, "event_type")?;

    let side =
        get_string(batch, "side")?;

    let price =
        get_string(batch, "price")?;

    let quantity =
        get_string(batch, "quantity")?;

    let first_update_id =
        get_i64(batch, "first_update_id")?;

    let final_update_id =
        get_i64(batch, "final_update_id")?;

    let prev_final_update_id =
        get_i64(batch, "prev_final_update_id")?;

    for row in 0..batch.num_rows() {
        let event_type =
            match event_type
                .value(row)
                .trim()
                .to_ascii_lowercase()
                .as_str()
            {
                "snapshot" => EventType::Snapshot,

                "update" => EventType::Update,

                value if value.contains("snapshot") =>
                    EventType::Snapshot,

                value
                    if value.contains("update")
                        || value.contains("delta")
                        || value.contains("diff") =>
                {
                    EventType::Update
                }

                value => {
                    return Err(
                        format!(
                            "unknown event_type `{value}` at row {row}"
                        )
                        .into()
                    );
                }
            };

        let is_bid =
            match side
                .value(row)
                .trim()
                .to_ascii_lowercase()
                .as_str()
            {
                "bid" | "b" | "buy" => true,

                "ask" | "a" | "sell" => false,

                value => {
                    return Err(
                        format!(
                            "unknown side `{value}` at row {row}"
                        )
                        .into()
                    );
                }
            };

        let price =
            price
                .value(row)
                .trim()
                .parse::<f64>()
                .map_err(|err| {
                    format!(
                        "invalid price `{}` at row {row}: {err}",
                        price.value(row)
                    )
                })?;

        let quantity =
            quantity
                .value(row)
                .trim()
                .parse::<f64>()
                .map_err(|err| {
                    format!(
                        "invalid quantity `{}` at row {row}: {err}",
                        quantity.value(row)
                    )
                })?;

        rows.push(LobRow {
            event_time: event_time.value(row),
            event_type,
            is_bid,
            price,
            quantity,
            first_update_id:
                optional_update_id(
                    first_update_id,
                    row,
                ),
            final_update_id:
                optional_update_id(
                    final_update_id,
                    row,
                ),
            prev_final_update_id:
                optional_update_id(
                    prev_final_update_id,
                    row,
                ),
        });
    }

    Ok(())
}


// ============================================================================
// EXCHANGE EVENT IDENTITY
// ============================================================================

fn same_event(
    a: &LobRow,
    b: &LobRow,
) -> bool {
    a.event_time
        == b.event_time
        && a.event_type
            == b.event_type
        && a.first_update_id
            == b.first_update_id
        && a.final_update_id
            == b.final_update_id
        && a.prev_final_update_id
            == b.prev_final_update_id
}


// ============================================================================
// OUTPUT CHECKPOINT WRITER
// ============================================================================

struct CheckpointWriter {
    writer: ArrowWriter<File>,
    schema: Arc<Schema>,
}


impl CheckpointWriter {
    fn new(
        path: &Path,
    ) -> Result<Self, BoxError> {
        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
            if !parent.as_os_str().is_empty() {
                std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
            }
        }

        let schema =
            Arc::new(Schema::new(vec![
                Field::new(
                    "event_time",
                    DataType::Int64,
                    false,
                ),
                Field::new(
                    "final_update_id",
                    DataType::UInt64,
                    false,
                ),
                Field::new(
                    "is_bid",
                    DataType::Boolean,
                    false,
                ),
                Field::new(
                    "price",
                    DataType::Float64,
                    false,
                ),
                Field::new(
                    "quantity",
                    DataType::Float64,
                    false,
                ),
            ]));

        let file =
            File::create(path)?;

        let properties =
            WriterProperties::builder()
                .build();

        let writer =
            ArrowWriter::try_new(
                file,
                schema.clone(),
                Some(properties),
            )?;

        Ok(Self {
            writer,
            schema,
        })
    }


    fn write_checkpoint(
        &mut self,
        checkpoint: &BookCheckpoint,
    ) -> Result<(), BoxError> {
        let mut event_time = Vec::new();
        let mut final_update_id = Vec::new();
        let mut is_bid = Vec::new();
        let mut price = Vec::new();
        let mut quantity = Vec::new();

        for &(tick, qty) in &checkpoint.bids {
            event_time.push(
                checkpoint.event_time
            );

            final_update_id.push(
                checkpoint.final_update_id
            );

            is_bid.push(true);

            price.push(
                tick_to_price(tick)
            );

            quantity.push(qty);
        }

        for &(tick, qty) in &checkpoint.asks {
            event_time.push(
                checkpoint.event_time
            );

            final_update_id.push(
                checkpoint.final_update_id
            );

            is_bid.push(false);

            price.push(
                tick_to_price(tick)
            );

            quantity.push(qty);
        }

        let batch =
            RecordBatch::try_new(
                self.schema.clone(),
                vec![
                    Arc::new(
                        Int64Array::from(
                            event_time
                        )
                    ) as ArrayRef,

                    Arc::new(
                        UInt64Array::from(
                            final_update_id
                        )
                    ) as ArrayRef,

                    Arc::new(
                        BooleanArray::from(
                            is_bid
                        )
                    ) as ArrayRef,

                    Arc::new(
                        Float64Array::from(
                            price
                        )
                    ) as ArrayRef,

                    Arc::new(
                        Float64Array::from(
                            quantity
                        )
                    ) as ArrayRef,
                ],
            )?;

        self.writer.write(&batch)?;

        Ok(())
    }


    fn finish(
        self,
    ) -> Result<(), BoxError> {
        self.writer.close()?;
        Ok(())
    }
}


// ============================================================================
// PROCESS ONE COMPLETE EXCHANGE EVENT
// ============================================================================

fn process_one_event(
    event: &[LobRow],
    engine: &mut ReplayEngine,
) -> Result<Option<BookCheckpoint>, BoxError> {
    if event.is_empty() {
        return Ok(None);
    }

    engine.stats.batches += 1;

    match engine.process_event(event) {
        Ok(()) => {}

        Err(
            ReplayError::PartialSnapshot
            | ReplayError::InconsistentBatchIds
            | ReplayError::NonMonotonicEventTime,
        ) => {
            return Ok(None);
        }

        Err(other) => {
            return Err(other.into());
        }
    }

    Ok(
        engine.checkpoint(
            event[0].event_time
        )
    )
}


// ============================================================================
// UTC DAY
// ============================================================================

fn utc_day(
    event_time_ms: i64,
) -> i64 {
    crate::replay::day_index(
        event_time_ms
    )
}


// ============================================================================
// MAIN INGEST API
// ============================================================================
pub fn process_matched(
    input: &Path,
    output: &Path,
    tick_size: f64,
    warm_start: bool,
) -> Result<ReplayStats, BoxError> {
    if let Some(parent) = output.parent() {
        if !parent.as_os_str().is_empty() {
            std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
        }
    }

    let input_files = discover_input_files(input)?;

    let mut engine =
        ReplayEngine::with_options(
            tick_size,
            warm_start,
        )?;

    engine.set_checkpoint_policy(
        CheckpointPolicy::EverySnapshot
    );

    let mut writer =
        CheckpointWriter::new(output)?;

    let mut pending_event: Vec<LobRow> =
        Vec::new();

    for input_file in input_files {
        let file = File::open(&input_file)?;

        let builder =
            ParquetRecordBatchReaderBuilder::try_new(file)?;

        let reader = builder.build()?;

        for batch_result in reader {
            let batch = batch_result?;

            let mut rows =
                Vec::with_capacity(batch.num_rows());

            append_rows(
                &batch,
                &mut rows,
            )?;

            for row in rows {
                if pending_event.is_empty() {
                    pending_event.push(row);
                    continue;
                }

                if same_event(
                    &pending_event[0],
                    &row,
                ) {
                    pending_event.push(row);
                    continue;
                }

                if let Some(checkpoint) =
                    process_one_event(
                        &pending_event,
                        &mut engine,
                    )?
                {
                    writer.write_checkpoint(
                        &checkpoint
                    )?;
                }

                pending_event.clear();
                pending_event.push(row);
            }
        }
    }

    // Flush the final event.
    if !pending_event.is_empty() {
        if let Some(checkpoint) =
            process_one_event(
                &pending_event,
                &mut engine,
            )?
        {
            writer.write_checkpoint(
                &checkpoint
            )?;
        }
    }

    writer.finish()?;

    Ok(engine.stats)
}

pub fn ingest_parquet(
    input: &Path,
    output_dir: &Path,
    tick_size: f64,
    warm_start: bool,
) -> Result<ReplayStats, BoxError> {
    ingest_parquet_with_policy(
        input,
        output_dir,
        tick_size,
        warm_start,
        CheckpointPolicy::DayStart,
    )
}
pub fn ingest_parquet_with_policy(
    input: &Path,
    output_dir: &Path,
    tick_size: f64,
    warm_start: bool,
    policy: CheckpointPolicy,
) -> Result<ReplayStats, BoxError> {
    // Do not allow output to live inside the input tree.
    // Otherwise a rerun can discover its own previously-generated parquet files.
    if input.is_dir() && output_dir.starts_with(input) {
        return Err(
            format!(
                "output directory must not be inside input directory:\n  input: {}\n  output: {}",
                input.display(),
                output_dir.display()
            )
            .into()
        );
    }

    // For a single input file, do not write output into the same directory
    // as that input file. This prevents accidentally opening the source
    // parquet for writing and avoids Windows sharing/access-denied errors.
    if input.is_file() {
        if let Some(parent) = input.parent() {
            if output_dir == parent {
                return Err(
                    format!(
                        "output directory must be different from the input file's directory:\n  input: {}\n  output: {}",
                        input.display(),
                        output_dir.display()
                    )
                    .into()
                );
            }
        }
    }

    // Discover inputs BEFORE creating output files.
    let input_files = discover_input_files(input)?;

    std::fs::create_dir_all(output_dir)?;

    let mut engine =
        ReplayEngine::with_options(
            tick_size,
            warm_start,
        )?;

    // Replay continuously through gaps in every-event mode.
    //
    // DayStart is implemented as an output filter below:
    // first eligible checkpoint of each UTC day.
    engine.set_checkpoint_policy(
        CheckpointPolicy::EverySnapshot
    );

    let mut written_days =
        std::collections::BTreeSet::<i64>::new();

    for input_file in input_files {
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
        // OUTPUT PATH
        //
        // Never use the same basename as the input parquet.
        //
        // Example:
        //   input/day1/book.parquet
        // becomes:
        //   output/day1/book_every_event.parquet
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------

        let mut current_writer: Option<CheckpointWriter> = None;

        if matches!(
            policy,
            CheckpointPolicy::EverySnapshot
        ) {
            let relative_path = if input.is_dir() {
                input_file
                    .strip_prefix(input)
                    .map_err(|err| {
                        format!(
                            "failed to determine relative path for {} under {}: {err}",
                            input_file.display(),
                            input.display()
                        )
                    })?
                    .to_path_buf()
            } else {
                input_file
                    .file_name()
                    .ok_or_else(|| {
                        format!(
                            "invalid input filename: {}",
                            input_file.display()
                        )
                    })?
                    .into()
            };

            let stem = relative_path
                .file_stem()
                .and_then(|x| x.to_str())
                .ok_or_else(|| {
                    format!(
                        "invalid parquet filename: {}",
                        relative_path.display()
                    )
                })?;

            let output_filename =
                format!("{stem}_every_event.parquet");

            let output_relative =
                relative_path
                    .parent()
                    .unwrap_or_else(|| Path::new(""))
                    .join(output_filename);

            let out_path =
                output_dir.join(output_relative);

            // Make the mirrored subdirectory if necessary.
            if let Some(parent) = out_path.parent() {
                std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
            }

            // Final safety check: never write to the source file.
            if out_path == input_file {
                return Err(
                    format!(
                        "refusing to overwrite input parquet: {}",
                        input_file.display()
                    )
                    .into()
                );
            }

            current_writer =
                Some(
                    CheckpointWriter::new(
                        &out_path
                    )?
                );
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
        // OPEN INPUT ONCE
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------

        let file =
            File::open(&input_file)?;

        let builder =
            ParquetRecordBatchReaderBuilder::try_new(
                file
            )?;

        let reader =
            builder.build()?;

        // Event grouping is per input parquet file.
        let mut pending_event =
            Vec::<LobRow>::new();

        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
        // STREAM RECORD BATCHES
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------

        for batch_result in reader {
            let batch =
                batch_result?;

            let mut rows =
                Vec::with_capacity(
                    batch.num_rows()
                );

            append_rows(
                &batch,
                &mut rows
            )?;

            for row in rows {
                if pending_event.is_empty() {
                    pending_event.push(row);
                    continue;
                }

                if same_event(
                    &pending_event[0],
                    &row,
                ) {
                    pending_event.push(row);
                    continue;
                }

                // The previous grouped exchange event is complete.
                if let Some(checkpoint) =
                    process_one_event(
                        &pending_event,
                        &mut engine,
                    )?
                {
                    match policy {
                        CheckpointPolicy::EverySnapshot => {
                            current_writer
                                .as_mut()
                                .expect(
                                    "every-event writer missing"
                                )
                                .write_checkpoint(
                                    &checkpoint
                                )?;
                        }

                        CheckpointPolicy::DayStart => {
                            let day =
                                utc_day(
                                    checkpoint.event_time
                                );

                            if written_days.insert(day) {
                                let path =
                                    output_dir.join(
                                        format!(
                                            "day_start_{day}.parquet"
                                        )
                                    );

                                let mut writer =
                                    CheckpointWriter::new(
                                        &path
                                    )?;

                                writer.write_checkpoint(
                                    &checkpoint
                                )?;

                                writer.finish()?;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                pending_event.clear();
                pending_event.push(row);
            }
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
        // FLUSH FINAL EVENT FROM THIS INPUT FILE
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------

        if !pending_event.is_empty() {
            if let Some(checkpoint) =
                process_one_event(
                    &pending_event,
                    &mut engine,
                )?
            {
                match policy {
                    CheckpointPolicy::EverySnapshot => {
                        current_writer
                            .as_mut()
                            .expect(
                                "every-event writer missing"
                            )
                            .write_checkpoint(
                                &checkpoint
                            )?;
                    }

                    CheckpointPolicy::DayStart => {
                        let day =
                            utc_day(
                                checkpoint.event_time
                            );

                        if written_days.insert(day) {
                            let path =
                                output_dir.join(
                                    format!(
                                        "day_start_{day}.parquet"
                                    )
                                );

                            let mut writer =
                                CheckpointWriter::new(
                                    &path
                                )?;

                            writer.write_checkpoint(
                                &checkpoint
                            )?;

                            writer.finish()?;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
        // CLOSE OUTPUT WRITER FOR THIS INPUT FILE
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------

        if let Some(writer) =
            current_writer
        {
            writer.finish()?;
        }

        // `reader` and its underlying input File are dropped here
        // before moving to the next input parquet.
    }

    Ok(engine.stats)
}

===== FILE: components/pipelines/lob_builder/btree_ver/replay.rs =====
use crate::book::{LobBook, PriceTick, tick_to_price};

/// Storage-budget policy default: exactly one full-book snapshot is written
/// per UTC day — at the day's hour-00 frame — instead of every snapshot
/// event. `should_checkpoint` encodes that rule; `parquet.rs::ingest_parquet`
/// drives it to write one `<YYYY-MM-DD>.parquet` file per day.
///
/// [`CheckpointPolicy::EverySnapshot`] lifts that restriction: every valid
/// event's book image is stored. `parquet.rs::process_matched` drives this
/// policy to write one full-book row per event.
///
/// This module only decides *whether* the book is trustworthy enough to
/// checkpoint and *which* events are checkpoint-eligible under a policy; it
/// never decides when to write a file — that belongs to `parquet.rs`.
pub const CHECKPOINT_HOUR_UTC: u32 = 0;

/// True when `event_time` (epoch ms) falls in the checkpoint hour (00 UTC).
pub fn should_checkpoint(event_time_ms: i64) -> bool {
    if event_time_ms < 0 {
        return false;
    }
    const DAY_MS: i64 = 86_400_000;
    const HOUR_MS: i64 = 3_600_000;
    let ms_of_day = event_time_ms % DAY_MS;
    let hour = (ms_of_day / HOUR_MS) as u32;
    hour == CHECKPOINT_HOUR_UTC
}

/// Which snapshot events materialize a stored full-book image.
///
/// * `DayStart`       — the storage-budget default: exactly one image per
///   UTC day, taken at the day's hour-00 frame (`should_checkpoint`).
/// * `EverySnapshot`  — no hour gate and no once-per-day guard: `checkpoint`
///   materializes an image for every call it is given while the engine is
///   valid. It is the caller's choice (see `parquet.rs::process_matched`)
///   whether "every call" means every event or only snapshot-type ones —
///   this policy itself does not filter by event type.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum CheckpointPolicy {
    DayStart,
    EverySnapshot,
}

impl CheckpointPolicy {
    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::DayStart => "day_start",
            Self::EverySnapshot => "every_event",
        }
    }

    pub fn parse(name: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        match name.trim().to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
            "day_start" => Some(Self::DayStart),
            "every_event" => Some(Self::EverySnapshot),
            "every_snapshot" => Some(Self::EverySnapshot),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
}
/// UTC day index of an epoch-ms timestamp (floor division, so pre-1970
/// timestamps stay aligned to the same day boundaries).
pub fn day_index(event_time_ms: i64) -> i64 {
    event_time_ms.div_euclid(86_400_000)
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum EventType {
    Snapshot,
    Update,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
pub struct LobRow {
    pub event_time: i64,
    pub event_type: EventType,
    pub is_bid: bool,
    pub price: f64,
    pub quantity: f64,
    pub first_update_id: Option<u64>,
    pub final_update_id: Option<u64>,
    pub prev_final_update_id: Option<u64>,
}

/// One stored full-book image.
///
/// Under [`CheckpointPolicy::DayStart`] emitted at most once per UTC day
/// (hour 00); under [`CheckpointPolicy::EverySnapshot`] emitted for every
/// checkpoint-eligible event. Carries integer-tick levels (bids desc, asks
/// asc) plus float mirrors so `parquet.rs` can write prices straight to disk
/// without re-scaling.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct BookCheckpoint {
    pub event_time: i64,
    pub final_update_id: u64,
    pub bids: Vec<(PriceTick, f64)>,
    pub asks: Vec<(PriceTick, f64)>,
    pub bid_prices: Vec<f64>,
    pub ask_prices: Vec<f64>,
}

impl BookCheckpoint {
    pub fn bid_levels(&self) -> usize {
        self.bids.len()
    }

    pub fn ask_levels(&self) -> usize {
        self.asks.len()
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Default, Clone, Copy)]
pub struct ReplayStats {
    /// Snapshot events that reseeded the book (every snapshot still resets
    /// state — the daily checkpoint policy only limits *writes*, not reseeds).
    pub snapshot_events: u64,
    pub update_events: u64,
    pub gaps: u64,
    pub malformed_events: u64,
    /// Crossed states seen at emission time (or refused at checkpoint time):
    /// `best_bid >= best_ask` is unverifiable, so it is never published.
    pub crossed_books: u64,
    pub rows_before_initial_snapshot: u64,
    /// Checkpoints actually materialized (at most one per UTC day, hour 00,
    /// under [`CheckpointPolicy::DayStart`]).
    pub snapshots: u64,
    /// Day-start boundaries where no image could be stored (state unseeded,
    /// invalid, one-sided or crossed) plus non-00-hour `checkpoint()` calls
    /// rejected by the storage policy.
    pub skipped_checkpoints: u64,
    /// `event_time` batches replayed (one diff frame each).
    pub batches: u64,
    /// Book-state rows actually emitted (one per published batch).
    pub emitted_rows: u64,
    /// Batches dropped because their state could not be published (unseeded,
    /// invalid, a side empty, or crossed).
    pub skipped_state_rows: u64,
    /// Batches applied on top of a diff-only (unseeded) or just-rebased book —
    /// i.e. batches whose starting state came from a warm start rather than
    /// from a trusted snapshot. Zero for a fully snapshot-seeded replay.
    pub warm_start_batches: u64,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum ReplayError {
    InvalidTickSize,
    InvalidPrice(f64),
    NegativeQuantity(f64),
    NonMonotonicEventTime,
    SnapshotMissingFinalId,
    UpdateMissingIds,
    InconsistentBatchIds,
    PartialSnapshot,
    /// Columnar input columns do not all carry the same number of rows.
    RaggedColumns,
    PriceNotAligned {
        price: f64,
        tick_size: f64,
    },
}

impl std::fmt::Display for ReplayError {
    fn fmt(
        &self,
        f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>,
    ) -> std::fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::InvalidTickSize =>
                write!(f, "tick_size must be > 0"),

            Self::InvalidPrice(p) =>
                write!(f, "invalid price: {p}"),

            Self::NegativeQuantity(q) =>
                write!(f, "negative quantity: {q}"),

            Self::NonMonotonicEventTime =>
                write!(f, "event_time is not monotonic"),

            Self::SnapshotMissingFinalId =>
                write!(f, "snapshot missing final_update_id"),

            Self::UpdateMissingIds =>
                write!(f, "update missing required update IDs"),

            Self::InconsistentBatchIds =>
                write!(f, "rows inside one event have inconsistent IDs"),

            Self::PartialSnapshot =>
                write!(f, "snapshot does not contain both bid and ask sides"),

            Self::RaggedColumns =>
                write!(f, "columnar input has mismatched column lengths"),

            Self::PriceNotAligned {
                price,
                tick_size,
            } =>
                write!(
                    f,
                    "price {price} is not aligned to tick size {tick_size}"
                ),
        }
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for ReplayError {}

pub struct ReplayEngine {
    pub book: LobBook,

    tick_size: f64,

    /// `true` = accept diff-only streams (no leading snapshot frame at all):
    /// the first diff batch itself seeds the book, and a gap rebases from
    /// diffs alone instead of latching invalid. `false` = strict: nothing is
    /// applied before a trusted snapshot, and a gap latches the state
    /// invalid until the next snapshot.
    warm_start: bool,

    /// True only after a complete trusted snapshot (or the first diff batch in
    /// warm-start mode).
    seeded: bool,

    /// False after a detected sequence gap (strict mode only).
    valid: bool,

    last_final_update_id: Option<u64>,
    last_event_time: Option<i64>,

    /// UTC day of the last materialized checkpoint, so a day is written once
    /// even when the stream re-enters its hour-00 window (e.g. a resumed run).
    last_checkpoint_day: Option<i64>,

    /// Which snapshot events materialize stored images (see
    /// [`CheckpointPolicy`]). Default `DayStart` — the storage-budget rule.
    checkpoint_policy: CheckpointPolicy,

    pub stats: ReplayStats,
}

impl ReplayEngine {
    /// Strict engine: a trusted snapshot must seed the book before any diff is
    /// applied, and a gap keeps the state invalid until the next snapshot.
    pub fn new(
        tick_size: f64,
    ) -> Result<Self, ReplayError> {
        Self::with_options(tick_size, false)
    }

    /// `warm_start = true` enables the data-driven fallback: the first diff
    /// batch itself seeds the book, and a gap rebases (clear + continue)
    /// instead of latching the state invalid. Both counters (`gaps`,
    /// `warm_start_batches`) still record what happened.
    pub fn with_options(
        tick_size: f64,
        warm_start: bool,
    ) -> Result<Self, ReplayError> {
        if !tick_size.is_finite() || tick_size <= 0.0 {
            return Err(ReplayError::InvalidTickSize);
        }

        Ok(Self {
            book: LobBook::new(),
            tick_size,
            warm_start,
            seeded: false,
            valid: false,
            last_final_update_id: None,
            last_event_time: None,
            last_checkpoint_day: None,
            checkpoint_policy: CheckpointPolicy::DayStart,
            stats: ReplayStats::default(),
        })
    }

    pub fn is_valid(&self) -> bool {
        self.seeded && self.valid
    }

    pub fn warm_start(&self) -> bool {
        self.warm_start
    }

    /// Which snapshot events materialize stored images.
    pub fn checkpoint_policy(&self) -> CheckpointPolicy {
        self.checkpoint_policy
    }

    /// Select the snapshot storage policy (see [`CheckpointPolicy`]).
    /// Must be set before replaying; switching mid-stream would make the
    /// stored-image history inconsistent.
    pub fn set_checkpoint_policy(&mut self, policy: CheckpointPolicy) {
        self.checkpoint_policy = policy;
    }

    /// The book under replay (emission/checkpoint reads only).
    pub fn book(&self) -> &LobBook {
        &self.book
    }

    /// Seed the engine with a stored day-start checkpoint so a later run can
    /// resume from it instead of replaying history (the storage-side reason
    /// those snapshots exist). The id chain continues from
    /// `final_update_id`, so the first batch after the seed must have
    /// `prev_final_update_id == final_update_id` to look gap-free.
    pub fn seed_snapshot(
        &mut self,
        event_time: i64,
        final_update_id: u64,
        bids: &[(crate::book::PriceTick, f64)],
        asks: &[(crate::book::PriceTick, f64)],
    ) -> Result<(), ReplayError> {
        if bids.is_empty() || asks.is_empty() {
            return Err(ReplayError::PartialSnapshot);
        }

        self.book.apply_snapshot(bids, asks);

        self.last_final_update_id = Some(final_update_id);
        self.last_event_time = Some(event_time);
        self.last_checkpoint_day = Some(day_index(event_time));
        self.seeded = true;
        self.valid = true;

        Ok(())
    }

    fn invalidate(&mut self) {
        self.book.clear();
        self.seeded = false;
        self.valid = false;
        self.last_final_update_id = None;
    }

    fn price_to_tick(
        &self,
        price: f64,
    ) -> Result<PriceTick, ReplayError> {
        if !price.is_finite() || price <= 0.0 {
            return Err(ReplayError::InvalidPrice(price));
        }

        let scaled = price / self.tick_size;
        let rounded = scaled.round();

        let tolerance =
            1e-9 * scaled.abs().max(1.0);

        if (scaled - rounded).abs() > tolerance {
            return Err(
                ReplayError::PriceNotAligned {
                    price,
                    tick_size: self.tick_size,
                }
            );
        }

        crate::book::price_to_tick(price).ok_or(ReplayError::InvalidPrice(price))
    }

    /// Process one complete exchange event.
    ///
    /// `rows` contains all L2 rows belonging to the same
    /// snapshot/update event.
    ///
    /// IMPORTANT:
    /// This function does NOT materialize top-N output.
    ///
    /// Hot path is:
    ///     validate -> HashMap mutations
    ///
    /// Daily checkpointing happens separately.
    pub fn process_event(
        &mut self,
        rows: &[LobRow],
    ) -> Result<(), ReplayError> {
        if rows.is_empty() {
            return Ok(());
        }

        let event_time = rows[0].event_time;

        if let Some(previous) = self.last_event_time {
            if event_time < previous {
                return Err(
                    ReplayError::NonMonotonicEventTime
                );
            }
        }

        self.last_event_time = Some(event_time);

        let event_type = rows[0].event_type;
        let first_id = rows[0].first_update_id;
        let final_id = rows[0].final_update_id;
        let prev_id = rows[0].prev_final_update_id;

        let consistent = rows.iter().all(|row| {
            row.event_time == event_time
                && row.event_type == event_type
                && row.first_update_id == first_id
                && row.final_update_id == final_id
                && row.prev_final_update_id == prev_id
        });

        if !consistent {
            self.stats.malformed_events += 1;
            self.invalidate();

            return Err(
                ReplayError::InconsistentBatchIds
            );
        }

        match event_type {
            EventType::Snapshot => {
                self.process_snapshot(
                    rows,
                    final_id,
                )
            }

            EventType::Update => {
                self.process_update(
                    rows,
                    first_id,
                    final_id,
                    prev_id,
                )
            }
        }
    }

    fn process_snapshot(
        &mut self,
        rows: &[LobRow],
        final_id: Option<u64>,
    ) -> Result<(), ReplayError> {
        self.stats.snapshot_events += 1;

        let final_id = final_id.ok_or(
            ReplayError::SnapshotMissingFinalId
        )?;

        let has_bid =
            rows.iter().any(|r| r.is_bid);

        let has_ask =
            rows.iter().any(|r| !r.is_bid);

        if !has_bid || !has_ask {
            self.invalidate();

            return Err(
                ReplayError::PartialSnapshot
            );
        }

        let mut bids =
            Vec::with_capacity(rows.len());

        let mut asks =
            Vec::with_capacity(rows.len());

        for row in rows {
            if row.quantity < 0.0 {
                return Err(
                    ReplayError::NegativeQuantity(
                        row.quantity
                    )
                );
            }

            let price =
                self.price_to_tick(row.price)?;

            if row.quantity == 0.0 {
                continue;
            }

            if row.is_bid {
                bids.push((price, row.quantity));
            } else {
                asks.push((price, row.quantity));
            }
        }

        self.book.apply_snapshot(
            &bids,
            &asks,
        );

        self.last_final_update_id =
            Some(final_id);

        self.seeded = true;
        self.valid = true;

        Ok(())
    }

    fn process_update(
        &mut self,
        rows: &[LobRow],
        first_id: Option<u64>,
        final_id: Option<u64>,
        prev_id: Option<u64>,
    ) -> Result<(), ReplayError> {
        self.stats.update_events += 1;

        // Missing continuity ids: the batch cannot be verified.
        //   * strict mode  -> refuse it (caller sees UpdateMissingIds)
        //   * warm start   -> treat it as diff-only data and apply it, counted
        //                     as a warm-start batch (diffs are absolute
        //                     upserts, so applying them is still meaningful)
        let ids = match (first_id, final_id, prev_id) {
            (Some(f), Some(l), Some(p)) => Some((f, l, p)),
            _ => {
                if !self.warm_start {
                    return Err(ReplayError::UpdateMissingIds);
                }

                // Applying it is still meaningful (diffs are absolute
                // upserts); the warm-start counter is bumped where the state
                // provenance is decided, not here.
                None
            }
        };

        // Ignore updates before the initial trusted snapshot — unless the
        // stream is diff-only (warm start), in which case the first diff
        // batch becomes the base of a warm-started book.
        if !self.seeded {
            if !self.warm_start {
                self.stats.rows_before_initial_snapshot +=
                    rows.len() as u64;

                return Ok(());
            }

            self.seeded = true;
            self.valid = true;
            self.stats.warm_start_batches += 1;
        }

        // Once invalid, remain invalid until another trusted snapshot.
        if !self.valid {
            return Ok(());
        }

        // Binance-style continuity check.
        //
        // U <= previous_final + 1 <= u
        // and pu == previous_final.
       if let (Some((first_id, final_id, prev_id)), Some(last_final)) =
            (ids, self.last_final_update_id)
        {
            let expected =
                last_final
                    .checked_add(1)
                    .ok_or(ReplayError::UpdateMissingIds)?;

            if prev_id != last_final
                || first_id > expected
                || final_id < expected
            {
                self.stats.gaps += 1;

                if self.warm_start {
                    self.book.clear();
                    self.last_final_update_id = None;
                    self.stats.warm_start_batches += 1;
                } else if matches!(
                    self.checkpoint_policy,
                    CheckpointPolicy::EverySnapshot
                ) {
                    // Keep replaying in every-event mode.
                    // A gap is recorded, but it does not suppress
                    // the requested per-event checkpoint stream.
                    self.seeded = true;
                    self.valid = true;
                } else {
                    self.invalidate();
                    return Ok(());
                }
            }
        }

        // Apply every changed level.
        //
        // This is the hot path:
        // HashMap lookup/insert/remove = O(1) expected.
        for row in rows {
            if row.quantity < 0.0 {
                return Err(
                    ReplayError::NegativeQuantity(
                        row.quantity
                    )
                );
            }

            let price =
                self.price_to_tick(row.price)?;

            self.book.apply_update(
                row.is_bid,
                price,
                row.quantity,
            );
        }

        if let Some((_, final_id, _)) = ids {
            self.last_final_update_id =
                Some(final_id);
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    /// Materialize a full-book checkpoint.
    ///
    /// STORAGE POLICY (`self.checkpoint_policy`):
    ///   * `DayStart` — only snapshot events whose `event_time` falls in
    ///     hour 00 UTC (`should_checkpoint`), at most ONCE per UTC day —
    ///     every snapshot still reseeds the book via `process_event`, but
    ///     only the day-start image is kept on disk. Callers that pass a
    ///     non-00-hour event get `None` and a `skipped_checkpoints` bump so
    ///     the daily print can report generated-vs-skipped counts.
    ///   * `EverySnapshot` — no hour gate and no once-per-day guard: every
    ///     call (one per snapshot event, gated by the columnar driver) stores
    ///     that instant's book image.
    pub fn checkpoint(
        &mut self,
        event_time: i64,
    ) -> Option<BookCheckpoint> {
        if let CheckpointPolicy::DayStart = self.checkpoint_policy {
            if !should_checkpoint(event_time) {
                self.stats.skipped_checkpoints += 1;
                return None;
            }

            if self.last_checkpoint_day == Some(day_index(event_time)) {
                // That UTC day is already on disk: keep the FIRST image of the
                // day (a resumed run re-enters the same hour-00 window).
                return None;
            }
        }

        if !self.is_valid() {
            return None;
        }

        if self.book.is_crossed() {
            self.stats.crossed_books += 1;
            return None;
        }

        let final_update_id =
            self.last_final_update_id?;

        let (bids, asks) =
            self.book.full_snapshot();

        if bids.is_empty() || asks.is_empty() {
            return None;
        }

        let bid_prices: Vec<f64> =
            bids.iter().map(|(t, _)| tick_to_price(*t)).collect();
        let ask_prices: Vec<f64> =
            asks.iter().map(|(t, _)| tick_to_price(*t)).collect();

        self.stats.snapshots += 1;
        self.last_checkpoint_day = Some(day_index(event_time));

        Some(BookCheckpoint {
            event_time,
            final_update_id,
            bids,
            asks,
            bid_prices,
            ask_prices,
        })
    }
}

===== FILE: components/pipelines/lob_builder/hash_ver/book.rs =====
use crate::book::{LobBook, PriceTick};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum EventType {
    Snapshot,
    Update,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
pub struct LobRow {
    pub event_time: i64,
    pub event_type: EventType,
    pub is_bid: bool,
    pub price: f64,
    pub quantity: f64,
    pub first_update_id: Option<u64>,
    pub final_update_id: Option<u64>,
    pub prev_final_update_id: Option<u64>,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct BookCheckpoint {
    pub event_time: i64,
    pub final_update_id: u64,
    pub bids: Vec<(PriceTick, f64)>,
    pub asks: Vec<(PriceTick, f64)>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct ReplayStats {
    pub snapshot_events: u64,
    pub update_events: u64,
    pub gaps: u64,
    pub malformed_events: u64,
    pub crossed_books: u64,
    pub rows_before_initial_snapshot: u64,
    pub checkpoints: u64,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum ReplayError {
    InvalidTickSize,
    InvalidPrice(f64),
    NegativeQuantity(f64),
    NonMonotonicEventTime,
    SnapshotMissingFinalId,
    UpdateMissingIds,
    InconsistentBatchIds,
    PartialSnapshot,
    PriceNotAligned {
        price: f64,
        tick_size: f64,
    },
}

impl std::fmt::Display for ReplayError {
    fn fmt(
        &self,
        f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>,
    ) -> std::fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::InvalidTickSize =>
                write!(f, "tick_size must be > 0"),

            Self::InvalidPrice(p) =>
                write!(f, "invalid price: {p}"),

            Self::NegativeQuantity(q) =>
                write!(f, "negative quantity: {q}"),

            Self::NonMonotonicEventTime =>
                write!(f, "event_time is not monotonic"),

            Self::SnapshotMissingFinalId =>
                write!(f, "snapshot missing final_update_id"),

            Self::UpdateMissingIds =>
                write!(f, "update missing required update IDs"),

            Self::InconsistentBatchIds =>
                write!(f, "rows inside one event have inconsistent IDs"),

            Self::PartialSnapshot =>
                write!(f, "snapshot does not contain both bid and ask sides"),

            Self::PriceNotAligned {
                price,
                tick_size,
            } =>
                write!(
                    f,
                    "price {price} is not aligned to tick size {tick_size}"
                ),
        }
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for ReplayError {}

pub struct ReplayEngine {
    pub book: LobBook,

    tick_size: f64,

    /// True only after a complete trusted snapshot.
    seeded: bool,

    /// False after a detected sequence gap.
    valid: bool,

    last_final_update_id: Option<u64>,
    last_event_time: Option<i64>,

    pub stats: ReplayStats,
}

impl ReplayEngine {
    pub fn new(
        tick_size: f64,
    ) -> Result<Self, ReplayError> {
        if !tick_size.is_finite() || tick_size <= 0.0 {
            return Err(ReplayError::InvalidTickSize);
        }

        Ok(Self {
            book: LobBook::new(),
            tick_size,
            seeded: false,
            valid: false,
            last_final_update_id: None,
            last_event_time: None,
            stats: ReplayStats::default(),
        })
    }

    pub fn is_valid(&self) -> bool {
        self.seeded && self.valid
    }

    fn invalidate(&mut self) {
        self.book.clear();
        self.seeded = false;
        self.valid = false;
        self.last_final_update_id = None;
    }

    fn price_to_tick(
        &self,
        price: f64,
    ) -> Result<PriceTick, ReplayError> {
        if !price.is_finite() || price <= 0.0 {
            return Err(ReplayError::InvalidPrice(price));
        }

        let scaled = price / self.tick_size;
        let rounded = scaled.round();

        let tolerance =
            1e-9 * scaled.abs().max(1.0);

        if (scaled - rounded).abs() > tolerance {
            return Err(
                ReplayError::PriceNotAligned {
                    price,
                    tick_size: self.tick_size,
                }
            );
        }

        Ok(rounded as PriceTick)
    }

    /// Process one complete exchange event.
    ///
    /// `rows` contains all L2 rows belonging to the same
    /// snapshot/update event.
    ///
    /// IMPORTANT:
    /// This function does NOT materialize top-N output.
    ///
    /// Hot path is:
    ///     validate -> HashMap mutations
    ///
    /// Daily checkpointing happens separately.
    pub fn process_event(
        &mut self,
        rows: &[LobRow],
    ) -> Result<(), ReplayError> {
        if rows.is_empty() {
            return Ok(());
        }

        let event_time = rows[0].event_time;

        if let Some(previous) = self.last_event_time {
            if event_time < previous {
                return Err(
                    ReplayError::NonMonotonicEventTime
                );
            }
        }

        self.last_event_time = Some(event_time);

        let event_type = rows[0].event_type;
        let first_id = rows[0].first_update_id;
        let final_id = rows[0].final_update_id;
        let prev_id = rows[0].prev_final_update_id;

        let consistent = rows.iter().all(|row| {
            row.event_time == event_time
                && row.event_type == event_type
                && row.first_update_id == first_id
                && row.final_update_id == final_id
                && row.prev_final_update_id == prev_id
        });

        if !consistent {
            self.stats.malformed_events += 1;
            self.invalidate();

            return Err(
                ReplayError::InconsistentBatchIds
            );
        }

        match event_type {
            EventType::Snapshot => {
                self.process_snapshot(
                    rows,
                    final_id,
                )
            }

            EventType::Update => {
                self.process_update(
                    rows,
                    first_id,
                    final_id,
                    prev_id,
                )
            }
        }
    }

    fn process_snapshot(
        &mut self,
        rows: &[LobRow],
        final_id: Option<u64>,
    ) -> Result<(), ReplayError> {
        self.stats.snapshot_events += 1;

        let final_id = final_id.ok_or(
            ReplayError::SnapshotMissingFinalId
        )?;

        let has_bid =
            rows.iter().any(|r| r.is_bid);

        let has_ask =
            rows.iter().any(|r| !r.is_bid);

        if !has_bid || !has_ask {
            self.invalidate();

            return Err(
                ReplayError::PartialSnapshot
            );
        }

        let mut bids =
            Vec::with_capacity(rows.len());

        let mut asks =
            Vec::with_capacity(rows.len());

        for row in rows {
            if row.quantity < 0.0 {
                return Err(
                    ReplayError::NegativeQuantity(
                        row.quantity
                    )
                );
            }

            let price =
                self.price_to_tick(row.price)?;

            if row.quantity == 0.0 {
                continue;
            }

            if row.is_bid {
                bids.push((price, row.quantity));
            } else {
                asks.push((price, row.quantity));
            }
        }

        self.book.apply_snapshot(
            &bids,
            &asks,
        );

        self.last_final_update_id =
            Some(final_id);

        self.seeded = true;
        self.valid = true;

        Ok(())
    }

    fn process_update(
        &mut self,
        rows: &[LobRow],
        first_id: Option<u64>,
        final_id: Option<u64>,
        prev_id: Option<u64>,
    ) -> Result<(), ReplayError> {
        self.stats.update_events += 1;

        let first_id = first_id.ok_or(
            ReplayError::UpdateMissingIds
        )?;

        let final_id = final_id.ok_or(
            ReplayError::UpdateMissingIds
        )?;

        let prev_id = prev_id.ok_or(
            ReplayError::UpdateMissingIds
        )?;

        // Ignore updates before the initial trusted snapshot.
        if !self.seeded {
            self.stats.rows_before_initial_snapshot +=
                rows.len() as u64;

            return Ok(());
        }

        // Once invalid, remain invalid until another trusted snapshot.
        if !self.valid {
            return Ok(());
        }

        // Binance-style continuity check.
        //
        // U <= previous_final + 1 <= u
        // and pu == previous_final.
        if let Some(last_final) =
            self.last_final_update_id
        {
            let expected = last_final
                .checked_add(1)
                .ok_or(
                    ReplayError::UpdateMissingIds
                )?;

            if prev_id != last_final
                || first_id > expected
                || final_id < expected
            {
                self.stats.gaps += 1;

                self.invalidate();

                return Ok(());
            }
        }

        // Apply every changed level.
        //
        // This is the hot path:
        // HashMap lookup/insert/remove = O(1) expected.
        for row in rows {
            if row.quantity < 0.0 {
                return Err(
                    ReplayError::NegativeQuantity(
                        row.quantity
                    )
                );
            }

            let price =
                self.price_to_tick(row.price)?;

            self.book.apply_update(
                row.is_bid,
                price,
                row.quantity,
            );
        }

        self.last_final_update_id =
            Some(final_id);

        Ok(())
    }

    /// Materialize a full-book checkpoint.
    ///
    /// Call this ONCE per UTC day, not once per event.
    pub fn checkpoint(
        &mut self,
        event_time: i64,
    ) -> Option<BookCheckpoint> {
        if !self.is_valid() {
            return None;
        }

        if self.book.is_crossed() {
            self.stats.crossed_books += 1;
            return None;
        }

        let final_update_id =
            self.last_final_update_id?;

        let (bids, asks) =
            self.book.full_snapshot();

        if bids.is_empty() || asks.is_empty() {
            return None;
        }

        self.stats.checkpoints += 1;

        Some(BookCheckpoint {
            event_time,
            final_update_id,
            bids,
            asks,
        })
    }
}

===== FILE: components/pipelines/lob_builder/hash_ver/replay.rs =====
use std::cmp::Reverse;
use std::collections::{BinaryHeap, HashMap, HashSet};

/// Price represented as an integer tick.
///
/// Example:
/// BTCUSDT tick size = 0.10
/// price 42100.10 -> 421001
pub type PriceTick = i64;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
pub struct Level {
    pub quantity: f64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct LobBook {
    /// Canonical full-depth state.
    ///
    /// HashMap gives O(1) expected lookup / insert / remove.
    bids: HashMap<PriceTick, Level>,
    asks: HashMap<PriceTick, Level>,

    /// Candidate best prices.
    ///
    /// These are maintained lazily:
    /// - stale prices may remain in the heap
    /// - stale entries are discarded only when queried
    ///
    /// This avoids scanning the entire HashMap when the best
    /// bid/ask changes.
    bid_heap: BinaryHeap<PriceTick>,
    ask_heap: BinaryHeap<Reverse<PriceTick>>,
}

impl LobBook {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    pub fn clear(&mut self) {
        self.bids.clear();
        self.asks.clear();
        self.bid_heap.clear();
        self.ask_heap.clear();
    }

    pub fn bid_levels(&self) -> usize {
        self.bids.len()
    }

    pub fn ask_levels(&self) -> usize {
        self.asks.len()
    }

    /// Replace the entire book with a trusted full snapshot.
    ///
    /// IMPORTANT:
    /// We retain the FULL book internally.
    /// Output depth is decided later.
    pub fn apply_snapshot(
        &mut self,
        bids: &[(PriceTick, f64)],
        asks: &[(PriceTick, f64)],
    ) {
        self.clear();

        for &(price, quantity) in bids {
            if quantity > 0.0 {
                self.bids.insert(price, Level { quantity });
                self.bid_heap.push(price);
            }
        }

        for &(price, quantity) in asks {
            if quantity > 0.0 {
                self.asks.insert(price, Level { quantity });
                self.ask_heap.push(Reverse(price));
            }
        }
    }

    /// Apply one absolute-quantity update.
    ///
    /// quantity > 0:
    ///     set absolute quantity at this price.
    ///
    /// quantity == 0:
    ///     remove this price level from this side.
    ///
    /// Expected complexity:
    ///     O(1) expected for HashMap mutation.
    ///
    /// Heap insertion only occurs when a previously absent price
    /// becomes active.
    pub fn apply_update(
        &mut self,
        is_bid: bool,
        price: PriceTick,
        quantity: f64,
    ) -> bool {
        if quantity < 0.0 {
            return false;
        }

        if quantity == 0.0 {
            return if is_bid {
                self.bids.remove(&price).is_some()
            } else {
                self.asks.remove(&price).is_some()
            };
        }

        if is_bid {
            // Enforce single-side invariant.
            self.asks.remove(&price);

            let existed = self.bids.contains_key(&price);

            self.bids.insert(
                price,
                Level { quantity },
            );

            if !existed {
                self.bid_heap.push(price);
            }

            true
        } else {
            // Enforce single-side invariant.
            self.bids.remove(&price);

            let existed = self.asks.contains_key(&price);

            self.asks.insert(
                price,
                Level { quantity },
            );

            if !existed {
                self.ask_heap.push(Reverse(price));
            }

            true
        }
    }

    /// Remove stale bid heap entries until the top points to
    /// an actually active bid.
    fn clean_bid_heap(&mut self) {
        while let Some(&price) = self.bid_heap.peek() {
            if self.bids.contains_key(&price) {
                break;
            }

            self.bid_heap.pop();
        }
    }

    /// Remove stale ask heap entries until the top points to
    /// an actually active ask.
    fn clean_ask_heap(&mut self) {
        while let Some(&Reverse(price)) = self.ask_heap.peek() {
            if self.asks.contains_key(&price) {
                break;
            }

            self.ask_heap.pop();
        }
    }

    /// Best bid.
    pub fn best_bid(&mut self) -> Option<(PriceTick, f64)> {
        self.clean_bid_heap();

        self.bid_heap
            .peek()
            .and_then(|&price| {
                self.bids
                    .get(&price)
                    .map(|level| (price, level.quantity))
            })
    }

    /// Best ask.
    pub fn best_ask(&mut self) -> Option<(PriceTick, f64)> {
        self.clean_ask_heap();

        self.ask_heap
            .peek()
            .and_then(|&Reverse(price)| {
                self.asks
                    .get(&price)
                    .map(|level| (price, level.quantity))
            })
    }

    /// Returns true if best bid >= best ask.
    pub fn is_crossed(&mut self) -> bool {
        match (self.best_bid(), self.best_ask()) {
            (Some((bid, _)), Some((ask, _))) => bid >= ask,
            _ => false,
        }
    }

    /// Extract top-N levels.
    ///
    /// This is intentionally NOT used on every event.
    /// It is for checkpointing / occasional strategy output.
    ///
    /// Complexity:
    ///     O(r log N)
    ///
    /// where r = number of active price levels.
    pub fn top_n(
        &self,
        levels: usize,
    ) -> (
        Vec<(PriceTick, f64)>,
        Vec<(PriceTick, f64)>,
    ) {
        if levels == 0 {
            return (Vec::new(), Vec::new());
        }

        // Small bounded heaps.
        // This avoids sorting the entire book just to obtain top N.
        let mut bid_candidates: BinaryHeap<PriceTick> =
            BinaryHeap::with_capacity(levels);

        let mut ask_candidates: BinaryHeap<Reverse<PriceTick>> =
            BinaryHeap::with_capacity(levels);

        for &price in self.bids.keys() {
            bid_candidates.push(price);

            if bid_candidates.len() > levels {
                bid_candidates.pop();
            }
        }

        for &price in self.asks.keys() {
            ask_candidates.push(Reverse(price));

            if ask_candidates.len() > levels {
                ask_candidates.pop();
            }
        }

        let mut bids = bid_candidates
            .into_iter()
            .filter_map(|price| {
                self.bids
                    .get(&price)
                    .map(|level| (price, level.quantity))
            })
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();

        let mut asks = ask_candidates
            .into_iter()
            .filter_map(|Reverse(price)| {
                self.asks
                    .get(&price)
                    .map(|level| (price, level.quantity))
            })
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();

        bids.sort_unstable_by(|a, b| b.0.cmp(&a.0));
        asks.sort_unstable_by(|a, b| a.0.cmp(&b.0));

        (bids, asks)
    }

    /// Extract the FULL current book for a daily checkpoint.
    ///
    /// This is intentionally called only at the checkpoint boundary.
    ///
    /// Complexity:
    ///     O(r log r)
    ///
    /// where r = active levels on both sides.
    pub fn full_snapshot(
        &self,
    ) -> (
        Vec<(PriceTick, f64)>,
        Vec<(PriceTick, f64)>,
    ) {
        let mut bids = self
            .bids
            .iter()
            .map(|(&price, level)| (price, level.quantity))
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();

        let mut asks = self
            .asks
            .iter()
            .map(|(&price, level)| (price, level.quantity))
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();

        // Preserve canonical book ordering for serialized checkpoints.
        bids.sort_unstable_by(|a, b| b.0.cmp(&a.0));
        asks.sort_unstable_by(|a, b| a.0.cmp(&b.0));

        (bids, asks)
    }
}
