===== FILE: components/pipelines/uets_db/src/lib.rs =====
//! `uets_db` — persistence layer for the UETS trading system.
//!
//! Owns the embedded key-value store (sled, RocksDB-compatible), record
//! schemas, serialization, CRUD operations, indexed queries, batched writes,
//! session management, schema versioning, and recovery.
//!
//! Logical structure (shared store, session-aware keys):
//! ```text
//! live_db/
//!   trades/     -> trade records (session, strategy, asset, time)
//!   portfolio/  -> portfolio snapshots (session, strategy, asset, time)
//! ```
//!
//! Other components must use this API and must not open or manipulate the
//! store directly.

pub mod persistence;
pub mod schema;
pub mod store;

pub use persistence::{
    PersistError, PersistItem, PersistStats, PersistenceConfig, PersistenceHandle, StatsHandle,
    SubmitOutcome,
};
pub use schema::{
    IntegrityReport, OrderEvent, PortfolioRecord, QueryFilter, SchemaVersion, SessionMeta,
    SessionSummary, StrategyInstance, TradeRecord,
};
pub use store::{DbError, UetsDb};

===== FILE: components/pipelines/uets_db/src/persistence.rs =====
//! Asynchronous persistence — bounded queue, explicit backpressure, and
//! guaranteed handling of critical records.
//!
//! Design:
//! - One dedicated writer thread performs all disk I/O, so neither the
//!   strategy loop nor the reporting calculation path ever blocks on disk
//!   (except critical-item backpressure when the queue is saturated).
//! - The queue is bounded (`PersistenceConfig::queue_capacity`).
//! - Critical items (trade records, session summaries) are **never silently
//!   dropped**: on a full queue they apply backpressure (blocking send); on
//!   storage failure they are retried, then quarantined with a loud error
//!   log and a stats counter, retried again on later cycles, and finally
//!   reported as an error from [`PersistenceWorker::shutdown`].
//! - Non-critical items (portfolio snapshots) are dropped under saturation,
//!   but every drop is counted in stats and logged (rate-limited), so
//!   nothing is discarded silently.
//!
//! ```text
//! producers ──try_send──▶ bounded queue ──writer thread──▶ UetsDb
//!    (strategy/reporter)    (backpressure)    (batched writes, retries)
//! ```

use crate::schema::{PortfolioRecord, SessionSummary, TradeRecord};
use crate::store::{DbError, UetsDb};
use std::collections::VecDeque;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, AtomicUsize, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::thread::JoinHandle;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

/// Retry cadence after a failed write attempt.
const RETRY_DELAY: Duration = Duration::from_millis(25);
/// Attempts before an item is quarantined (≈ 1 s with [`RETRY_DELAY`]).
const MAX_ATTEMPTS: u32 = 40;

/// One item to persist.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub enum PersistItem {
    /// Critical: a trade/order record. Must never be lost.
    Trade(TradeRecord),
    /// Non-critical: a portfolio snapshot at reporting cadence.
    /// Droppable under saturation (but counted, never silent).
    Portfolio(PortfolioRecord),
    /// Critical: a finalized session summary. Must never be lost.
    SessionSummary(SessionSummary),
}

impl PersistItem {
    pub fn is_critical(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(
            self,
            PersistItem::Trade(_) | PersistItem::SessionSummary(_)
        )
    }

    fn describe(&self) -> String {
        match self {
            PersistItem::Trade(t) => format!("trade {}/{}", t.session_id, t.instance.composite()),
            PersistItem::Portfolio(p) => {
                format!("portfolio {}/{}", p.session_id, p.instance.composite())
            }
            PersistItem::SessionSummary(s) => format!("session summary {}", s.session_id),
        }
    }
}

/// Outcome of a submit attempt.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum SubmitOutcome {
    /// Queued without waiting.
    Accepted,
    /// Critical item queued only after waiting for space (backpressure).
    AcceptedBlocked,
    /// Non-critical item dropped because the queue was full — counted in
    /// [`PersistStats::dropped_snapshots`] and logged.
    Dropped,
}

/// Errors returned to producers.
#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum PersistError {
    #[error("persistence is closed; {0} item(s) not accepted")]
    Closed(usize),
    #[error("shutdown finished with {critical} critical and {total} total item(s) unwritten")]
    Unwritten { critical: usize, total: usize },
}

/// Point-in-time persistence statistics (for monitoring/GUI).
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default)]
pub struct PersistStats {
    /// Items currently waiting in the queue.
    pub queue_depth: usize,
    /// Items accepted into the queue (all kinds).
    pub enqueued: u64,
    /// Items durably written.
    pub written: u64,
    /// Failed write attempts (storage errors surfaced, not hidden).
    pub write_errors: u64,
    /// Portfolio snapshots dropped due to queue saturation.
    pub dropped_snapshots: u64,
    /// Items currently in quarantine (failed after all retries).
    pub quarantined: usize,
    /// Items that ever entered quarantine (some may have recovered).
    pub quarantined_total: u64,
    /// Latency of the most recent successful write.
    pub last_write_micros: u64,
    /// Total time spent writing (successful writes).
    pub total_write_micros: u64,
    /// Slowest successful write.
    pub max_write_micros: u64,
}

/// Configuration for the persistence writer.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct PersistenceConfig {
    /// Bounded queue capacity.
    pub queue_capacity: usize,
    /// Maximum items coalesced into one write cycle.
    pub batch_max: usize,
}

impl Default for PersistenceConfig {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            queue_capacity: 4096,
            batch_max: 128,
        }
    }
}


#[derive(Default)]
struct StatsAtomics {
    enqueued: AtomicU64,
    written: AtomicU64,
    write_errors: AtomicU64,
    dropped_snapshots: AtomicU64,
    quarantined: AtomicUsize,
    quarantined_total: AtomicU64,
    last_write_micros: AtomicU64,
    total_write_micros: AtomicU64,
    max_write_micros: AtomicU64,
}

struct Shared {
    /// `None` once persistence is closed (sender dropped).
    tx: Mutex<Option<async_channel::Sender<PersistItem>>>,
    rx: async_channel::Receiver<PersistItem>,
    quarantine: Mutex<VecDeque<PersistItem>>,
    stats: StatsAtomics,
}

impl Shared {
    fn push_quarantine(&self, items: impl Iterator<Item = PersistItem>) {
        let mut q = self.quarantine.lock().unwrap();
        for item in items {
            tracing::error!(item = %item.describe(), "persistence: write failed after all retries; quarantined (retained, will be retried)");
            q.push_back(item);
            self.stats.quarantined.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
            self.stats.quarantined_total.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
        }
    }

    fn record_write(&self, items: usize, elapsed: Duration) {
        let micros = elapsed.as_micros() as u64;
        self.stats.written.fetch_add(items as u64, Ordering::Relaxed);
        self.stats.last_write_micros.store(micros, Ordering::Relaxed);
        self.stats
            .total_write_micros
            .fetch_add(micros, Ordering::Relaxed);
        self.stats.max_write_micros.fetch_max(micros, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }
}

/// Producer-side handle: enqueue items and read stats.
pub struct PersistenceHandle {
    shared: Arc<Shared>,
}

/// Read-only view of persistence stats, safe to hand to a GUI consumer.
/// Dropping it never affects persistence (GUI-close independence).
#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct StatsHandle {
    shared: Arc<Shared>,
}

impl StatsHandle {
    pub fn stats(&self) -> PersistStats {
        snapshot(&self.shared)
    }
}

fn snapshot(shared: &Shared) -> PersistStats {
    let s = &shared.stats;
    PersistStats {
        queue_depth: shared.rx.len(),
        enqueued: s.enqueued.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        written: s.written.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        write_errors: s.write_errors.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        dropped_snapshots: s.dropped_snapshots.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        quarantined: shared.quarantine.lock().unwrap().len(),
        quarantined_total: s.quarantined_total.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        last_write_micros: s.last_write_micros.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        total_write_micros: s.total_write_micros.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        max_write_micros: s.max_write_micros.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
    }
}

impl PersistenceHandle {
    /// Current persistence statistics (queue depth, write latency, errors).
    pub fn stats(&self) -> PersistStats {
        snapshot(&self.shared)
    }

    /// A read-only stats view for external consumers (e.g. `uets_gui`).
    /// Dropping the returned handle does not affect the writer.
    pub fn stats_handle(&self) -> StatsHandle {
        StatsHandle {
            shared: Arc::clone(&self.shared),
        }
    }

    /// Submit an item for asynchronous persistence.
    ///
    /// - Critical items: queued; if the queue is full, blocks until space
    ///   becomes available (backpressure) instead of dropping. Returns
    ///   [`PersistError::Closed`] only after persistence has shut down —
    ///   the caller then retains the item itself.
    /// - Portfolio snapshots: never block; dropped when full, but the drop
    ///   is counted and logged (never silent).
    pub fn submit(&self, item: PersistItem) -> Result<SubmitOutcome, PersistError> {
        let critical = item.is_critical();
        let tx = self
            .shared
            .tx
            .lock()
            .unwrap()
            .clone()
            .ok_or(PersistError::Closed(1))?;

        match tx.try_send(item) {
            Ok(()) => {
                self.shared.stats.enqueued.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                Ok(SubmitOutcome::Accepted)
            }
            Err(async_channel::TrySendError::Full(item)) => {
                if critical {
                    match tx.send_blocking(item) {
                        Ok(()) => {
                            self.shared.stats.enqueued.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                            Ok(SubmitOutcome::AcceptedBlocked)
                        }
                        Err(_) => Err(PersistError::Closed(1)),
                    }
                } else {
                    let dropped = self
                        .shared
                        .stats
                        .dropped_snapshots
                        .fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
                        + 1;
                    // Rate-limited logging: powers of two, never silent.
                    if dropped.is_power_of_two() {
                        tracing::warn!(
                            dropped_total = dropped,
                            "persistence queue full: portfolio snapshot dropped (counted in stats)"
                        );
                    }
                    Ok(SubmitOutcome::Dropped)
                }
            }
            Err(async_channel::TrySendError::Closed(_)) => Err(PersistError::Closed(1)),
        }
    }
}

/// Writer-thread handle.
pub struct PersistenceWorker {
    shared: Arc<Shared>,
    thread: Option<JoinHandle<()>>,
}

impl PersistenceWorker {
    /// Close the queue, drain it, flush quarantined items, and join the
    /// writer thread. Returns [`PersistError::Unwritten`] if any item could
    /// not be written (surfaced loudly — never silent).
    pub fn shutdown(mut self) -> Result<(), PersistError> {
        // Close the channel: drop the sender so the writer drains and exits.
        *self.shared.tx.lock().unwrap() = None;
        if let Some(thread) = self.thread.take() {
            let _ = thread.join();
        }
        let q = self.shared.quarantine.lock().unwrap();
        let total = q.len();
        let critical = q.iter().filter(|i| i.is_critical()).count();
        drop(q);
        if total > 0 {
            tracing::error!(
                critical,
                total,
                "persistence shutdown: items remain unwritten"
            );
            return Err(PersistError::Unwritten { critical, total });
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

/// Spawn the asynchronous persistence writer over `db`.
///
/// Returns a producer handle and a worker. The worker must eventually be
/// [`PersistenceWorker::shutdown`] to drain and flush everything.
pub fn spawn(db: Arc<UetsDb>, config: PersistenceConfig) -> (PersistenceHandle, PersistenceWorker) {
    let (tx, rx) = async_channel::bounded(config.queue_capacity.max(1));
    let shared = Arc::new(Shared {
        tx: Mutex::new(Some(tx)),
        rx,
        quarantine: Mutex::new(VecDeque::new()),
        stats: StatsAtomics::default(),
    });

    let thread_shared = Arc::clone(&shared);
    let thread = std::thread::Builder::new()
        .name("uets-persistence".into())
        .spawn(move || writer_loop(db, thread_shared, config.batch_max.max(1)))
        .expect("failed to spawn persistence writer thread");

    (
        PersistenceHandle {
            shared: Arc::clone(&shared),
        },
        PersistenceWorker {
            shared,
            thread: Some(thread),
        },
    )
}

/// Attempt to write a batch of items; returns how many were written.
fn write_items(db: &UetsDb, items: &[PersistItem], shared: &Shared) -> Result<usize, DbError> {
    let started = Instant::now();

    let mut trades_owned: Vec<TradeRecord> = Vec::new();
    let mut portfolios_owned: Vec<PortfolioRecord> = Vec::new();
    let mut summaries_owned: Vec<SessionSummary> = Vec::new();
    for item in items {
        match item {
            PersistItem::Trade(t) => trades_owned.push(t.clone()),
            PersistItem::Portfolio(p) => portfolios_owned.push(p.clone()),
            PersistItem::SessionSummary(s) => summaries_owned.push(s.clone()),
        }
    }

    let mut written = 0usize;
    match db.write_batch(&trades_owned, &portfolios_owned) {
        Ok(()) => written += trades_owned.len() + portfolios_owned.len(),
        Err(DbError::Serialization(msg)) => {
            // Isolate poison records so one bad record never blocks the rest.
            tracing::error!("batch serialization failed ({msg}); isolating records individually");
            for t in trades_owned {
                match db.write_trade(&t) {
                    Ok(()) => written += 1,
                    Err(DbError::Serialization(m)) => {
                        tracing::error!(record = %t.instance.composite(), "poison trade record ({m}) quarantined");
                        shared.push_quarantine(std::iter::once(PersistItem::Trade(t)));
                    }
                    Err(other) => return Err(other),
                }
            }
            for p in portfolios_owned {
                match db.write_portfolio(&p) {
                    Ok(()) => written += 1,
                    Err(DbError::Serialization(m)) => {
                        tracing::error!(record = %p.instance.composite(), "poison portfolio record ({m}) quarantined");
                        shared.push_quarantine(std::iter::once(PersistItem::Portfolio(p)));
                    }
                    Err(other) => return Err(other),
                }
            }
        }
        Err(other) => return Err(other),
    }

    for s in summaries_owned {
        db.finalize_session(&s)?;
        written += 1;
    }

    shared.record_write(written, started.elapsed());
    Ok(written)
}

fn writer_loop(db: Arc<UetsDb>, shared: Arc<Shared>, batch_max: usize) {
    let mut pending: VecDeque<PersistItem> = VecDeque::new();
    let mut attempts: u32 = 0;
    let mut channel_closed = false;

    loop {
        // Once shutdown closes the channel, stop retry loops and let the
        // bounded final flush below take over (shutdown must terminate).
        let closed = shared.tx.lock().unwrap().is_none();

        // Recover one quarantined item first (storage may have recovered).
        // After close, the final flush below owns quarantine.
        if pending.is_empty() && attempts == 0 && !closed {
            let recovered = shared.quarantine.lock().unwrap().pop_front();
            if let Some(item) = recovered {
                shared.stats.quarantined.fetch_sub(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                pending.push_back(item);
            }
        }

        // Fill the batch from the queue.
        while pending.len() < batch_max {
            if channel_closed {
                match shared.rx.try_recv() {
                    Ok(item) => pending.push_back(item),
                    Err(_) => break,
                }
            } else if pending.is_empty() {
                match shared.rx.recv_blocking() {
                    Ok(item) => pending.push_back(item),
                    Err(_) => {
                        channel_closed = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }
            } else {
                match shared.rx.try_recv() {
                    Ok(item) => pending.push_back(item),
                    Err(async_channel::TryRecvError::Closed) => {
                        channel_closed = true;
                        break;
                    }
                    Err(async_channel::TryRecvError::Empty) => break,
                }
            }
        }

        if pending.is_empty() && channel_closed {
            break; // Queue drained and closed.
        }
        if pending.is_empty() {
            continue;
        }

        let batch: Vec<PersistItem> = pending.drain(..).collect();
        match write_items(&db, &batch, &shared) {
            Ok(_n) => {
                attempts = 0;
            }
            Err(err) => {
                shared.stats.write_errors.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                attempts += 1;
                let closed_now = shared.tx.lock().unwrap().is_none();
                if attempts >= MAX_ATTEMPTS || closed_now {
                    tracing::error!(
                        error = %err,
                        attempts,
                        closed_now,
                        "persistence write failing; quarantining batch (retained for bounded final flush)"
                    );
                    shared.push_quarantine(batch.into_iter());
                    attempts = 0;
                } else {
                    std::thread::sleep(RETRY_DELAY);
                    // Retry the same batch next cycle (keep its head position).
                    let mut retried = VecDeque::new();
                    retried.extend(batch);
                    retried.append(&mut pending);
                    pending = retried;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Queue is closed and drained: final flush of quarantined items.
    loop {
        let next = shared.quarantine.lock().unwrap().pop_front();
        let Some(item) = next else { break };
        shared.stats.quarantined.fetch_sub(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
        let mut flushed = false;
        for _ in 0..MAX_ATTEMPTS {
            if write_items(&db, std::slice::from_ref(&item), &shared).is_ok() {
                flushed = true;
                break;
            }
            shared.stats.write_errors.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
            std::thread::sleep(RETRY_DELAY);
        }
        if !flushed {
            // Put it back so shutdown() reports it as unwritten (never silent).
            shared.stats.quarantined.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
            shared.quarantine.lock().unwrap().push_front(item);
            break;
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::schema::{
        PortfolioRecord, QueryFilter, SchemaVersion, StrategyInstance, TradeRecord,
    };
    use chrono::{TimeZone, Utc};
    use std::sync::atomic::AtomicBool;
    use uets_trades::types::TradeIntent;

    fn sample_trade(n: i64) -> TradeRecord {
        TradeRecord {
            schema: SchemaVersion::default(),
            session_id: "session_1".into(),
            instance: StrategyInstance::new("ofmr", "btcusdt"),
            symbol: "BTCUSDT".into(),
            event_time: Utc.with_ymd_and_hms(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0).unwrap()
                + chrono::Duration::nanoseconds(n),
            feature_inputs: serde_json::json!({"price": 100.0}).to_string(),
            decision: TradeIntent::Long,
            decision_metadata: serde_json::json!({}).to_string(),
            intent: TradeIntent::Long,
            order_events: Vec::new(),
            entry_price: Some(100.0),
            exit_price: None,
            tp_price: Some(100.5),
            sl_price: Some(99.5),
            vertical_time: None,
            fill_qty: 1.0,
            fees: 0.1,
            realized_pnl: 0.0,
            model_version: "v1.0".into(),
            config_id: "cfg_1".into(),
        }
    }

    fn sample_portfolio() -> PortfolioRecord {
        PortfolioRecord {
            schema: SchemaVersion::default(),
            session_id: "session_1".into(),
            instance: StrategyInstance::new("ofmr", "btcusdt"),
            asset_id: "btcusdt".into(),
            timestamp: Utc.with_ymd_and_hms(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0).unwrap(),
            allocated_capital: 10_000.0,
            cash: 10_000.0,
            equity: 10_000.0,
            realized_pnl: 0.0,
            unrealized_pnl: 0.0,
            cumulative_return: 0.0,
            sortino: 0.0,
            drawdown: 0.0,
            max_drawdown: 0.0,
            gross_exposure: 0.0,
            net_exposure: 0.0,
            margin_used: 0.0,
            available_margin: 10_000.0,
            effective_leverage: 0.0,
            fees_paid: 0.0,
            trade_count: 0,
            win_count: 0,
            loss_count: 0,
            win_rate: 0.0,
        }
    }

    fn temp_db_path() -> std::path::PathBuf {
        std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("uets_db_persist_{}", uuid::Uuid::new_v4()))
    }

    #[test]
    fn saturation_never_drops_critical_items() {
        let path = temp_db_path();
        let db = Arc::new(UetsDb::open(&path).unwrap());
        let (handle, worker) = spawn(
            Arc::clone(&db),
            PersistenceConfig {
                queue_capacity: 8,
                batch_max: 4,
            },
        );

        for i in 0..100i64 {
            let outcome = handle
                .submit(PersistItem::Trade(sample_trade(i)))
                .unwrap();
            assert!(
                matches!(
                    outcome,
                    SubmitOutcome::Accepted | SubmitOutcome::AcceptedBlocked
                ),
                "critical trade must always be queued (backpressure if full)"
            );
            // Snapshots may be accepted or dropped; drops must be counted.
            let snap = handle.submit(PersistItem::Portfolio(sample_portfolio()));
            assert!(snap.is_ok());
        }

        worker.shutdown().unwrap();

        let records = db
            .query_trades(&QueryFilter::new().session("session_1"))
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(records.len(), 100, "every critical record must persist");
        let stats = handle.stats();
        assert_eq!(stats.queue_depth, 0);
        assert!(stats.written >= 100);
        assert_eq!(stats.write_errors, 0);

        drop(db);
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn full_queue_applies_backpressure_and_counts_drops() {
        let (tx, rx) = async_channel::bounded(2);
        let shared = Arc::new(Shared {
            tx: Mutex::new(Some(tx)),
            rx: rx.clone(),
            quarantine: Mutex::new(VecDeque::new()),
            stats: StatsAtomics::default(),
        });
        let handle_a = PersistenceHandle {
            shared: Arc::clone(&shared),
        };
        let handle_b = PersistenceHandle {
            shared: Arc::clone(&shared),
        };

        // Fill the bounded queue with non-critical snapshots.
        assert_eq!(
            handle_a
                .submit(PersistItem::Portfolio(sample_portfolio()))
                .unwrap(),
            SubmitOutcome::Accepted
        );
        assert_eq!(
            handle_a
                .submit(PersistItem::Portfolio(sample_portfolio()))
                .unwrap(),
            SubmitOutcome::Accepted
        );
        // Third snapshot: dropped, but counted — never silent.
        assert_eq!(
            handle_a
                .submit(PersistItem::Portfolio(sample_portfolio()))
                .unwrap(),
            SubmitOutcome::Dropped
        );
        assert_eq!(handle_a.stats().dropped_snapshots, 1);

        // Critical item on a full queue blocks (backpressure), not dropped.
        let done = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));
        let flag = Arc::clone(&done);
        let thread = std::thread::spawn(move || {
            let outcome = handle_b
                .submit(PersistItem::Trade(sample_trade(0)))
                .unwrap();
            assert_eq!(outcome, SubmitOutcome::AcceptedBlocked);
            flag.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);
        });

        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100));
        assert!(
            !done.load(Ordering::SeqCst),
            "critical submit must block while the queue is full"
        );

        // Draining frees space; the blocked submit completes with its item intact.
        let first = rx.recv_blocking().unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(first, PersistItem::Portfolio(_)));
        thread.join().unwrap();
        assert!(done.load(Ordering::SeqCst));
        let _second = rx.recv_blocking().unwrap();
        let third = rx.recv_blocking().unwrap();
        assert!(
            matches!(third, PersistItem::Trade(_)),
            "critical item must arrive after backpressure releases"
        );
        // Enqueued counts accepted + blocked, not dropped.
        assert_eq!(handle_a.stats().enqueued, 3);
    }

    #[test]
    fn storage_failure_quarantines_but_never_drops_critical() {
        let path = temp_db_path();
        let db = Arc::new(UetsDb::open(&path).unwrap());
        let (handle, worker) = spawn(Arc::clone(&db), PersistenceConfig::default());

        db.inject_write_failure(true);
        handle.submit(PersistItem::Trade(sample_trade(0))).unwrap();

        // Wait for the retry cycle to exhaust attempts and quarantine (≈1 s).
        let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(5);
        while handle.stats().quarantined_total == 0 && Instant::now() < deadline {
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20));
        }
        let stats = handle.stats();
        assert!(stats.write_errors > 0, "storage errors must be surfaced");
        assert!(
            stats.quarantined_total >= 1,
            "critical item must be quarantined, not lost"
        );

        // Storage recovers; a wakeup item triggers reprocessing.
        db.inject_write_failure(false);
        handle
            .submit(PersistItem::Portfolio(sample_portfolio()))
            .unwrap();

        let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(5);
        loop {
            let records = db
                .query_trades(&QueryFilter::new().session("session_1"))
                .unwrap();
            if records.len() == 1 {
                break;
            }
            assert!(
                Instant::now() < deadline,
                "quarantined critical item must be rewritten after recovery"
            );
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20));
        }

        worker.shutdown().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(handle.stats().quarantined, 0);

        drop(db);
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn shutdown_reports_unwritten_critical_items() {
        let path = temp_db_path();
        let db = Arc::new(UetsDb::open(&path).unwrap());
        let (handle, worker) = spawn(Arc::clone(&db), PersistenceConfig::default());

        db.inject_write_failure(true);
        handle.submit(PersistItem::Trade(sample_trade(0))).unwrap();

        let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(5);
        while handle.stats().quarantined_total == 0 && Instant::now() < deadline {
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20));
        }
        assert!(handle.stats().quarantined_total >= 1);

        // Storage stays down: shutdown must report the loss loudly, not swallow it.
        let result = worker.shutdown();
        assert!(
            matches!(result, Err(PersistError::Unwritten { critical, .. }) if critical >= 1),
            "shutdown must report unwritten critical items, got {result:?}"
        );
        assert!(handle.stats().quarantined >= 1);

        drop(db);
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn dropping_stats_consumer_does_not_stop_persistence() {
        let path = temp_db_path();
        let db = Arc::new(UetsDb::open(&path).unwrap());
        let (handle, worker) = spawn(Arc::clone(&db), PersistenceConfig::default());

        // A GUI-like consumer subscribes, then closes.
        {
            let stats_view = handle.stats_handle();
            let s = stats_view.stats();
            assert_eq!(s.written, 0);
        } // stats_view dropped — GUI closed

        handle.submit(PersistItem::Trade(sample_trade(0))).unwrap();
        worker.shutdown().unwrap();

        let records = db
            .query_trades(&QueryFilter::new().session("session_1"))
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            records.len(),
            1,
            "persistence must continue after the GUI consumer closes"
        );

        drop(db);
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&path);
    }
}










===== FILE: components/pipelines/uets_db/src/schema.rs =====
//! Record schemas and serialization for the uets_db store.
//!
//! Uses compact, versioned binary serialization (bincode). JSON is avoided
//! for high-volume records.

use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uets_trades::types::{OrderStatus, TradeIntent};

/// Schema version — increment when the record format changes.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct SchemaVersion {
    pub major: u16,
    pub minor: u16,
}

impl Default for SchemaVersion {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self { major: 1, minor: 0 }
    }
}

/// Strategy-instance identity: `strategy_id@asset_id`, e.g. `ofmr@btcusdt`.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct StrategyInstance {
    pub strategy_id: String,
    pub asset_id: String,
}

impl StrategyInstance {
    pub fn new(strategy_id: impl Into<String>, asset_id: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            strategy_id: strategy_id.into(),
            asset_id: asset_id.into(),
        }
    }

    /// Composite key: `ofmr@btcusdt`.
    pub fn composite(&self) -> String {
        format!("{}@{}", self.strategy_id, self.asset_id)
    }
}

/// A trade record — what the strategy observed, decided, and executed.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct TradeRecord {
    /// Schema version for forward compatibility.
    pub schema: SchemaVersion,
    /// Stable session ID.
    pub session_id: String,
    /// Strategy instance identity.
    pub instance: StrategyInstance,
    /// Asset/ticker (stored separately from strategy identity).
    pub symbol: String,
    /// Event timestamp.
    pub event_time: DateTime<Utc>,
    /// The feature values and market inputs consumed by compute_features.
    ///
    /// Stored as JSON text inside the binary envelope: bincode serializes
    /// the record structure compactly, while `serde_json::Value` cannot
    /// round-trip through bincode (it requires `deserialize_any`). Use
    /// [`TradeRecord::set_feature_inputs`] / [`TradeRecord::feature_inputs_json`].
    pub feature_inputs: String,
    /// Strategy decision and metadata (JSON text; see [`TradeRecord::feature_inputs`]).
    pub decision: TradeIntent,
    pub decision_metadata: String,
    /// Trade intent (what was requested).
    pub intent: TradeIntent,
    /// Order lifecycle events.
    pub order_events: Vec<OrderEvent>,
    /// Entry and exit prices.
    pub entry_price: Option<f64>,
    pub exit_price: Option<f64>,
    /// Triple-barrier information (OFMR).
    pub tp_price: Option<f64>,
    pub sl_price: Option<f64>,
    pub vertical_time: Option<DateTime<Utc>>,
    /// Fill quantity, fees, realized PnL.
    pub fill_qty: f64,
    pub fees: f64,
    pub realized_pnl: f64,
    /// Strategy/model version and configuration identifiers.
    pub model_version: String,
    pub config_id: String,
}

impl TradeRecord {
    /// Replace the feature payload (encoded to JSON text).
    pub fn set_feature_inputs(&mut self, value: &serde_json::Value) {
        self.feature_inputs = value.to_string();
    }

    /// Decode the feature payload. Errors are returned, never swallowed.
    pub fn feature_inputs_json(&self) -> serde_json::Result<serde_json::Value> {
        serde_json::from_str(&self.feature_inputs)
    }

    /// Replace the decision metadata payload (encoded to JSON text).
    pub fn set_decision_metadata(&mut self, value: &serde_json::Value) {
        self.decision_metadata = value.to_string();
    }

    /// Decode the decision metadata payload. Errors are returned, never swallowed.
    pub fn decision_metadata_json(&self) -> serde_json::Result<serde_json::Value> {
        serde_json::from_str(&self.decision_metadata)
    }
}

/// One order lifecycle event within a trade record.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct OrderEvent {
    pub order_id: String,
    pub status: OrderStatus,
    pub filled_qty: f64,
    pub fill_price: Option<f64>,
    pub fee: f64,
    pub timestamp: DateTime<Utc>,
}

/// A portfolio snapshot — calculated reporting metrics persisted at cadence.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct PortfolioRecord {
    pub schema: SchemaVersion,
    pub session_id: String,
    /// Strategy instance identity.
    pub instance: StrategyInstance,
    /// Asset ID.
    pub asset_id: String,
    /// Snapshot timestamp.
    pub timestamp: DateTime<Utc>,
    pub allocated_capital: f64,
    pub cash: f64,
    pub equity: f64,
    pub realized_pnl: f64,
    pub unrealized_pnl: f64,
    pub cumulative_return: f64,
    pub sortino: f64,
    pub drawdown: f64,
    pub max_drawdown: f64,
    pub gross_exposure: f64,
    pub net_exposure: f64,
    pub margin_used: f64,
    pub available_margin: f64,
    pub effective_leverage: f64,
    pub fees_paid: f64,
    pub trade_count: usize,
    pub win_count: usize,
    pub loss_count: usize,
    pub win_rate: f64,
}

/// Session metadata — enables independent querying, reporting, archival
/// and recovery per session without separate physical database instances.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct SessionMeta {
    pub id: String,
    pub started_at: DateTime<Utc>,
    /// Set when the session is finalized.
    pub ended_at: Option<DateTime<Utc>>,
    pub active: bool,
}

/// Final summary written when a session ends.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct SessionSummary {
    pub schema: SchemaVersion,
    pub session_id: String,
    pub finalized_at: DateTime<Utc>,
    pub trade_count: usize,
    pub portfolio_snapshots: usize,
    pub final_equity: f64,
    pub cumulative_return: f64,
    pub sortino: f64,
    pub max_drawdown: f64,
}

/// Integrity check result.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default)]
pub struct IntegrityReport {
    /// Number of decodable trade records.
    pub trade_records: usize,
    /// Number of decodable portfolio records.
    pub portfolio_records: usize,
    /// Keys that failed to decode (corruption detected, not silently discarded).
    pub corrupt_keys: Vec<String>,
    /// Number of active sessions.
    pub active_sessions: usize,
}

impl IntegrityReport {
    pub fn is_clean(&self) -> bool {
        self.corrupt_keys.is_empty()
    }
}

impl PortfolioRecord {
    /// Build a portfolio record from a shared `calc` performance snapshot —
    /// the GUI/database never recompute metrics, they only store what `calc`
    /// produced.
    pub fn from_snapshot(
        session_id: impl Into<String>,
        snapshot: &uets_trades::calc::metrics::PerformanceSnapshot,
    ) -> Self {
        let (strategy_id, asset_id) = match snapshot.strategy_id.split_once('@') {
            Some((s, a)) => (s.to_string(), a.to_string()),
            None => (snapshot.strategy_id.clone(), snapshot.asset_id.clone()),
        };
        let starting = snapshot.starting_capital;
        let unrealized = snapshot.unrealized_pnl;
        let (win_count, loss_count) = (snapshot.wins, snapshot.losses.max(0.0) as usize);
        Self {
            schema: SchemaVersion::default(),
            session_id: session_id.into(),
            instance: StrategyInstance::new(strategy_id, asset_id.clone()),
            asset_id,
            timestamp: snapshot.timestamp,
            allocated_capital: starting,
            cash: snapshot.cash,
            equity: snapshot.equity,
            realized_pnl: snapshot.realized_pnl,
            unrealized_pnl: unrealized,
            cumulative_return: snapshot.cumulative_return,
            sortino: snapshot.sortino,
            drawdown: snapshot.current_drawdown,
            max_drawdown: snapshot.max_drawdown,
            gross_exposure: snapshot.gross_exposure,
            net_exposure: snapshot.net_exposure,
            margin_used: snapshot.margin_used,
            available_margin: snapshot.available_margin,
            effective_leverage: snapshot.effective_leverage,
            fees_paid: snapshot.fees_paid,
            trade_count: snapshot.trades,
            win_count,
            loss_count,
            win_rate: snapshot.win_rate,
        }
    }
}

/// Query filter for retrieving records.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct QueryFilter {
    pub session_id: Option<String>,
    pub strategy_id: Option<String>,
    pub asset_id: Option<String>,
    pub start_time: Option<DateTime<Utc>>,
    pub end_time: Option<DateTime<Utc>>,
}

impl QueryFilter {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            session_id: None,
            strategy_id: None,
            asset_id: None,
            start_time: None,
            end_time: None,
        }
    }

    pub fn session(mut self, id: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        self.session_id = Some(id.into());
        self
    }

    pub fn strategy(mut self, id: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        self.strategy_id = Some(id.into());
        self
    }

    pub fn asset(mut self, id: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        self.asset_id = Some(id.into());
        self
    }

    pub fn time_range(mut self, start: DateTime<Utc>, end: DateTime<Utc>) -> Self {
        self.start_time = Some(start);
        self.end_time = Some(end);
        self
    }
}

impl Default for QueryFilter {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn instance_composite_key() {
        let inst = StrategyInstance::new("ofmr", "btcusdt");
        assert_eq!(inst.composite(), "ofmr@btcusdt");
    }
}

===== FILE: components/pipelines/uets_db/src/store.rs =====
//! Database store — wraps the embedded key-value store with session-aware
//! keys, indexes, batched writes, and async persistence.

use crate::schema::{
    IntegrityReport, PortfolioRecord, QueryFilter, SessionMeta, SessionSummary, TradeRecord,
};
use chrono::Utc;
use sled::{Db, Tree};
use std::path::Path;
use thiserror::Error;

use crate::schema::SchemaVersion;

/// Database errors.
#[derive(Debug, Error)]
pub enum DbError {
    #[error("sled error: {0}")]
    Sled(#[from] sled::Error),
    #[error("serialization error: {0}")]
    Serialization(String),
    #[error("record not found: {key}")]
    NotFound { key: String },
    #[error("corrupt record: {key}")]
    Corrupt { key: String },
    #[error("incompatible schema version: found {found}, supported {supported}")]
    IncompatibleSchema { found: String, supported: String },
    #[error("session already exists: {id}")]
    SessionExists { id: String },
    #[error("session not found: {id}")]
    SessionNotFound { id: String },
    /// Test-only injected failure (never produced outside tests).
    #[cfg(test)]
    #[error("injected write failure")]
    Injected,
}

/// The database store. Wraps a single sled instance with multiple logical
/// trees (trades, portfolio, metadata).
pub struct UetsDb {
    db: Db,
    trades: Tree,
    portfolio: Tree,
    metadata: Tree,
    /// Test-only: simulate storage failures for failure-handling tests.
    #[cfg(test)]
    fail_writes: std::sync::atomic::AtomicBool,
}

impl UetsDb {
    /// Open or create the database at `path`.
    pub fn open(path: impl AsRef<Path>) -> Result<Self, DbError> {
        let db = sled::open(path)?;
        let trades = db.open_tree(b"trades")?;
        let portfolio = db.open_tree(b"portfolio")?;
        let metadata = db.open_tree(b"metadata")?;

        // Write schema version if not present; validate it if present.
        if let Some(raw) = metadata.get(b"schema_version")? {
            let stored: SchemaVersion = bincode::deserialize(raw.as_ref())
                .map_err(|e| DbError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
            let current = SchemaVersion::default();
            if stored.major != current.major {
                return Err(DbError::IncompatibleSchema {
                    found: format!("{}.{}", stored.major, stored.minor),
                    supported: format!("{}.{}", current.major, current.minor),
                });
            }
        } else {
            let version = SchemaVersion::default();
            let encoded = bincode::serialize(&version)
                .map_err(|e| DbError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
            metadata.insert(b"schema_version", encoded)?;
        }

        Ok(Self {
            db,
            trades,
            portfolio,
            metadata,
            #[cfg(test)]
            fail_writes: std::sync::atomic::AtomicBool::new(false),
        })
    }

    /// Flush all writes to disk.
    pub fn flush(&self) -> Result<(), DbError> {
        self.db.flush()?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Shut down the database cleanly.
    pub fn shutdown(self) -> Result<(), DbError> {
        // sled handles cleanup on drop, but we can explicitly flush.
        self.db.flush()?;
        Ok(())
    }

    // ------- Trades -------

    /// Insert a trade record (record + index atomically, same tree).
    pub fn write_trade(&self, record: &TradeRecord) -> Result<(), DbError> {
        self.ensure_writable()?;
        let key = Self::trade_key(record);
        let encoded = bincode::serialize(record)
            .map_err(|e| DbError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
        let ikey = Self::trade_index_key(record);

        let mut batch = sled::Batch::default();
        batch.insert(key.clone(), encoded);
        batch.insert(ikey, key);
        self.trades.apply_batch(batch)?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Query trade records matching the filter.
    ///
    /// Uses the session/instance index prefix when a session is specified;
    /// falls back to a full scan otherwise.
    pub fn query_trades(&self, filter: &QueryFilter) -> Result<Vec<TradeRecord>, DbError> {
        let mut results = Vec::new();

        if let Some(prefix) = Self::trade_index_prefix(filter) {
            let prefix_bytes = prefix.into_bytes();
            for result in self.trades.range(prefix_bytes.clone()..) {
                let (key, value) = result?;
                if !key.starts_with(&prefix_bytes) {
                    break;
                }
                // Index value holds the primary record key.
                if let Some(raw) = self.trades.get(&value)? {
                    let record: TradeRecord = bincode::deserialize(raw.as_ref())
                        .map_err(|e| DbError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
                    if Self::matches_trade_filter(&record, filter) {
                        results.push(record);
                    }
                }
            }
        } else {
            for result in self.trades.iter() {
                let (key, value) = result?;
                if Self::is_index_key(&key) {
                    continue;
                }
                let record: TradeRecord = bincode::deserialize(value.as_ref())
                    .map_err(|e| DbError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
                if Self::matches_trade_filter(&record, filter) {
                    results.push(record);
                }
            }
        }

        Ok(results)
    }

    // ------- Portfolio -------

    /// Insert a portfolio record (record + index atomically, same tree).
    pub fn write_portfolio(&self, record: &PortfolioRecord) -> Result<(), DbError> {
        self.ensure_writable()?;
        let key = Self::portfolio_key(record);
        let encoded = bincode::serialize(record)
            .map_err(|e| DbError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
        let ikey = Self::portfolio_index_key(record);

        let mut batch = sled::Batch::default();
        batch.insert(key.clone(), encoded);
        batch.insert(ikey, key);
        self.portfolio.apply_batch(batch)?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Query portfolio records matching the filter (indexed when possible).
    pub fn query_portfolio(&self, filter: &QueryFilter) -> Result<Vec<PortfolioRecord>, DbError> {
        let mut results = Vec::new();

        if let Some(prefix) = Self::portfolio_index_prefix(filter) {
            let prefix_bytes = prefix.into_bytes();
            for result in self.portfolio.range(prefix_bytes.clone()..) {
                let (key, value) = result?;
                if !key.starts_with(&prefix_bytes) {
                    break;
                }
                if let Some(raw) = self.portfolio.get(&value)? {
                    let record: PortfolioRecord = bincode::deserialize(raw.as_ref())
                        .map_err(|e| DbError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
                    if Self::matches_portfolio_filter(&record, filter) {
                        results.push(record);
                    }
                }
            }
        } else {
            for result in self.portfolio.iter() {
                let (key, value) = result?;
                if Self::is_index_key(&key) {
                    continue;
                }
                let record: PortfolioRecord = bincode::deserialize(value.as_ref())
                    .map_err(|e| DbError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
                if Self::matches_portfolio_filter(&record, filter) {
                    results.push(record);
                }
            }
        }

        Ok(results)
    }

    // ------- Batched writes -------

    /// Atomically write multiple trade and portfolio records in one batch.
    ///
    /// Records and their index entries are applied atomically per domain
    /// (trades and portfolio are separate logical trees).
    pub fn write_batch(
        &self,
        trades: &[TradeRecord],
        portfolios: &[PortfolioRecord],
    ) -> Result<(), DbError> {
        self.ensure_writable()?;
        if trades.is_empty() && portfolios.is_empty() {
            return Ok(());
        }

        if !trades.is_empty() {
            let mut batch = sled::Batch::default();
            for record in trades {
                let key = Self::trade_key(record);
                let encoded = bincode::serialize(record)
                    .map_err(|e| DbError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
                let ikey = Self::trade_index_key(record);
                batch.insert(key.clone(), encoded);
                batch.insert(ikey, key);
            }
            self.trades.apply_batch(batch)?;
        }

        if !portfolios.is_empty() {
            let mut batch = sled::Batch::default();
            for record in portfolios {
                let key = Self::portfolio_key(record);
                let encoded = bincode::serialize(record)
                    .map_err(|e| DbError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
                let ikey = Self::portfolio_index_key(record);
                batch.insert(key.clone(), encoded);
                batch.insert(ikey, key);
            }
            self.portfolio.apply_batch(batch)?;
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    // ------- Sessions -------

    fn session_key(id: &str) -> Vec<u8> {
        format!("session|{}", id).into_bytes()
    }

    fn summary_key(id: &str) -> Vec<u8> {
        format!("summary|{}", id).into_bytes()
    }

    /// Create a new session. Fails if the id already exists.
    pub fn create_session(&self, id: impl Into<String>) -> Result<SessionMeta, DbError> {
        let id = id.into();
        let key = Self::session_key(&id);
        if self.metadata.get(&key)?.is_some() {
            return Err(DbError::SessionExists { id });
        }
        let meta = SessionMeta {
            id,
            started_at: Utc::now(),
            ended_at: None,
            active: true,
        };
        let encoded = bincode::serialize(&meta)
            .map_err(|e| DbError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
        self.metadata.insert(key, encoded)?;
        Ok(meta)
    }

    /// Look up one session by id.
    pub fn get_session(&self, id: &str) -> Result<Option<SessionMeta>, DbError> {
        match self.metadata.get(Self::session_key(id))? {
            Some(raw) => {
                let meta: SessionMeta = bincode::deserialize(raw.as_ref())
                    .map_err(|e| DbError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
                Ok(Some(meta))
            }
            None => Ok(None),
        }
    }

    /// All sessions, ordered by id.
    pub fn list_sessions(&self) -> Result<Vec<SessionMeta>, DbError> {
        let prefix = b"session|";
        let mut sessions = Vec::new();
        for result in self.metadata.range(prefix.as_slice()..) {
            let (key, value) = result?;
            if !key.starts_with(prefix) {
                break;
            }
            let meta: SessionMeta = bincode::deserialize(value.as_ref())
                .map_err(|e| DbError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
            sessions.push(meta);
        }
        Ok(sessions)
    }

    /// Finalize a session: mark it inactive and persist the session summary.
    /// Metadata + summary are written atomically (metadata tree batch).
    pub fn finalize_session(&self, summary: &SessionSummary) -> Result<SessionMeta, DbError> {
        self.ensure_writable()?;
        let id = &summary.session_id;
        let raw = self
            .metadata
            .get(Self::session_key(id))?
            .ok_or_else(|| DbError::SessionNotFound { id: id.clone() })?;
        let mut meta: SessionMeta = bincode::deserialize(raw.as_ref())
            .map_err(|e| DbError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
        meta.active = false;
        meta.ended_at = Some(summary.finalized_at);

        let mut batch = sled::Batch::default();
        let meta_encoded = bincode::serialize(&meta)
            .map_err(|e| DbError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
        let sum_encoded = bincode::serialize(summary)
            .map_err(|e| DbError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
        batch.insert(Self::session_key(id), meta_encoded);
        batch.insert(Self::summary_key(id), sum_encoded);
        self.metadata.apply_batch(batch)?;
        Ok(meta)
    }

    /// The final summary of a session, if it was finalized.
    pub fn session_summary(&self, id: &str) -> Result<Option<SessionSummary>, DbError> {
        match self.metadata.get(Self::summary_key(id))? {
            Some(raw) => {
                let summary: SessionSummary = bincode::deserialize(raw.as_ref())
                    .map_err(|e| DbError::Serialization(e.to_string()))?;
                Ok(Some(summary))
            }
            None => Ok(None),
        }
    }

    // ------- Integrity -------

    /// Scan all records for decodability. Corruption is reported explicitly,
    /// never hidden: `IntegrityReport.corrupt_keys` lists every bad key.
    pub fn integrity_check(&self) -> Result<IntegrityReport, DbError> {
        let mut report = IntegrityReport::default();

        for result in self.trades.iter() {
            let (key, value) = result?;
            if Self::is_index_key(&key) {
                continue;
            }
            match bincode::deserialize::<TradeRecord>(value.as_ref()) {
                Ok(_) => report.trade_records += 1,
                Err(_) => report.corrupt_keys.push(format!(
                    "trades:{}",
                    String::from_utf8_lossy(&key)
                )),
            }
        }

        for result in self.portfolio.iter() {
            let (key, value) = result?;
            if Self::is_index_key(&key) {
                continue;
            }
            match bincode::deserialize::<PortfolioRecord>(value.as_ref()) {
                Ok(_) => report.portfolio_records += 1,
                Err(_) => report.corrupt_keys.push(format!(
                    "portfolio:{}",
                    String::from_utf8_lossy(&key)
                )),
            }
        }

        report.active_sessions = self
            .list_sessions()?
            .iter()
            .filter(|s| s.active)
            .count();
        Ok(report)
    }

    // ------- Private helpers -------

    /// Test-only: toggle injected storage failures (all record writes fail).
    #[cfg(test)]
    pub fn inject_write_failure(&self, on: bool) {
        self.fail_writes
            .store(on, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    fn ensure_writable(&self) -> Result<(), DbError> {
        #[cfg(test)]
        if self
            .fail_writes
            .load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed)
        {
            return Err(DbError::Injected);
        }
        #[cfg(not(test))]
        {
            Ok(())
        }
        #[cfg(test)]
        {
            Ok(())
        }
    }

    fn trade_key(record: &TradeRecord) -> Vec<u8> {
        let ts = record.event_time.timestamp_nanos_opt().unwrap_or(0);
        format!(
            "trades|{}|{}|{}|{:020}",
            record.session_id,
            record.instance.composite(),
            record.symbol,
            ts
        )
        .into_bytes()
    }

    fn portfolio_key(record: &PortfolioRecord) -> Vec<u8> {
        let ts = record.timestamp.timestamp_nanos_opt().unwrap_or(0);
        format!(
            "portfolio|{}|{}|{}|{:020}",
            record.session_id,
            record.instance.composite(),
            record.asset_id,
            ts
        )
        .into_bytes()
    }

    /// In-tree index key for a trade record: `ix|{session}|{composite}|{ts}`.
    /// Value = primary record key. Lives in the same tree so record + index
    /// writes are atomic.
    fn trade_index_key(record: &TradeRecord) -> Vec<u8> {
        let ts = record.event_time.timestamp_nanos_opt().unwrap_or(0);
        format!(
            "ix|{}|{}|{:020}",
            record.session_id,
            record.instance.composite(),
            ts
        )
        .into_bytes()
    }

    fn portfolio_index_key(record: &PortfolioRecord) -> Vec<u8> {
        let ts = record.timestamp.timestamp_nanos_opt().unwrap_or(0);
        format!(
            "ix|{}|{}|{:020}",
            record.session_id,
            record.instance.composite(),
            ts
        )
        .into_bytes()
    }

    fn is_index_key(key: &[u8]) -> bool {
        key.starts_with(b"ix|")
    }

    /// Index prefix for narrowing trade candidates from a filter.
    fn trade_index_prefix(filter: &QueryFilter) -> Option<String> {
        match (
            filter.session_id.as_ref(),
            filter.strategy_id.as_ref(),
            filter.asset_id.as_ref(),
        ) {
            (Some(s), Some(st), Some(a)) => Some(format!("ix|{}|{}@{}|", s, st, a)),
            (Some(s), Some(st), None) => Some(format!("ix|{}|{}@", s, st)),
            (Some(s), None, _) => Some(format!("ix|{}|", s)),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    fn portfolio_index_prefix(filter: &QueryFilter) -> Option<String> {
        Self::trade_index_prefix(filter)
    }

    fn matches_trade_filter(record: &TradeRecord, filter: &QueryFilter) -> bool {
        if let Some(ref sid) = filter.session_id {
            if record.session_id != *sid {
                return false;
            }
        }
        if let Some(ref strat) = filter.strategy_id {
            if record.instance.strategy_id != *strat {
                return false;
            }
        }
        if let Some(ref asset) = filter.asset_id {
            if record.instance.asset_id != *asset && record.symbol != *asset {
                return false;
            }
        }
        if let Some(start) = filter.start_time {
            if record.event_time < start {
                return false;
            }
        }
        if let Some(end) = filter.end_time {
            if record.event_time > end {
                return false;
            }
        }
        true
    }

    fn matches_portfolio_filter(record: &PortfolioRecord, filter: &QueryFilter) -> bool {
        if let Some(ref sid) = filter.session_id {
            if record.session_id != *sid {
                return false;
            }
        }
        if let Some(ref strat) = filter.strategy_id {
            if record.instance.strategy_id != *strat {
                return false;
            }
        }
        if let Some(ref asset) = filter.asset_id {
            if record.asset_id != *asset {
                return false;
            }
        }
        if let Some(start) = filter.start_time {
            if record.timestamp < start {
                return false;
            }
        }
        if let Some(end) = filter.end_time {
            if record.timestamp > end {
                return false;
            }
        }
        true
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::schema::{PortfolioRecord, SchemaVersion, SessionSummary, StrategyInstance};
    use uets_trades::types::TradeIntent;
    use chrono::{TimeZone, Utc};
    use std::env;
    use std::fs;

    fn temp_db_path() -> std::path::PathBuf {
        // Unique per call so parallel tests never share (or delete) each other's DB.
        env::temp_dir().join(format!("uets_db_test_{}_{:x}", std::process::id(), uuid::Uuid::new_v4().as_u128()))
    }

    fn sample_trade() -> TradeRecord {
        TradeRecord {
            schema: SchemaVersion::default(),
            session_id: "session_1".to_string(),
            instance: StrategyInstance::new("ofmr", "btcusdt"),
            symbol: "BTCUSDT".to_string(),
            event_time: Utc.with_ymd_and_hms(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0).unwrap(),
            feature_inputs: serde_json::json!({"price": 100.0}).to_string(),
            decision: TradeIntent::Long,
            decision_metadata: serde_json::json!({}).to_string(),
            intent: TradeIntent::Long,
            order_events: Vec::new(),
            entry_price: Some(100.0),
            exit_price: None,
            tp_price: Some(100.5),
            sl_price: Some(99.5),
            vertical_time: Some(
                Utc.with_ymd_and_hms(2026, 1, 1, 0, 5, 0).unwrap(),
            ),
            fill_qty: 1.0,
            fees: 0.1,
            realized_pnl: 0.0,
            model_version: "v1.0".to_string(),
            config_id: "config_1".to_string(),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn open_and_write_trade() {
        let path = temp_db_path();
        let db = UetsDb::open(&path).unwrap();
        let trade = sample_trade();
        db.write_trade(&trade).unwrap();
        db.flush().unwrap();

        let filter = QueryFilter::new().session("session_1");
        let results = db.query_trades(&filter).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(results.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(results[0].symbol, "BTCUSDT");
        assert_eq!(results[0].entry_price, Some(100.0));

        let _ = db.shutdown();
        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn write_and_query_portfolio() {
        let path = temp_db_path();
        let db = UetsDb::open(&path).unwrap();
        let record = PortfolioRecord {
            schema: SchemaVersion::default(),
            session_id: "session_1".to_string(),
            instance: StrategyInstance::new("ofmr", "btcusdt"),
            asset_id: "btcusdt".to_string(),
            timestamp: Utc.with_ymd_and_hms(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0).unwrap(),
            allocated_capital: 10_000.0,
            cash: 10_000.0,
            equity: 10_500.0,
            realized_pnl: 0.0,
            unrealized_pnl: 500.0,
            cumulative_return: 0.05,
            sortino: 1.5,
            drawdown: 0.02,
            max_drawdown: 0.02,
            gross_exposure: 0.0,
            net_exposure: 0.0,
            margin_used: 0.0,
            available_margin: 10_500.0,
            effective_leverage: 0.0,
            fees_paid: 0.0,
            trade_count: 0,
            win_count: 0,
            loss_count: 0,
            win_rate: 0.0,
        };
        db.write_portfolio(&record).unwrap();
        db.flush().unwrap();

        let filter = QueryFilter::new().session("session_1");
        let results = db.query_portfolio(&filter).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(results.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(results[0].equity, 10_500.0);

        let _ = db.shutdown();
        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn batch_write() {
        let path = temp_db_path();
        let db = UetsDb::open(&path).unwrap();
        let trade = sample_trade();
        db.write_batch(&[trade], &[]).unwrap();
        db.flush().unwrap();

        let filter = QueryFilter::new().session("session_1");
        let results = db.query_trades(&filter).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(results.len(), 1);

        let _ = db.shutdown();
        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn session_lifecycle_create_finalize_query() {
        let path = temp_db_path();
        let db = UetsDb::open(&path).unwrap();

        let meta = db.create_session("session_1").unwrap();
        assert!(meta.active);
        assert!(db.get_session("session_1").unwrap().is_some());

        // Duplicate id rejected — sessions are never overwritten silently.
        assert!(matches!(
            db.create_session("session_1"),
            Err(DbError::SessionExists { .. })
        ));

        let summary = SessionSummary {
            schema: SchemaVersion::default(),
            session_id: "session_1".to_string(),
            finalized_at: Utc::now(),
            trade_count: 3,
            portfolio_snapshots: 10,
            final_equity: 10_500.0,
            cumulative_return: 0.05,
            sortino: 1.2,
            max_drawdown: 0.02,
        };
        let finalized = db.finalize_session(&summary).unwrap();
        assert!(!finalized.active);
        assert!(finalized.ended_at.is_some());

        let stored = db.session_summary("session_1").unwrap().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(stored.trade_count, 3);
        assert!(!db.get_session("session_1").unwrap().unwrap().active);
        assert_eq!(db.list_sessions().unwrap().len(), 1);

        // Finalizing an unknown session is an explicit error, not a no-op.
        assert!(matches!(
            db.finalize_session(&SessionSummary {
                session_id: "missing".to_string(),
                ..summary
            }),
            Err(DbError::SessionNotFound { .. })
        ));

        let _ = db.shutdown();
        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn combo_filters_return_correct_records() {
        let path = temp_db_path();
        let db = UetsDb::open(&path).unwrap();

        let base = Utc.with_ymd_and_hms(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0).unwrap();
        let mut trades = Vec::new();
        for (sess, strat, asset, sym, minute) in [
            ("session_1", "ofmr", "btcusdt", "BTCUSDT", 0),
            ("session_1", "ofmr", "ethusdt", "ETHUSDT", 1),
            ("session_1", "moe", "btcusdt", "BTCUSDT", 2),
            ("session_2", "ofmr", "btcusdt", "BTCUSDT", 0),
        ] {
            let mut t = sample_trade();
            t.session_id = sess.to_string();
            t.instance = StrategyInstance::new(strat, asset);
            t.symbol = sym.to_string();
            t.event_time = base + chrono::Duration::minutes(minute);
            trades.push(t);
        }
        db.write_batch(&trades, &[]).unwrap();

        // Session only (indexed prefix scan).
        let r = db
            .query_trades(&QueryFilter::new().session("session_1"))
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(r.len(), 3);

        // Session + strategy.
        let r = db
            .query_trades(&QueryFilter::new().session("session_1").strategy("ofmr"))
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(r.len(), 2);

        // Session + strategy + asset.
        let r = db
            .query_trades(
                &QueryFilter::new()
                    .session("session_1")
                    .strategy("ofmr")
                    .asset("btcusdt"),
            )
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(r.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(r[0].symbol, "BTCUSDT");

        // Asset only (no session -> full scan path).
        let r = db.query_trades(&QueryFilter::new().asset("btcusdt")).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(r.len(), 3);

        // Strategy only (crosses sessions).
        let r = db
            .query_trades(&QueryFilter::new().strategy("ofmr"))
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(r.len(), 3);

        // Time range within a session.
        let r = db
            .query_trades(
                &QueryFilter::new().session("session_1").time_range(
                    base + chrono::Duration::minutes(1),
                    base + chrono::Duration::minutes(2),
                ),
            )
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(r.len(), 2);

        let _ = db.shutdown();
        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn restart_recovers_written_records() {
        let path = temp_db_path();
        {
            let db = UetsDb::open(&path).unwrap();
            db.write_trade(&sample_trade()).unwrap();
            db.create_session("session_1").unwrap();
            db.flush().unwrap();
            db.shutdown().unwrap();
        }

        // Reopen: records, indexes and sessions survive.
        let db = UetsDb::open(&path).unwrap();
        let results = db
            .query_trades(&QueryFilter::new().session("session_1"))
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(results.len(), 1);
        assert!(db.get_session("session_1").unwrap().unwrap().active);

        let report = db.integrity_check().unwrap();
        assert!(report.is_clean());
        assert_eq!(report.trade_records, 1);
        assert_eq!(report.active_sessions, 1);

        let _ = db.shutdown();
        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&path);
    }

    #[test]
    fn integrity_reports_corrupt_records() {
        let path = temp_db_path();
        let db = UetsDb::open(&path).unwrap();
        db.write_trade(&sample_trade()).unwrap();

        // Write garbage directly (bypassing the API) to simulate corruption.
        db.trades
            .insert(
                &b"trades|session_1|ofmr@btcusdt|BTCUSDT|99"[..],
                &b"not-a-valid-record"[..],
            )
            .unwrap();

        let report = db.integrity_check().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(report.trade_records, 1);
        assert_eq!(report.corrupt_keys.len(), 1, "corruption must be reported");
        assert!(!report.is_clean());

        let _ = db.shutdown();
        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&path);
    }
}
