use crossterm::event::{KeyCode, KeyEvent};

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Action {
    Quit,
    Up,
    Down,
    Select,
    Clear,
    None,
}

pub fn action(key: KeyEvent) -> Action {
    match key.code {
        KeyCode::Char('q') => Action::Quit,
        KeyCode::Char('j') | KeyCode::Down => Action::Down,
        KeyCode::Char('k') | KeyCode::Up => Action::Up,
        KeyCode::Enter => Action::Select,
        KeyCode::Esc => Action::Clear,
        _ => Action::None,
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crossterm::event::KeyModifiers;

    #[test]
    fn vim_navigation_and_selection_keys_are_mapped() {
        assert_eq!(
            action(KeyEvent::new(KeyCode::Char('j'), KeyModifiers::NONE)),
            Action::Down
        );
        assert_eq!(
            action(KeyEvent::new(KeyCode::Char('k'), KeyModifiers::NONE)),
            Action::Up
        );
        assert_eq!(
            action(KeyEvent::new(KeyCode::Enter, KeyModifiers::NONE)),
            Action::Select
        );
        assert_eq!(
            action(KeyEvent::new(KeyCode::Esc, KeyModifiers::NONE)),
            Action::Clear
        );
        assert_eq!(
            action(KeyEvent::new(KeyCode::Char('q'), KeyModifiers::NONE)),
            Action::Quit
        );
    }
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/uets_gui/src/gui.rs =====
use std::io::{self, Stdout};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use crossterm::event::{self, Event};
use crossterm::execute;
use crossterm::terminal::{
    EnterAlternateScreen, LeaveAlternateScreen, disable_raw_mode, enable_raw_mode,
};
use ratatui::backend::CrosstermBackend;
use ratatui::layout::{Constraint, Direction, Layout};
use ratatui::style::{Color, Modifier, Style};
use ratatui::symbols;
use ratatui::text::Line;
use ratatui::widgets::{
    Axis, Block, Borders, Chart, Dataset, GraphType, List, ListItem, ListState, Paragraph,
};
use ratatui::{Frame, Terminal};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use thiserror::Error;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, Deserialize, Serialize)]
pub struct Statistics {
    pub sortino: Option<f64>,
    pub expectancy_rr: Option<f64>,
    pub max_drawdown: Option<f64>,
    pub cumulative_return: Option<f64>,
    pub absolute_return: Option<f64>,
    pub cash: Option<f64>,
    pub equity: Option<f64>,
    pub realized_pnl: Option<f64>,
    pub unrealized_pnl: Option<f64>,
    pub trades: Option<usize>,
    pub open_positions: Option<usize>,
    pub win_rate: Option<f64>,
    pub exposure: Option<f64>,
    pub status: Option<String>,
    pub latest_market_data: Option<String>,
    pub latest_decision: Option<String>,
    pub exchange_latency_ms: Option<f64>,
    pub processing_latency_ms: Option<f64>,
    pub latency_p05_ms: Option<f64>,
    pub latency_p95_ms: Option<f64>,
    pub latency_p99_ms: Option<f64>,
    pub clock_skew_mean_ms: Option<f64>,
    pub clock_skew_max_ms: Option<f64>,
    pub decision_to_order_ms: Option<f64>,
    pub order_to_fill_ms: Option<f64>,
    pub memory_mb: Option<f64>,
    pub cpu_percent: Option<f64>,
    pub persistence_queue: Option<usize>,
    pub persistence_write_ms: Option<f64>,
    pub persistence_errors: Option<u64>,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, Deserialize, Serialize)]
pub struct InstanceSnapshot {
    pub strategy_id: String,
    pub asset_id: String,
    pub statistics: Statistics,
    pub equity_history: Vec<f64>,
}

impl InstanceSnapshot {
    pub fn identity(&self) -> String {
        format!("{}@{}", self.strategy_id, self.asset_id)
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default)]
pub struct DashboardSnapshot {
    pub timestamp: Option<String>,
    pub aggregate: Statistics,
    pub aggregate_equity_history: Vec<f64>,
    pub instances: Vec<InstanceSnapshot>,
}

/// The producer should publish already-calculated snapshots. `snapshot` must
/// return quickly; the GUI polls it independently at the display cadence.
pub trait SnapshotSource {
    fn snapshot(&mut self) -> DashboardSnapshot;
}

#[derive(Debug, Error)]
pub enum MonitorError {
    #[error("terminal I/O failed: {0}")]
    Io(#[from] io::Error),
}

pub struct Monitor<S> {
    source: S,
    selected: Option<usize>,
    interval: Duration,
}

impl<S: SnapshotSource> Monitor<S> {
    pub fn new(source: S) -> Self {
        Self {
            source,
            selected: None,
            interval: Duration::from_millis(100),
        }
    }
    pub fn with_interval(mut self, interval: Duration) -> Self {
        self.interval = interval.max(Duration::from_millis(10));
        self
    }

    pub fn run(mut self) -> Result<(), MonitorError> {
        enable_raw_mode()?;
        let mut stdout = io::stdout();
        execute!(stdout, EnterAlternateScreen)?;
        let backend = CrosstermBackend::new(stdout);
        let mut terminal = Terminal::new(backend)?;
        let result = self.event_loop(&mut terminal);
        disable_raw_mode()?;
        execute!(terminal.backend_mut(), LeaveAlternateScreen)?;
        terminal.show_cursor()?;
        result
    }

    fn event_loop(
        &mut self,
        terminal: &mut Terminal<CrosstermBackend<Stdout>>,
    ) -> Result<(), MonitorError> {
        loop {
            let started = Instant::now();
            let snapshot = self.source.snapshot();
            if self
                .selected
                .is_some_and(|index| index >= snapshot.instances.len())
            {
                self.selected = None;
            }
            terminal.draw(|frame| render(frame, &snapshot, self.selected))?;
            let timeout = self.interval.saturating_sub(started.elapsed());
            if event::poll(timeout)? {
                if let Event::Key(key) = event::read()? {
                    match crate::controls::action(key) {
                        crate::controls::Action::Quit => return Ok(()),
                        crate::controls::Action::Up => self.move_selection(&snapshot, -1),
                        crate::controls::Action::Down => self.move_selection(&snapshot, 1),
                        crate::controls::Action::Select => {
                            if !snapshot.instances.is_empty() {
                                self.selected = Some(
                                    self.selected.unwrap_or(0).min(snapshot.instances.len() - 1),
                                );
                            }
                        }
                        crate::controls::Action::Clear => self.selected = None,
                        crate::controls::Action::None => {}
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fn move_selection(&mut self, snapshot: &DashboardSnapshot, delta: isize) {
        if snapshot.instances.is_empty() {
            self.selected = None;
            return;
        }
        let current = self.selected.unwrap_or(0) as isize;
        self.selected =
            Some((current + delta).clamp(0, snapshot.instances.len() as isize - 1) as usize);
    }
}

fn render(frame: &mut Frame<'_>, snapshot: &DashboardSnapshot, selected: Option<usize>) {
    let areas = Layout::default()
        .direction(Direction::Vertical)
        .constraints(
            [
                Constraint::Percentage(40),
                Constraint::Length(10),
                Constraint::Min(7),
            ]
            .as_ref(),
        )
        .split(frame.area());
    let (identity, stats, equity) = selected
        .and_then(|i| snapshot.instances.get(i))
        .map(|instance| {
            (
                instance.identity(),
                &instance.statistics,
                &instance.equity_history,
            )
        })
        .unwrap_or_else(|| {
            (
                "Portfolio aggregate".to_string(),
                &snapshot.aggregate,
                &snapshot.aggregate_equity_history,
            )
        });
    render_chart(
        frame,
        areas[0],
        &identity,
        equity,
        snapshot.timestamp.as_deref(),
    );
    render_statistics(frame, areas[1], stats, &identity);
    render_instances(frame, areas[2], snapshot, selected);
}

fn render_chart(
    frame: &mut Frame<'_>,
    area: ratatui::layout::Rect,
    identity: &str,
    equity: &[f64],
    timestamp: Option<&str>,
) {
    let points: Vec<(f64, f64)> = equity
        .iter()
        .enumerate()
        .map(|(i, value)| (i as f64, *value))
        .collect();
    let x_max = points.last().map(|p| p.0.max(1.0)).unwrap_or(1.0);
    let (y_min, y_max) = equity
        .iter()
        .fold((f64::INFINITY, f64::NEG_INFINITY), |(lo, hi), v| {
            (lo.min(*v), hi.max(*v))
        });
    let bounds = if y_min.is_finite() && y_max.is_finite() {
        (y_min, if y_min == y_max { y_max + 1.0 } else { y_max })
    } else {
        (0.0, 1.0)
    };
    let dataset = Dataset::default()
        .name("Equity")
        .marker(symbols::Marker::Braille)
        .graph_type(GraphType::Line)
        .style(Style::default().fg(Color::Cyan))
        .data(&points);
    let title = format!(
        " {identity} · Equity{} ",
        timestamp.map(|t| format!(" · {t}")).unwrap_or_default()
    );
    let chart = Chart::new(vec![dataset])
        .block(Block::default().title(title).borders(Borders::ALL))
        .x_axis(
            Axis::default()
                .bounds([0.0, x_max])
                .labels(vec![Line::from("oldest"), Line::from("latest")]),
        )
        .y_axis(Axis::default().bounds([bounds.0, bounds.1]).labels(vec![
            Line::from(format!("{:.2}", bounds.0)),
            Line::from(format!("{:.2}", bounds.1)),
        ]));
    frame.render_widget(chart, area);
}

fn metric(name: &str, value: Option<String>) -> String {
    format!("{name}: {}", value.unwrap_or_else(|| "—".into()))
}
fn number(value: Option<f64>, percent: bool) -> Option<String> {
    value.map(|v| {
        if percent {
            format!("{:.2}%", v * 100.0)
        } else {
            format!("{v:.3}")
        }
    })
}

fn render_statistics(
    frame: &mut Frame<'_>,
    area: ratatui::layout::Rect,
    stats: &Statistics,
    identity: &str,
) {
    let values = [
        metric("Sortino", number(stats.sortino, false)),
        metric("Expectancy R", number(stats.expectancy_rr, false)),
        metric("MDD", number(stats.max_drawdown, true)),
        metric("Return", number(stats.cumulative_return, true)),
        metric("Equity", number(stats.equity, false)),
        metric("Cash", number(stats.cash, false)),
        metric("Realized", number(stats.realized_pnl, false)),
        metric("Unrealized", number(stats.unrealized_pnl, false)),
        metric("Exposure", number(stats.exposure, false)),
        metric(
            "Open positions",
            stats.open_positions.map(|v| v.to_string()),
        ),
        metric("Win rate", number(stats.win_rate, true)),
        metric("Status", stats.status.clone()),
        metric("Market data", stats.latest_market_data.clone()),
        metric("Decision", stats.latest_decision.clone()),
        metric("Strategy ms", number(stats.processing_latency_ms, false)),
        metric(
            "Exchange→local ms",
            number(stats.exchange_latency_ms, false),
        ),
        metric("Latency p05 ms", number(stats.latency_p05_ms, false)),
        metric("Latency p95 ms", number(stats.latency_p95_ms, false)),
        metric("Latency p99 ms", number(stats.latency_p99_ms, false)),
        metric(
            "Clock skew mean ms",
            number(stats.clock_skew_mean_ms, false),
        ),
        metric("Clock skew max ms", number(stats.clock_skew_max_ms, false)),
        metric(
            "Decision→order ms",
            number(stats.decision_to_order_ms, false),
        ),
        metric("Order→fill ms", number(stats.order_to_fill_ms, false)),
        metric("Memory MB", number(stats.memory_mb, false)),
        metric("CPU %", number(stats.cpu_percent, false)),
        metric(
            "Persist queue",
            stats.persistence_queue.map(|v| v.to_string()),
        ),
        metric("Write ms", number(stats.persistence_write_ms, false)),
        metric(
            "Storage errors",
            stats.persistence_errors.map(|v| v.to_string()),
        ),
        metric("Return $", number(stats.absolute_return, false)),
        metric("Trades", stats.trades.map(|v| v.to_string())),
    ];
    let rows = values
        .chunks(4)
        .take(8)
        .map(|row| row.join("   │   "))
        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        .join("\n");
    frame.render_widget(
        Paragraph::new(rows).block(
            Block::default()
                .title(format!(" {identity} · Performance "))
                .borders(Borders::ALL),
        ),
        area,
    );
}

fn render_instances(
    frame: &mut Frame<'_>,
    area: ratatui::layout::Rect,
    snapshot: &DashboardSnapshot,
    selected: Option<usize>,
) {
    let chunks = Layout::default()
        .direction(Direction::Horizontal)
        .constraints([Constraint::Percentage(32), Constraint::Percentage(68)].as_ref())
        .split(area);
    let entries = snapshot
        .instances
        .iter()
        .map(|instance| {
            let stats = &instance.statistics;
            let label = format!(
                "{}  {}  ret {}  Sortino {}",
                instance.identity(),
                stats.status.as_deref().unwrap_or("unknown"),
                number(stats.cumulative_return, true).unwrap_or_else(|| "—".into()),
                number(stats.sortino, false).unwrap_or_else(|| "—".into())
            );
            ListItem::new(label)
        })
        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let list = List::new(entries)
        .block(
            Block::default()
                .title(" Strategies · j/k · Enter ")
                .borders(Borders::ALL),
        )
        .highlight_style(
            Style::default()
                .fg(Color::Yellow)
                .add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD),
        );
    let mut state = ListState::default();
    state.select(selected);
    frame.render_stateful_widget(list, chunks[0], &mut state);
    let rows = snapshot
        .instances
        .iter()
        .map(|i| {
            let s = &i.statistics;
            format!(
                "{:<20} {:>8} {:>12} {:>12} {:>9} {:>7} {:>7}",
                i.identity(),
                s.status.as_deref().unwrap_or("—"),
                number(s.equity, false).unwrap_or_else(|| "—".into()),
                number(s.absolute_return, false).unwrap_or_else(|| "—".into()),
                number(s.win_rate, true).unwrap_or_else(|| "—".into()),
                s.trades
                    .map(|v| v.to_string())
                    .unwrap_or_else(|| "—".into()),
                s.open_positions
                    .map(|v| v.to_string())
                    .unwrap_or_else(|| "—".into())
            )
        })
        .collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let table = std::iter::once(
        "INSTANCE             STATUS       EQUITY       PNL       WIN% TRADES POS".to_string(),
    )
    .chain(rows)
    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
    .join("\n");
    frame.render_widget(
        Paragraph::new(table).block(
            Block::default()
                .title(" Instance detail · Esc aggregate · q quit ")
                .borders(Borders::ALL),
        ),
        chunks[1],
    );
}

//! Read-only monitoring TUI. The application supplies authoritative snapshots;
//! this crate only renders, selects, and transports them over local sockets.

pub mod controls;

===== FILE: components/pipelines/uets_gui/src/main.rs =====
use std::path::PathBuf;

#[cfg(unix)]
use uets_gui::publish_instance;
#[cfg(unix)]
use uets_gui::{InstanceSnapshot, Statistics};
use uets_gui::{Monitor, SocketSnapshotSource};

fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let path = std::env::var_os("UETS_GUI_SOCKET")
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("sockets/gui.sock"));
    let args = std::env::args().skip(1).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if args.first().is_some_and(|value| value == "publish") {
        return publish_status(&path, &args[1..]);
    }
    let source = SocketSnapshotSource::bind(&path)?;
    eprintln!("UETS monitor listening on {}", path.display());
    Monitor::new(source).run()?;
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(unix)]
fn publish_status(
    path: &std::path::Path,
    args: &[String],
) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    if args.len() != 2 {
        return Err("usage: uets_gui publish <strategy>@<asset> <enabled|disabled|error>".into());
    }
    let Some((strategy_id, asset_id)) = args[0].split_once('@') else {
        return Err("strategy instance must use strategy@asset".into());
    };
    if !["enabled", "disabled", "error"].contains(&args[1].as_str()) {
        return Err("status must be enabled, disabled, or error".into());
    }
    let instance = InstanceSnapshot {
        strategy_id: strategy_id.to_string(),
        asset_id: asset_id.to_string(),
        statistics: Statistics {
            status: Some(args[1].clone()),
            ..Statistics::default()
        },
        equity_history: Vec::new(),
    };
    publish_instance(path, &instance)?;
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(not(unix))]
fn publish_status(
    _path: &std::path::Path,
    _args: &[String],
) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    Err("status publishing requires Unix-domain sockets".into())
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/uets_gui/src/socket.rs =====
#[cfg(unix)]
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::io;
use std::path::Path;
#[cfg(unix)]
use std::time::{Duration, Instant, SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

#[cfg(unix)]
use crate::gui::InstanceSnapshot;
use crate::gui::{DashboardSnapshot, SnapshotSource};

#[cfg(unix)]
pub fn publish_instance(path: impl AsRef<Path>, instance: &InstanceSnapshot) -> io::Result<()> {
    use std::os::unix::net::UnixDatagram;

    let socket = UnixDatagram::unbound()?;
    socket.connect(path)?;
    let payload = serde_json::to_vec(instance)
        .map_err(|error| io::Error::new(io::ErrorKind::InvalidData, error))?;
    socket.send(&payload)?;
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(not(unix))]
pub fn publish_instance(
    _path: impl AsRef<Path>,
    _instance: &crate::gui::InstanceSnapshot,
) -> io::Result<()> {
    Err(io::Error::new(
        io::ErrorKind::Unsupported,
        "UETS instance publishing requires Unix-domain sockets",
    ))
}

#[cfg(unix)]
const INSTANCE_STALE_AFTER: Duration = Duration::from_secs(3);

#[cfg(unix)]
pub struct SocketSnapshotSource {
    socket: std::os::unix::net::UnixDatagram,
    instances: BTreeMap<String, (InstanceSnapshot, Instant)>,
}

#[cfg(unix)]
impl SocketSnapshotSource {
    pub fn bind(path: impl AsRef<Path>) -> io::Result<Self> {
        use std::os::unix::fs::FileTypeExt;
        let path = path.as_ref();
        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
            std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
        }
        if path.exists() {
            let socket_type = std::fs::symlink_metadata(path)?.file_type().is_socket();
            if !socket_type {
                return Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists,
                    format!("refusing to replace non-socket GUI path {}", path.display()),
                ));
            }
            let probe = std::os::unix::net::UnixDatagram::unbound()?;
            if probe.connect(path).is_ok() {
                return Err(io::Error::new(
                    io::ErrorKind::AddrInUse,
                    format!("GUI socket is already active at {}", path.display()),
                ));
            }
            std::fs::remove_file(path)?;
        }
        let socket = std::os::unix::net::UnixDatagram::bind(path)?;
        socket.set_nonblocking(true)?;
        Ok(Self {
            socket,
            instances: BTreeMap::new(),
        })
    }
}

#[cfg(not(unix))]
impl SnapshotSource for SocketSnapshotSource {
    fn snapshot(&mut self) -> DashboardSnapshot {
        DashboardSnapshot::default()
    }
}

#[cfg(unix)]
impl SnapshotSource for SocketSnapshotSource {
    fn snapshot(&mut self) -> DashboardSnapshot {
        let mut buffer = [0u8; 64 * 1024];
        loop {
            match self.socket.recv(&mut buffer) {
                Ok(size) => {
                    if let Ok(instance) =
                        serde_json::from_slice::<InstanceSnapshot>(&buffer[..size])
                    {
                        let identity = instance.identity();
                        if !instance.strategy_id.is_empty() && !instance.asset_id.is_empty() {
                            self.instances.insert(identity, (instance, Instant::now()));
                        }
                    }
                }
                Err(error) if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::WouldBlock => break,
                Err(_) => break,
            }
        }

        let now = Instant::now();
        let instances = self
            .instances
            .values_mut()
            .map(|(instance, last_seen)| {
                if instance.statistics.status.as_deref() != Some("disabled")
                    && now.duration_since(*last_seen) > INSTANCE_STALE_AFTER
                {
                    instance.statistics.status = Some("error".to_string());
                }
                instance.clone()
            })
            .collect();
        DashboardSnapshot {
            timestamp: SystemTime::now()
                .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
                .ok()
                .map(|elapsed| format!("{} ms UTC", elapsed.as_millis())),
            instances,
            ..DashboardSnapshot::default()
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(not(unix))]
pub struct SocketSnapshotSource;

#[cfg(not(unix))]
impl SocketSnapshotSource {
    pub fn bind(_path: impl AsRef<Path>) -> io::Result<Self> {
        Err(io::Error::new(
            io::ErrorKind::Unsupported,
            "the live UETS monitor requires Unix-domain sockets on a Unix host",
        ))
    }
}

//! Execution ownership and mode selection for UETS.

mod router;
mod venue;

pub use router::{ManagerConfig, ManagerRouter};
pub use venue::{UetsExecutionConnectorAdapter, VenueConnector, VenueError};

use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use uets_trades::backend::{BackendError, ExecutionBackend};
use uets_trades::live_test::LiveTestBackend;
use uets_trades::live_trade::LiveTradeBackend;
use uets_trades::playback::PlaybackBackend;
use uets_trades::sim::SimConfig;
use uets_trades::types::{
    Capital, ExecutionEvent, FillEvent, Order, OrderId, OrderRequest, TradeIntent,
};

/// Explicitly selected execution mode. Live mode cannot be created without a venue.
pub enum ExecutionMode {
    LiveTest {
        config: SimConfig,
    },
    LiveTrade {
        router: ManagerRouter,
    },
    Playback {
        config: SimConfig,
        acceleration: f64,
    },
}

/// Owns the selected backend and exposes the shared trading interface.
pub struct ManagerEngine {
    backend: Box<dyn ExecutionBackend>,
}

impl ManagerEngine {
    pub fn new(mode: ExecutionMode) -> Self {
        let backend: Box<dyn ExecutionBackend> = match mode {
            ExecutionMode::LiveTest { config } => Box::new(LiveTestBackend::with_config(config)),
            ExecutionMode::LiveTrade { router } => {
                Box::new(LiveTradeBackend::with_router(Box::new(router)))
            }
            ExecutionMode::Playback {
                config,
                acceleration,
            } => Box::new(PlaybackBackend::with_config(config, acceleration)),
        };
        Self { backend }
    }

    pub fn live_test(config: SimConfig) -> Self {
        Self::new(ExecutionMode::LiveTest { config })
    }
    pub fn live_trade(router: ManagerRouter) -> Self {
        Self::new(ExecutionMode::LiveTrade { router })
    }
    pub fn playback(config: SimConfig, acceleration: f64) -> Self {
        Self::new(ExecutionMode::Playback {
            config,
            acceleration,
        })
    }

    pub fn backend(&self) -> &dyn ExecutionBackend {
        self.backend.as_ref()
    }
    pub fn backend_mut(&mut self) -> &mut dyn ExecutionBackend {
        self.backend.as_mut()
    }
}

// Delegation keeps all modes on the one uets_trades contract.
impl ExecutionBackend for ManagerEngine {
    fn submit_intent(&mut self, intent: TradeIntent) -> Result<OrderId, BackendError> {
        self.backend.submit_intent(intent)
    }
    fn submit_order(&mut self, request: OrderRequest) -> Result<OrderId, BackendError> {
        self.backend.submit_order(request)
    }
    fn cancel_order(&mut self, id: &OrderId) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
        self.backend.cancel_order(id)
    }
    fn order_state(&self, id: &OrderId) -> Option<&Order> {
        self.backend.order_state(id)
    }
    fn orders(&self) -> Vec<&Order> {
        self.backend.orders()
    }
    fn drain_events(&mut self) -> Vec<ExecutionEvent> {
        self.backend.drain_events()
    }
    fn capital(&self, strategy: &str) -> Option<Capital> {
        self.backend.capital(strategy)
    }
    fn apply_fill(&mut self, fill: &FillEvent) {
        self.backend.apply_fill(fill)
    }
    fn on_market_update(&mut self, marks: &BTreeMap<String, f64>, now: DateTime<Utc>) {
        self.backend.on_market_update(marks, now)
    }
    fn is_connected(&self) -> bool {
        self.backend.is_connected()
    }
    fn acceleration(&self) -> f64 {
        self.backend.acceleration()
    }
    fn position_qty(&self, strategy: &str, symbol: &str) -> Option<f64> {
        self.backend.position_qty(strategy, symbol)
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::{ManagerConfig, ManagerRouter, VenueConnector, VenueError};
    use chrono::Duration;
    use std::sync::{
        Arc,
        atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering},
    };
    use std::time::Duration as StdDuration;
    use uets_trades::backend::{LiveRouter, RouterError};
    use uets_trades::types::{ExecutionEvent, OrderId, OrderRequest, OrderSide};

    struct FakeVenue(Arc<AtomicUsize>);
    impl VenueConnector for FakeVenue {
        fn place_order(
            &mut self,
            request: &OrderRequest,
            now: DateTime<Utc>,
        ) -> Result<Vec<ExecutionEvent>, VenueError> {
            self.0.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
            Ok(vec![ExecutionEvent::OrderAccepted {
                order_id: request.id.clone(),
                symbol: request.symbol.clone(),
                at: now,
            }])
        }
        fn cancel_order(
            &mut self,
            id: &OrderId,
            _symbol: &str,
            now: DateTime<Utc>,
        ) -> Result<Vec<ExecutionEvent>, VenueError> {
            Ok(vec![ExecutionEvent::OrderCancelled {
                order_id: id.clone(),
                at: now,
            }])
        }
    }

    fn request() -> OrderRequest {
        OrderRequest::new(
            "one",
            "BTCUSDT",
            OrderSide::Buy,
            1.0,
            "ofmr@btcusdt",
            "btcusdt",
        )
    }

    #[test]
    fn manager_router_routes_once_and_rejects_duplicate_ids() {
        let calls = Arc::new(AtomicUsize::new(0));
        let mut router =
            ManagerRouter::new(Box::new(FakeVenue(calls.clone())), ManagerConfig::default());
        let now = Utc::now();
        router.note_market_tick(now);
        assert_eq!(router.route(&request(), now).unwrap().len(), 1);
        assert!(matches!(
            router.route(&request(), now),
            Err(RouterError::DuplicateOrderId(_))
        ));
        assert_eq!(calls.load(Ordering::SeqCst), 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn live_routing_rejects_missing_connector_and_stale_data() {
        let now = Utc::now();
        let mut missing = ManagerRouter::unconfigured(ManagerConfig::default());
        missing.note_market_tick(now);
        assert!(matches!(
            missing.route(&request(), now),
            Err(RouterError::NotConfigured)
        ));

        let mut stale = ManagerRouter::new(
            Box::new(FakeVenue(Arc::new(AtomicUsize::new(0)))),
            ManagerConfig {
                max_market_data_age: StdDuration::from_millis(50),
            },
        );
        stale.note_market_tick(now - Duration::seconds(1));
        assert!(matches!(
            stale.route(&request(), now),
            Err(RouterError::StaleMarketData { .. })
        ));
    }
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/uets_manager/src/router.rs =====
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use std::collections::BTreeSet;
use std::time::Duration;
use uets_trades::backend::{LiveRouter, RouterError};
use uets_trades::types::{ExecutionEvent, OrderId, OrderRequest};

use crate::{VenueConnector, VenueError};

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ManagerConfig {
    pub max_market_data_age: Duration,
}

impl Default for ManagerConfig {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            max_market_data_age: Duration::from_secs(2),
        }
    }
}

/// Validates and forwards live orders to the configured venue connector.
pub struct ManagerRouter {
    venue: Option<Box<dyn VenueConnector>>,
    config: ManagerConfig,
    last_market_tick: Option<DateTime<Utc>>,
    order_ids: BTreeSet<OrderId>,
    events: Vec<ExecutionEvent>,
}

impl ManagerRouter {
    pub fn new(venue: Box<dyn VenueConnector>, config: ManagerConfig) -> Self {
        Self {
            venue: Some(venue),
            config,
            last_market_tick: None,
            order_ids: BTreeSet::new(),
            events: Vec::new(),
        }
    }
    pub fn unconfigured(config: ManagerConfig) -> Self {
        Self {
            venue: None,
            config,
            last_market_tick: None,
            order_ids: BTreeSet::new(),
            events: Vec::new(),
        }
    }
}

impl LiveRouter for ManagerRouter {
    fn note_market_tick(&mut self, ts: DateTime<Utc>) {
        self.last_market_tick = Some(ts);
    }

    fn route(
        &mut self,
        request: &OrderRequest,
        now: DateTime<Utc>,
    ) -> Result<Vec<ExecutionEvent>, RouterError> {
        if !self.order_ids.insert(request.id.clone()) {
            return Err(RouterError::DuplicateOrderId(request.id.0.clone()));
        }
        if request.symbol.trim().is_empty()
            || request.strategy_id.trim().is_empty()
            || request.asset_id.trim().is_empty()
            || !request.qty.is_finite()
            || request.qty <= 0.0
        {
            self.order_ids.remove(&request.id);
            return Err(RouterError::Invalid(
                "symbol, strategy, asset and positive finite quantity are required".into(),
            ));
        }
        let Some(tick) = self.last_market_tick else {
            self.order_ids.remove(&request.id);
            return Err(RouterError::StaleMarketData {
                age_ms: u64::MAX,
                threshold_ms: self.config.max_market_data_age.as_millis() as u64,
            });
        };
        let age_ms = now.signed_duration_since(tick).num_milliseconds().max(0) as u64;
        let threshold_ms = self
            .config
            .max_market_data_age
            .as_millis()
            .min(u64::MAX as u128) as u64;
        if age_ms > threshold_ms {
            self.order_ids.remove(&request.id);
            return Err(RouterError::StaleMarketData {
                age_ms,
                threshold_ms,
            });
        }
        let Some(venue) = self.venue.as_mut() else {
            self.order_ids.remove(&request.id);
            return Err(RouterError::NotConfigured);
        };
        match venue.place_order(request, now) {
            Ok(events) => Ok(events),
            Err(VenueError::Rejected(reason)) => {
                self.order_ids.remove(&request.id);
                Err(RouterError::Rejected(reason))
            }
            Err(err) => Err(RouterError::Transport(err.to_string())),
        }
    }

    fn cancel(&mut self, order_id: &OrderId, symbol: &str) -> Result<(), RouterError> {
        let Some(venue) = self.venue.as_mut() else {
            return Err(RouterError::NotConfigured);
        };
        let events = venue
            .cancel_order(order_id, symbol, Utc::now())
            .map_err(|e| RouterError::Transport(e.to_string()))?;
        self.events.extend(events);
        Ok(())
    }

    fn drain_events(&mut self) -> Vec<ExecutionEvent> {
        std::mem::take(&mut self.events)
    }
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/uets_manager/src/venue.rs =====
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use thiserror::Error;
use uets::core::order::{
    OrderRequest as ConnectorOrderRequest, Side as ConnectorSide,
    TimeInForce as ConnectorTimeInForce,
};
use uets::exchange::ExecutionConnector;
use uets_trades::types::{
    ExecutionEvent, FillEvent, OrderId, OrderRequest, OrderSide, OrderType, TimeInForce,
};

#[derive(Debug, Error)]
pub enum VenueError {
    #[error("venue rejected order: {0}")]
    Rejected(String),
    #[error("venue transport error: {0}")]
    Transport(String),
}

/// Synchronous venue boundary. Implementations own exchange/broker clients;
/// strategy code receives only execution events through the manager.
pub trait VenueConnector: Send + Sync {
    fn place_order(
        &mut self,
        request: &OrderRequest,
        now: DateTime<Utc>,
    ) -> Result<Vec<ExecutionEvent>, VenueError>;
    fn cancel_order(
        &mut self,
        order_id: &OrderId,
        symbol: &str,
        now: DateTime<Utc>,
    ) -> Result<Vec<ExecutionEvent>, VenueError>;
}

/// Adapter for the repository's venue connectors, keeping connector calls
/// behind `ManagerRouter`. Market orders with immediate fills are supported;
/// the current connector contract cannot safely correlate an unfilled resting
/// limit order to its later venue order ID, so those are refused.
pub struct UetsExecutionConnectorAdapter<C> {
    connector: C,
    venue_order_ids: BTreeMap<OrderId, String>,
}

impl<C> UetsExecutionConnectorAdapter<C> {
    pub fn new(connector: C) -> Self {
        Self {
            connector,
            venue_order_ids: BTreeMap::new(),
        }
    }
    pub fn into_inner(self) -> C {
        self.connector
    }
}

impl<C: ExecutionConnector + Send + Sync> VenueConnector for UetsExecutionConnectorAdapter<C> {
    fn place_order(
        &mut self,
        request: &OrderRequest,
        now: DateTime<Utc>,
    ) -> Result<Vec<ExecutionEvent>, VenueError> {
        if request.order_type == OrderType::Limit {
            return Err(VenueError::Rejected(
                "the current repository connector contract cannot correlate resting limit orders"
                    .into(),
            ));
        }
        let mut connector_request = ConnectorOrderRequest::market(
            request.symbol.clone(),
            match request.side {
                OrderSide::Buy => ConnectorSide::Buy,
                OrderSide::Sell => ConnectorSide::Sell,
            },
            request.qty,
        );
        connector_request.time_in_force = match request.time_in_force {
            TimeInForce::Gtc => ConnectorTimeInForce::Gtc,
            TimeInForce::Ioc => ConnectorTimeInForce::Ioc,
            TimeInForce::Fok => ConnectorTimeInForce::Fok,
        };
        let fills = self
            .connector
            .submit(&connector_request)
            .map_err(|err| match err {
                uets::exchange::ConnectorError::Rejected(message)
                | uets::exchange::ConnectorError::Unsupported(message) => {
                    VenueError::Rejected(message)
                }
                uets::exchange::ConnectorError::Malformed(error) => {
                    VenueError::Rejected(error.to_string())
                }
                other => VenueError::Transport(other.to_string()),
            })?;
        if fills.is_empty() {
            return Err(VenueError::Transport(
                "venue returned no fill; reconcile order state before retry".into(),
            ));
        }
        let venue_id = fills[0].order_id.clone();
        self.venue_order_ids
            .insert(request.id.clone(), venue_id.as_str().to_string());
        let mut events = vec![ExecutionEvent::OrderAccepted {
            order_id: request.id.clone(),
            symbol: request.symbol.clone(),
            at: now,
        }];
        for fill in fills {
            if !fill.qty.is_finite()
                || fill.qty <= 0.0
                || !fill.price.is_finite()
                || fill.price <= 0.0
                || !fill.fee.is_finite()
            {
                return Err(VenueError::Transport(
                    "venue returned invalid fill data; reconciliation is required".into(),
                ));
            }
            events.push(ExecutionEvent::Fill(FillEvent {
                order_id: request.id.clone(),
                symbol: fill.symbol,
                side: match fill.side {
                    ConnectorSide::Buy => OrderSide::Buy,
                    ConnectorSide::Sell => OrderSide::Sell,
                },
                qty: fill.qty,
                price: fill.price,
                fee: fill.fee,
                executed_at: now,
            }));
        }
        Ok(events)
    }

    fn cancel_order(
        &mut self,
        order_id: &OrderId,
        symbol: &str,
        now: DateTime<Utc>,
    ) -> Result<Vec<ExecutionEvent>, VenueError> {
        let venue_id = self.venue_order_ids.get(order_id).ok_or_else(|| {
            VenueError::Rejected(format!("unknown routed venue order {order_id}"))
        })?;
        self.connector
            .cancel(symbol, &uets::core::order::OrderId::new(venue_id.clone()))
            .map_err(|err| VenueError::Transport(err.to_string()))?;
        self.venue_order_ids.remove(order_id);
        Ok(vec![ExecutionEvent::OrderCancelled {
            order_id: order_id.clone(),
            at: now,
        }])
    }
}

===== FILE: components/pipelines/uets_trades/src/backend.rs =====
//! Execution backend trait — the interface between strategy decisions and
//! the selected execution mode (live_test / live_trade / playback).
//!
//! The strategy never talks to an exchange directly. It emits `TradeIntent`s
//! through this trait, and the backend decides how to handle them.

use crate::types::{
    Capital, ExecutionEvent, FillEvent, Order, OrderId, OrderRequest, OrderSide, TradeIntent,
};
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};

/// Error returned by an execution backend.
#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum BackendError {
    #[error("order rejected: {0}")]
    OrderRejected(String),
    #[error("insufficient margin: required {required}, available {available}")]
    InsufficientMargin { required: f64, available: f64 },
    #[error("backend not connected")]
    NotConnected,
    #[error("duplicate order id: {0}")]
    DuplicateOrderId(String),
    #[error("internal backend error: {0}")]
    Internal(String),
}

/// The execution backend trait. Each mode (live_test, live_trade, playback)
/// implements this trait to handle trade intents its own way.
pub trait ExecutionBackend: Send + Sync {
    /// Submit a trade intent. Returns the order id assigned by the backend.
    fn submit_intent(&mut self, intent: TradeIntent) -> Result<OrderId, BackendError>;

    /// Submit a full order request (with barrier, size, etc.).
    fn submit_order(&mut self, request: OrderRequest) -> Result<OrderId, BackendError>;

    /// Cancel an order by id.
    fn cancel_order(&mut self, order_id: &OrderId) -> Result<(), BackendError>;

    /// Retrieve the current state of a tracked order.
    fn order_state(&self, order_id: &OrderId) -> Option<&Order>;

    /// All orders tracked by this backend.
    fn orders(&self) -> Vec<&Order>;

    /// Drain execution events since the last call.
    fn drain_events(&mut self) -> Vec<ExecutionEvent>;

    /// Current capital snapshot for a strategy instance.
    fn capital(&self, strategy_id: &str) -> Option<Capital>;

    /// Apply a fill event to the backend's internal position tracking.
    fn apply_fill(&mut self, fill: &FillEvent);

    /// Mark-to-market update: latest prices plus the update timestamp
    /// (timestamps drive barrier checks and stale-data tracking).
    fn on_market_update(
        &mut self,
        marks: &std::collections::BTreeMap<String, f64>,
        now: DateTime<Utc>,
    );

    /// Check if the backend is connected and healthy.
    fn is_connected(&self) -> bool;

    /// Acceleration factor for playback (1.0 = real-time, 10.0 = 10x, etc.).
    /// Returns 1.0 for non-playback backends.
    fn acceleration(&self) -> f64 {
        1.0
    }

    /// Open quantity for (strategy, symbol), signed (positive = long).
    fn position_qty(&self, strategy_id: &str, symbol: &str) -> Option<f64>;

    // ---- Strategy action interface (consistent across all modes) ----
    //
    // The strategy calls `long`/`short`/`exit`; the selected backend
    // decides how each action is handled (simulated vs venue-routed).

    /// Go long: submits a market order with the attached barrier.
    fn long(&mut self, ctx: &ActionContext) -> Result<OrderId, BackendError> {
        let mut request = OrderRequest::new(
            ctx.order_id.clone(),
            ctx.symbol.clone(),
            OrderSide::Buy,
            ctx.qty,
            ctx.strategy_id.clone(),
            ctx.asset_id.clone(),
        );
        request.barrier = ctx.barrier.clone();
        self.submit_order(request)
    }

    /// Go short: submits a market order with the attached barrier.
    fn short(&mut self, ctx: &ActionContext) -> Result<OrderId, BackendError> {
        let mut request = OrderRequest::new(
            ctx.order_id.clone(),
            ctx.symbol.clone(),
            OrderSide::Sell,
            ctx.qty,
            ctx.strategy_id.clone(),
            ctx.asset_id.clone(),
        );
        request.barrier = ctx.barrier.clone();
        self.submit_order(request)
    }

    /// Exit the open position for (strategy, symbol): opposite side, full
    /// quantity, no barrier. Errors when there is nothing to exit.
    fn exit(&mut self, ctx: &ActionContext) -> Result<OrderId, BackendError> {
        let Some(qty) = self.position_qty(&ctx.strategy_id, &ctx.symbol) else {
            return Err(BackendError::Internal(format!(
                "no open position to exit for {}/{}",
                ctx.strategy_id, ctx.symbol
            )));
        };
        if qty == 0.0 {
            return Err(BackendError::Internal(format!(
                "no open position to exit for {}/{}",
                ctx.strategy_id, ctx.symbol
            )));
        }
        let side = if qty > 0.0 {
            OrderSide::Sell
        } else {
            OrderSide::Buy
        };
        let request = OrderRequest::new(
            ctx.order_id.clone(),
            ctx.symbol.clone(),
            side,
            qty.abs(),
            ctx.strategy_id.clone(),
            ctx.asset_id.clone(),
        );
        self.submit_order(request)
    }
}

/// Context for the `long`/`short`/`exit` strategy actions.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ActionContext {
    /// Caller-assigned order id (deduplication key).
    pub order_id: OrderId,
    /// Strategy instance identity, e.g. `ofmr@btcusdt`.
    pub strategy_id: String,
    /// Asset id, e.g. `btcusdt`.
    pub asset_id: String,
    /// Traded symbol, e.g. `BTCUSDT`.
    pub symbol: String,
    /// Order quantity (positive).
    pub qty: f64,
    /// Triple barrier attached to entries.
    pub barrier: Option<crate::triple_barrier::TripleBarrier>,
}

impl ActionContext {
    /// Entry/exit context with a fresh unique order id.
    pub fn new(
        strategy_id: impl Into<String>,
        asset_id: impl Into<String>,
        symbol: impl Into<String>,
        qty: f64,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            order_id: OrderId::new(format!("act-{}", uuid::Uuid::new_v4())),
            strategy_id: strategy_id.into(),
            asset_id: asset_id.into(),
            symbol: symbol.into(),
            qty,
            barrier: None,
        }
    }

    pub fn with_barrier(mut self, barrier: crate::triple_barrier::TripleBarrier) -> Self {
        self.barrier = Some(barrier);
        self
    }

    pub fn with_order_id(mut self, id: OrderId) -> Self {
        self.order_id = id;
        self
    }
}

/// Error returned when routing a live order through `uets_manager`.
#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum RouterError {
    /// Live routing is not configured (no venue connector). Never falls back
    /// to paper execution — the caller must configure live execution.
    #[error("live routing not configured through uets_manager")]
    NotConfigured,
    /// Duplicate order id — protects against double submissions.
    #[error("duplicate order id: {0}")]
    DuplicateOrderId(String),
    /// Market data is older than the allowed threshold.
    #[error("stale market data: last tick {age_ms} ms old (threshold {threshold_ms} ms)")]
    StaleMarketData { age_ms: u64, threshold_ms: u64 },
    /// The venue rejected the order.
    #[error("order rejected: {0}")]
    Rejected(String),
    /// Transport/rate-limit failure at the venue.
    #[error("venue error: {0}")]
    Transport(String),
    /// Locally invalid request (failed validation before any round trip).
    #[error("invalid request: {0}")]
    Invalid(String),
}

/// Port through which live orders flow to `uets_manager`.
///
/// Implemented by `uets_manager::ManagerRouter`. A live backend holds this
/// router and has **no other path to a venue** — every live order is
/// validated (duplicate ids, stale market data, request sanity) and routed
/// by `uets_manager`, satisfying the execution-ownership requirement.
pub trait LiveRouter: Send + Sync {
    /// Note the latest market-data timestamp (drives stale-data checks).
    fn note_market_tick(&mut self, ts: DateTime<Utc>);

    /// Validate and route one order request. Returns the execution events
    /// the venue produced (acceptance, fills) so the backend can update
    /// local order state.
    fn route(
        &mut self,
        request: &OrderRequest,
        now: DateTime<Utc>,
    ) -> Result<Vec<ExecutionEvent>, RouterError>;

    /// Cancel a previously routed order.
    fn cancel(&mut self, order_id: &OrderId, symbol: &str) -> Result<(), RouterError>;

    /// Async execution events since the last call (cancel acks, etc.).
    fn drain_events(&mut self) -> Vec<ExecutionEvent>;
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/uets_trades/src/calc/metrics.rs =====
//! Performance metrics — identical formulas across all execution modes.
//!
//! These are pure functions over an equity curve and trade list, so every
//! number on screen (or in a report) is recomputable from the log.

use crate::types::Capital;

/// One complete reporting snapshot, computed incrementally.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct PerformanceSnapshot {
    /// Strategy instance identity, e.g. `ofmr@btcusdt`.
    pub strategy_id: String,
    /// Asset ticker, e.g. `btcusdt`.
    pub asset_id: String,
    /// Snapshot timestamp.
    pub timestamp: chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>,
    /// Starting capital allocated to this instance.
    pub starting_capital: f64,
    /// Current cash balance.
    pub cash: f64,
    /// Current equity (cash + unrealized PnL).
    pub equity: f64,
    /// Unrealized PnL on open positions.
    pub unrealized_pnl: f64,
    /// Realized PnL from closed trades.
    pub realized_pnl: f64,
    /// Total fees paid.
    pub fees_paid: f64,
    /// `(equity - starting) / starting`.
    pub cumulative_return: f64,
    /// Absolute return in quote currency.
    pub absolute_return: f64,
    /// Largest peak-to-trough fall, as a fraction.
    pub max_drawdown: f64,
    /// Current drawdown from peak.
    pub current_drawdown: f64,
    /// Annualised Sortino ratio (downside deviation only).
    pub sortino: f64,
    /// Winning trades / all trades, in [0, 1].
    pub win_rate: f64,
    /// Mean realized R multiple over trades whose initial risk was recorded.
    pub expectancy_rr: f64,
    /// Total trade count.
    pub trades: usize,
    /// Winning trade count.
    pub wins: usize,
    /// Losing trade count.
    pub losses: f64,
    /// Gross exposure (sum of |position| * mark).
    pub gross_exposure: f64,
    /// Net exposure (sum of signed position * mark).
    pub net_exposure: f64,
    /// Margin used at current leverage.
    pub margin_used: f64,
    /// Available margin.
    pub available_margin: f64,
    /// Effective leverage.
    pub effective_leverage: f64,
}

/// Combine strategy-instance accounting for portfolio-level reporting.
/// Ratios that depend on a time series (Sortino and drawdown) are calculated
/// from the caller's aggregated equity curve, not averaged from instances.
pub fn aggregate_capital(capitals: &[Capital]) -> Option<Capital> {
    if capitals.is_empty() {
        return None;
    }
    let mut aggregate = Capital::new(0.0);
    for capital in capitals {
        aggregate.starting += capital.starting;
        aggregate.cash += capital.cash;
        aggregate.equity += capital.equity;
        aggregate.unrealized_pnl += capital.unrealized_pnl;
        aggregate.realized_pnl += capital.realized_pnl;
        aggregate.fees_paid += capital.fees_paid;
        aggregate.gross_exposure += capital.gross_exposure;
        aggregate.net_exposure += capital.net_exposure;
        aggregate.margin_used += capital.margin_used;
        aggregate.available_margin += capital.available_margin;
        aggregate.trade_count += capital.trade_count;
        aggregate.win_count += capital.win_count;
        aggregate.loss_count += capital.loss_count;
        aggregate.r_multiple_sum += capital.r_multiple_sum;
        aggregate.r_trades += capital.r_trades;
    }
    aggregate.effective_leverage = if aggregate.equity > 0.0 {
        aggregate.gross_exposure / aggregate.equity
    } else {
        0.0
    };
    Some(aggregate)
}

impl PerformanceSnapshot {
    pub fn from_capital(
        strategy_id: &str,
        asset_id: &str,
        timestamp: chrono::DateTime<chrono::Utc>,
        capital: &Capital,
        equity_curve: &[f64],
        periods_per_year: f64,
    ) -> Self {
        let starting = capital.starting;
        let equity = capital.equity;
        let cumulative_return = if starting > 0.0 {
            (equity - starting) / starting
        } else {
            0.0
        };
        let absolute_return = equity - starting;

        // Drawdown from equity curve
        let (max_dd, current_dd) = drawdown_stats(equity_curve);

        // Sortino from returns
        let returns = returns_from_equity(equity_curve);
        let sortino = sortino(&returns, 0.0, periods_per_year);

        let win_rate = if capital.trades() > 0 {
            capital.wins() as f64 / capital.trades() as f64
        } else {
            0.0
        };

        Self {
            strategy_id: strategy_id.to_string(),
            asset_id: asset_id.to_string(),
            timestamp,
            starting_capital: starting,
            cash: capital.cash,
            equity,
            unrealized_pnl: capital.unrealized_pnl,
            realized_pnl: capital.realized_pnl,
            fees_paid: capital.fees_paid,
            cumulative_return,
            absolute_return,
            max_drawdown: max_dd,
            current_drawdown: current_dd,
            sortino,
            win_rate,
            expectancy_rr: capital.expectancy_rr(),
            trades: capital.trades(),
            wins: capital.wins(),
            losses: capital.losses() as f64,
            gross_exposure: capital.gross_exposure,
            net_exposure: capital.net_exposure,
            margin_used: capital.margin_used,
            available_margin: capital.available_margin,
            effective_leverage: capital.effective_leverage,
        }
    }
}

/// Largest peak-to-trough fall and current drawdown from an equity curve.
pub fn drawdown_stats(equity: &[f64]) -> (f64, f64) {
    let mut peak = f64::NEG_INFINITY;
    let mut worst: f64 = 0.0;
    for &value in equity {
        if value > peak {
            peak = value;
        }
        if peak > 0.0 {
            let dd = (peak - value) / peak;
            if dd > worst {
                worst = dd;
            }
        }
    }
    let current = equity.last().copied().unwrap_or(0.0);
    let current_dd = if peak > 0.0 && current < peak {
        (peak - current) / peak
    } else {
        0.0
    };
    (worst, current_dd)
}

/// Period-over-period simple returns from an equity curve.
pub fn returns_from_equity(equity: &[f64]) -> Vec<f64> {
    equity
        .windows(2)
        .filter_map(|w| {
            if w[0] > 0.0 {
                Some((w[1] - w[0]) / w[0])
            } else {
                None
            }
        })
        .collect()
}

/// Annualised Sortino ratio.
pub fn sortino(returns: &[f64], target: f64, periods_per_year: f64) -> f64 {
    if returns.is_empty() {
        return 0.0;
    }
    let n = returns.len() as f64;
    let excess_sum: f64 = returns.iter().map(|r| r - target).sum();
    let mean = excess_sum / n;
    let downside_sq: f64 = returns
        .iter()
        .map(|r| {
            let e = r - target;
            if e < 0.0 { e * e } else { 0.0 }
        })
        .sum();
    let downside = (downside_sq / n).sqrt();
    if downside > 0.0 {
        let annualise = if periods_per_year > 0.0 {
            periods_per_year.sqrt()
        } else {
            1.0
        };
        (mean / downside) * annualise
    } else if mean > 0.0 {
        f64::INFINITY
    } else {
        0.0
    }
}

/// Win rate: wins / total trades.
pub fn win_rate(wins: usize, total: usize) -> f64 {
    if total == 0 {
        0.0
    } else {
        wins as f64 / total as f64
    }
}

/// Cumulative return: (ending - starting) / starting.
pub fn cumulative_return(starting: f64, ending: f64) -> f64 {
    if starting > 0.0 {
        (ending - starting) / starting
    } else {
        0.0
    }
}

/// Max drawdown from an equity curve alone.
pub fn max_drawdown(equity: &[f64]) -> f64 {
    let mut peak = f64::NEG_INFINITY;
    let mut worst: f64 = 0.0;
    for &value in equity {
        if value > peak {
            peak = value;
        }
        if peak > 0.0 {
            let dd = (peak - value) / peak;
            if dd > worst {
                worst = dd;
            }
        }
    }
    worst
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn drawdown_finds_worst_peak_to_trough() {
        let (mdd, _) = drawdown_stats(&[100.0, 120.0, 90.0, 110.0]);
        assert!((mdd - 0.25).abs() < 1e-12);
    }

    #[test]
    fn drawdown_monotonic_rise_is_zero() {
        let (mdd, _) = drawdown_stats(&[100.0, 110.0, 120.0]);
        assert_eq!(mdd, 0.0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn sortino_penalises_only_downside() {
        assert_eq!(sortino(&[0.1, -0.1], 0.0, 1.0), 0.0);
        assert_eq!(sortino(&[0.1, 0.1], 0.0, 1.0), f64::INFINITY);
        assert_eq!(sortino(&[], 0.0, 1.0), 0.0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn cumulative_return_is_fraction() {
        assert_eq!(cumulative_return(1_000.0, 1_250.0), 0.25);
        assert_eq!(cumulative_return(1_000.0, 750.0), -0.25);
    }

    #[test]
    fn win_rate_zero_when_no_trades() {
        assert_eq!(win_rate(0, 0), 0.0);
        assert!((win_rate(1, 3) - 1.0 / 3.0).abs() < 1e-12);
    }

    #[test]
    fn aggregate_capital_sums_portfolio_values_and_rr_denominators() {
        let mut first = Capital::new(1_000.0);
        first.cash = 950.0;
        first.equity = 1_050.0;
        first.r_multiple_sum = 2.0;
        first.r_trades = 2;
        let mut second = Capital::new(2_000.0);
        second.cash = 1_900.0;
        second.equity = 2_100.0;
        second.r_multiple_sum = 3.0;
        second.r_trades = 1;
        let total = aggregate_capital(&[first, second]).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(total.starting, 3_000.0);
        assert_eq!(total.cash, 2_850.0);
        assert_eq!(total.equity, 3_150.0);
        assert!((total.expectancy_rr() - 5.0 / 3.0).abs() < 1e-12);
    }
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/uets_trades/src/calc/mod.rs =====
//! Shared calculation layer — one implementation used by all three modes.
//!
//! All metrics (PnL, equity, drawdown, Sortino, win rate, etc.) are computed
//! here with identical formulas regardless of execution mode.

pub mod metrics;
pub mod reporter;

pub use metrics::PerformanceSnapshot;
pub use reporter::Reporter;


===== FILE: components/pipelines/uets_trades/src/calc/reporter.rs =====
//! 100 ms reporting cadence — a report update must not block strategy
//! execution or order reporting.

use crate::calc::metrics::{PerformanceSnapshot, aggregate_capital};
use crate::types::Capital;
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};

/// Reporting configuration.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ReportConfig {
    /// Reporting cadence (nominal 100 ms).
    pub interval_ms: u64,
    /// Periods per year for Sortino annualisation.
    pub periods_per_year: f64,
}

impl Default for ReportConfig {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            interval_ms: 100,
            periods_per_year: 1.0,
        }
    }
}

/// Incremental reporter — maintains live state and produces snapshots.
pub struct Reporter {
    config: ReportConfig,
    /// Last report timestamp.
    last_report: Option<DateTime<Utc>>,
    /// Equity curve samples (bounded).
    equity_curve: Vec<f64>,
}

impl Reporter {
    pub fn new(config: ReportConfig) -> Self {
        Self {
            config,
            last_report: None,
            equity_curve: Vec::new(),
        }
    }

    /// Check whether it's time to produce a report based on `now`.
    pub fn should_report(&self, now: DateTime<Utc>) -> bool {
        match self.last_report {
            None => true,
            Some(last) => (now - last).num_milliseconds() >= self.config.interval_ms as i64,
        }
    }

    /// Produce a report snapshot if the cadence has elapsed.
    /// Returns `None` if not yet time to report.
    pub fn maybe_report(
        &mut self,
        now: DateTime<Utc>,
        strategy_id: &str,
        asset_id: &str,
        capital: &Capital,
    ) -> Option<PerformanceSnapshot> {
        if !self.should_report(now) {
            return None;
        }

        self.equity_curve.push(capital.equity);
        // Keep the curve bounded (last 10_000 samples ~ 16 minutes at 100ms)
        if self.equity_curve.len() > 10_000 {
            self.equity_curve.drain(..self.equity_curve.len() - 10_000);
        }

        self.last_report = Some(now);

        Some(PerformanceSnapshot::from_capital(
            strategy_id,
            asset_id,
            now,
            capital,
            &self.equity_curve,
            self.config.periods_per_year,
        ))
    }

    /// Produce one portfolio-level report from all active instance books.
    /// The aggregate equity sample is used for portfolio Sortino and drawdown.
    pub fn maybe_report_aggregate(
        &mut self,
        now: DateTime<Utc>,
        capitals: &[Capital],
    ) -> Option<PerformanceSnapshot> {
        if !self.should_report(now) {
            return None;
        }
        let capital = aggregate_capital(capitals)?;
        self.push_equity(capital.equity);
        self.last_report = Some(now);
        Some(PerformanceSnapshot::from_capital(
            "portfolio",
            "all",
            now,
            &capital,
            &self.equity_curve,
            self.config.periods_per_year,
        ))
    }

    /// Force a report immediately (e.g. on session end).
    pub fn force_report(
        &mut self,
        now: DateTime<Utc>,
        strategy_id: &str,
        asset_id: &str,
        capital: &Capital,
    ) -> PerformanceSnapshot {
        self.equity_curve.push(capital.equity);
        if self.equity_curve.len() > 10_000 {
            self.equity_curve.drain(..self.equity_curve.len() - 10_000);
        }
        self.last_report = Some(now);

        PerformanceSnapshot::from_capital(
            strategy_id,
            asset_id,
            now,
            capital,
            &self.equity_curve,
            self.config.periods_per_year,
        )
    }

    pub fn force_report_aggregate(
        &mut self,
        now: DateTime<Utc>,
        capitals: &[Capital],
    ) -> Option<PerformanceSnapshot> {
        let capital = aggregate_capital(capitals)?;
        self.push_equity(capital.equity);
        self.last_report = Some(now);
        Some(PerformanceSnapshot::from_capital(
            "portfolio",
            "all",
            now,
            &capital,
            &self.equity_curve,
            self.config.periods_per_year,
        ))
    }

    fn push_equity(&mut self, equity: f64) {
        self.equity_curve.push(equity);
        if self.equity_curve.len() > 10_000 {
            self.equity_curve.drain(..self.equity_curve.len() - 10_000);
        }
    }

    pub fn equity_curve(&self) -> &[f64] {
        &self.equity_curve
    }
}

impl Default for Reporter {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new(ReportConfig::default())
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::types::Capital;
    use chrono::{TimeZone, Utc};

    fn sample_capital() -> Capital {
        Capital {
            starting: 10_000.0,
            cash: 10_000.0,
            equity: 10_500.0,
            unrealized_pnl: 500.0,
            realized_pnl: 0.0,
            fees_paid: 0.0,
            gross_exposure: 0.0,
            net_exposure: 0.0,
            margin_used: 0.0,
            available_margin: 10_500.0,
            effective_leverage: 0.0,
            trade_count: 0,
            win_count: 0,
            loss_count: 0,
            r_multiple_sum: 0.0,
            r_trades: 0,
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn report_respects_100ms_cadence() {
        let mut reporter = Reporter::new(ReportConfig {
            interval_ms: 100,
            periods_per_year: 1.0,
        });
        let base = Utc.with_ymd_and_hms(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0).unwrap();

        // First call should report
        let r1 = reporter.maybe_report(base, "ofmr@btcusdt", "btcusdt", &sample_capital());
        assert!(r1.is_some());

        // Immediate second call should not report (same timestamp)
        let r2 = reporter.maybe_report(base, "ofmr@btcusdt", "btcusdt", &sample_capital());
        assert!(r2.is_none());

        // After 100ms should report again
        let later = base + chrono::Duration::milliseconds(100);
        let r3 = reporter.maybe_report(later, "ofmr@btcusdt", "btcusdt", &sample_capital());
        assert!(r3.is_some());
    }

    #[test]
    fn force_report_ignores_cadence() {
        let mut reporter = Reporter::new(ReportConfig {
            interval_ms: 1000,
            periods_per_year: 1.0,
        });
        let base = Utc.with_ymd_and_hms(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0).unwrap();

        let r1 = reporter.maybe_report(base, "ofmr@btcusdt", "btcusdt", &sample_capital());
        assert!(r1.is_some());

        // Force report immediately after
        let r2 = reporter.force_report(base, "ofmr@btcusdt", "btcusdt", &sample_capital());
        assert!(r2.equity == 10_500.0);
    }
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/uets_trades/src/lib.rs =====
//! `uets_trades` — shared trading and execution library.
//!
//! This crate is the trading layer shared by all three execution modes
//! (live_test, live_trade, playback). It is independent of exchange-specific
//! execution logic: the execution backend lives in `components/pipelines/uets_manager`.
//!
//! ```text
//! uets_trades/
//! ├── calc/        # shared PnL, equity, capital, risk, performance
//! ├── live_test/   # paper trading against live market data
//! ├── live_trade/  # real trading via uets_manager
//! └── playback/    # accelerated historical replay
//!
//! Execution flow:
//!   market data → UETS features/decision → trade intent → uets_manager
//!   → execution backend → execution events → uets_trades/calc
//!

pub mod backend;
pub mod calc;
pub mod live_test;
pub mod live_trade;
pub mod playback;
pub mod sim;
pub mod triple_barrier;
pub mod types;

pub use backend::ExecutionBackend;
pub use calc::metrics::PerformanceSnapshot;
pub use calc::reporter::Reporter;
pub use triple_barrier::{BarrierExit, BarrierReason, TripleBarrier};
pub use types::{
    Capital, DecisionIntent, ExecutionEvent, FillEvent, Order, OrderRequest, OrderSide,
    OrderStatus, TradeIntent,
};


===== FILE: components/pipelines/uets_trades/src/live_test/mod.rs =====
//! Paper trading using live market data.
//!
//! Never submits real orders: fills are simulated by the shared
//! [`crate::sim::SimEngine`] with configurable spread, slippage and fees —
//! the same engine `playback` uses, so paper and replay produce identical
//! results for identical inputs.

use crate::backend::{BackendError, ExecutionBackend};
use crate::sim::{SimConfig, SimEngine};
use crate::types::{Capital, ExecutionEvent, FillEvent, Order, OrderId, OrderRequest, TradeIntent};
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

/// Paper trading backend — simulated execution against live market data.
pub struct LiveTestBackend {
    engine: SimEngine,
    connected: bool,
}

impl LiveTestBackend {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::with_config(SimConfig::default())
    }

    /// Paper backend with explicit execution assumptions.
    pub fn with_config(config: SimConfig) -> Self {
        Self {
            engine: SimEngine::new(config),
            connected: true,
        }
    }

    /// Paper backend with pre-allocated strategy capital.
    pub fn with_capital(strategy_id: impl Into<String>, starting: f64) -> Self {
        Self::with_config_capital(SimConfig::default(), strategy_id, starting)
    }

    pub fn with_config_capital(
        config: SimConfig,
        strategy_id: impl Into<String>,
        starting: f64,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            engine: SimEngine::with_capital(config, strategy_id, starting),
            connected: true,
        }
    }

    /// Shared read access to the accounting engine (reporting integration).
    pub fn engine(&self) -> &SimEngine {
        &self.engine
    }
}

impl Default for LiveTestBackend {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl ExecutionBackend for LiveTestBackend {
    fn submit_intent(&mut self, _intent: TradeIntent) -> Result<OrderId, BackendError> {
        Err(BackendError::Internal(
            "use long()/short()/exit() or submit_order with a full OrderRequest".into(),
        ))
    }

    fn submit_order(&mut self, request: OrderRequest) -> Result<OrderId, BackendError> {
        self.engine.submit(request, Utc::now())
    }

    fn cancel_order(&mut self, order_id: &OrderId) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
        self.engine.cancel(order_id, Utc::now())
    }

    fn order_state(&self, order_id: &OrderId) -> Option<&Order> {
        self.engine.order_state(order_id)
    }

    fn orders(&self) -> Vec<&Order> {
        self.engine.orders()
    }

    fn drain_events(&mut self) -> Vec<ExecutionEvent> {
        self.engine.drain_events()
    }

    fn capital(&self, strategy_id: &str) -> Option<Capital> {
        self.engine.capital(strategy_id)
    }

    /// Apply an **externally sourced** fill (fills drained from this backend
    /// were already applied internally).
    fn apply_fill(&mut self, fill: &FillEvent) {
        let (strategy, barrier) = match self.engine.order_state(&fill.order_id) {
            Some(order) => (
                order.request.strategy_id.clone(),
                order.request.barrier.clone(),
            ),
            None => (String::new(), None),
        };
        if let Err(err) = self.engine.book.apply_fill(
            &strategy,
            &fill.symbol,
            fill.side,
            fill.qty,
            fill.price,
            fill.fee,
            barrier.as_ref(),
        ) {
            tracing::error!(%err, order = %fill.order_id, "apply_fill failed");
        }
    }

    fn on_market_update(&mut self, marks: &BTreeMap<String, f64>, now: DateTime<Utc>) {
        self.engine.on_market_update(marks, now);
    }

    fn is_connected(&self) -> bool {
        self.connected
    }

    fn position_qty(&self, strategy_id: &str, symbol: &str) -> Option<f64> {
        self.engine.book.position_qty(strategy_id, symbol)
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::backend::ActionContext;
    use crate::types::{OrderSide, OrderStatus};

    fn marks(price: f64) -> BTreeMap<String, f64> {
        let mut m = BTreeMap::new();
        m.insert("BTCUSDT".to_string(), price);
        m
    }

    #[test]
    fn paper_backend_never_sends_real_orders() {
        let mut backend = LiveTestBackend::new();
        assert!(backend.is_connected());
        let _ = backend.submit_order(OrderRequest::new(
            OrderId::new("test-1"),
            "BTCUSDT",
            OrderSide::Buy,
            1.0,
            "ofmr@btcusdt",
            "btcusdt",
        ));
        let events = backend.drain_events();
        assert_eq!(events.len(), 1);
        // Only an acceptance event, no fill (no mark known yet).
        assert!(matches!(events[0], ExecutionEvent::OrderAccepted { .. }));
    }

    #[test]
    fn market_order_fills_at_mark_with_slippage_and_fees() {
        let mut backend = LiveTestBackend::new();
        backend.on_market_update(&marks(100.0), Utc::now());
        let ctx = ActionContext::new("ofmr@btcusdt", "btcusdt", "BTCUSDT", 1.0);
        backend.long(&ctx).unwrap();

        let capital = backend.capital("ofmr@btcusdt").unwrap();
        // Buy executes at mark + (spread/2 + slippage) = +1.5 bps.
        let px = 100.0 * 1.00015;
        let fee = 0.0004 * px;
        assert!((capital.cash - (10_000.0 - px - fee)).abs() < 1e-9);
        assert!(capital.fees_paid > 0.0);
        assert!(capital.gross_exposure > 0.0);

        let events = backend.drain_events();
        assert!(
            events
                .iter()
                .any(|e| matches!(e, ExecutionEvent::OrderFilled { .. }))
        );
        assert!(events.iter().any(|e| matches!(e, ExecutionEvent::Fill(_))));
    }

    #[test]
    fn partial_fill_ratio_completes_on_next_update() {
        let mut config = SimConfig::default();
        config.partial_fill_ratio = Some(0.5);
        let mut backend = LiveTestBackend::with_config(config);
        backend.on_market_update(&marks(100.0), Utc::now());
        let ctx = ActionContext::new("ofmr@btcusdt", "btcusdt", "BTCUSDT", 1.0);
        let id = backend.long(&ctx).unwrap();

        assert_eq!(backend.order_state(&id).unwrap().filled_qty, 0.5);
        let events = backend.drain_events();
        assert!(
            events
                .iter()
                .any(|e| matches!(e, ExecutionEvent::OrderPartiallyFilled { .. }))
        );

        backend.on_market_update(&marks(100.0), Utc::now());
        assert_eq!(
            backend.order_state(&id).unwrap().status,
            OrderStatus::Filled
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn cancel_before_fill_marks_cancelled_and_prevents_fill() {
        let mut backend = LiveTestBackend::new();
        let ctx = ActionContext::new("ofmr@btcusdt", "btcusdt", "BTCUSDT", 1.0);
        // No mark yet: order waits.
        let id = backend.long(&ctx).unwrap();
        backend.cancel_order(&id).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            backend.order_state(&id).unwrap().status,
            OrderStatus::Cancelled
        );

        // A later mark must not fill the cancelled order.
        backend.on_market_update(&marks(100.0), Utc::now());
        assert_eq!(backend.order_state(&id).unwrap().filled_qty, 0.0);
        let capital = backend.capital("ofmr@btcusdt").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(capital.cash, 10_000.0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn insufficient_cash_rejects_with_state_update() {
        let mut backend = LiveTestBackend::with_capital("ofmr@btcusdt", 50.0);
        backend.on_market_update(&marks(100.0), Utc::now());
        let ctx = ActionContext::new("ofmr@btcusdt", "btcusdt", "BTCUSDT", 1.0);
        assert!(
            backend.long(&ctx).is_err(),
            "1 BTC at ~100 must exceed 50 cash"
        );

        let events = backend.drain_events();
        assert!(
            events
                .iter()
                .any(|e| matches!(e, ExecutionEvent::OrderRejected { .. }))
        );
        let order = backend.orders();
        assert_eq!(order[0].status, OrderStatus::Rejected);
    }

    #[test]
    fn exit_action_closes_the_open_position() {
        let mut backend = LiveTestBackend::new();
        backend.on_market_update(&marks(100.0), Utc::now());
        let ctx = ActionContext::new("ofmr@btcusdt", "btcusdt", "BTCUSDT", 1.0);
        backend.long(&ctx).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(backend.position_qty("ofmr@btcusdt", "BTCUSDT"), Some(1.0));

        let exit_ctx = ActionContext::new("ofmr@btcusdt", "btcusdt", "BTCUSDT", 1.0);
        backend.exit(&exit_ctx).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            backend.position_qty("ofmr@btcusdt", "BTCUSDT"),
            None,
            "closed position is removed from the book"
        );
        let capital = backend.capital("ofmr@btcusdt").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(capital.trade_count, 1, "round trip counts as one trade");
    }

    #[test]
    fn paper_and_playback_share_identical_accounting() {
        use crate::playback::PlaybackBackend;

        let config = SimConfig::default();
        let mut paper =
            LiveTestBackend::with_config_capital(config.clone(), "ofmr@btcusdt", 10_000.0);
        let mut playback =
            PlaybackBackend::with_config_capital(config, "ofmr@btcusdt", 10_000.0, 1.0);
        let start = Utc::now();
        let initial_marks = marks(100.0);
        paper.on_market_update(&initial_marks, start);
        playback.on_market_update(&initial_marks, start);
        let entry = OrderRequest::new(
            "entry",
            "BTCUSDT",
            OrderSide::Buy,
            1.0,
            "ofmr@btcusdt",
            "btcusdt",
        );
        paper.submit_order(entry.clone()).unwrap();
        playback.submit_order(entry).unwrap();
        let exit_marks = marks(101.0);
        paper.on_market_update(&exit_marks, start + chrono::Duration::seconds(1));
        playback.on_market_update(&exit_marks, start + chrono::Duration::seconds(1));
        let exit = OrderRequest::new(
            "exit",
            "BTCUSDT",
            OrderSide::Sell,
            1.0,
            "ofmr@btcusdt",
            "btcusdt",
        );
        paper.submit_order(exit.clone()).unwrap();
        playback.submit_order(exit).unwrap();

        let paper_capital = paper.capital("ofmr@btcusdt").unwrap();
        let playback_capital = playback.capital("ofmr@btcusdt").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(paper_capital.trade_count, playback_capital.trade_count);
        assert_eq!(paper_capital.win_count, playback_capital.win_count);
        assert!((paper_capital.cash - playback_capital.cash).abs() < 1e-9);
        assert!((paper_capital.equity - playback_capital.equity).abs() < 1e-9);
        assert!((paper_capital.realized_pnl - playback_capital.realized_pnl).abs() < 1e-9);
        assert!((paper_capital.fees_paid - playback_capital.fees_paid).abs() < 1e-9);
        assert_eq!(
            paper.position_qty("ofmr@btcusdt", "BTCUSDT"),
            playback.position_qty("ofmr@btcusdt", "BTCUSDT")
        );
    }
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/uets_trades/src/live_trade/mod.rs =====
//! Real trading using live market data and broker/exchange execution.
//!
//! Every order is routed through a [`crate::backend::LiveRouter`] — the port
//! implemented by `uets_manager::ManagerRouter`, which owns the venue
//! connector and all validation (duplicate ids, stale market data, request
//! sanity). This backend has **no other path to a venue** and never falls
//! back to paper execution: without a router, every submission fails with
//! [`BackendError::NotConnected`].

use crate::backend::{BackendError, ExecutionBackend, LiveRouter, RouterError};
use crate::sim::Book;
use crate::types::{Capital, ExecutionEvent, FillEvent, Order, OrderId, OrderRequest, TradeIntent};
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

/// Live trading backend — routes through `uets_manager` to a real broker.
pub struct LiveTradeBackend {
    router: Option<Box<dyn LiveRouter>>,
    orders: BTreeMap<OrderId, Order>,
    events: Vec<ExecutionEvent>,
    book: Book,
    starting_capital: f64,
    barrier_exits_pending: BTreeSet<(String, String)>,
    barrier_order_seq: u64,
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::backend::{LiveRouter, RouterError};
    use crate::triple_barrier::TripleBarrier;
    use crate::types::{ExecutionEvent, OrderSide};
    use chrono::TimeZone;
    use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};

    struct RecordingRouter(Arc<Mutex<Vec<OrderRequest>>>);
    impl LiveRouter for RecordingRouter {
        fn note_market_tick(&mut self, _ts: DateTime<Utc>) {}
        fn route(
            &mut self,
            request: &OrderRequest,
            now: DateTime<Utc>,
        ) -> Result<Vec<ExecutionEvent>, RouterError> {
            self.0.lock().unwrap().push(request.clone());
            Ok(vec![ExecutionEvent::OrderAccepted {
                order_id: request.id.clone(),
                symbol: request.symbol.clone(),
                at: now,
            }])
        }
        fn cancel(&mut self, _id: &OrderId, _symbol: &str) -> Result<(), RouterError> {
            Ok(())
        }
        fn drain_events(&mut self) -> Vec<ExecutionEvent> {
            Vec::new()
        }
    }

    struct UncertainRouter;
    impl LiveRouter for UncertainRouter {
        fn note_market_tick(&mut self, _ts: DateTime<Utc>) {}
        fn route(
            &mut self,
            _request: &OrderRequest,
            _now: DateTime<Utc>,
        ) -> Result<Vec<ExecutionEvent>, RouterError> {
            Err(RouterError::Transport(
                "connection lost after submit".into(),
            ))
        }
        fn cancel(&mut self, _id: &OrderId, _symbol: &str) -> Result<(), RouterError> {
            Err(RouterError::Transport("not reachable".into()))
        }
        fn drain_events(&mut self) -> Vec<ExecutionEvent> {
            Vec::new()
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn live_triple_barrier_routes_exit_through_live_router() {
        let routed = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new()));
        let mut backend = LiveTradeBackend::with_router(Box::new(RecordingRouter(routed.clone())));
        let entered_at = Utc.with_ymd_and_hms(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0).unwrap();
        let mut marks = BTreeMap::new();
        marks.insert("BTCUSDT".to_string(), 100.0);
        backend.on_market_update(&marks, entered_at);
        let barrier = TripleBarrier::long(100.0, entered_at, 5.0, 5.0, 300);
        let request = OrderRequest::new(
            "entry",
            "BTCUSDT",
            OrderSide::Buy,
            1.0,
            "ofmr@btcusdt",
            "btcusdt",
        )
        .with_barrier(barrier);
        let order_id = backend.submit_order(request).unwrap();
        backend.apply_fill(&FillEvent {
            order_id,
            symbol: "BTCUSDT".into(),
            side: OrderSide::Buy,
            qty: 1.0,
            price: 100.0,
            fee: 0.04,
            executed_at: entered_at,
        });

        marks.insert("BTCUSDT".to_string(), 100.06);
        backend.on_market_update(&marks, entered_at + chrono::Duration::seconds(1));

        let routes = routed.lock().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(routes.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(routes[1].side, OrderSide::Sell);
