        assert_eq!(routes[1].qty, 1.0);
        assert!(routes[1].barrier.is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn lost_venue_response_is_tracked_as_uncertain_not_rejected() {
        let mut backend = LiveTradeBackend::with_router(Box::new(UncertainRouter));
        let request = OrderRequest::new(
            "uncertain",
            "BTCUSDT",
            OrderSide::Buy,
            1.0,
            "ofmr@btcusdt",
            "btcusdt",
        );
        assert!(backend.submit_order(request).is_err());
        assert_eq!(
            backend
                .order_state(&OrderId::new("uncertain"))
                .unwrap()
                .status,
            crate::types::OrderStatus::Uncertain
        );
        assert!(
            backend
                .drain_events()
                .iter()
                .any(|event| matches!(event, ExecutionEvent::OrderUncertain { .. }))
        );
    }
}

impl LiveTradeBackend {
    /// A live backend **without** a router: every order is refused
    /// (explicit `NotConnected` — never a silent paper fallback).
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            router: None,
            orders: BTreeMap::new(),
            events: Vec::new(),
            book: Book::new(),
            starting_capital: 10_000.0,
            barrier_exits_pending: BTreeSet::new(),
            barrier_order_seq: 0,
        }
    }

    /// A live backend wired to `uets_manager` (required for live trading).
    pub fn with_router(router: Box<dyn LiveRouter>) -> Self {
        Self {
            router: Some(router),
            orders: BTreeMap::new(),
            events: Vec::new(),
            book: Book::new(),
            starting_capital: 10_000.0,
            barrier_exits_pending: BTreeSet::new(),
            barrier_order_seq: 0,
        }
    }

    /// Pre-allocate capital for a strategy instance.
    pub fn with_capital(mut self, strategy_id: impl Into<String>, starting: f64) -> Self {
        self.book.set_capital(strategy_id, starting);
        self
    }

    /// Mutable access to the router (market-tick notes from the pipeline).
    pub fn router_mut(&mut self) -> Option<&mut dyn LiveRouter> {
        self.router
            .as_mut()
            .map(|r| r.as_mut() as &mut dyn LiveRouter)
    }

    /// Apply one venue execution event to local order state.
    fn apply_event(&mut self, event: &ExecutionEvent, now: DateTime<Utc>) {
        match event {
            ExecutionEvent::OrderAccepted { order_id, .. } => {
                if let Some(order) = self.orders.get_mut(order_id) {
                    order.status = crate::types::OrderStatus::Accepted;
                    order.updated_at = now;
                }
            }
            ExecutionEvent::OrderPartiallyFilled {
                order_id,
                filled_qty,
                fill_price,
                fee,
                at,
            } => {
                if let Some(order) = self.orders.get_mut(order_id) {
                    order.record_fill(*filled_qty, *fill_price, *fee, *at);
                }
            }
            ExecutionEvent::OrderFilled {
                order_id,
                filled_qty,
                fill_price,
                fee,
                at,
            } => {
                if let Some(order) = self.orders.get_mut(order_id) {
                    order.record_fill(*filled_qty, *fill_price, *fee, *at);
                }
            }
            ExecutionEvent::OrderRejected { order_id, at, .. } => {
                if let Some(order) = self.orders.get_mut(order_id) {
                    order.status = crate::types::OrderStatus::Rejected;
                    order.updated_at = *at;
                }
            }
            ExecutionEvent::OrderCancelled { order_id, at } => {
                if let Some(order) = self.orders.get_mut(order_id) {
                    order.status = crate::types::OrderStatus::Cancelled;
                    order.updated_at = *at;
                }
            }
            ExecutionEvent::OrderUncertain { order_id, at, .. } => {
                if let Some(order) = self.orders.get_mut(order_id) {
                    order.status = crate::types::OrderStatus::Uncertain;
                    order.updated_at = *at;
                }
            }
            ExecutionEvent::Fill(fill) => {
                if let Some(order) = self.orders.get_mut(&fill.order_id) {
                    order.record_fill(fill.qty, fill.price, fill.fee, fill.executed_at);
                }
                self.apply_fill(fill);
            }
        }
    }
}

impl Default for LiveTradeBackend {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl ExecutionBackend for LiveTradeBackend {
    fn submit_intent(&mut self, _intent: TradeIntent) -> Result<OrderId, BackendError> {
        Err(BackendError::Internal(
            "use long()/short()/exit() or submit_order with a full OrderRequest".into(),
        ))
    }

    /// Route the order through `uets_manager` (the only live path).
    fn submit_order(&mut self, request: OrderRequest) -> Result<OrderId, BackendError> {
        let now = Utc::now();
        let Some(router) = self.router.as_mut() else {
            // Explicit failure: no router means no venue access. There is
            // no paper fallback anywhere in this backend.
            return Err(BackendError::NotConnected);
        };
        if self.orders.contains_key(&request.id) {
            return Err(BackendError::DuplicateOrderId(request.id.0.clone()));
        }
        self.book
            .ensure_capital(&request.strategy_id, self.starting_capital);

        let id = request.id.clone();
        let mut order = Order::new(id.clone(), request.clone(), now);
        order.status = crate::types::OrderStatus::Submitted;
        self.orders.insert(id.clone(), order);

        match router.route(&request, now) {
            Ok(events) => {
                for event in &events {
                    self.apply_event(event, now);
                }
                self.events.extend(events);
                Ok(id)
            }
            Err(err) => {
                if let Some(order) = self.orders.get_mut(&id) {
                    order.status = if matches!(&err, RouterError::Transport(_)) {
                        crate::types::OrderStatus::Uncertain
                    } else {
                        crate::types::OrderStatus::Rejected
                    };
                    order.updated_at = now;
                }
                if matches!(&err, RouterError::Transport(_)) {
                    self.events.push(ExecutionEvent::OrderUncertain {
                        order_id: id.clone(),
                        reason: err.to_string(),
                        at: now,
                    });
                    Err(BackendError::Internal(format!(
                        "execution state uncertain for {id}: {err}"
                    )))
                } else {
                    self.events.push(ExecutionEvent::OrderRejected {
                        order_id: id.clone(),
                        reason: err.to_string(),
                        at: now,
                    });
                    Err(BackendError::OrderRejected(err.to_string()))
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fn cancel_order(&mut self, order_id: &OrderId) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
        let now = Utc::now();
        let Some(router) = self.router.as_mut() else {
            return Err(BackendError::NotConnected);
        };
        let Some(order) = self.orders.get(order_id) else {
            return Err(BackendError::Internal(format!("unknown order {order_id}")));
        };
        if order.status.is_terminal() {
            return Err(BackendError::Internal(format!(
                "order {order_id} already {:?}",
                order.status
            )));
        }
        let symbol = order.request.symbol.clone();
        router.cancel(order_id, &symbol).map_err(|e| match e {
            RouterError::NotConfigured => BackendError::NotConnected,
            RouterError::DuplicateOrderId(id) => BackendError::DuplicateOrderId(id),
            other => BackendError::OrderRejected(other.to_string()),
        })?;
        if let Some(order) = self.orders.get_mut(order_id) {
            order.status = crate::types::OrderStatus::Cancelled;
            order.updated_at = now;
        }
        self.events.push(ExecutionEvent::OrderCancelled {
            order_id: order_id.clone(),
            at: now,
        });
        Ok(())
    }

    fn order_state(&self, order_id: &OrderId) -> Option<&Order> {
        self.orders.get(order_id)
    }

    fn orders(&self) -> Vec<&Order> {
        self.orders.values().collect()
    }

    fn drain_events(&mut self) -> Vec<ExecutionEvent> {
        let mut drained = std::mem::take(&mut self.events);
        if let Some(router) = self.router.as_mut() {
            drained.extend(router.drain_events());
        }
        drained
    }

    fn capital(&self, strategy_id: &str) -> Option<Capital> {
        self.book.capital(strategy_id)
    }

    /// Apply an **externally sourced** fill (fills drained from this backend
    /// were already applied internally by `apply_event`).
    fn apply_fill(&mut self, fill: &FillEvent) {
        let (strategy, barrier) = match self.orders.get(&fill.order_id) {
            Some(order) => (
                order.request.strategy_id.clone(),
                order.request.barrier.clone(),
            ),
            None => (String::new(), None),
        };
        if let Err(err) = self.book.apply_fill(
            &strategy,
            &fill.symbol,
            fill.side,
            fill.qty,
            fill.price,
            fill.fee,
            barrier.as_ref(),
        ) {
            tracing::error!(%err, order = %fill.order_id, "apply_fill failed");
        }
    }

    fn on_market_update(&mut self, marks: &BTreeMap<String, f64>, now: DateTime<Utc>) {
        self.book.set_marks(marks);
        self.book.mark_to_market();
        // Feed the router so it can enforce stale-market-data checks.
        if let Some(router) = self.router.as_mut() {
            router.note_market_tick(now);
        }

        let active_positions: BTreeSet<(String, String)> = self
            .book
            .barrier_positions()
            .into_iter()
            .map(|(strategy, symbol, _, _, _)| (strategy, symbol))
            .collect();
        self.barrier_exits_pending
            .retain(|key| active_positions.contains(key));
        let triggered: Vec<_> = self
            .book
            .barrier_positions()
            .into_iter()
            .filter_map(|(strategy, symbol, qty, is_long, barrier)| {
                let mark = marks.get(&symbol).copied()?;
                let check = barrier.check(mark, now);
                (check.should_exit
                    && !self
                        .barrier_exits_pending
                        .contains(&(strategy.clone(), symbol.clone())))
                .then_some((strategy, symbol, qty, is_long, check.exit_price))
            })
            .collect();

        for (strategy_id, symbol, qty, is_long, _exit_price) in triggered {
            self.barrier_order_seq += 1;
            let key = (strategy_id.clone(), symbol.clone());
            let side = if is_long {
                crate::types::OrderSide::Sell
            } else {
                crate::types::OrderSide::Buy
            };
            let request = OrderRequest::new(
                OrderId::new(format!(
                    "live-barrier-{}-{}",
                    self.barrier_order_seq,
                    symbol.to_ascii_lowercase()
                )),
                symbol.clone(),
                side,
                qty.abs(),
                strategy_id,
                symbol.to_ascii_lowercase(),
            );
            match self.submit_order(request) {
                Ok(_) => {
                    self.barrier_exits_pending.insert(key);
                }
                Err(err) => {
                    tracing::error!(%err, symbol, "live triple-barrier exit routing failed; position remains open")
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fn is_connected(&self) -> bool {
        self.router.is_some()
    }

    fn position_qty(&self, strategy_id: &str, symbol: &str) -> Option<f64> {
        self.book.position_qty(strategy_id, symbol)
    }
}

// __END__


===== FILE: components/pipelines/uets_trades/src/playback/mod.rs =====
//! Offline backtesting using historical or synthetic data.
//!
//! Replays market events through the same deterministic [`crate::sim::SimEngine`]
//! that paper trading uses, with a configurable acceleration factor (the
//! replay driver feeds marks at accelerated pace). Never submits real orders.

use crate::backend::{BackendError, ExecutionBackend};
use crate::sim::{SimConfig, SimEngine};
use crate::types::{Capital, ExecutionEvent, FillEvent, Order, OrderId, OrderRequest, TradeIntent};
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

/// One ordered item in a deterministic market-data replay.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct MarketEvent {
    pub timestamp: DateTime<Utc>,
    pub marks: BTreeMap<String, f64>,
}

/// Playback backend — simulated execution against replayed events.
pub struct PlaybackBackend {
    engine: SimEngine,
    acceleration: f64,
    connected: bool,
}

impl PlaybackBackend {
    /// `acceleration`: replay speed multiplier (1.0 = real time, 10 = 10x).
    pub fn new(acceleration: f64) -> Self {
        Self::with_config(SimConfig::default(), acceleration)
    }

    pub fn with_config(config: SimConfig, acceleration: f64) -> Self {
        Self {
            engine: SimEngine::new(config),
            acceleration,
            connected: true,
        }
    }

    pub fn with_capital(strategy_id: impl Into<String>, starting: f64, acceleration: f64) -> Self {
        Self::with_config_capital(SimConfig::default(), strategy_id, starting, acceleration)
    }

    pub fn with_config_capital(
        config: SimConfig,
        strategy_id: impl Into<String>,
        starting: f64,
        acceleration: f64,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            engine: SimEngine::with_capital(config, strategy_id, starting),
            acceleration,
            connected: true,
        }
    }

    /// Shared read access to the accounting engine (reporting integration).
    pub fn engine(&self) -> &SimEngine {
        &self.engine
    }

    /// Replay every input event in order, applying its marks before invoking
    /// strategy processing. Events are paced by the configured acceleration;
    /// no event is skipped to meet a speed target.
    pub fn replay_events<F>(
        &mut self,
        events: impl IntoIterator<Item = MarketEvent>,
        mut process: F,
    ) -> Result<usize, BackendError>
    where
        F: FnMut(&mut Self, &MarketEvent) -> Result<(), BackendError>,
    {
        if !self.acceleration.is_finite() || self.acceleration <= 0.0 {
            return Err(BackendError::Internal(
                "playback acceleration must be finite and positive".into(),
            ));
        }
        let mut count = 0usize;
        let mut first_timestamp = None;
        let mut previous_timestamp = None;
        let wall_start = Instant::now();
        for event in events {
            if previous_timestamp.is_some_and(|previous| event.timestamp < previous) {
                return Err(BackendError::Internal(
                    "playback events must be timestamp ordered".into(),
                ));
            }
            let first = *first_timestamp.get_or_insert(event.timestamp);
            let elapsed_ms = event
                .timestamp
                .signed_duration_since(first)
                .num_milliseconds()
                .max(0) as u64;
            let target_ms = (elapsed_ms as f64 / self.acceleration).min(u64::MAX as f64) as u64;
            let wait = Duration::from_millis(target_ms).saturating_sub(wall_start.elapsed());
            if !wait.is_zero() {
                std::thread::sleep(wait);
            }
            self.on_market_update(&event.marks, event.timestamp);
            process(self, &event)?;
            previous_timestamp = Some(event.timestamp);
            count += 1;
        }
        Ok(count)
    }
}

impl Default for PlaybackBackend {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new(1.0)
    }
}

impl ExecutionBackend for PlaybackBackend {
    fn submit_intent(&mut self, _intent: TradeIntent) -> Result<OrderId, BackendError> {
        Err(BackendError::Internal(
            "use long()/short()/exit() or submit_order with a full OrderRequest".into(),
        ))
    }

    fn submit_order(&mut self, request: OrderRequest) -> Result<OrderId, BackendError> {
        self.engine.submit(request, Utc::now())
    }

    fn cancel_order(&mut self, order_id: &OrderId) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
        self.engine.cancel(order_id, Utc::now())
    }

    fn order_state(&self, order_id: &OrderId) -> Option<&Order> {
        self.engine.order_state(order_id)
    }

    fn orders(&self) -> Vec<&Order> {
        self.engine.orders()
    }

    fn drain_events(&mut self) -> Vec<ExecutionEvent> {
        self.engine.drain_events()
    }

    fn capital(&self, strategy_id: &str) -> Option<Capital> {
        self.engine.capital(strategy_id)
    }

    /// Apply an **externally sourced** fill (fills drained from this backend
    /// were already applied internally).
    fn apply_fill(&mut self, fill: &FillEvent) {
        let (strategy, barrier) = match self.engine.order_state(&fill.order_id) {
            Some(order) => (
                order.request.strategy_id.clone(),
                order.request.barrier.clone(),
            ),
            None => (String::new(), None),
        };
        if let Err(err) = self.engine.book.apply_fill(
            &strategy,
            &fill.symbol,
            fill.side,
            fill.qty,
            fill.price,
            fill.fee,
            barrier.as_ref(),
        ) {
            tracing::error!(%err, order = %fill.order_id, "apply_fill failed");
        }
    }

    fn on_market_update(&mut self, marks: &BTreeMap<String, f64>, now: DateTime<Utc>) {
        self.engine.on_market_update(marks, now);
    }

    fn is_connected(&self) -> bool {
        self.connected
    }

    fn acceleration(&self) -> f64 {
        self.acceleration
    }

    fn position_qty(&self, strategy_id: &str, symbol: &str) -> Option<f64> {
        self.engine.book.position_qty(strategy_id, symbol)
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::backend::ActionContext;
    use crate::triple_barrier::TripleBarrier;
    use crate::types::{OrderSide, OrderStatus};
    use chrono::TimeZone;

    fn marks(price: f64) -> BTreeMap<String, f64> {
        let mut m = BTreeMap::new();
        m.insert("BTCUSDT".to_string(), price);
        m
    }

    #[test]
    fn playback_never_sends_real_orders() {
        let mut backend = PlaybackBackend::new(10.0);
        assert!(backend.is_connected());
        let _ = backend.submit_order(OrderRequest::new(
            OrderId::new("test-1"),
            "BTCUSDT",
            OrderSide::Buy,
            1.0,
            "ofmr@btcusdt",
            "btcusdt",
        ));
        let events = backend.drain_events();
        assert_eq!(events.len(), 1);
        // Only simulated acceptance, no real fill.
        assert!(matches!(events[0], ExecutionEvent::OrderAccepted { .. }));
    }

    #[test]
    fn acceleration_factor_is_exposed() {
        let backend = PlaybackBackend::new(25.0);
        assert_eq!(backend.acceleration(), 25.0);
        let realtime = PlaybackBackend::default();
        assert_eq!(ExecutionBackend::acceleration(&realtime), 1.0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn barrier_take_profit_produces_exit_trade() {
        let mut backend = PlaybackBackend::new(10.0);
        let base = Utc.with_ymd_and_hms(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0).unwrap();

        // Entry with a 5 bps TP / 5 bps SL / 300 s vertical barrier.
        let barrier = TripleBarrier::long(100.0, base, 5.0, 5.0, 300);
        let ctx =
            ActionContext::new("ofmr@btcusdt", "btcusdt", "BTCUSDT", 1.0).with_barrier(barrier);
        backend.on_market_update(&marks(100.0), base);
        backend.long(&ctx).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(backend.position_qty("ofmr@btcusdt", "BTCUSDT"), Some(1.0));

        // Mark crosses the take-profit barrier.
        let later = base + chrono::Duration::seconds(5);
        backend.on_market_update(&marks(100.6), later);

        assert_eq!(
            backend.position_qty("ofmr@btcusdt", "BTCUSDT"),
            None,
            "TP exit must close the position"
        );
        let capital = backend.capital("ofmr@btcusdt").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(capital.trade_count, 1);
        assert_eq!(capital.win_count, 1);
        assert_eq!(capital.r_trades, 1, "barrier risk makes R known");
        assert!(
            capital.expectancy_rr() > 0.9,
            "TP at ~+6 bps over 5 bps risk ≈ 1.1R, got {}",
            capital.expectancy_rr()
        );

        let events = backend.drain_events();
        let exit_fills = events
            .iter()
            .filter(
                |e| matches!(e, ExecutionEvent::Fill(f) if f.order_id.0.starts_with("barrier-")),
            )
            .count();
        assert_eq!(exit_fills, 1, "barrier exit emits exactly one fill");
    }

    #[test]
    fn barrier_vertical_exit_fires_on_time_without_price_move() {
        let mut backend = PlaybackBackend::new(10.0);
        let base = Utc.with_ymd_and_hms(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0).unwrap();
        let barrier = TripleBarrier::long(100.0, base, 5.0, 5.0, 10);
        let ctx =
            ActionContext::new("ofmr@btcusdt", "btcusdt", "BTCUSDT", 1.0).with_barrier(barrier);
        backend.on_market_update(&marks(100.0), base);
        backend.long(&ctx).unwrap();

        // 11 seconds later, price unchanged: vertical barrier fires.
        let late = base + chrono::Duration::seconds(11);
        backend.on_market_update(&marks(100.0), late);
        assert_eq!(backend.position_qty("ofmr@btcusdt", "BTCUSDT"), None);
        let orders = backend.orders();
        let exit = orders
            .iter()
            .find(|o| o.id.0.starts_with("barrier-"))
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(exit.status, OrderStatus::Filled);
    }

    #[test]
    fn accelerated_replay_preserves_every_event_and_rejects_time_reversal() {
        use super::MarketEvent;
        let start = Utc.with_ymd_and_hms(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0).unwrap();
        let events = [0, 1, 2].map(|second| MarketEvent {
            timestamp: start + chrono::Duration::seconds(second),
            marks: marks(100.0 + second as f64),
        });
        let mut backend = PlaybackBackend::new(100.0);
        let mut seen = Vec::new();
        let count = backend
            .replay_events(events.clone(), |_, event| {
                seen.push(event.timestamp);
                Ok(())
            })
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(count, 3);
        assert_eq!(
            seen,
            events
                .iter()
                .map(|event| event.timestamp)
                .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        );
        assert_eq!(backend.engine().book.mark("BTCUSDT"), Some(102.0));

        let reversed = [events[1].clone(), events[0].clone()];
        assert!(backend.replay_events(reversed, |_, _| Ok(())).is_err());
    }
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/uets_trades/src/sim.rs =====
//! Shared simulated execution — one implementation of fill mechanics,
//! slippage, fees, positions and barriers for **both** `live_test` (paper)
//! and `playback` (replay), so simulated results are identical across modes.
//!
//! Accounting lives in [`Book`] (also used by `live_trade`), which means
//! portfolio math — cash, equity, exposure, realized/unrealized PnL, wins,
//! losses and R-multiples — is the same code in every execution mode.

use crate::backend::BackendError;
use crate::triple_barrier::TripleBarrier;
use crate::types::{
    Capital, ExecutionEvent, FillEvent, Order, OrderId, OrderRequest, OrderSide, OrderStatus,
    OrderType,
};
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

/// Tolerance for position "flat" comparisons.
const EPS: f64 = 1e-12;

/// Execution assumptions for simulated fills.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct SimConfig {
    /// Half-spread applied at execution, in bps.
    pub spread_bps: f64,
    /// Adverse slippage applied at execution, in bps.
    pub slippage_bps: f64,
    /// Taker fee per fill notional, in bps.
    pub fee_bps: f64,
    /// Capital allocated lazily to strategies with no explicit allocation.
    pub starting_capital: f64,
    /// When set, a market order's *first* fill covers this fraction of the
    /// quantity (partial fill); the remainder fills on the next market
    /// update. `None` = fill fully in one print.
    pub partial_fill_ratio: Option<f64>,
}

impl Default for SimConfig {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            spread_bps: 1.0,
            slippage_bps: 1.0,
            fee_bps: 4.0,
            starting_capital: 10_000.0,
            partial_fill_ratio: None,
        }
    }
}

/// Position key: strategy instance + symbol (tuple identity, never parsed).
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord)]
struct PosKey {
    strategy_id: String,
    symbol: String,
}

/// An open (or partially closed) position with its entry risk.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
struct Position {
    /// Signed quantity: positive = long, negative = short.
    qty: f64,
    /// Quantity-weighted average entry price.
    avg_entry: f64,
    /// Entry fees still attached to the open portion.
    entry_fee: f64,
    /// Net realised pnl accumulated while scaling out (settled at flat).
    realized_bucket: f64,
    /// Total initial risk in quote units (from the entry triple barrier).
    risk_total: Option<f64>,
    /// Entry barrier, monitored while the position is open.
    barrier: Option<TripleBarrier>,
}

/// Portfolio accounting shared by all execution modes.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default)]
pub struct Book {
    capital: BTreeMap<String, Capital>,
    positions: BTreeMap<PosKey, Position>,
    marks: BTreeMap<String, f64>,
}

impl Book {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    /// Pre-allocate capital for a strategy instance.
    pub fn with_capital(mut self, strategy_id: impl Into<String>, starting: f64) -> Self {
        self.capital
            .insert(strategy_id.into(), Capital::new(starting));
        self
    }

    /// Ensure a strategy has capital (lazy allocation at `starting`).
    pub fn ensure_capital(&mut self, strategy_id: &str, starting: f64) {
        self.capital
            .entry(strategy_id.to_string())
            .or_insert_with(|| Capital::new(starting));
    }

    /// Replace a strategy's allocation (used by explicit `with_capital`
    /// constructors).
    pub fn set_capital(&mut self, strategy_id: impl Into<String>, starting: f64) {
        self.capital
            .insert(strategy_id.into(), Capital::new(starting));
    }

    /// Latest capital snapshot for a strategy instance.
    pub fn capital(&self, strategy_id: &str) -> Option<Capital> {
        self.capital.get(strategy_id).cloned()
    }

    /// Merge the latest marks.
    pub fn set_marks(&mut self, marks: &BTreeMap<String, f64>) {
        for (symbol, price) in marks {
            self.marks.insert(symbol.clone(), *price);
        }
    }

    /// Latest known mark for a symbol.
    pub fn mark(&self, symbol: &str) -> Option<f64> {
        self.marks.get(symbol).copied()
    }

    /// Open quantity for (strategy, symbol), signed.
    pub fn position_qty(&self, strategy_id: &str, symbol: &str) -> Option<f64> {
        self.positions
            .get(&PosKey {
                strategy_id: strategy_id.to_string(),
                symbol: symbol.to_string(),
            })
            .map(|p| p.qty)
    }

    /// How a fill splits against the existing position: the quantity that
    /// closes the current position and the quantity that opens (or adds).
    fn split_fill(existing_qty: f64, dir: f64, qty: f64) -> (f64, f64) {
        let closing = if existing_qty != 0.0 && existing_qty.signum() != dir {
            existing_qty.abs().min(qty)
        } else {
            0.0
        };
        (closing, qty - closing)
    }

    /// Why a *buy* of `qty` at `price` (opening portion) cannot execute, or
    /// `None` if it can. Sells always execute (they free cash or collect
    /// proceeds).
    pub fn capacity_error(
        &self,
        strategy_id: &str,
        side: OrderSide,
        qty: f64,
        price: f64,
        fee: f64,
    ) -> Option<String> {
        if side == OrderSide::Sell {
            return None;
        }
        let capital = self.capital.get(strategy_id)?;
        let required = price * qty + fee;
        if capital.cash + EPS < required {
            Some(format!(
                "insufficient cash: required {required:.4}, available {:.4}",
                capital.cash
            ))
        } else {
            None
        }
    }

    /// Apply one fill: updates cash, position, realised/unrealized PnL,
    /// fees, exposure, win/loss counts and R-multiples.
    ///
    /// `entry_barrier` is attached when this fill opens (or adds to) a
    /// position; it defines the initial risk used for R-multiples.
    pub fn apply_fill(
        &mut self,
        strategy_id: &str,
        symbol: &str,
        side: OrderSide,
        qty: f64,
        price: f64,
        fee: f64,
        entry_barrier: Option<&TripleBarrier>,
    ) -> Result<(), String> {
        if !(qty.is_finite() && qty > 0.0 && price.is_finite() && price > 0.0) {
            return Err(format!("invalid fill: qty={qty} price={price}"));
        }
        self.ensure_capital(strategy_id, 0.0);
        let dir = match side {
            OrderSide::Buy => 1.0,
            OrderSide::Sell => -1.0,
        };
        let key = PosKey {
            strategy_id: strategy_id.to_string(),
            symbol: symbol.to_string(),
        };

        let existing_qty = self.positions.get(&key).map(|p| p.qty).unwrap_or(0.0);
        let (closing, opening) = Self::split_fill(existing_qty, dir, qty);

        // ---- Closing portion: realise PnL, settle fees, count trades ----
        if closing > 0.0 {
            let exit_fee = fee * (closing / qty);
            let entry_fee_share = {
                let p = self.positions.get(&key).unwrap();
                let open_abs = p.qty.abs();
                if open_abs > 0.0 {
                    p.entry_fee * (closing / open_abs)
                } else {
                    0.0
                }
            };
            let (net, became_flat, trade_stats) = {
                let p = self.positions.get_mut(&key).unwrap();
                let gross = (price - p.avg_entry) * closing * p.qty.signum();
                let net = gross - entry_fee_share - exit_fee;
                p.realized_bucket += net;
                p.entry_fee -= entry_fee_share;
                p.qty -= closing * p.qty.signum();
                let flat = p.qty.abs() < EPS;
                if flat {
                    p.qty = 0.0;
                }
                let stats = if flat {
                    Some((p.realized_bucket, p.risk_total.filter(|r| *r > 0.0)))
                } else {
                    None
                };
                (net, flat, stats)
            };

            // Cash movement for the closing portion (sell = +, buy-back = -).
            let cash_delta = if dir > 0.0 {
                price * closing - exit_fee
            } else {
                -(price * closing + exit_fee)
            };
            let capital = self.capital.get_mut(strategy_id).unwrap();
            capital.cash += cash_delta;
            capital.realized_pnl += net;
            capital.fees_paid += exit_fee;

            if became_flat {
                if let Some((bucket, risk)) = trade_stats {
                    let capital = self.capital.get_mut(strategy_id).unwrap();
                    capital.trade_count += 1;
                    if bucket > 0.0 {
                        capital.win_count += 1;
                    } else if bucket < 0.0 {
                        capital.loss_count += 1;
                    }
                    if let Some(risk) = risk {
                        capital.r_multiple_sum += bucket / risk;
                        capital.r_trades += 1;
                    }
                }
                self.positions.remove(&key);
            }
        }
        // ---- Opening portion: consume cash, upsert position ----
        if opening > 0.0 {
            let open_fee = fee * (opening / qty);
            let capital = self.capital.get_mut(strategy_id).unwrap();
            if dir > 0.0 && capital.cash + EPS < price * opening + open_fee {
                return Err(format!(
                    "insufficient cash: required {}, available {:.4}",
                    price * opening + open_fee,
                    capital.cash
                ));
            }
            let cash_delta = if dir > 0.0 {
                -(price * opening + open_fee)
            } else {
                price * opening - open_fee
            };
            capital.cash += cash_delta;
            capital.fees_paid += open_fee;

            let risk_total = entry_barrier.map(|b| b.risk_per_unit() * opening);
            let entry = self.positions.entry(key).or_insert_with(|| Position {
                qty: 0.0,
                avg_entry: price,
                entry_fee: 0.0,
                realized_bucket: 0.0,
                risk_total,
                barrier: entry_barrier.cloned(),
            });
            if entry.qty.abs() < EPS {
                entry.qty = dir * opening;
                entry.avg_entry = price;
                entry.entry_fee = open_fee;
                entry.realized_bucket = 0.0;
                entry.risk_total = risk_total;
                entry.barrier = entry_barrier.cloned();
            } else {
                // Adding to an existing position: weighted average entry.
                let total = entry.qty.abs() + opening;
                entry.avg_entry = (entry.avg_entry * entry.qty.abs() + price * opening) / total;
                entry.qty += dir * opening;
                entry.entry_fee += open_fee;
                // Keep the first barrier: its risk defines the trade.
            }
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    /// Revalue every strategy: equity, unrealized, exposure and margin.
    pub fn mark_to_market(&mut self) {
        let mut per: BTreeMap<String, (f64, f64, f64)> = BTreeMap::new();
        for (key, pos) in &self.positions {
            let mark = self
                .marks
                .get(&key.symbol)
                .copied()
                .unwrap_or(pos.avg_entry);
            let value = pos.qty * mark;
            let unreal = (mark - pos.avg_entry) * pos.qty;
            let slot = per
                .entry(key.strategy_id.clone())
                .or_insert((0.0, 0.0, 0.0));
            slot.0 += unreal;
            slot.1 += value.abs();
            slot.2 += value;
        }
        for (strategy_id, capital) in self.capital.iter_mut() {
            let (unreal, gross, net) = per.get(strategy_id).cloned().unwrap_or((0.0, 0.0, 0.0));
            capital.unrealized_pnl = unreal;
            capital.equity = capital.cash + net;
            capital.gross_exposure = gross;
            capital.net_exposure = net;
            capital.margin_used = gross;
            capital.available_margin = (capital.equity - gross).max(0.0);
            capital.effective_leverage = if capital.equity > 0.0 {
                gross / capital.equity
            } else {
                0.0
            };
        }
    }

    /// Open positions that carry a barrier (for exit monitoring):
    /// (strategy, symbol, qty, is_long, barrier).
    pub(crate) fn barrier_positions(&self) -> Vec<(String, String, f64, bool, TripleBarrier)> {
        self.positions
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|(key, pos)| {
                pos.barrier.as_ref().map(|b| {
                    (
                        key.strategy_id.clone(),
                        key.symbol.clone(),
                        pos.qty,
                        b.is_long,
                        b.clone(),
                    )
                })
            })
            .collect()
    }
}

/// Fill outcome for one fill attempt.
enum FillOutcome {
    Idle,
    Waiting,
    Filled,
    Rejected(String),
}

/// The simulated execution engine shared by paper and playback backends.
///
/// Lifecycle: `OrderAccepted` at submission; fills happen immediately when a
/// mark is known, otherwise on the next market update. Barrier exits are
/// synthesised as internal exit orders.
pub struct SimEngine {
    pub config: SimConfig,
    pub book: Book,
    orders: BTreeMap<OrderId, Order>,
    events: Vec<ExecutionEvent>,
    barrier_seq: u64,
}

impl SimEngine {
    pub fn new(config: SimConfig) -> Self {
        Self {
            config,
            book: Book::new(),
            orders: BTreeMap::new(),
            events: Vec::new(),
            barrier_seq: 0,
        }
    }

    /// Engine with a pre-allocated strategy capital.
    pub fn with_capital(config: SimConfig, strategy_id: impl Into<String>, starting: f64) -> Self {
        let mut engine = Self::new(config);
        engine.book.set_capital(strategy_id, starting);
        engine
    }

    /// Accept an order: validates, records it, and fills immediately if a
    /// mark for the symbol is already known.
    pub fn submit(
        &mut self,
        request: OrderRequest,
        now: DateTime<Utc>,
    ) -> Result<OrderId, BackendError> {
        if !(request.qty.is_finite() && request.qty > 0.0) {
            let reason = format!("invalid quantity: {}", request.qty);
            self.events.push(ExecutionEvent::OrderRejected {
                order_id: request.id.clone(),
                reason: reason.clone(),
                at: now,
            });
            return Err(BackendError::OrderRejected(reason));
        }
        if self.orders.contains_key(&request.id) {
            return Err(BackendError::DuplicateOrderId(request.id.0.clone()));
        }
        self.book
            .ensure_capital(&request.strategy_id, self.config.starting_capital);

        let id = request.id.clone();
        let symbol = request.symbol.clone();
        let mut order = Order::new(id.clone(), request, now);
        order.status = OrderStatus::Accepted;
        order.updated_at = now;
        self.orders.insert(id.clone(), order);
        self.events.push(ExecutionEvent::OrderAccepted {
            order_id: id.clone(),
            symbol,
            at: now,
        });

        // Fill immediately when a mark is known; otherwise wait for the
        // next market update.
        match self.try_fill(&id, now) {
            FillOutcome::Rejected(reason) => Err(BackendError::OrderRejected(reason)),
            _ => Ok(id),
        }
    }

    /// Cancel a live order. Terminal orders cannot be cancelled.
    pub fn cancel(&mut self, order_id: &OrderId, now: DateTime<Utc>) -> Result<(), BackendError> {
        let order = self
            .orders
            .get_mut(order_id)
            .ok_or_else(|| BackendError::Internal(format!("unknown order {order_id}")))?;
        if order.status.is_terminal() {
            return Err(BackendError::Internal(format!(
                "order {order_id} already {:?}",
                order.status
            )));
        }
        order.status = OrderStatus::Cancelled;
        order.updated_at = now;
        self.events.push(ExecutionEvent::OrderCancelled {
            order_id: order_id.clone(),
            at: now,
        });
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Attempt to fill one live order at the current mark.
    fn try_fill(&mut self, order_id: &OrderId, now: DateTime<Utc>) -> FillOutcome {
        let Some(order) = self.orders.get(order_id) else {
            return FillOutcome::Idle;
        };
        if !matches!(
            order.status,
            OrderStatus::Accepted | OrderStatus::PartiallyFilled
        ) {
            return FillOutcome::Idle;
        }
        let request = order.request.clone();
        let filled_so_far = order.filled_qty;
        let remaining = (request.qty - filled_so_far).max(0.0);
        if remaining < EPS {
            return FillOutcome::Idle;
        }
        let Some(mark) = self.book.mark(&request.symbol) else {
            return FillOutcome::Waiting;
        };

        // Decide fill quantity and execution price.
        let (fill_qty, price) = match request.order_type {
            OrderType::Market => {
                let mut q = remaining;
                if filled_so_far == 0.0 {
                    if let Some(ratio) = self.config.partial_fill_ratio {
                        q = (remaining * ratio).clamp(EPS, remaining);
                    }
                }
                let edge = (self.config.spread_bps / 2.0 + self.config.slippage_bps) / 10_000.0;
                let px = match request.side {
                    OrderSide::Buy => mark * (1.0 + edge),
                    OrderSide::Sell => mark * (1.0 - edge),
                };
                (q, px)
            }
            OrderType::Limit => {
                let Some(limit) = request.price else {
                    let reason = "limit order without a price".to_string();
                    self.reject_order(order_id, reason.clone(), now);
                    return FillOutcome::Rejected(reason);
                };
                let cross = match request.side {
                    OrderSide::Buy => mark <= limit,
                    OrderSide::Sell => mark >= limit,
                };
                if !cross {
                    return FillOutcome::Waiting;
                }
                (remaining, limit)
            }
        };

        let fee = self.config.fee_bps / 10_000.0 * price * fill_qty;
        let apply = self.book.apply_fill(
            &request.strategy_id,
            &request.symbol,
            request.side,
            fill_qty,
            price,
            fee,
            request.barrier.as_ref(),
        );
        if let Err(reason) = apply {
            self.reject_order(order_id, reason.clone(), now);
            return FillOutcome::Rejected(reason);
        }

        // Update order state and emit lifecycle events.
        let fully_filled = {
            let order = self.orders.get_mut(order_id).unwrap();
            order.record_fill(fill_qty, price, fee, now);
            order.filled_qty + EPS >= request.qty
        };
        self.book.mark_to_market();
        if fully_filled {
            self.events.push(ExecutionEvent::OrderFilled {
                order_id: order_id.clone(),
                filled_qty: fill_qty,
                fill_price: price,
                fee,
                at: now,
            });
        } else {
            self.events.push(ExecutionEvent::OrderPartiallyFilled {
                order_id: order_id.clone(),
                filled_qty: fill_qty,
                fill_price: price,
                fee,
                at: now,
            });
        }
        self.events.push(ExecutionEvent::Fill(FillEvent {
            order_id: order_id.clone(),
            symbol: request.symbol.clone(),
            side: request.side,
            qty: fill_qty,
            price,
            fee,
            executed_at: now,
        }));

        if fully_filled {
            FillOutcome::Filled
        } else {
            FillOutcome::Filled
        }
    }

    /// Mark an order rejected (fill-time validation failure).
    fn reject_order(&mut self, order_id: &OrderId, reason: String, now: DateTime<Utc>) {
        if let Some(order) = self.orders.get_mut(order_id) {
            order.status = OrderStatus::Rejected;
            order.updated_at = now;
        }
        self.events.push(ExecutionEvent::OrderRejected {
            order_id: order_id.clone(),
            reason,
            at: now,
        });
    }

    /// Mark-to-market update: merge marks, fill live orders, monitor
    /// triple barriers, and revalue every strategy.
    pub fn on_market_update(&mut self, marks: &BTreeMap<String, f64>, now: DateTime<Utc>) {
        self.book.set_marks(marks);

        let live_ids: Vec<OrderId> = self
            .orders
            .iter()
            .filter(|(_, o)| {
                matches!(
                    o.status,
                    OrderStatus::Accepted | OrderStatus::PartiallyFilled
                )
            })
            .map(|(id, _)| id.clone())
            .collect();
        for id in live_ids {
            self.try_fill(&id, now);
        }

        self.check_barriers(now);
        self.book.mark_to_market();
    }

    /// Monitor open positions' triple barriers and synthesise exit orders
    /// when TP, SL or the vertical barrier is breached.
    fn check_barriers(&mut self, now: DateTime<Utc>) {
        let monitored = self.book.barrier_positions();
        for (strategy_id, symbol, qty, is_long, barrier) in monitored {
            let Some(mark) = self.book.mark(&symbol) else {
                continue;
            };
            let exit = barrier.check(mark, now);
            if !exit.should_exit {
                continue;
            }
            self.barrier_seq += 1;
            let exit_id = OrderId::new(format!(
                "barrier-{}-{}",
                self.barrier_seq,
                UuidLike(&symbol)
            ));
            let side = if is_long {
                OrderSide::Sell
            } else {
                OrderSide::Buy
            };
            let asset_id = strategy_id.split('@').nth(1).unwrap_or(&symbol).to_string();
            let request = OrderRequest {
                id: exit_id.clone(),
                symbol: symbol.clone(),
                side,
                order_type: OrderType::Market,
                qty: qty.abs(),
                price: None,
                time_in_force: crate::types::TimeInForce::Gtc,
                strategy_id: strategy_id.clone(),
                asset_id,
                barrier: None, // exits never carry a barrier (no recursion)
            };

            // Record and immediately fill at the barrier exit price.
            let mut order = Order::new(exit_id.clone(), request.clone(), now);
            order.status = OrderStatus::Accepted;
            order.updated_at = now;
            self.orders.insert(exit_id.clone(), order);
            self.events.push(ExecutionEvent::OrderAccepted {
                order_id: exit_id.clone(),
                symbol: symbol.clone(),
                at: now,
            });

            let fee = self.config.fee_bps / 10_000.0 * exit.exit_price * qty.abs();
            let applied = self.book.apply_fill(
                &strategy_id,
                &symbol,
                side,
                qty.abs(),
                exit.exit_price,
                fee,
                None,
            );
            if applied.is_err() {
                // A closing fill cannot fail capacity checks; log loudly if
                // it ever does rather than swallowing it.
                tracing::error!(
                    %strategy_id,
                    %symbol,
                    "barrier exit fill failed unexpectedly: {applied:?}"
                );
                continue;
            }
            let order = self.orders.get_mut(&exit_id).unwrap();
            order.record_fill(qty.abs(), exit.exit_price, fee, now);
            self.events.push(ExecutionEvent::OrderFilled {
                order_id: exit_id.clone(),
                filled_qty: qty.abs(),
                fill_price: exit.exit_price,
                fee,
                at: now,
            });
            self.events.push(ExecutionEvent::Fill(FillEvent {
                order_id: exit_id,
                symbol,
                side,
                qty: qty.abs(),
                price: exit.exit_price,
                fee,
                executed_at: now,
            }));
        }
    }

    /// Drain execution events since the last call.
    pub fn drain_events(&mut self) -> Vec<ExecutionEvent> {
        std::mem::take(&mut self.events)
    }

    /// Tracked state of one order.
    pub fn order_state(&self, order_id: &OrderId) -> Option<&Order> {
        self.orders.get(order_id)
    }

    /// All tracked orders.
    pub fn orders(&self) -> Vec<&Order> {
        self.orders.values().collect()
    }

    /// Capital snapshot for a strategy instance.
    pub fn capital(&self, strategy_id: &str) -> Option<Capital> {
        self.book.capital(strategy_id)
    }
}

/// Small helper so barrier order ids embed the symbol legibly.
struct UuidLike<'a>(&'a str);
impl std::fmt::Display for UuidLike<'_> {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        for c in self.0.chars() {
            if c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() {
                write!(f, "{c}")?;
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/uets_trades/src/triple_barrier.rs =====
//! OFMR triple-barrier handling.
//!
//! Inspected from `strategy_system/strategy/ofmr/config.py` and
//! `strategy_system/strategy/moe/outcome/triple_barrier.py`:
//! - TP/SL widths are in bps (stop_loss_bps=5, take_profit_bps=5)
//! - Vertical barrier = timeframe_barrier_secs (300s)
//! - Barriers are used for BOTH training labels (Python) and live exits (Rust).
//!
//! The barrier is attached to an entry order and monitored during the trade.
//! On breach, an Exit intent is emitted with the exit reason.

use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

/// Why a triple-barrier exit occurred.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum BarrierReason {
    TakeProfit,
    StopLoss,
    Vertical,
}

/// Result of a barrier check: whether to exit and why.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct BarrierExit {
    pub should_exit: bool,
    pub reason: Option<BarrierReason>,
    pub exit_price: f64,
}

/// Triple-barrier configuration attached to an entry.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct TripleBarrier {
    /// Entry price.
    pub entry_price: f64,
    /// Entry timestamp.
    pub entry_time: DateTime<Utc>,
    /// Take-profit barrier as a fraction of entry (e.g. 0.0005 = 5 bps).
    pub tp_fraction: f64,
    /// Stop-loss barrier as a fraction of entry (e.g. 0.0005 = 5 bps).
    pub sl_fraction: f64,
    /// Maximum holding duration.
    pub max_hold_seconds: u64,
    /// Position side: true = long, false = short.
    pub is_long: bool,
    /// Actual TP price (entry_price * (1 + tp_fraction) for long).
    pub tp_price: f64,
    /// Actual SL price (entry_price * (1 - sl_fraction) for long).
    pub sl_price: f64,
    /// Vertical barrier timestamp (entry_time + max_hold_seconds).
    pub vertical_time: DateTime<Utc>,
}

impl TripleBarrier {
    /// Build barriers for a long position.
    pub fn long(
        entry_price: f64,
        entry_time: DateTime<Utc>,
        tp_bps: f64,
        sl_bps: f64,
        max_hold_seconds: u64,
    ) -> Self {
        let tp_fraction = tp_bps / 10_000.0;
        let sl_fraction = sl_bps / 10_000.0;
        Self {
            entry_price,
            entry_time,
            tp_fraction,
            sl_fraction,
            max_hold_seconds,
            is_long: true,
            tp_price: entry_price * (1.0 + tp_fraction),
            sl_price: entry_price * (1.0 - sl_fraction),
            vertical_time: entry_time + chrono::Duration::seconds(max_hold_seconds as i64),
        }
    }

    /// Build barriers for a short position.
    pub fn short(
        entry_price: f64,
        entry_time: DateTime<Utc>,
        tp_bps: f64,
        sl_bps: f64,
        max_hold_seconds: u64,
    ) -> Self {
        let tp_fraction = tp_bps / 10_000.0;
        let sl_fraction = sl_bps / 10_000.0;
        Self {
            entry_price,
            entry_time,
            tp_fraction,
            sl_fraction,
            max_hold_seconds,
            is_long: false,
            tp_price: entry_price * (1.0 - tp_fraction),
            sl_price: entry_price * (1.0 + sl_fraction),
            vertical_time: entry_time + chrono::Duration::seconds(max_hold_seconds as i64),
        }
    }

    /// Check the current mark price and time against all three barriers.
    /// Price barriers (TP/SL) take precedence over the vertical barrier.
    pub fn check(&self, mark_price: f64, now: DateTime<Utc>) -> BarrierExit {
        if self.is_long {
            if mark_price >= self.tp_price {
                return BarrierExit {
                    should_exit: true,
                    reason: Some(BarrierReason::TakeProfit),
                    exit_price: mark_price,
                };
            }
            if mark_price <= self.sl_price {
                return BarrierExit {
                    should_exit: true,
                    reason: Some(BarrierReason::StopLoss),
                    exit_price: mark_price,
                };
            }
        } else {
            if mark_price <= self.tp_price {
                return BarrierExit {
                    should_exit: true,
                    reason: Some(BarrierReason::TakeProfit),
                    exit_price: mark_price,
                };
            }
            if mark_price >= self.sl_price {
                return BarrierExit {
                    should_exit: true,
                    reason: Some(BarrierReason::StopLoss),
                    exit_price: mark_price,
                };
            }
        }

        // Vertical barrier (maximum holding time) is checked last.
        if now >= self.vertical_time {
            return BarrierExit {
                should_exit: true,
                reason: Some(BarrierReason::Vertical),
                exit_price: mark_price,
            };
        }

        BarrierExit {
            should_exit: false,
            reason: None,
            exit_price: mark_price,
        }
    }

    /// The risk distance from entry to stop, per unit.
    pub fn risk_per_unit(&self) -> f64 {
        (self.entry_price - self.sl_price).abs()
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use chrono::{TimeZone, Utc};

    fn entry_time() -> DateTime<Utc> {
        Utc.with_ymd_and_hms(2026, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0).unwrap()
    }

    #[test]
    fn long_tp_hit() {
        let tb = TripleBarrier::long(100.0, entry_time(), 5.0, 5.0, 300);
        let exit = tb.check(100.501, entry_time());
        assert!(exit.should_exit);
        assert_eq!(exit.reason, Some(BarrierReason::TakeProfit));
    }

    #[test]
    fn long_sl_hit() {
        let tb = TripleBarrier::long(100.0, entry_time(), 5.0, 5.0, 300);
        let exit = tb.check(99.499, entry_time());
        assert!(exit.should_exit);
        assert_eq!(exit.reason, Some(BarrierReason::StopLoss));
    }

    #[test]
    fn short_tp_hit() {
        let tb = TripleBarrier::short(100.0, entry_time(), 5.0, 5.0, 300);
        let exit = tb.check(99.499, entry_time());
        assert!(exit.should_exit);
        assert_eq!(exit.reason, Some(BarrierReason::TakeProfit));
    }

    #[test]
    fn short_sl_hit() {
        let tb = TripleBarrier::short(100.0, entry_time(), 5.0, 5.0, 300);
        let exit = tb.check(100.501, entry_time());
        assert!(exit.should_exit);
        assert_eq!(exit.reason, Some(BarrierReason::StopLoss));
    }

    #[test]
    fn vertical_barrier_hit() {
        let entry = entry_time();
        let tb = TripleBarrier::long(100.0, entry, 5.0, 5.0, 10);
        let late = entry + chrono::Duration::seconds(11);
        let exit = tb.check(100.0, late);
        assert!(exit.should_exit);
        assert_eq!(exit.reason, Some(BarrierReason::Vertical));
    }

    #[test]
    fn no_barrier_hit() {
        let tb = TripleBarrier::long(100.0, entry_time(), 5.0, 5.0, 300);
        let exit = tb.check(100.02, entry_time());
        assert!(!exit.should_exit);
        assert_eq!(exit.reason, None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn tp_wins_tie_over_vertical() {
        let entry = entry_time();
        let tb = TripleBarrier::long(100.0, entry, 0.1, 5.0, 1);
        let late = entry + chrono::Duration::seconds(2);
        let exit = tb.check(100.001, late);
        assert!(exit.should_exit);
        assert_eq!(exit.reason, Some(BarrierReason::TakeProfit));
    }
}


===== FILE: components/pipelines/uets_trades/src/types.rs =====
//! Core trading types shared by all execution modes.
//!
//! Order lifecycle: submitted → accepted → [partially filled]* → filled
//!                                                      → rejected / cancelled
//!
//! A `TradeIntent` is the strategy's request; an `Order` is the tracked
//! lifecycle object; a `FillEvent` is an execution report from the backend.

use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::fmt;
use uets::core::order::Side;
use uets::core::strategy::Decision;

/// Strategy action: what the strategy wants to do.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum TradeIntent {
    Long,
    Short,
    Exit,
    Hold,
}

impl TradeIntent {
    pub fn from_decision(decision: Decision) -> Self {
        match decision {
            Decision::EnterLong => TradeIntent::Long,
            Decision::EnterShort => TradeIntent::Short,
            Decision::Exit => TradeIntent::Exit,
            Decision::Hold => TradeIntent::Hold,
        }
    }
}

impl fmt::Display for TradeIntent {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        match self {
            TradeIntent::Long => write!(f, "LONG"),
            TradeIntent::Short => write!(f, "SHORT"),
            TradeIntent::Exit => write!(f, "EXIT"),
            TradeIntent::Hold => write!(f, "HOLD"),
        }
    }
}

/// Which side of the book an order takes.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum OrderSide {
    Buy,
    Sell,
}

impl OrderSide {
    pub fn from_side(side: Side) -> Self {
        match side {
            Side::Buy => OrderSide::Buy,
            Side::Sell => OrderSide::Sell,
        }
    }

    pub fn to_side(self) -> Side {
        match self {
            OrderSide::Buy => Side::Buy,
            OrderSide::Sell => Side::Sell,
        }
    }

    pub fn opposite(self) -> Self {
        match self {
            OrderSide::Buy => OrderSide::Sell,
            OrderSide::Sell => OrderSide::Buy,
        }
    }
}

/// How the order is priced.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum OrderType {
    Market,
    Limit,
}

/// How long a resting order may wait.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum TimeInForce {
    Gtc,
    Ioc,
    Fok,
}

/// Order lifecycle status.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum OrderStatus {
    Submitted,
    Accepted,
    PartiallyFilled,
    Filled,
    Rejected,
    Cancelled,
    /// Request may have reached the venue, but its response was lost.
    Uncertain,
}

impl OrderStatus {
    pub fn is_live(self) -> bool {
        matches!(
            self,
            OrderStatus::Submitted
                | OrderStatus::Accepted
                | OrderStatus::PartiallyFilled
                | OrderStatus::Uncertain
        )
    }

    pub fn is_terminal(self) -> bool {
        !self.is_live()
    }
}

/// A client-side order identifier.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Ord, PartialOrd, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct OrderId(pub String);

impl OrderId {
    pub fn new(id: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self(id.into())
    }

    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &str {
        &self.0
    }
}

impl fmt::Display for OrderId {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(&self.0)
    }
}

impl From<&str> for OrderId {
    fn from(s: &str) -> Self {
        Self(s.to_string())
    }
}

impl From<String> for OrderId {
    fn from(s: String) -> Self {
        Self(s)
    }
}

/// What the strategy hands to the execution backend to open or close exposure.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct OrderRequest {
    pub id: OrderId,
    pub symbol: String,
    pub side: OrderSide,
    pub order_type: OrderType,
    pub qty: f64,
    pub price: Option<f64>,
    pub time_in_force: TimeInForce,
    /// Strategy instance identity, e.g. `ofmr@btcusdt`.
    pub strategy_id: String,
    pub asset_id: String,
    /// Triple-barrier parameters attached to the entry order (OFMR).
    pub barrier: Option<crate::triple_barrier::TripleBarrier>,
}

impl OrderRequest {
    pub fn new(
        id: impl Into<OrderId>,
        symbol: impl Into<String>,
        side: OrderSide,
        qty: f64,
        strategy_id: impl Into<String>,
        asset_id: impl Into<String>,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            id: id.into(),
            symbol: symbol.into(),
            side,
            order_type: OrderType::Market,
            qty,
            price: None,
            time_in_force: TimeInForce::Gtc,
            strategy_id: strategy_id.into(),
            asset_id: asset_id.into(),
            barrier: None,
        }
    }

    pub fn with_barrier(mut self, barrier: crate::triple_barrier::TripleBarrier) -> Self {
        self.barrier = Some(barrier);
        self
    }
}

/// An order as tracked locally, fed by the backend's fill events.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Order {
    pub id: OrderId,
    pub request: OrderRequest,
    pub status: OrderStatus,
    pub filled_qty: f64,
    pub avg_fill_price: Option<f64>,
    pub fee_paid: f64,
    pub submitted_at: DateTime<Utc>,
    pub updated_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

impl Order {
    pub fn new(id: OrderId, request: OrderRequest, submitted_at: DateTime<Utc>) -> Self {
        Self {
            id: id.clone(),
            request,
            status: OrderStatus::Submitted,
            filled_qty: 0.0,
            avg_fill_price: None,
            fee_paid: 0.0,
            submitted_at,
            updated_at: submitted_at,
        }
    }

    pub fn remaining(&self) -> f64 {
        (self.request.qty - self.filled_qty).max(0.0)
    }

    pub fn record_fill(&mut self, qty: f64, price: f64, fee: f64, now: DateTime<Utc>) {
        let prior_notional = self.avg_fill_price.unwrap_or(0.0) * self.filled_qty;
        self.filled_qty += qty;
        self.fee_paid += fee;
        self.avg_fill_price = if self.filled_qty > 0.0 {
            Some((prior_notional + price * qty) / self.filled_qty)
        } else {
            None
        };
        self.status = if self.filled_qty + 1e-12 >= self.request.qty {
            OrderStatus::Filled
        } else {
            OrderStatus::PartiallyFilled
        };
        self.updated_at = now;
    }
}

/// One execution report from the backend.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct FillEvent {
    pub order_id: OrderId,
    pub symbol: String,
    pub side: OrderSide,
    pub qty: f64,
    pub price: f64,
    pub fee: f64,
    pub executed_at: DateTime<Utc>,
}

/// One execution event emitted by a backend.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum ExecutionEvent {
    OrderAccepted {
        order_id: OrderId,
        symbol: String,
        at: DateTime<Utc>,
    },
    OrderPartiallyFilled {
        order_id: OrderId,
        filled_qty: f64,
        fill_price: f64,
        fee: f64,
        at: DateTime<Utc>,
    },
    OrderFilled {
        order_id: OrderId,
        filled_qty: f64,
        fill_price: f64,
        fee: f64,
        at: DateTime<Utc>,
    },
    OrderRejected {
        order_id: OrderId,
        reason: String,
        at: DateTime<Utc>,
    },
    OrderCancelled {
        order_id: OrderId,
        at: DateTime<Utc>,
    },
    OrderUncertain {
        order_id: OrderId,
        reason: String,
        at: DateTime<Utc>,
    },
    Fill(FillEvent),
}

/// Capital snapshot for a strategy instance.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Capital {
    pub starting: f64,
    pub cash: f64,
    pub equity: f64,
    pub unrealized_pnl: f64,
    pub realized_pnl: f64,
    pub fees_paid: f64,
    pub gross_exposure: f64,
    pub net_exposure: f64,
    pub margin_used: f64,
    pub available_margin: f64,
    pub effective_leverage: f64,
    /// Total trade count.
    pub trade_count: usize,
    /// Winning trade count.
    pub win_count: usize,
    /// Losing trade count.
    pub loss_count: usize,
    /// Sum of realised R-multiples (net pnl / initial risk) over closed
    /// trades whose entry risk was known (i.e. a triple barrier was set).
    pub r_multiple_sum: f64,
    /// Number of closed trades with a known risk (R) — denominator for
    /// expectancy in RR units.
    pub r_trades: usize,
}

impl Capital {
    /// A fresh allocation: everything flat at `starting`.
    pub fn new(starting: f64) -> Self {
        Self {
            starting,
            cash: starting,
            equity: starting,
            unrealized_pnl: 0.0,
            realized_pnl: 0.0,
            fees_paid: 0.0,
            gross_exposure: 0.0,
            net_exposure: 0.0,
            margin_used: 0.0,
            available_margin: starting,
            effective_leverage: 0.0,
            trade_count: 0,
            win_count: 0,
            loss_count: 0,
            r_multiple_sum: 0.0,
            r_trades: 0,
        }
    }

    pub fn trades(&self) -> usize {
        self.trade_count
    }

    pub fn wins(&self) -> usize {
        self.win_count
    }

    pub fn losses(&self) -> usize {
        self.loss_count
    }

    /// Expectancy per closed trade in RR units (R multiple of the average
    /// trade whose initial risk was known). 0.0 when no R is known.
    pub fn expectancy_rr(&self) -> f64 {
        if self.r_trades > 0 {
            self.r_multiple_sum / self.r_trades as f64
        } else {
            0.0
        }
    }
}

/// Decision intent wrapper: what the strategy decided plus context.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct DecisionIntent {
    pub strategy_id: String,
    pub asset_id: String,
    pub symbol: String,
    pub intent: TradeIntent,
    pub price: f64,
    pub timestamp: DateTime<Utc>,
    pub barrier: Option<crate::triple_barrier::TripleBarrier>,
    pub quantity: Option<f64>,
}


===== FILE: uets/aggregator/cleanup.rs =====
//! Stale-socket cleanup with ownership checks.
//!
//! Never blindly `unlink`s at startup. A socket path is only claimed when it
//! is safe: if a live peer answers on it, the bind fails and the second
//! instance exits instead of stealing the socket. Lock file holds the owner's
//! pid (`<path>.lock`); a lock whose pid is dead may be reclaimed.

use std::path::Path;

/// Outcome of trying to claim a socket path.
#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Claim {
    /// Safe to bind: no live owner.
    Free,
    /// Another live process owns this path; do not unlink.
    OwnedByLive(u32),
}

/// Check whether `sock` may be bound: a live peer or a live lock-holder
/// means hands off. Stale paths (dead pid, no listener) are unlinked here so
/// the caller can bind immediately after a `Free`.
#[cfg(unix)]
pub fn claim(sock: &Path) -> std::io::Result<Claim> {
    use std::io::{Read, Write};
    use std::os::unix::net::UnixStream;

    // 1. A listener answers -> someone owns it. Do not touch.
    if UnixStream::connect(sock).is_ok() {
        return Ok(Claim::OwnedByLive(0));
    }
    // 2. Lock file with a live pid -> owned. Dead pid -> reclaim.
    let lock = sock.with_extension("lock");
    if lock.exists() {
        if let Ok(mut f) = std::fs::File::open(&lock) {
            let mut text = String::new();
            if f.read_to_string(&mut text).is_ok() {
                if let Ok(pid) = text.trim().parse::<u32>() {
                    if pid_alive(pid) {
                        return Ok(Claim::OwnedByLive(pid));
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&lock);
    }
    // 3. No live owner: remove the stale node (if any) and record our pid.
    if sock.exists() {
        std::fs::remove_file(sock)?;
    }
    if let Ok(mut f) = std::fs::File::create(&lock) {
        let _ = writeln!(f, "{}", std::process::id());
    }
    Ok(Claim::Free)
}

/// Fallback on non-unix: nothing to clean.
#[cfg(not(unix))]
pub fn claim(_sock: &Path) -> std::io::Result<Claim> {
    Ok(Claim::Free)
}

#[cfg(unix)]
fn pid_alive(pid: u32) -> bool {
    // Signal 0 performs error checking without delivering a signal.
    unsafe { libc_kill(pid, 0) == 0 }
}

#[cfg(unix)]
fn libc_kill(pid: u32, sig: i32) -> i32 {
    unsafe extern "C" {
        fn kill(pid: i32, sig: i32) -> i32;
    }
    unsafe { kill(pid as i32, sig) }
}


===== FILE: uets/aggregator/ipc.rs =====
//! Fixed framing for Unix-domain datagrams: `u32` length prefix (little-endian)
//! + payload, payload cap [`MAX_FRAME`].
//!
//! Message boundaries come from the framing, not from the socket type, so the
//! code does not depend on `SOCK_SEQPACKET` semantics (payload limits and
//! availability differ across platforms -- see the user's correction).
//! Payloads are raw bytes (`Arc<[u8]>` at the fan-out point: one allocation
//! per frame, shared by every subscriber, zero copies).

/// Largest single frame accepted, in bytes.
pub const MAX_FRAME: usize = 1 << 20;

/// Encode a payload into a length-prefixed frame. `None` when over [`MAX_FRAME`].
pub fn encode(payload: &[u8]) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
    if payload.len() > MAX_FRAME {
        return None;
    }
    let mut buf = Vec::with_capacity(4 + payload.len());
    buf.extend_from_slice(&(payload.len() as u32).to_le_bytes());
    buf.extend_from_slice(payload);
    Some(buf)
}

/// Decode one frame from the front of `buf`, consuming it. Returns `None`
/// when fewer than a full frame is buffered.
pub fn decode(buf: &mut Vec<u8>) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
    if buf.len() < 4 {
        return None;
    }
    let len = u32::from_le_bytes([buf[0], buf[1], buf[2], buf[3]]) as usize;
    if len > MAX_FRAME || buf.len() < 4 + len {
        return None;
    }
    let payload = buf[4..4 + len].to_vec();
    buf.drain(..4 + len);
    Some(payload)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn roundtrip() {
        let mut buf = encode(b"hello").expect("fits");
        buf.extend_from_slice(&encode(b"world").expect("fits"));
        assert_eq!(decode(&mut buf).expect("frame 1"), b"hello");
        assert_eq!(decode(&mut buf).expect("frame 2"), b"world");
        assert!(decode(&mut buf).is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn oversize_rejected() {
        assert!(encode(&vec![0u8; MAX_FRAME + 1]).is_none());
    }
}


===== FILE: uets/aggregator/latency.rs =====
//! End-to-end latency: proven-bucket histogram, atomics only on the hot path.
//!
//! Correction applied: a ring buffer alone cannot give correct concurrent
//! percentiles (sorting a concurrently-mutated ring is a data race with a
//! histogram costume on). This uses fixed log-spaced nanosecond buckets with
//! one atomic per bucket -- increments are lock-free and every percentile is
//! computed from a stable snapshot. Production percentiles come from the
//! buckets; an exact sort is kept as an offline oracle in tests only, never
//! on the hot path.

use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};

/// Bucket edges in nanoseconds, log-spaced from 1us to ~17s.
const EDGES: [u64; 24] = [
    1_000,
    2_000,
    4_000,
    8_000,
    16_000,
    32_000,
    64_000,
    128_000,
    256_000,
    512_000,
    1_000_000,
    2_000_000,
    4_000_000,
    8_000_000,
    16_000_000,
    32_000_000,
    64_000_000,
    128_000_000,
    256_000_000,
    512_000_000,
    1_000_000_000,
    2_000_000_000,
    4_000_000_000,
    8_000_000_000,
];

/// Lock-free latency histogram: `observe_ns` is a single atomic increment.
pub struct LatencyHist {
    buckets: Vec<AtomicU64>,
    overflow: AtomicU64,
    count: AtomicU64,
}

impl LatencyHist {
    /// Number of buckets (== `EDGES.len()`).
    pub const N: usize = 24;

    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            buckets: (0..Self::N).map(|_| AtomicU64::new(0)).collect(),
            overflow: AtomicU64::new(0),
            count: AtomicU64::new(0),
        }
    }

    /// Hot path: exactly one atomic increment, no locks, no allocation.
    pub fn observe_ns(&self, ns: u64) {
        self.count.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
        match EDGES.iter().position(|&e| ns <= e) {
            Some(i) => {
                self.buckets[i].fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
            }
            None => {
                self.overflow.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
            }
        }
    }

    /// Snapshot percentile from bucket upper edges (`q` in (0,1]).
    /// Reads each bucket once; safe to call off the hot path at 1 Hz.
    pub fn percentile(&self, q: f64) -> Option<u64> {
        let total = self.count.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
        if total == 0 {
            return None;
        }
        let rank = ((q.clamp(0.0, 1.0) * total as f64).ceil() as u64).max(1);
        let mut acc = 0u64;
        for (i, edge) in EDGES.iter().enumerate() {
            acc += self.buckets[i].load(Ordering::Relaxed);
            if acc >= rank {
                return Some(*edge);
            }
        }
        None // rank falls in overflow: value exceeds every edge
    }

    pub fn count(&self) -> u64 {
        self.count.load(Ordering::Relaxed)
    }
}

impl Default for LatencyHist {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    /// Offline oracle: exact nearest-rank on a frozen snapshot. Test-only.
    fn exact(mut xs: Vec<u64>, q: f64) -> u64 {
        xs.sort_unstable();
        xs[((q * xs.len() as f64).ceil() as usize).max(1) - 1]
    }

    #[test]
    fn buckets_bracket_exact() {
        let h = LatencyHist::new();
        let xs: Vec<u64> = (1..=1000).map(|i| i * 10_000).collect(); // 10us..10ms
        for &x in &xs {
            h.observe_ns(x);
        }
        // Bucket edges bracket the exact value within one doubling step.
        for q in [0.5, 0.99, 0.999] {
            let approx = h.percentile(q).expect("nonempty");
            let e = exact(xs.clone(), q);
            assert!(approx >= e && approx <= e * 2, "q={q}: {approx} vs {e}");
        }
    }
}


===== FILE: uets/aggregator/mod.rs =====
//! `aggregator` — the fan-out point between exchange feeds and consumers.
//!
//! ```text
//!   exchange WS (per symbol) --+--> sockets/exchanges/<venue>.sock
//!                               |
//!                          aggregator (this module)
//!                               |
//!              +-----------------+-----------------+
//!              |                 |                 |
//!    sockets/strategies/   recorder.rs        health socket
//!    strategyN.sock        (CSV now,            (latency/
//!                           RocksDB later)      resources)
//! ```
//!
//! Today the `record` path writes CSVs directly; this module is the socket
//! scaffolding that lets strategies attach without touching that path.
//! Everything here is `#[cfg(unix)]`-gated: Unix-domain sockets do not exist
//! on Windows, and the crate must keep building on the win32 dev machine.
//! The Linux runtime (Docker) uses the real implementation.

pub mod cleanup;
pub mod ipc;
pub mod latency;
pub mod recorder;
pub mod resources;
pub mod risk;
pub mod supervisor;


===== FILE: uets/aggregator/recorder.rs =====
//! Recorder: market-data capture. CSV today, RocksDB when the replay path needs it.
//!
//! Correction applied: drops are explicit. The recorder is the designated
//! dropper in the pipeline (bounded queue + `try_send`), and every drop is
//! counted AND marks the recording incomplete -- a capture that lost an event
//! is never presented as a complete replay dataset. Consumers check
//! [`RecorderState::complete`] before trusting a file.

use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};

/// Recorder lifecycle state, reported at shutdown and on the health socket.
pub struct RecorderState {
    rows: AtomicU64,
    drops: AtomicU64,
}

impl RecorderState {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            rows: AtomicU64::new(0),
            drops: AtomicU64::new(0),
        }
    }

    pub fn record_row(&self) {
        self.rows.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    /// Explicit drop: counted, and the recording is incomplete from here on.
    pub fn record_drop(&self) {
        self.drops.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    /// A recording with any drop is NOT a complete replay dataset.
    pub fn complete(&self) -> bool {
        self.drops.load(Ordering::Relaxed) == 0
    }

    pub fn rows(&self) -> u64 {
        self.rows.load(Ordering::Relaxed)
    }

    pub fn drops(&self) -> u64 {
        self.drops.load(Ordering::Relaxed)
    }
}

impl Default for RecorderState {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}


===== FILE: uets/aggregator/resources.rs =====
//! Resource accounting, sampled off the hot path at 1 Hz: RSS, queue depth,
//! drop counters, fd count. The hot path only touches atomics; this module
//! reads them. Bounded queues + buffer reuse live at the fan-out point; this
//! module is the dial that proves they hold.

use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};

/// Counters every producer/consumer updates with a single atomic op.
#[derive(Default)]
pub struct Resources {
    /// Rows dropped by the recorder (the designated dropper).
    pub drops: AtomicU64,
    /// Deepest queue depth observed since last scrape.
    pub queue_depth_max: AtomicU64,
    /// Current queue depth (producers inc, consumers dec).
    pub queue_depth: AtomicU64,
}

impl Resources {
    /// 1 Hz scrape:RSS + fd count are read here, never on the hot path.
    pub fn scrape(&self) -> ResourceSample {
        ResourceSample {
            rss_bytes: rss_bytes(),
            queue_depth: self.queue_depth.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
            queue_depth_max: self.queue_depth_max.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
            drops: self.drops.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
        }
    }
}

/// One off-hot-path sample, served on the health socket.
pub struct ResourceSample {
    pub rss_bytes: u64,
    pub queue_depth: u64,
    pub queue_depth_max: u64,
    pub drops: u64,
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
fn rss_bytes() -> u64 {
    std::fs::read_to_string("/proc/self/statm")
        .ok()
        .and_then(|t| t.split_whitespace().nth(1)?.parse::<u64>().ok())
        .map(|pages| pages * 4096)
        .unwrap_or(0)
}

#[cfg(not(target_os = "linux"))]
fn rss_bytes() -> u64 {
    0 // unimplemented outside Linux; the counters still work
}


===== FILE: uets/aggregator/risk.rs =====
//! Risk: the only write path to the exchange. Owns limits + kill switch.
//!
//! Correction applied: a stale-data watchdog must NOT blindly flatten --
//! flattening into a dead book or a halted venue turns a data problem into an
//! execution disaster. Policy: staleness *blocks new orders* immediately and
//! follows a defined emergency policy (cancel-open, then flatten only when
