//! the book is verifiably tradeable). The kill switch is reachable from the
//! control socket so an operator can halt from outside the process.

use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, AtomicU64, Ordering};
use std::time::Duration;

/// Staleness that blocks new orders.
pub const STALE_BLOCK_AFTER: Duration = Duration::from_secs(5);

/// What the watchdog decided.
#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum RiskDecision {
    /// Healthy: orders flow.
    Ok,
    /// Feed stale: no NEW orders; working orders stand.
    BlockNew,
    /// Operator kill switch: nothing leaves this process.
    Killed,
}

/// Owns the exchange write handle's gate. Hot path: two atomic loads.
pub struct RiskGate {
    killed: AtomicBool,
    last_event_ms: AtomicU64,
    now_ms: AtomicU64,
}

impl RiskGate {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            killed: AtomicBool::new(false),
            last_event_ms: AtomicU64::new(0),
            now_ms: AtomicU64::new(0),
        }
    }

    /// Operator kill switch (control socket). Irreversible within the process.
    pub fn kill(&self) {
        self.killed.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);
    }

    /// Hot path: called per decision. Blocks new orders when stale/killed.
    pub fn check(&self) -> RiskDecision {
        if self.killed.load(Ordering::SeqCst) {
            return RiskDecision::Killed;
        }
        let age = self
            .now_ms
            .load(Ordering::Relaxed)
            .saturating_sub(self.last_event_ms.load(Ordering::Relaxed));
        if age > STALE_BLOCK_AFTER.as_millis() as u64 {
            return RiskDecision::BlockNew;
        }
        RiskDecision::Ok
    }
}

impl Default for RiskGate {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn stale_blocks_but_does_not_flatten() {
        let g = RiskGate::new();
        g.now_ms.store(10_000, Ordering::Relaxed);
        g.last_event_ms.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
        // Blocks new orders -- the emergency flatten is a separate,
        // operator-confirmed step, never an automatic side effect.
        assert_eq!(g.check(), RiskDecision::BlockNew);
    }

    #[test]
    fn kill_is_sticky() {
        let g = RiskGate::new();
        g.kill();
        g.now_ms.store(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
        g.last_event_ms.store(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
        assert_eq!(g.check(), RiskDecision::Killed);
    }
}


===== FILE: uets/aggregator/supervisor.rs =====
//! Supervisor: process lifecycle, health, restart-with-backoff.
//!
//! Spawns one child per symbol-capture (the `record` workers graduate into
//! supervised children here), restarts with backoff, and reaps the child's
//! sockets on death so no garbage accumulates. Three missed heartbeats =
//! restart; restart storms are capped by the backoff ceiling.

use std::time::Duration;

/// Miss this many heartbeats and the child is restarted.
pub const MISS_LIMIT: u32 = 3;
/// Backoff ceiling between restarts.
pub const BACKOFF_MAX: Duration = Duration::from_secs(60);

/// Health of one supervised child.
#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum ChildHealth {
    Healthy,
    Missed(u32),
    Down,
}

/// Backoff for restart attempt `n` (1-based): 1s, 2s, 4s ... capped.
pub fn backoff(n: u32) -> Duration {
    let secs = 1u64.checked_shl(n.saturating_sub(1)).unwrap_or(u64::MAX);
    Duration::from_secs(secs.min(BACKOFF_MAX.as_secs()))
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn backoff_caps() {
        assert_eq!(backoff(1), Duration::from_secs(1));
        assert_eq!(backoff(3), Duration::from_secs(4));
        assert_eq!(backoff(100), BACKOFF_MAX);
    }
}

===== FILE: uets/executor/connectors/binance/account.rs =====
//! Binance spot: the balance view used for reconciliation.
//!
//! The job of this file is one translation: `/api/v3/account` returns *every*
//! asset the venue has ever listed — several hundred rows, nearly all zero —
//! while reconciliation only needs the assets that carry value. Rows whose free
//! and locked quantities are both zero are therefore dropped, and the contract
//! published to `core` is: **an asset absent from `balances` has balance zero**.
//!
//! Fees arrive in basis points (`makerCommission: 10` is 0.10%) and are divided
//! by 10 000 here. That is the only arithmetic in the file, and the test below
//! pins it, because getting it wrong would scale every fee in the ledger by
//! four orders of magnitude.
//!
//! What this adapter deliberately does not model: `can_withdraw`/`can_deposit`.
//! Neither affects an order, and inventing a meaning for them would be worse
//! than leaving them out. `can_trade` *does* affect an order, so it is kept.

use binance::account::Account;
use binance::api::Binance;
use binance::model::{AccountInformation, Balance};

use crate::exchange::{
    AccountConnector, AccountSnapshot, AssetBalance, ConnectorError, Venue, VenueSpec,
};

use super::trade::map_error;

/// Binance spot account view over REST.
pub struct BinanceAccount {
    account: Account,
    spec: VenueSpec,
}

impl BinanceAccount {
    pub fn new(api_key: Option<String>, secret_key: Option<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            account: Binance::new(api_key, secret_key),
            spec: super::spot_spec(),
        }
    }

    /// Point the client at testnet, whose balances are not real money.
    pub fn testnet(mut self, on: bool) -> Self {
        self.account.set_testnet(on);
        self
    }

    /// Replace the declared schedule — e.g. with fees read from a snapshot.
    pub fn with_spec(mut self, spec: VenueSpec) -> Self {
        self.spec = spec;
        self
    }

    /// The venue's raw answer, for anything this adapter does not model.
    pub fn raw_account(&self) -> Result<AccountInformation, ConnectorError> {
        self.account.get_account().map_err(map_error)
    }
}

impl Venue for BinanceAccount {
    fn venue(&self) -> VenueSpec {
        self.spec.clone()
    }
}

impl AccountConnector for BinanceAccount {
    fn snapshot(&self) -> Result<AccountSnapshot, ConnectorError> {
        let information = self.raw_account()?;
        Ok(snapshot_from_account(
            &information,
            self.spec.name,
            &self.spec,
        ))
    }
}

/// The venue's answer as an [`AccountSnapshot`].
///
/// Pure, so the whole translation — including the basis-point conversion and
/// the zero-row filter — is testable without a network.
pub fn snapshot_from_account(
    information: &AccountInformation,
    venue: &'static str,
    fallback: &VenueSpec,
) -> AccountSnapshot {
    let balances = information
        .balances
        .iter()
        .filter(|balance| !is_empty(balance))
        .map(|balance| (balance.asset.clone(), asset_balance(balance)))
        .collect();
    // A venue reporting 0/0 has said nothing useful, and the declared schedule
    // is the better guess. When they disagree, overstating the fee understates
    // pnl — the direction worth being wrong in.
    let reports_fees = information.maker_commission > 0.0 || information.taker_commission > 0.0;
    let (maker_fee, taker_fee) = if reports_fees {
        (
            fee_from_basis_points(information.maker_commission),
            fee_from_basis_points(information.taker_commission),
        )
    } else {
        (fallback.maker_fee, fallback.taker_fee)
    };
    AccountSnapshot {
        balances,
        can_trade: information.can_trade,
        maker_fee,
        taker_fee,
        ..AccountSnapshot::new(venue)
    }
}

/// Binance quotes fees in basis points: 10 means 0.10%.
pub fn fee_from_basis_points(basis_points: f32) -> f64 {
    basis_points as f64 / 10_000.0
}

/// Parse one of the venue's fixed-width decimal strings. An unreadable value
/// is zero rather than a reason to fail the whole reconciliation.
fn parse_quantity(text: &str) -> f64 {
    text.trim().parse().unwrap_or(0.0)
}

/// One asset row, both quantities parsed leniently.
fn asset_balance(balance: &Balance) -> AssetBalance {
    AssetBalance {
        free: parse_quantity(&balance.free),
        locked: parse_quantity(&balance.locked),
    }
}

/// True when the row carries nothing. Zero exactly, not "small": an asset with
/// 1e-9 left is a balance the venue still reports and the ledger should see.
fn is_empty(balance: &Balance) -> bool {
    parse_quantity(&balance.free) == 0.0 && parse_quantity(&balance.locked) == 0.0
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn information(maker: f32, taker: f32, balances: Vec<Balance>) -> AccountInformation {
        AccountInformation {
            maker_commission: maker,
            taker_commission: taker,
            buyer_commission: 0.0,
            seller_commission: 0.0,
            can_trade: true,
            can_withdraw: true,
            can_deposit: true,
            balances,
        }
    }

    fn balance(asset: &str, free: &str, locked: &str) -> Balance {
        Balance {
            asset: asset.to_string(),
            free: free.to_string(),
            locked: locked.to_string(),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn only_assets_carrying_value_survive_the_translation() {
        let venue = information(
            10.0,
            10.0,
            vec![
                balance("BTC", "1.50000000", "0.00000000"),
                balance("DOGE", "0.00000000", "0.00000000"),
                balance("USDT", "900.00000000", "100.00000000"),
                // Dust is still a balance: the venue reports it, so the ledger
                // must see it rather than silently agreeing with a zero.
                balance("SHIB", "0.00000001", "0.00000000"),
                // Unreadable text is treated as zero, so the row goes too.
                balance("BROKEN", "not-a-number", "0.00000000"),
            ],
        );
        let snapshot = snapshot_from_account(&venue, "binance", &super::super::spot_spec());

        assert_eq!(snapshot.venue, "binance");
        assert!(
            snapshot.can_trade,
            "can_trade decides whether an order is legal"
        );
        assert_eq!(snapshot.balances.len(), 3, "DOGE and BROKEN dropped");
        assert_eq!(snapshot.balances["BTC"].free, 1.5);
        assert_eq!(snapshot.balances["USDT"].locked, 100.0);
        assert_eq!(snapshot.balances["SHIB"].free, 1e-8);
        assert!(!snapshot.balances.contains_key("DOGE"), "absent means zero");
    }

    #[test]
    fn basis_points_become_fee_fractions() {
        // Binance's makerCommission is 10 for a 0.10% spot account.
        assert!((fee_from_basis_points(10.0) - 0.001).abs() < 1e-12);
        assert!((fee_from_basis_points(7.5) - 0.00075).abs() < 1e-12);
        assert_eq!(fee_from_basis_points(0.0), 0.0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn fees_the_venue_does_not_report_fall_back_to_the_declared_schedule() {
        let venue = information(0.0, 0.0, vec![balance("USDT", "10.0", "0.0")]);
        let spec = super::super::spot_spec();
        let snapshot = snapshot_from_account(&venue, "binance", &spec);
        assert_eq!(snapshot.maker_fee, spec.maker_fee);
        assert_eq!(snapshot.taker_fee, spec.taker_fee);

        let venue = information(10.0, 15.0, vec![balance("USDT", "10.0", "0.0")]);
        let snapshot = snapshot_from_account(&venue, "binance", &spec);
        assert!(
            (snapshot.maker_fee - 0.001).abs() < 1e-12,
            "the account's own fee"
        );
        assert!(
            (snapshot.taker_fee - 0.0015).abs() < 1e-12,
            "and its taker tier"
        );
    }
}


===== FILE: uets/executor/connectors/binance/mod.rs =====
//! Binance spot: one adapter per trait of the contract in `crate::exchange`.
//!
//! | file | trait | transport |
//! |------|-------|-----------|
//! | [`trade`] | `ExecutionConnector` + `MarketDataConnector` | REST (`/api/v3/order`, `/api/v3/ticker/price`) |
//! | [`account`] | `AccountConnector` | REST (`/api/v3/account`) |
//! | [`websocket`] | none — it feeds the feature engine | websockets |
//!
//! `trade` and `account` are separate files for the same reason the traits are
//! separate: the order path runs on every decision and must stay short enough to
//! audit, while the balance path runs once a session and is mostly a
//! translation table.
//!
//! The default fee schedule below is Binance spot's published 0.1%: it is a
//! *default*, replaced per-account by whatever `account::BinanceAccount` reads
//! from the venue. Nothing here assumes 0.1% is what any given account pays.

pub mod account;
pub mod trade;
pub mod websocket;

pub use account::BinanceAccount;
pub use trade::BinanceTrade;

use crate::exchange::VenueSpec;

/// The published spot schedule, used until the account reports its own.
///
/// BTCUSDT tick is 0.01 (1 cent): with filters set, `round_price` snaps every
/// limit to the grid and `submit` rejects off-tick limits locally instead of
/// paying a round trip for the venue's INVALID_TICK_SIZE.
pub fn spot_spec() -> VenueSpec {
    VenueSpec::new("binance", "USDT")
        .with_fees(0.001, 0.001)
        .with_filters(0.01, 0.00001)
}


===== FILE: uets/executor/connectors/binance/trade.rs =====
//! Binance spot: REST execution and marks.
//!
//! Implements `ExecutionConnector` and `MarketDataConnector` over the `binance`
//! crate's synchronous REST client. The streams under `websocket/` are a
//! separate concern; nothing here touches a socket.
//!
//! # Order identity
//!
//! The `binance` crate always generates its own `newClientOrderId` (a uuid) and
//! offers no way to supply one, so a `Fill` carries the *venue's* client id
//! rather than an id chosen here. That is accepted rather than worked around:
//! the venue's id is the only one a later cancel or reconciliation can be sure
//! refers to the same order, so the executor keys on what the venue reports.
//!
//! # Fees
//!
//! Commission is taken from each fill's `commission` field, but only counted
//! when it was charged in the pair's quote asset. With BNB fee payment enabled
//! Binance charges commission *in BNB*, which needs a BNB mark to convert; this
//! adapter reports `0.0` for such a fill instead of inventing a conversion.
//!
//! # `reduce_only` and `time_in_force`
//!
//! Spot has no reduce-only flag, so that hint is dropped here: a caller that
//! must not flip a position has to size the order itself, which is exactly what
//! `ExecutionConnector::exit` does. Time in force is GTC for every limit order
//! because the crate hard-codes it on that path — a limit that must not rest
//! would need `custom_order`, and pretending otherwise would be worse than the
//! limitation.

use binance::account::Account;
use binance::api::Binance;
use binance::errors::{Error as BinanceError, ErrorKind as BinanceErrorKind};
use binance::market::Market;
use binance::model::{Order as VenueOrder, Transaction};

use crate::core::order::{Fill, Order, OrderId, OrderRequest, OrderStatus, OrderType, Side};
use crate::exchange::{
    AccountSnapshot, ConnectorError, ExecutionConnector, MarketDataConnector, Venue, VenueSpec,
};

/// Binance spot over REST.
pub struct BinanceTrade {
    account: Account,
    market: Market,
    spec: VenueSpec,
}

impl BinanceTrade {
    /// Market data needs no credentials, so only the account client is keyed.
    pub fn new(api_key: Option<String>, secret_key: Option<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            account: Binance::new(api_key, secret_key),
            market: Binance::new(None, None),
            spec: super::spot_spec(),
        }
    }

    /// Point both clients at testnet, where orders are validated by the real
    /// matching engine's rules but never filled against the book.
    pub fn testnet(mut self, on: bool) -> Self {
        self.account.set_testnet(on);
        self.market.set_testnet(on);
        self
    }

    /// Replace the declared schedule, e.g. with one an account snapshot read.
    pub fn with_spec(mut self, spec: VenueSpec) -> Self {
        self.spec = spec;
        self
    }
}

impl Venue for BinanceTrade {
    fn venue(&self) -> VenueSpec {
        self.spec.clone()
    }
}

impl MarketDataConnector for BinanceTrade {
    fn mark_price(&self, symbol: &str) -> Result<f64, ConnectorError> {
        let price = self
            .market
            .get_price(symbol.to_string())
            .map_err(map_error)?;
        Ok(price.price)
    }
}

impl ExecutionConnector for BinanceTrade {
    fn submit(&mut self, request: &OrderRequest) -> Result<Vec<Fill>, ConnectorError> {
        request.validate()?;
        // Tick-grid gate: an off-tick limit fails HERE with a local
        // OffTickPrice rejection instead of costing a round trip for the
        // venue's INVALID_TICK_SIZE.
        if request.order_type == OrderType::Limit {
            request.validate_on_tick(self.spec.price_tick)?;
        }
        let symbol = request.symbol.clone();
        // Snap to the venue's step *before* sending: an oversized lot is
        // rejected outright, and a rejection costs a round trip that the check
        // below makes unnecessary.
        let qty = self.spec.round_qty(request.qty);
        if qty <= 0.0 {
            return Err(ConnectorError::rejected(format!(
                "{qty} is below the venue quantity step"
            )));
        }
        let quote = AccountSnapshot::quote_asset(&symbol).to_string();
        let transaction = match (request.order_type, request.side) {
            (OrderType::Market, Side::Buy) => self.account.market_buy(symbol, qty),
            (OrderType::Market, Side::Sell) => self.account.market_sell(symbol, qty),
            (OrderType::Limit, Side::Buy) => {
                let price = self.spec.round_price(
                    request
                        .limit_price
                        .ok_or(crate::core::order::OrderError::MissingLimitPrice)?,
                );
                self.account.limit_buy(symbol, qty, price)
            }
            (OrderType::Limit, Side::Sell) => {
                let price = self.spec.round_price(
                    request
                        .limit_price
                        .ok_or(crate::core::order::OrderError::MissingLimitPrice)?,
                );
                self.account.limit_sell(symbol, qty, price)
            }
        }
        .map_err(map_error)?;
        fills_from_transaction(&transaction, &quote)
    }

    fn cancel(&mut self, symbol: &str, order_id: &OrderId) -> Result<(), ConnectorError> {
        // A numeric id is the venue's own; anything else is a client id. Both
        // are accepted because both are what a caller can hold after a restart.
        match order_id.as_str().parse::<u64>() {
            Ok(numeric) => self.account.cancel_order(symbol.to_string(), numeric),
            Err(_) => self
                .account
                .cancel_order_with_client_id(symbol.to_string(), order_id.as_str().to_string()),
        }
        .map(|_| ())
        .map_err(map_error)
    }

    fn open_orders(&mut self, symbol: Option<&str>) -> Result<Vec<Order>, ConnectorError> {
        let orders = match symbol {
            Some(symbol) => self.account.get_open_orders(symbol.to_string()),
            None => self.account.get_all_open_orders(),
        }
        .map_err(map_error)?;
        orders.iter().map(order_from_venue).collect()
    }
}
/// Binance's "too many requests" code, which arrives inside a 400 body rather
/// than as an HTTP 429 on some endpoints.
const RATE_LIMIT_CODE: i16 = -1003;

/// Translate a venue error into the one thing the caller needs to know:
/// whether to back off and retry, or to give up on this order.
pub fn map_error(error: BinanceError) -> ConnectorError {
    if let BinanceErrorKind::BinanceError(content) = error.kind() {
        let text = format!("[{}] {}", content.code, content.msg);
        return if content.code == RATE_LIMIT_CODE {
            ConnectorError::RateLimited(text)
        } else {
            ConnectorError::Rejected(text)
        };
    }
    if let BinanceErrorKind::Msg(msg) = error.kind() {
        return classify_message(msg);
    }
    let text = error.to_string();
    match error.kind() {
        BinanceErrorKind::ReqError(_)
        | BinanceErrorKind::IoError(_)
        | BinanceErrorKind::Tungstenite(_)
        | BinanceErrorKind::TimestampError(_) => ConnectorError::Transport(text),
        // A Json/ParseFloat/Url error means the answer arrived and could not be
        // read, which is a decoding defect on this side, not a quiet venue.
        _ => ConnectorError::Decode(text),
    }
}

/// Classify a status-level failure. The crate reports HTTP 5xx, 401 and 429 as
/// bare messages, so the distinction has to be recovered from the text.
fn classify_message(msg: &str) -> ConnectorError {
    let lower = msg.to_ascii_lowercase();
    if lower.contains("429") || lower.contains("too many") {
        ConnectorError::RateLimited(msg.to_string())
    } else if lower.contains("received response") || lower.contains("unauthorized") {
        ConnectorError::Rejected(msg.to_string())
    } else {
        // "Internal Server Error" / "Service Unavailable": the venue is broken,
        // and the same request may well succeed shortly.
        ConnectorError::Transport(msg.to_string())
    }
}

/// A venue acknowledgement as the fills `core` understands.
pub fn fills_from_transaction(
    transaction: &Transaction,
    quote_asset: &str,
) -> Result<Vec<Fill>, ConnectorError> {
    let side = side_from_str(&transaction.side)?;
    let order_id = OrderId::new(transaction.client_order_id.clone());
    let Some(fills) = transaction.fills.as_ref().filter(|f| !f.is_empty()) else {
        // No executions: a resting limit order. `executed_qty` is zero, and
        // emitting a fill for it would invent a trade that has not happened.
        if transaction.executed_qty <= 0.0 {
            return Ok(Vec::new());
        }
        // A market order can report a fill without the array; the volume-weighted
        // price is then the only honest price available.
        let price = if transaction.price > 0.0 {
            transaction.price
        } else {
            transaction.cummulative_quote_qty / transaction.executed_qty
        };
        return Ok(vec![Fill::new(
            order_id,
            transaction.symbol.clone(),
            side,
            transaction.executed_qty,
            price,
            0.0,
        )]);
    };
    Ok(fills
        .iter()
        .map(|fill| {
            Fill::new(
                order_id.clone(),
                transaction.symbol.clone(),
                side,
                fill.qty,
                fill.price,
                fee_in_quote(fill.commission, &fill.commission_asset, quote_asset),
            )
        })
        .collect())
}

/// Commission, counted only when it was charged in the quote asset.
///
/// A discount-schedule fill is commissioned in BNB, and converting that needs
/// the BNB mark. Reporting `0.0` (and so slightly overstating pnl) is a stated
/// gap; converting at a guessed rate would be an unstated error.
fn fee_in_quote(commission: f64, commission_asset: &str, quote_asset: &str) -> f64 {
    if commission_asset.eq_ignore_ascii_case(quote_asset) {
        commission
    } else {
        0.0
    }
}

/// `"BUY"` / `"SELL"` as a [`Side`].
pub fn side_from_str(side: &str) -> Result<Side, ConnectorError> {
    match side.to_ascii_uppercase().as_str() {
        "BUY" => Ok(Side::Buy),
        "SELL" => Ok(Side::Sell),
        other => Err(ConnectorError::decode(format!(
            "unknown order side {other:?}"
        ))),
    }
}

/// The venue's order status as a local [`OrderStatus`].
pub fn status_from_str(status: &str) -> Result<OrderStatus, ConnectorError> {
    match status.to_ascii_uppercase().as_str() {
        "NEW" => Ok(OrderStatus::Open),
        "PENDING_CANCEL" => Ok(OrderStatus::Pending),
        "PARTIALLY_FILLED" => Ok(OrderStatus::PartiallyFilled),
        "FILLED" => Ok(OrderStatus::Filled),
        "CANCELED" | "EXPIRED" => Ok(OrderStatus::Cancelled),
        "REJECTED" => Ok(OrderStatus::Rejected),
        other => Err(ConnectorError::decode(format!(
            "unknown order status {other:?}"
        ))),
    }
}

/// A quantity the venue reports as a string.
///
/// Unparseable text is `0.0` rather than an error: the venue sends an empty
/// string for "no quantity", and failing on it would turn every unfilled order
/// into a spurious failure.
fn qty_from_str(text: &str) -> f64 {
    text.parse().unwrap_or(0.0)
}

/// One of the venue's open orders as a locally tracked [`Order`].
pub fn order_from_venue(venue: &VenueOrder) -> Result<Order, ConnectorError> {
    let side = side_from_str(&venue.side)?;
    let order_type = match venue.type_name.to_ascii_uppercase().as_str() {
        "LIMIT" | "LIMIT_MAKER" | "STOP_LOSS_LIMIT" | "TAKE_PROFIT_LIMIT" => OrderType::Limit,
        "MARKET" => OrderType::Market,
        other => {
            return Err(ConnectorError::decode(format!(
                "unsupported order type {other:?}"
            )));
        }
    };
    let qty = qty_from_str(&venue.orig_qty);
    let request = match order_type {
        // Venue-reported price is already on-tick; re-snap defensively so a
        // decoded order always carries the tick-grid value.
        OrderType::Limit => OrderRequest::limit(venue.symbol.clone(), side, qty, venue.price, 0.01),
        OrderType::Market => OrderRequest::market(venue.symbol.clone(), side, qty),
    };
    let mut order = Order::new(OrderId::new(venue.client_order_id.clone()), request);
    order.status = status_from_str(&venue.status)?;
    order.filled_qty = qty_from_str(&venue.executed_qty);
    // The venue leaves `price` at zero for market orders; an average of zero
    // would be a wrong entry price on every such order, so it stays absent.
    order.avg_fill_price = (order.filled_qty > 0.0 && venue.price > 0.0).then_some(venue.price);
    Ok(order)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use binance::errors::BinanceContentError;
    use binance::model::FillInfo;

    /// A venue acknowledgement. Every field is set explicitly: a defaulted
    /// struct would hide the difference between "zero" and "the venue sent 0".
    fn ack(side: &str, executed_qty: f64, fills: Option<Vec<FillInfo>>) -> Transaction {
        Transaction {
            symbol: "BTCUSDT".to_string(),
            order_id: 42,
            order_list_id: None,
            client_order_id: "uuid-1".to_string(),
            transact_time: 1_700_000_000_000,
            price: 0.0,
            orig_qty: 1.0,
            executed_qty,
            cummulative_quote_qty: 100.0 * executed_qty,
            stop_price: 0.0,
            status: "FILLED".to_string(),
            time_in_force: "GTC".to_string(),
            type_name: "MARKET".to_string(),
            side: side.to_string(),
            fills,
        }
    }

    fn fill_info(price: f64, qty: f64, commission: f64, asset: &str) -> FillInfo {
        FillInfo {
            price,
            qty,
            commission,
            commission_asset: asset.to_string(),
            trade_id: None,
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_acknowledgement_with_executions_becomes_one_fill_per_print() {
        let transaction = ack(
            "BUY",
            1.0,
            Some(vec![
                fill_info(100.0, 0.6, 0.06, "USDT"),
                fill_info(101.0, 0.4, 0.04, "USDT"),
            ]),
        );
        let fills = fills_from_transaction(&transaction, "USDT").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(fills.len(), 2, "one Fill per print, not one per order");
        assert_eq!(fills[0].side, Side::Buy);
        assert_eq!(fills[0].order_id, OrderId::new("uuid-1"));
        assert_eq!(fills[1].qty, 0.4);
        assert_eq!(fills[1].price, 101.0);
        assert_eq!(fills[1].fee, 0.04);
        assert_eq!(fills[1].symbol, "BTCUSDT");
    }

    #[test]
    fn commission_paid_in_bnb_is_not_counted_as_quote() {
        let transaction = ack(
            "SELL",
            1.0,
            Some(vec![fill_info(100.0, 1.0, 0.0004, "BNB")]),
        );
        let fills = fills_from_transaction(&transaction, "USDT").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            fills[0].fee, 0.0,
            "a BNB fee needs the BNB mark, which this adapter does not fetch"
        );

        let transaction = ack("SELL", 1.0, Some(vec![fill_info(100.0, 1.0, 0.1, "usdt")]));
        let fills = fills_from_transaction(&transaction, "USDT").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            fills[0].fee, 0.1,
            "asset comparison ignores case: the venue is inconsistent about it"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_resting_limit_acknowledgement_produces_no_fills() {
        let mut transaction = ack("BUY", 0.0, None);
        transaction.status = "NEW".to_string();
        assert!(
            fills_from_transaction(&transaction, "USDT")
                .unwrap()
                .is_empty(),
            "an order waiting on the book has traded nothing"
        );

        // A fill reported without the array still counts, priced by the
        // volume-weighted total the venue reports.
        let mut transaction = ack("BUY", 2.0, None);
        transaction.price = 0.0;
        transaction.cummulative_quote_qty = 204.0;
        let fills = fills_from_transaction(&transaction, "USDT").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(fills.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(fills[0].qty, 2.0);
        assert_eq!(fills[0].price, 102.0, "204 / 2");
    }
    #[test]
    fn an_open_order_is_tracked_with_its_own_quantity_and_status() {
        let venue = VenueOrder {
            symbol: "ETHUSDT".to_string(),
            order_id: 7,
            order_list_id: -1,
            client_order_id: "uuid-2".to_string(),
            price: 2000.0,
            orig_qty: "0.500".to_string(),
            executed_qty: "0.250".to_string(),
            cummulative_quote_qty: "500.0".to_string(),
            status: "PARTIALLY_FILLED".to_string(),
            time_in_force: "GTC".to_string(),
            type_name: "LIMIT".to_string(),
            side: "sell".to_string(),
            stop_price: 0.0,
            iceberg_qty: "0".to_string(),
            time: 0,
            update_time: 0,
            is_working: true,
            orig_quote_order_qty: "0".to_string(),
        };
        let order = order_from_venue(&venue).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(order.status, OrderStatus::PartiallyFilled);
        assert_eq!(order.filled_qty, 0.25);
        assert_eq!(order.avg_fill_price, Some(2000.0));
        assert_eq!(order.request.side, Side::Sell);
        assert_eq!(order.request.qty, 0.5);
        assert_eq!(order.request.order_type, OrderType::Limit);
        assert_eq!(order.remaining(), 0.25, "0.5 ordered, 0.25 filled");

        // A market order has no resting price; reporting 0.0 as an average
        // fill price would poison any entry price that reads it.
        let mut market = venue.clone();
        market.type_name = "MARKET".to_string();
        market.price = 0.0;
        market.status = "NEW".to_string();
        let order = order_from_venue(&market).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(order.request.order_type, OrderType::Market);
        assert_eq!(order.avg_fill_price, None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_unreadable_side_or_status_is_a_decoding_defect() {
        assert!(matches!(
            side_from_str("HOLD"),
            Err(ConnectorError::Decode(_))
        ));
        assert!(matches!(
            status_from_str("MAYBE"),
            Err(ConnectorError::Decode(_))
        ));
        assert_eq!(status_from_str("canceled").unwrap(), OrderStatus::Cancelled);
        assert_eq!(status_from_str("NEW").unwrap(), OrderStatus::Open);
    }

    #[test]
    fn venue_errors_classify_by_what_the_caller_should_do() {
        let rate_limited: BinanceError = BinanceErrorKind::BinanceError(BinanceContentError {
            code: RATE_LIMIT_CODE,
            msg: "Too many requests".to_string(),
        })
        .into();
        assert!(map_error(rate_limited).is_retryable());

        let rejected: BinanceError = BinanceErrorKind::BinanceError(BinanceContentError {
            code: -2010,
            msg: "Account has insufficient balance.".to_string(),
        })
        .into();
        assert!(matches!(map_error(rejected), ConnectorError::Rejected(_)));

        let unauthorized: BinanceError = BinanceErrorKind::Msg("Unauthorized".into()).into();
        assert!(matches!(
            map_error(unauthorized),
            ConnectorError::Rejected(_)
        ));

        let throttled: BinanceError = BinanceErrorKind::Msg("429 Too Many".into()).into();
        assert!(map_error(throttled).is_retryable());

        let unavailable: BinanceError = BinanceErrorKind::Msg("Service Unavailable".into()).into();
        assert!(
            map_error(unavailable).is_retryable(),
            "a broken venue is worth retrying; a rejected order is not"
        );

        let unreadable: BinanceError =
            BinanceErrorKind::ParseFloatError("x".parse::<f64>().unwrap_err()).into();
        assert!(matches!(map_error(unreadable), ConnectorError::Decode(_)));
    }
}


===== FILE: uets/executor/connectors/binance/websocket/diagnostic.rs =====
//! `diagnostic.rs` — one row per websocket event, never merged.
//!
//! The canonical capture in [`live`] merges aggTrades and LOB updates into
//! one wide row (absent blocks left empty) so the table matches history.
//! This module deliberately does the *opposite*, because a diagnostic wants
//! to see each frame exactly as it arrived:
//!
//! ```text
//! timestamp,update_type,agg__price,...,lob__bid_p1,...,lob__ask_q5
//! 1751245200083,aggTrades,108665.6,...,NaN,...,NaN
//! 1751245200094,orderbook,NaN,...,108663.2,...,108666.0
//! ```
//!
//! | column group | filled when | otherwise |
//! |--------------|-------------|-----------|
//! | `agg__*` | `update_type = aggTrades` | `NaN` |
//! | `lob__*` | `update_type = orderbook` | `NaN` |
//!
//! `update_type` is therefore the discriminator: a row belongs to exactly one
//! stream, and the other stream's columns are `NaN` — the CSV spelling a
//! reader like pandas already treats as missing. "Latest info available"
//! means a row carries that stream's *latest* frame and nothing back-filled
//! from the other one.
//!
//! ```text
//! cargo run --bin dev_test -- diag btcusdt
//! ```
//!
//! runs both streams (`@aggTrade` + `@depth20@100ms`), prints each row, and
//! appends them to `out/live/<symbol>_diag_<epoch>.csv`.

use std::io::{self, Write};
use std::sync::mpsc::{self, Sender};
use std::time::{Duration, SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

use serde_json::Value;
use tungstenite::{Message, connect};

/// Book levels carried per orderbook row. Beyond this the frame's levels are
/// dropped: a diagnostic row is for eyeballing, not for reconstructing depth.
pub const BOOK_LEVELS: usize = 5;

/// Which stream a row came from.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum UpdateType {
    /// The aggregated-trade stream.
    AggTrades,
    /// The order-book stream (partial or diff).
    Orderbook,
}

impl UpdateType {
    pub fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::AggTrades => "aggTrades",
            Self::Orderbook => "orderbook",
        }
    }
}

/// The trade half of a row.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct AggTradeData {
    pub price: f64,
    pub quantity: f64,
    /// Aggressor side: `is_buyer_maker` in, taker side out (`m = true` means
    /// the buyer rested, so the aggressor sold).
    pub side: &'static str,
    pub trade_id: Option<u64>,
    pub event_time_ms: Option<u64>,
    pub transact_time_ms: Option<u64>,
}

/// The book half of a row: whatever levels the frame carried, as arrived.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct OrderbookData {
    pub update_id: Option<u64>,
    pub event_time_ms: Option<u64>,
    pub bids: Vec<(f64, f64)>,
    pub asks: Vec<(f64, f64)>,
}

/// One diagnostic row: exactly one stream's latest frame.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct DiagnosticRow {
    /// Local receive time, milliseconds since the Unix epoch.
    pub timestamp: u64,
    pub update_type: UpdateType,
    pub agg: Option<AggTradeData>,
    pub lob: Option<OrderbookData>,
}

/// A numeric field that may arrive as a JSON string or a number.
fn num(value: &Value, key: &str) -> Option<f64> {
    let raw = value.get(key)?;
    raw.as_str()
        .and_then(|s| s.parse().ok())
        .or_else(|| raw.as_f64())
}

/// An integer field that may arrive as a JSON string or a number.
fn uint(value: &Value, key: &str) -> Option<u64> {
    let raw = value.get(key)?;
    raw.as_u64()
        .or_else(|| raw.as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()))
}

/// Price/qty levels from a `[[p, q], ...]` array, capped at [`BOOK_LEVELS`].
fn levels(value: Option<&Value>) -> Vec<(f64, f64)> {
    value
        .and_then(Value::as_array)
        .map(|entries| {
            entries
                .iter()
                .take(BOOK_LEVELS)
                .filter_map(|entry| {
                    let pair = entry.as_array()?;
                    let price = pair.first()?.as_str()?.parse().ok()?;
                    let qty = pair.get(1)?.as_str()?.parse().ok()?;
                    Some((price, qty))
                })
                .collect()
        })
        .unwrap_or_default()
}

impl DiagnosticRow {
    /// Parse one websocket text frame. Returns `None` for anything that is
    /// neither an aggTrade nor an order-book update, so unrelated streams
    /// (klines, tickers, heartbeats) cannot pollute the diagnostic table.
    pub fn from_frame(text: &str, received_ms: u64) -> Option<Self> {
        let mut value: Value = serde_json::from_str(text).ok()?;
        // Combined-stream envelopes wrap the payload in `data`.
        if value.get("stream").is_some() {
            value = value.get("data")?.clone();
        }
        let event = value.get("e").and_then(Value::as_str);

        if event == Some("aggTrade") {
            let maker = value.get("m").and_then(Value::as_bool).unwrap_or(false);
            return Some(Self {
                timestamp: received_ms,
                update_type: UpdateType::AggTrades,
                agg: Some(AggTradeData {
                    price: num(&value, "p")?,
                    quantity: num(&value, "q")?,
                    side: if maker { "sell" } else { "buy" },
                    trade_id: uint(&value, "a"),
                    event_time_ms: uint(&value, "E"),
                    transact_time_ms: uint(&value, "T"),
                }),
                lob: None,
            });
        }

        // Partial book: `{lastUpdateId, bids, asks}` (full snapshots).
        // Diff book: `{e:"depthUpdate", U, u, b, a}` (changed levels only).
        let partial = value.get("lastUpdateId").is_some() && value.get("bids").is_some();
        let diff = (value.get("b").is_some() || value.get("a").is_some())
            && (event == Some("depthUpdate") || value.get("u").is_some());
        if partial || diff {
            return Some(Self {
                timestamp: received_ms,
                update_type: UpdateType::Orderbook,
                agg: None,
                lob: Some(OrderbookData {
                    update_id: if partial {
                        uint(&value, "lastUpdateId")
                    } else {
                        uint(&value, "u")
                    },
                    event_time_ms: uint(&value, "E"),
                    bids: levels(value.get(if partial { "bids" } else { "b" })),
                    asks: levels(value.get(if partial { "asks" } else { "a" })),
                }),
            });
        }
        None
    }
}

/// The literal written into a cell whose data does not exist on this row —
/// the CSV spelling readers (pandas included) already treat as missing.
const NAN: &str = "NaN";

/// The full CSV header, in column order.
pub fn csv_header() -> String {
    let mut columns = vec![
        "timestamp".to_string(),
        "update_type".to_string(),
        "agg__price".to_string(),
        "agg__quantity".to_string(),
        "agg__side".to_string(),
        "agg__trade_id".to_string(),
        "agg__event_time_ms".to_string(),
        "agg__transact_time_ms".to_string(),
        "lob__update_id".to_string(),
        "lob__event_time_ms".to_string(),
    ];
    for level in 1..=BOOK_LEVELS {
        columns.push(format!("lob__bid_p{level}"));
        columns.push(format!("lob__bid_q{level}"));
        columns.push(format!("lob__ask_p{level}"));
        columns.push(format!("lob__ask_q{level}"));
    }
    columns.join(",")
}

fn cell(value: Option<u64>) -> String {
    value.map_or_else(|| NAN.to_string(), |v| v.to_string())
}

impl DiagnosticRow {
    /// One CSV line: this row's own stream filled, the other stream `NaN`.
    pub fn to_csv(&self) -> String {
        let mut cells = vec![
            self.timestamp.to_string(),
            self.update_type.as_str().to_string(),
        ];
        match &self.agg {
            Some(agg) => {
                cells.push(format!("{}", agg.price));
                cells.push(format!("{}", agg.quantity));
                cells.push(agg.side.to_string());
                cells.push(cell(agg.trade_id));
                cells.push(cell(agg.event_time_ms));
                cells.push(cell(agg.transact_time_ms));
            }
            None => cells.extend(vec![NAN.to_string(); 6]),
        }
        match &self.lob {
            Some(lob) => {
                cells.push(cell(lob.update_id));
                cells.push(cell(lob.event_time_ms));
                for level in 0..BOOK_LEVELS {
                    for side in [&lob.bids, &lob.asks] {
                        match side.get(level) {
                            Some((price, qty)) => {
                                cells.push(format!("{price}"));
                                cells.push(format!("{qty}"));
                            }
                            None => {
                                cells.push(NAN.to_string());
                                cells.push(NAN.to_string());
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            None => cells.extend(vec![NAN.to_string(); 2 + BOOK_LEVELS * 4]),
        }
        cells.join(",")
    }
}

/// Appends header + rows to a writer, flushing on demand. Generic over
/// [`Write`], so the tests drive it from a `Vec<u8>` and `run` from a file.
pub struct DiagnosticCsv<W: Write> {
    inner: W,
}

impl<W: Write> DiagnosticCsv<W> {
    /// Wrap `inner` and write the header line immediately.
    pub fn new(mut inner: W) -> io::Result<Self> {
        writeln!(inner, "{}", csv_header())?;
        Ok(Self { inner })
    }

    pub fn write_row(&mut self, row: &DiagnosticRow) -> io::Result<()> {
        writeln!(self.inner, "{}", row.to_csv())
    }

    pub fn flush(&mut self) -> io::Result<()> {
        self.inner.flush()
    }
}

fn now_ms() -> u64 {
    SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
        .unwrap_or_default()
        .as_millis() as u64
}

/// Reconnect a stream forever, forwarding every text frame to `tx`.
fn spawn_stream(url: String, tx: Sender<String>) {
    std::thread::spawn(move || {
        loop {
            match connect(&url) {
                Ok((socket, _)) => {
                    let mut socket = socket;
                    loop {
                        match socket.read() {
                            Ok(Message::Text(text)) => {
                                if tx.send(text.to_string()).is_err() {
                                    return; // the reader is gone; nothing left to do
                                }
                            }
                            Ok(
                                Message::Ping(_)
                                | Message::Pong(_)
                                | Message::Binary(_)
                                | Message::Frame(_),
                            ) => {}
                            Ok(Message::Close(frame)) => {
                                eprintln!("[{url}] closed by server: {frame:?}");
                                break;
                            }
                            Err(e) => {
                                eprintln!("[{url}] error: {e}");
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                Err(e) => eprintln!("[{url}] connect failed: {e}"),
            }
            eprintln!("[{url}] reconnecting in 3s...");
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_secs(3));
        }
    });
}

/// Stream both feeds and print/write one diagnostic row per frame until the
/// process is interrupted.
///
/// The file is created (header included) before the sockets open, so a feed
/// that never connects still leaves a well-formed, empty diagnostic table
/// rather than nothing at all.
pub fn run(
    symbol: &str,
    output_dir: impl Into<std::path::PathBuf>,
) -> io::Result<std::path::PathBuf> {
    let symbol = symbol.to_uppercase();
    let low = symbol.to_lowercase();
    let dir = output_dir.into();
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir)?;
    let epoch = SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
        .unwrap_or_default()
        .as_secs();
    let path = dir.join(format!("{symbol}_diag_{epoch}.csv"));
    let file = std::fs::File::create(&path)?;
    let mut csv = DiagnosticCsv::new(io::BufWriter::new(file))?;

    let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel::<String>();
    spawn_stream(
        format!("wss://stream.binance.com:9443/ws/{low}@aggTrade"),
        tx.clone(),
    );
    spawn_stream(
        format!("wss://stream.binance.com:9443/ws/{low}@depth20@100ms"),
        tx,
    );

    println!("diag:    {symbol}  (aggTrades + depth20@100ms)");
    println!("columns: {}", csv_header());
    println!("file:    {}", path.display());
    println!("stop:    Ctrl+C");

    while let Ok(text) = rx.recv() {
        let Some(row) = DiagnosticRow::from_frame(&text, now_ms()) else {
            continue; // not a trade and not a book: not a diagnostic row
        };
        let line = row.to_csv();
        println!("{line}");
        csv.write_row(&row)?;
        csv.flush()?;
    }
    Ok(path)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    const AGG_FRAME: &str = r#"{"e":"aggTrade","E":1751245200083,"a":9,"p":"108665.600","q":"0.340","f":1,"l":5,"T":1751245200083,"m":false,"M":true}"#;
    const BOOK_FRAME: &str = r#"{"lastUpdateId":1017,"bids":[["108663.20","1.500"],["108662.90","0.400"]],"asks":[["108666.00","2.000"],["108667.10","0.250"]]}"#;
    const DIFF_FRAME: &str = r#"{"e":"depthUpdate","E":1751245200094,"s":"BTCUSDT","U":57,"u":58,"b":[["108660.00","0.600"]],"a":[["108668.00","1.100"]]}"#;

    fn cells(row: &DiagnosticRow) -> Vec<String> {
        row.to_csv().split(',').map(str::to_string).collect()
    }

    fn index_of(column: &str) -> usize {
        csv_header()
            .split(',')
            .position(|c| c == column)
            .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("no column {column}"))
    }

    fn columns_with(prefix: &str) -> Vec<String> {
        csv_header()
            .split(',')
            .filter(|c| c.starts_with(prefix))
            .map(str::to_string)
            .collect()
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_aggtrade_row_fills_the_trade_columns_and_nans_the_book() {
        let row = DiagnosticRow::from_frame(AGG_FRAME, 1_751_245_200_500).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(row.update_type, UpdateType::AggTrades);
        let agg = row.agg.as_ref().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(agg.price, 108665.6);
        assert_eq!(agg.quantity, 0.34);
        assert_eq!(agg.side, "buy", "m=false: the taker bought");
        assert_eq!(agg.trade_id, Some(9));
        assert_eq!(agg.event_time_ms, Some(1_751_245_200_083));
        assert!(row.lob.is_none(), "a trade carries no book");

        let row_cells = cells(&row);
        assert_eq!(row_cells[index_of("timestamp")], "1751245200500");
        assert_eq!(row_cells[index_of("update_type")], "aggTrades");
        assert_eq!(row_cells[index_of("agg__price")], "108665.6");
        assert_eq!(row_cells[index_of("agg__side")], "buy");
        for column in columns_with("lob__") {
            assert_eq!(
                row_cells[index_of(&column)],
                "NaN",
                "{column} on an aggTrades row"
            );
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_orderbook_row_fills_the_book_columns_and_nans_the_trade() {
        let row = DiagnosticRow::from_frame(BOOK_FRAME, 1_751_245_200_600).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(row.update_type, UpdateType::Orderbook);
        let lob = row.lob.as_ref().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(lob.update_id, Some(1017));
        assert_eq!(lob.bids.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(lob.bids[0], (108663.2, 1.5));
        assert!(row.agg.is_none(), "a book update carries no trade");

        let row_cells = cells(&row);
        assert_eq!(row_cells[index_of("update_type")], "orderbook");
        assert_eq!(row_cells[index_of("lob__update_id")], "1017");
        assert_eq!(row_cells[index_of("lob__bid_p1")], "108663.2");
        assert_eq!(row_cells[index_of("lob__ask_q1")], "2");
        assert_eq!(
            row_cells[index_of("lob__bid_p5")],
            "NaN",
            "levels the frame did not carry are NaN, never 0"
        );
        for column in columns_with("agg__") {
            assert_eq!(
                row_cells[index_of(&column)],
                "NaN",
                "{column} on an orderbook row"
            );
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn trades_and_book_updates_are_separate_rows_never_merged() {
        let rows = [
            DiagnosticRow::from_frame(AGG_FRAME, 1_000).unwrap(),
            DiagnosticRow::from_frame(BOOK_FRAME, 1_100).unwrap(),
        ];
        assert_eq!(rows.len(), 2, "one event, one row");
        assert_ne!(
            rows[0].update_type, rows[1].update_type,
            "the two streams stay distinguishable"
        );
        for row in &rows {
            assert_ne!(
                row.agg.is_some(),
                row.lob.is_some(),
                "a row belongs to exactly one stream"
            );
            let row_cells = cells(row);
            let agg_absent = row_cells[index_of("agg__price")] == "NaN";
            let lob_absent = row_cells[index_of("lob__bid_p1")] == "NaN";
            assert_ne!(
                agg_absent, lob_absent,
                "exactly one stream's columns are NaN"
            );
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_header_lists_every_column_once_and_rows_match_its_width() {
        let header = csv_header();
        let columns: Vec<&str> = header.split(',').collect();
        assert_eq!(columns[0], "timestamp");
        assert_eq!(columns[1], "update_type");
        assert_eq!(columns.iter().filter(|c| c.starts_with("agg__")).count(), 6);
        assert_eq!(
            columns.iter().filter(|c| c.starts_with("lob__")).count(),
            2 + BOOK_LEVELS * 4
        );
        let mut dedup = columns.clone();
        dedup.sort();
        dedup.dedup();
        assert_eq!(dedup.len(), columns.len(), "no duplicate column names");
        for frame in [AGG_FRAME, BOOK_FRAME, DIFF_FRAME] {
            let row = DiagnosticRow::from_frame(frame, 1).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(row.to_csv().split(',').count(), columns.len());
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn diff_depth_frames_are_orderbook_rows_too() {
        let row = DiagnosticRow::from_frame(DIFF_FRAME, 2_000).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(row.update_type, UpdateType::Orderbook);
        let lob = row.lob.as_ref().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            lob.update_id,
            Some(58),
            "the final update id identifies the book"
        );
        assert_eq!(lob.event_time_ms, Some(1_751_245_200_094));
        assert_eq!(lob.bids, vec![(108660.0, 0.6)]);
        assert_eq!(lob.asks, vec![(108668.0, 1.1)]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn unrelated_and_wrapped_frames_are_handled() {
        // A kline belongs to neither stream, so it produces no row.
        let kline = r#"{"e":"kline","E":1,"k":{"t":1,"s":"BTCUSDT","c":"1.0"}}"#;
        assert!(DiagnosticRow::from_frame(kline, 1).is_none());
        // Garbage is ignored, never a panic.
        assert!(DiagnosticRow::from_frame("not json", 1).is_none());
        assert!(DiagnosticRow::from_frame("", 1).is_none());
        // A combined-stream envelope unwraps to its payload.
        let wrapped = format!(r#"{{"stream":"btcusdt@aggTrade","data":{AGG_FRAME}}}"#);
        let row = DiagnosticRow::from_frame(&wrapped, 1).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(row.update_type, UpdateType::AggTrades);
        assert_eq!(row.agg.as_ref().unwrap().price, 108665.6);
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_csv_writer_emits_header_then_one_row_per_event() {
        let mut sink: Vec<u8> = Vec::new();
        {
            let mut csv = DiagnosticCsv::new(&mut sink).unwrap();
            let trade = DiagnosticRow::from_frame(AGG_FRAME, 1_000).unwrap();
            let book = DiagnosticRow::from_frame(BOOK_FRAME, 1_100).unwrap();
            csv.write_row(&trade).unwrap();
            csv.write_row(&book).unwrap();
            csv.flush().unwrap();
        }
        let text = String::from_utf8(sink).unwrap();
        let lines: Vec<&str> = text.lines().collect();
        assert_eq!(lines.len(), 3, "header + two rows, never merged");
        assert_eq!(lines[0], csv_header());
        assert!(lines[1].contains(",aggTrades,"));
        assert!(lines[2].contains(",orderbook,"));
        assert!(
            lines[1].contains(",NaN"),
            "book columns NaN on the trade row"
        );
        assert!(
            lines[2].contains(",NaN"),
            "trade columns NaN on the book row"
        );
    }
}


===== FILE: uets/executor/connectors/binance/websocket/live.rs =====
//! `live.rs` — the live capture pipeline: websocket -> merge -> CSV.
//!
//! This is the module that makes `executor.rs` produce the merged table. It is
//! the only place that knows about Binance's wire format *and* about writing
//! files; `event_merger_live` stays free of both, and `core::feature` stays free
//! of both, so each piece remains independently testable.
//!
//! ```text
//!   wss aggTrade   --\
//!                     >-- parse --> ReorderBuffer --> Merger --> CsvRecorder
//!   wss depth@100ms--/                                |
//!                                                      +--> FeatureRow (core::feature)
//! ```
//!
//! # Why a REST seed is mandatory
//!
//! The diff-depth stream carries only *changed* levels, so without a REST
//! snapshot the book would be built from partial information and every
//! `book_state__*` column would be wrong in a way that is invisible in the
//! output. The book is therefore seeded from REST before the diffs are
//! applied, and re-seeded whenever a sequence gap is detected.
//!
//! # Two sources of truth for `lob__final_update_id`
//!
//! This uses `@depth@100ms` (diffs) plus a REST seed, so the id is the last
//! *diff* applied to a full-depth book -- the same meaning it has in the
//! historical pipeline. The partial `@depth20@100ms` stream is deliberately
//! not used: its `lastUpdateId` identifies a different object.

use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, AtomicU64, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::{Duration, SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

use serde_json::Value;
use tungstenite::{Message, connect};

use event_merger_live::{
    BookSnapshot, CsvRecorder, DEFAULT_LATENESS_MS, DEFAULT_LEVELS, DiffFrame, MarketEvent, Merger,
    ReorderBuffer, Trade,
};

/// How long a capture may sit with zero rows before it is treated as broken.
///
/// A live feed that is working produces rows continuously, so a long silence
/// means the socket never connected rather than a quiet market.
const STALL_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(30);

/// How the capture is configured.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct CaptureConfig {
    pub symbol: String,
    /// Where the CSV is written. Parent directories are created on demand.
    pub output: std::path::PathBuf,
    pub levels: usize,
    /// Reorder window for the two independently-arriving sockets.
    pub lateness_ms: i64,
    /// Stop after this many canonical rows. `None` runs until interrupted.
    pub max_rows: Option<u64>,
    /// Append to an existing capture instead of truncating it.
    pub append: bool,
    /// Strategy instances that should receive framed feature-input rows.
    pub fanout_instances: Vec<String>,
}

impl CaptureConfig {
    /// A capture for `symbol` writing into `output_dir`.
    ///
    /// The default filename is stamped with the UTC start time so repeated
    /// captures never silently overwrite each other.
    pub fn new(symbol: &str, output_dir: impl Into<std::path::PathBuf>) -> Self {
        let symbol = symbol.to_uppercase();
        let output = output_dir
            .into()
            .join(format!("{symbol}_{}.csv", now_utc_compact()));
        Self {
            symbol,
            output,
            levels: DEFAULT_LEVELS,
            lateness_ms: DEFAULT_LATENESS_MS,
            max_rows: None,
            append: false,
            fanout_instances: Vec::new(),
        }
    }
}

/// Shared, mutex-guarded capture state.
///
/// Both stream threads push into this, so emission order is decided in exactly
/// one place: the [`ReorderBuffer`].
struct Capture {
    merger: Merger,
    reorder: ReorderBuffer,
    csv: CsvRecorder,
    rows_written: u64,
    reseeds_pending: bool,
    fanout: Option<std::sync::mpsc::SyncSender<Vec<u8>>>,
    fanout_failed: Arc<AtomicBool>,
}

impl Capture {
    /// Merge one event and write whatever rows become emittable.
    fn offer(&mut self, event: MarketEvent) -> Result<u64, String> {
        let mut written = 0;
        for ready in self.reorder.push(event) {
            let trade = match &ready {
                MarketEvent::Trade(trade) => Some(*trade),
                _ => None,
            };
            let (row, outcome) = self.merger.merge_event(ready);
            if outcome.needs_reseed {
                // Visible, not silent: the book is now wrong, so the next
                // row's book_state__* block cannot be trusted.
                self.reseeds_pending = true;
            }
            self.csv
                .write_row(&mut self.merger, &row)
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if let Some(fanout) = &self.fanout {
                let frame = match strategy_frame(&self.merger, trade, row.timestamp_ns / 1_000_000)
                {
                    Ok(frame) => frame,
                    Err(error) => {
                        self.fanout_failed.store(true, Ordering::Release);
                        return Err(error);
                    }
                };
                if let Err(error) = fanout.try_send(frame) {
                    self.fanout_failed.store(true, Ordering::Release);
                    return Err(match error {
                        std::sync::mpsc::TrySendError::Full(_) => {
                            "strategy socket queue saturated; stopping rather than dropping market data".to_string()
                        }
                        std::sync::mpsc::TrySendError::Disconnected(_) => {
                            "strategy socket sender disconnected".to_string()
                        }
                    });
                }
            }
            written += 1;
        }
        self.rows_written += written;
        Ok(written)
    }

    /// Emit everything still buffered, in total order.
    fn flush_pending(&mut self) -> Result<u64, String> {
        let mut written = 0;
        for ready in self.reorder.flush() {
            let (row, _) = self.merger.merge_event(ready);
            self.csv
                .write_row(&mut self.merger, &row)
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            written += 1;
        }
        self.rows_written += written;
        Ok(written)
    }
}

fn strategy_frame(
    merger: &Merger,
    trade: Option<Trade>,
    event_time_ms: i64,
) -> Result<Vec<u8>, String> {
    let state = merger.state();
    if !state.book_valid() {
        return Err("refusing to fan out a strategy row with an invalid order book".into());
    }
    let (bid_tick, bid_qty) = state
        .book
        .best_bid()
        .ok_or_else(|| "best bid unavailable".to_string())?;
    let (ask_tick, ask_qty) = state
        .book
        .best_ask()
        .ok_or_else(|| "best ask unavailable".to_string())?;
    let mut payload = format!(
        "event_time_ms:{event_time_ms}\nbid_price:{}\nbid_qty:{bid_qty}\nask_price:{}\nask_qty:{ask_qty}\n",
        event_merger_live::tick_to_price(bid_tick),
        event_merger_live::tick_to_price(ask_tick),
    );
    if let Some(trade) = trade {
        payload.push_str(&format!(
            "price:{}\namount:{}\nis_buyer_maker:{}\n",
            trade.price,
            trade.quantity,
            u8::from(trade.is_buyer_maker),
        ));
    } else {
        payload.push_str("amount:0\nis_buyer_maker:0\n");
    }
    let payload = payload.into_bytes();
    let mut frame = Vec::with_capacity(4 + payload.len());
    frame.extend_from_slice(&(payload.len() as u32).to_le_bytes());
    frame.extend_from_slice(&payload);
    Ok(frame)
}

#[cfg(unix)]
fn start_strategy_fanout(
    instances: &[String],
    symbol: &str,
    failed: Arc<AtomicBool>,
) -> Result<std::sync::mpsc::SyncSender<Vec<u8>>, String> {
    use std::io::Write;
    use std::os::unix::net::UnixStream;
    use std::path::PathBuf;

    let mut paths = Vec::new();
    for instance in instances {
        let Some((strategy, asset)) = instance.split_once('@') else {
            return Err(format!(
                "invalid strategy instance '{instance}'; expected strategy@asset"
            ));
        };
        if !strategy.eq_ignore_ascii_case("ofmr") || !asset.eq_ignore_ascii_case(symbol) {
            return Err(format!(
                "feed for {symbol} only supports ofmr@{symbol}, got '{instance}'"
            ));
        }
        paths.push(PathBuf::from(format!(
            "sockets/strategies/ofmr_{symbol}.sock"
        )));
    }
    if paths.is_empty() {
        return Err("strategy fan-out requires at least one configured instance".into());
    }
    let (sender, receiver) = std::sync::mpsc::sync_channel::<Vec<u8>>(4096);
    std::thread::Builder::new()
        .name(format!("uds-fanout-{symbol}"))
        .spawn(move || {
            let mut streams: Vec<Option<UnixStream>> = paths.iter().map(|_| None).collect();
            while let Ok(frame) = receiver.recv() {
                for (path, stream) in paths.iter().zip(streams.iter_mut()) {
                    if stream.is_none() {
                        match UnixStream::connect(path) {
                            Ok(connection) => *stream = Some(connection),
                            Err(error) => {
                                eprintln!("[fanout] connect {} failed: {error}", path.display());
                                failed.store(true, Ordering::Release);
                                return;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if let Some(connection) = stream {
                        if let Err(error) = connection.write_all(&frame) {
                            eprintln!("[fanout] write {} failed: {error}", path.display());
                            failed.store(true, Ordering::Release);
                            return;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        })
        .map_err(|error| format!("start strategy fan-out: {error}"))?;
    Ok(sender)
}

#[cfg(not(unix))]
fn start_strategy_fanout(
    _instances: &[String],
    _symbol: &str,
    _failed: Arc<AtomicBool>,
) -> Result<std::sync::mpsc::SyncSender<Vec<u8>>, String> {
    Err("live strategy fan-out requires Unix-domain sockets on a Unix host".into())
}

/// UTC timestamp as `YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS`, from the Unix epoch.
fn now_utc_compact() -> String {
    let secs = SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
        .map(|d| d.as_secs())
        .unwrap_or_default();
    let days = (secs / 86_400) as i64;
    let rem = secs % 86_400;
    let z = days + 719_468;
    let era = z.div_euclid(146_097);
    let doe = z.rem_euclid(146_097);
    let yoe = (doe - doe / 1_460 + doe / 36_524 - doe / 146_096) / 365;
    let y = yoe + era * 400;
    let doy = doe - (365 * yoe + yoe / 4 - yoe / 100);
    let mp = (5 * doy + 2) / 153;
    let d = doy - (153 * mp + 2) / 5 + 1;
    let m = if mp < 10 { mp + 3 } else { mp - 9 };
    let y = if m <= 2 { y + 1 } else { y };
    format!(
        "{y:04}{m:02}{d:02}_{:02}{:02}{:02}",
        rem / 3_600,
        (rem % 3_600) / 60,
        rem % 60
    )
}

/// Fetch a REST depth snapshot to seed the book.
///
/// The `binance` crate is the only HTTP client already in the dependency
/// graph, so the seed uses it rather than adding a new dependency.
pub fn seed_from_rest(symbol: &str) -> Result<BookSnapshot, String> {
    use binance::api::*;
    use binance::futures::market::*;
    use binance::futures::model::*;

    // `Binance` is a trait, not a client. The concrete futures client is
    // `FuturesMarket`, which matches the `fstream` websocket used below.
    let client = <FuturesMarket as Binance>::new(None, None);
    let book: OrderBook = client
        .get_depth(symbol)
        .map_err(|e| format!("REST depth for {symbol} failed: {e}"))?;
    Ok(BookSnapshot {
        last_update_id: book.last_update_id,
        bids: book.bids.iter().map(|l| (l.price, l.qty)).collect(),
        asks: book.asks.iter().map(|l| (l.price, l.qty)).collect(),
    })
}

/// Fire-and-forget reconnecting stream thread.
fn spawn_stream(url: String, on_msg: impl FnMut(&str) + Send + 'static) {
    std::thread::spawn(move || {
        let mut on_msg = on_msg;
        loop {
            match connect(url.as_str()) {
                Ok((mut ws, _)) => loop {
                    match ws.read() {
                        Ok(Message::Text(text)) => on_msg(&text),
                        Ok(Message::Ping(_))
                        | Ok(Message::Pong(_))
                        | Ok(Message::Binary(_))
                        | Ok(Message::Frame(_)) => (),
                        Ok(Message::Close(frame)) => {
                            eprintln!("[capture] closed by server: {:?}", frame.map(|f| f.code));
                            break;
                        }
                        Err(e) => {
                            eprintln!("[capture] socket error: {e}");
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                },
                Err(e) => eprintln!("[capture] connect failed: {e}"),
            }
            eprintln!("[capture] reconnecting in 3s...");
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_secs(3));
        }
    });
}

/// Binance sends numbers as JSON strings; parse either form.
fn num(value: Option<&Value>) -> Option<f64> {
    match value? {
        Value::Number(n) => n.as_f64(),
        Value::String(s) => s.parse().ok(),
        _ => None,
    }
}

fn u64_of(value: Option<&Value>) -> Option<u64> {
    match value? {
        Value::Number(n) => n.as_u64(),
        Value::String(s) => s.parse().ok(),
        _ => None,
    }
}

/// Unwrap a combined-stream envelope: `{"stream":..,"data":{..}}` -> data.
fn payload(text: &str) -> Option<Value> {
    let value: Value = serde_json::from_str(text).ok()?;
    Some(match value.get("data") {
        Some(inner) => inner.clone(),
        None => value,
    })
}

/// Parse one `aggTrade` push into a [`Trade`].
///
/// `m = true` means the buyer was the maker, i.e. the taker sold.
pub fn parse_trade(text: &str) -> Option<MarketEvent> {
    let v = payload(text)?;
    if v.get("e").and_then(Value::as_str) != Some("aggTrade") {
        return None;
    }
    Some(MarketEvent::Trade(Trade {
        event_time_ms: num(v.get("E"))? as i64,
        transact_time_ms: num(v.get("T")).unwrap_or_else(|| num(v.get("E")).unwrap_or(0.0)) as i64,
        agg_trade_id: u64_of(v.get("a"))?,
        first_trade_id: u64_of(v.get("f")).unwrap_or(0),
        last_trade_id: u64_of(v.get("l")).unwrap_or(0),
        price: num(v.get("p"))?,
        quantity: num(v.get("q"))?,
        is_buyer_maker: v.get("m").and_then(Value::as_bool).unwrap_or(false),
    }))
}

/// Parse one `depthUpdate` push into a [`DiffFrame`].
///
/// A quantity of `0.0` is meaningful here: it is how Binance removes a level,
/// so it is passed through rather than filtered out.
pub fn parse_diff(text: &str) -> Option<MarketEvent> {
    let v = payload(text)?;
    if v.get("e").and_then(Value::as_str) != Some("depthUpdate") {
        return None;
    }
    let mut updates = Vec::new();
    for (key, is_bid) in [("b", true), ("a", false)] {
        let Some(Value::Array(levels)) = v.get(key) else {
            continue;
        };
        for level in levels {
            let Value::Array(pair) = level else { continue };
            if pair.len() < 2 {
                continue;
            }
            let (Some(price), Some(quantity)) = (num(pair.first()), num(pair.get(1))) else {
                continue;
            };
            updates.push(event_merger_live::BookUpdate {
                is_bid,
                price,
                quantity,
            });
        }
    }
    Some(MarketEvent::Diff(DiffFrame {
        event_time_ms: num(v.get("E"))? as i64,
        first_update_id: u64_of(v.get("U")).unwrap_or(0),
        final_update_id: u64_of(v.get("u"))?,
        updates,
    }))
}

/// Run a live capture until interrupted, or until `max_rows` is reached.
pub fn run_capture(config: CaptureConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
    let symbol = config.symbol.to_lowercase();
    let keep_running = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(true));
    let rows_seen = Arc::new(AtomicU64::new(0));
    let fanout_failed = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));
    let fanout = if config.fanout_instances.is_empty() {
        None
    } else {
        Some(start_strategy_fanout(
            &config.fanout_instances,
            &config.symbol.to_ascii_lowercase(),
            Arc::clone(&fanout_failed),
        )?)
    };

    let csv = if config.append {
        CsvRecorder::append(&config.output)
    } else {
        CsvRecorder::create(&config.output)
    }
    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
    .flush_every(64);

    eprintln!("[capture] output   {}", config.output.display());
    eprintln!("[capture] symbol   {}", config.symbol);
    eprintln!("[capture] columns  {}", event_merger_live::schema().len());

    let capture = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Capture {
        merger: Merger::new(config.levels),
        reorder: ReorderBuffer::new(config.lateness_ms, 65_536),
        csv,
        rows_written: 0,
        reseeds_pending: false,
        fanout,
        fanout_failed: Arc::clone(&fanout_failed),
    }));

    // Write the header immediately, before any data. Deferring it to the first
    // row leaves a zero-byte file whenever a capture connects but never
    // receives data, and a headerless CSV is readable by nothing downstream.
    {
        let mut guard = capture.lock().expect("capture mutex poisoned");
        guard.csv.write_header().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        guard.csv.flush().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    }

    // Seed first. The diff stream alone cannot build a correct book.
    match seed_from_rest(&config.symbol) {
        Ok(snapshot) => {
            let mut guard = capture.lock().expect("capture mutex poisoned");
            // Applied straight to the merger, never through the reorder
            // buffer: a snapshot carries no exchange event time, so ordering
            // it against live events would corrupt the watermark.
            guard.merger.apply_seed(&snapshot);
            eprintln!("[capture] book seeded from REST");
        }
        Err(e) => eprintln!("[capture] WARNING: REST seed failed: {e}"),
    }

    let max_rows = config.max_rows;
    spawn_stream(
        format!("wss://fstream.binance.com/stream?streams={symbol}@aggTrade"),
        {
            let shared = Arc::clone(&capture);
            let seen = Arc::clone(&rows_seen);
            let keep = Arc::clone(&keep_running);
            move |text| {
                if let Some(event) = parse_trade(text) {
                    push(&shared, &seen, &keep, max_rows, event);
                }
            }
        },
    );
    spawn_stream(
        format!("wss://fstream.binance.com/stream?streams={symbol}@depth@100ms"),
        {
            let shared = Arc::clone(&capture);
            let seen = Arc::clone(&rows_seen);
            let keep = Arc::clone(&keep_running);
            move |text| {
                if let Some(event) = parse_diff(text) {
                    push(&shared, &seen, &keep, max_rows, event);
                }
            }
        },
    );

    // Recover the book from a detected sequence gap without a restart.
    {
        let shared = Arc::clone(&capture);
        let sym = config.symbol.clone();
        let keep = Arc::clone(&keep_running);
        std::thread::spawn(move || {
            while keep.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
                std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(500));
                let stale = shared.lock().map(|g| g.reseeds_pending).unwrap_or(false);
                if !stale {
                    continue;
                }
                match seed_from_rest(&sym) {
                    Ok(snapshot) => {
                        if let Ok(mut guard) = shared.lock() {
                            guard.merger.apply_seed(&snapshot);
                            guard.reseeds_pending = false;
                            eprintln!("[capture] book re-seeded after a sequence gap");
                        }
                    }
                    Err(e) => eprintln!("[capture] re-seed failed: {e}"),
                }
            }
        });
    }

    // Stall guard. Without it a capture that cannot reach the exchange (no
    // network, blocked host, wrong endpoint) spins forever writing an empty
    // file, which looks like "the market is quiet" rather than "nothing is
    // connected". Refuse to keep a capture alive that never saw a row.
    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
    let mut last_beat: u64 = 0;
    loop {
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(200));
        let total = rows_seen.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
        if let Some(limit) = max_rows {
            if total >= limit {
                break;
            }
        }
        if fanout_failed.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
            keep_running.store(false, Ordering::Release);
            return Err("strategy socket disconnected; feed process stopping".into());
        }
        if !keep_running.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
            break;
        }
        if total == 0 && started.elapsed() > STALL_TIMEOUT {
            eprintln!(
                "[capture] no rows after {:?}: cannot reach the exchange. \
                 Check the network, then retry.",
                STALL_TIMEOUT
            );
            keep_running.store(false, Ordering::Relaxed);
            return Err(format!(
                "no market data received within {:?}",
                STALL_TIMEOUT
            ));
        }

        // Heartbeat. Without it a healthy capture is indistinguishable from a
        // hung one: both print nothing. Once a second, show the row count and
        // whether the book is trustworthy, so a silent file growing on disk is
        // backed by evidence the feed is actually live.
        if started.elapsed().as_secs() > last_beat {
            last_beat = started.elapsed().as_secs();
            let (book_ok, gaps, late, seeds) = {
                let guard = capture.lock().expect("capture mutex poisoned");
                let c = guard.merger.counters();
                (
                    guard.merger.state().book_valid(),
                    c.gaps,
                    c.late_dropped,
                    c.seeds_applied,
                )
            };
            eprintln!(
                "[capture] rows={total} book={} gaps={gaps} late={late} seeds={seeds}",
                if book_ok { "valid" } else { "UNSEEDED/STALE" }
            );
        }
    }
    keep_running.store(false, Ordering::Relaxed);

    let mut guard = capture.lock().expect("capture mutex poisoned");
    let trailing = guard.flush_pending()?;
    guard.csv.flush().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let counters = guard.merger.counters();
    eprintln!(
        "[capture] wrote {} rows ({} trailing)",
        guard.csv.rows_written(),
        trailing
    );
    eprintln!(
        "[capture] applied={} diffs={} trades={} late_dropped={} gaps={} seeds={}",
        counters.events_applied,
        counters.diff_frames,
        counters.trades,
        counters.late_dropped,
        counters.gaps,
        counters.seeds_applied
    );
    Ok(())
}

/// Merge one event and count the row against the cap.
fn push(
    shared: &Arc<Mutex<Capture>>,
    rows_seen: &Arc<AtomicU64>,
    keep: &Arc<AtomicBool>,
    max_rows: Option<u64>,
    event: MarketEvent,
) {
    let Ok(mut guard) = shared.lock() else { return };
    match guard.offer(event) {
        Ok(written) => {
            let total = rows_seen.fetch_add(written, Ordering::Relaxed) + written;
            if max_rows.is_some_and(|limit| total >= limit) {
                keep.store(false, Ordering::Relaxed);
            }
        }
        Err(e) => {
            eprintln!("[capture] write failed: {e}");
            keep.store(false, Ordering::Relaxed);
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn parses_a_combined_stream_agg_trade() {
        let text = r#"{"stream":"btcusdt@aggTrade","data":{"e":"aggTrade","E":1751245200354,"a":2773899591,"p":"108665.6","q":"0.002","f":6438488841,"l":6438488841,"T":1751245200354,"m":true}}"#;
        match parse_trade(text).expect("parsed") {
            MarketEvent::Trade(t) => {
                assert_eq!(t.agg_trade_id, 2_773_899_591);
                assert_eq!(t.price, 108665.6);
                assert_eq!(t.quantity, 0.002);
                assert_eq!(t.event_time_ms, 1_751_245_200_354);
                assert!(t.is_buyer_maker, "m=true means the buyer was the maker");
            }
            other => panic!("expected a trade, got {other:?}"),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_a_bare_agg_trade_frame() {
        let text = r#"{"e":"aggTrade","E":10,"a":5,"p":"1.5","q":"2.0","m":false}"#;
        assert!(parse_trade(text).is_some());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_a_non_trade_frame() {
        assert!(parse_trade(r#"{"e":"depthUpdate","E":1}"#).is_none());
        assert!(parse_trade("not json").is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_a_diff_frame_with_both_sides() {
        let text = r#"{"e":"depthUpdate","E":1751245200406,"U":1,"u":10,"b":[["108665.5","9.141"]],"a":[["108665.6","12.556"]]}"#;
        match parse_diff(text).expect("parsed") {
            MarketEvent::Diff(f) => {
                assert_eq!(f.first_update_id, 1);
                assert_eq!(f.final_update_id, 10);
                assert_eq!(f.updates.len(), 2);
                assert!(f.updates[0].is_bid, "bids come first");
                assert_eq!(f.updates[0].price, 108665.5);
                assert!(!f.updates[1].is_bid);
            }
            other => panic!("expected a diff, got {other:?}"),
        }
    }

    #[test]
