        /// Diffs dropped because the snapshot already covers them (the
        /// Binance "discard events with `u < lastUpdateId`" rule).
        covered_dropped: u64,
        /// Trade rows refused because the other venue owns the trade stream.
        foreign_dropped: u64,
        /// Every event that entered [`Capture::offer`], seeded or not.
        pub(crate) events_seen: u64,
        /// Venue currently allowed to emit trade rows.
        pub(crate) trade_owner: u8,
        /// When the owner last emitted a trade (drives takeover/fallback).
        pub(crate) last_owner_trade_ms: u64,
    }

    impl Capture {
        /// Open (or append to) the capture file and write the header
        /// immediately, so a capture that never connects still leaves a
        /// readable CSV.
        pub(crate) fn new(
            path: &std::path::Path,
            append: bool,
            levels: usize,
            lateness_ms: i64,
        ) -> Result<Self, String> {
            let csv = if append {
                CsvRecorder::append(path)
            } else {
                CsvRecorder::create(path)
            }
            // One row, one syscall.
            //
            // This is the live capture path, and its output is read by a human
            // or a trader watching the file *while it is being written*. The
            // previous `.flush_every(64)` batched 64 rows -- roughly a minute
            // of market time -- into the OS at once, so the file sat flat and
            // then jumped. Measured: 104 of 116 samples had zero new rows and
            // 5 had >=20, which is exactly that flat-then-burst profile.
            //
            // At ~10 rows/s the syscall cost is irrelevant; what matters is
            // that a row lands on disk when it is produced, not a minute later.
            // The reorder buffer is what actually decides *when* a row becomes
            // emittable, and that is a separate problem (see the watermark
            // note on `Capture::emit`); this only removes the sink-side batching.
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
            .flush_every(1);
            let mut capture = Self {
                merger: Merger::new(levels),
                reorder: ReorderBuffer::new(lateness_ms, REORDER_CAP),
                csv,
                reseeds_pending: false,
                seeded: false,
                staged: Vec::new(),
                staged_dropped: 0,
                covered_dropped: 0,
                foreign_dropped: 0,
                events_seen: 0,
                trade_owner: VENUE_NONE,
                last_owner_trade_ms: now_ms(),
            };
            capture.csv.write_header().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            capture.csv.flush().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            Ok(capture)
        }

        /// Single-venue ownership for trade rows.
        ///
        /// The first venue to deliver a trade claims the stream. Ownership
        /// moves to the other feed only after the owner has been silent for
        /// [`VENUE_TAKEOVER`], so a healthy feed is never overruled and a
        /// dead one never blocks recovery. Returns false when the event must
        /// be dropped.
        fn claim_trade(&mut self, venue: u8) -> bool {
            if venue == VENUE_NONE {
                return true;
            }
            let now = now_ms();
            if self.trade_owner == venue {
                self.last_owner_trade_ms = now;
                return true;
            }
            if self.trade_owner == VENUE_NONE {
                self.trade_owner = venue;
                self.last_owner_trade_ms = now;
                eprintln!("[capture] trade source: {}", venue_name(venue));
                return true;
            }
            if now.saturating_sub(self.last_owner_trade_ms) >= VENUE_TAKEOVER.as_millis() as u64 {
                eprintln!(
                    "[capture] trade source: {} -> {} (old source silent {:?})",
                    venue_name(self.trade_owner),
                    venue_name(venue),
                    VENUE_TAKEOVER
                );
                self.trade_owner = venue;
                self.last_owner_trade_ms = now;
                return true;
            }
            self.foreign_dropped += 1;
            false
        }

        /// Accept one event: gate trades by venue, stage until seeded, then emit.
        pub(crate) fn offer(&mut self, event: MarketEvent, venue: u8) -> Result<u64, String> {
            self.events_seen += 1;
            if matches!(event, MarketEvent::Trade(_)) && !self.claim_trade(venue) {
                return Ok(0);
            }
            if !self.seeded {
                if self.staged.len() < STAGE_CAP {
                    self.staged.push(event);
                } else {
                    self.staged_dropped += 1;
                }
                return Ok(0);
            }
            self.emit(event)
        }

        /// Merge one already-reordered event and write its row.
        ///
        /// Shared by the live path ([`Capture::emit`]) and the shutdown path
        /// ([`Capture::flush_pending`]), so both apply the covered-diff rule
        /// and both flag re-seeds identically.
        fn write_ready(&mut self, event: MarketEvent) -> Result<u64, String> {
            // Binance sync rule: drop diffs the snapshot already covers.
            // Without this, applying an old diff after a fresh seed would
            // rewind book levels the seed has superseded. A re-seed lands
            // directly on the merger, so diffs waiting in the reorder buffer
            // can become covered at any moment -- checked here for both paths.
            if let MarketEvent::Diff(frame) = &event {
                if let Some(last) = self.merger.state().last_update_id {
                    if frame.final_update_id <= last {
                        self.covered_dropped += 1;
                        return Ok(0);
                    }
                }
            }
            let (row, outcome) = self.merger.merge_event(event);
            if outcome.needs_reseed {
                // Visible, not silent: the book is wrong until the seed
                // thread lands a fresh snapshot.
                self.reseeds_pending = true;
            }
            self.csv
                .write_row(&mut self.merger, &row)
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            Ok(1)
        }

        /// Merge one event and write whatever rows become emittable.
        fn emit(&mut self, event: MarketEvent) -> Result<u64, String> {
            let ready = self.reorder.push(event);
            let mut written = 0;
            for ready_event in ready {
                written += self.write_ready(ready_event)?;
            }
            Ok(written)
        }

        /// Apply a REST snapshot, then flush everything staged before it.
        ///
        /// Staged diffs covered by the snapshot vanish under the same rule as
        /// in [`Capture::emit`], so the first applied diff joins the snapshot
        /// without a phantom gap.
        pub(crate) fn apply_seed(&mut self, snapshot: &BookSnapshot) -> Result<u64, String> {
            self.merger.apply_seed(snapshot);
            self.seeded = true;
            self.reseeds_pending = false;
            let staged = std::mem::take(&mut self.staged);
            let mut written = 0;
            for event in staged {
                written += self.emit(event)?;
            }
            Ok(written)
        }

        /// Emit everything still held by the reorder buffer (shutdown path).
        ///
        /// Drained events go straight to the merger -- pushing them back
        /// through [`Capture::emit`] would only re-buffer them behind the
        /// lateness window and lose them on exit.
        pub(crate) fn flush_pending(&mut self) -> Result<u64, String> {
            let mut written = 0;
            for event in self.reorder.flush() {
                written += self.write_ready(event)?;
            }
            Ok(written)
        }
    }

    /// Merge one event and count it against the row cap.
    fn push(
        shared: &Arc<Mutex<Capture>>,
        rows_seen: &Arc<AtomicU64>,
        keep: &Arc<AtomicBool>,
        max_rows: Option<u64>,
        event: MarketEvent,
        venue: u8,
    ) {
        let Ok(mut guard) = shared.lock() else { return };
        match guard.offer(event, venue) {
            Ok(written) => {
                let total = rows_seen.fetch_add(written, Ordering::Relaxed) + written;
                if max_rows.is_some_and(|limit| total >= limit) {
                    keep.store(false, Ordering::Relaxed);
                }
            }
            Err(e) => {
                eprintln!("[capture] write failed: {e}");
                keep.store(false, Ordering::Relaxed);
            }
        }
    }

    /// Fire-and-forget reconnecting stream thread (same shape as the private
    /// helper in `live.rs`, plus the venue tag that gates trade rows).
    fn spawn_stream(
        url: String,
        venue: u8,
        parser: fn(&str) -> Option<MarketEvent>,
        shared: Arc<Mutex<Capture>>,
        rows_seen: Arc<AtomicU64>,
        keep: Arc<AtomicBool>,
        max_rows: Option<u64>,
    ) {
        std::thread::spawn(move || {
            loop {
                match connect(url.as_str()) {
                    Ok((mut ws, _)) => {
                        eprintln!("[capture] connected: {url}");
                        loop {
                            match ws.read() {
                                Ok(Message::Text(text)) => {
                                    if let Some(event) = parser(&text) {
                                        push(&shared, &rows_seen, &keep, max_rows, event, venue);
                                    }
                                }
                                Ok(Message::Ping(_))
                                | Ok(Message::Pong(_))
                                | Ok(Message::Binary(_))
                                | Ok(Message::Frame(_)) => (),
                                Ok(Message::Close(frame)) => {
                                    eprintln!(
                                        "[capture] closed by server: {:?} ({url})",
                                        frame.map(|f| f.code)
                                    );
                                    break;
                                }
                                Err(e) => {
                                    eprintln!("[capture] socket error: {e} ({url})");
                                    break;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    Err(e) => eprintln!("[capture] connect failed: {e} ({url})"),
                }
                if !keep.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
                    break;
                }
                eprintln!("[capture] reconnecting in 3s... ({url})");
                std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_secs(3));
            }
        });
    }

    /// Background seed thread: applies the first REST snapshot (retrying
    /// until it lands) and keeps re-seeding after sequence gaps.
    ///
    /// The old flow attempted the seed exactly once, *before* connecting, and
    /// never again -- one dropped DNS answer left the book UNSEEDED forever.
    /// This thread makes the seed self-healing: it runs while `!seeded` or
    /// while `reseeds_pending`, and backs off between failures so a dead
    /// resolver produces a line every few seconds instead of a firehose.
    fn spawn_seed_thread(
        symbol: String,
        capture: Arc<Mutex<Capture>>,
        rows_seen: Arc<AtomicU64>,
        keep: Arc<AtomicBool>,
        max_rows: Option<u64>,
    ) {
        std::thread::spawn(move || {
            let mut attempts = 0u32;
            while keep.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
                let needed = capture
                    .lock()
                    .map(|g| !g.seeded || g.reseeds_pending)
                    .unwrap_or(false);
                if !needed {
                    std::thread::sleep(RESEED_POLL);
                    continue;
                }
                attempts += 1;
                match live::seed_from_rest(&symbol) {
                    Ok(snapshot) => {
                        let outcome = {
                            let mut guard = match capture.lock() {
                                Ok(g) => g,
                                Err(_) => return,
                            };
                            let seeded = guard.seeded;
                            guard.apply_seed(&snapshot).map(|written| (seeded, written))
                        };
                        match outcome {
                            Ok((was_seeded, written)) => {
                                let total =
                                    rows_seen.fetch_add(written, Ordering::Relaxed) + written;
                                if max_rows.is_some_and(|limit| total >= limit) {
                                    keep.store(false, Ordering::Relaxed);
                                }
                                if was_seeded {
                                    eprintln!("[capture] book re-seeded after a sequence gap");
                                } else {
                                    eprintln!(
                                        "[capture] book seeded from REST (attempt {attempts})"
                                    );
                                }
                            }
                            Err(e) => {
                                eprintln!("[capture] write failed after seed: {e}");
                                keep.store(false, Ordering::Relaxed);
                                return;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    Err(e) => {
                        eprintln!("[capture] REST seed attempt {attempts} failed: {e}");
                        std::thread::sleep(RESEED_BACKOFF);
                    }
                }
            }
        });
    }

    /// Run a live capture until interrupted, or until `max_rows` is reached.
    ///
    /// Ordering matters: sockets connect *first*, events are staged, and the
    /// seed thread lands the REST snapshot whenever the network allows. That
    /// is the reverse of the old flow and eliminates both the "UNSEEDED
    /// forever" state (the seed is retried until it succeeds) and the
    /// phantom startup gap (the snapshot covers every buffered diff).
    pub(crate) fn run(config: super::live::CaptureConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
        let symbol = config.symbol.to_lowercase();
        let keep_running = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(true));
        let rows_seen = Arc::new(AtomicU64::new(0));

        let capture = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Capture::new(
            &config.output,
            config.append,
            config.levels,
            config.lateness_ms,
        )?));

        eprintln!("[capture] output   {}", config.output.display());
        eprintln!("[capture] symbol   {}", config.symbol);
        eprintln!("[capture] columns  {}", event_merger_live::schema().len());

        let max_rows = config.max_rows;

        // 1. Sockets first; events stage until the seed lands.
        spawn_stream(
            format!("wss://fstream.binance.com/stream?streams={symbol}@depth@100ms"),
            VENUE_NONE,
            live::parse_diff,
            Arc::clone(&capture),
            Arc::clone(&rows_seen),
            Arc::clone(&keep_running),
            max_rows,
        );
        spawn_stream(
            format!("wss://fstream.binance.com/stream?streams={symbol}@aggTrade"),
            VENUE_FUTURES,
            live::parse_trade,
            Arc::clone(&capture),
            Arc::clone(&rows_seen),
            Arc::clone(&keep_running),
            max_rows,
        );

        // 2. Seed thread: first snapshot + every gap re-seed, retrying forever.
        spawn_seed_thread(
            config.symbol.clone(),
            Arc::clone(&capture),
            Arc::clone(&rows_seen),
            Arc::clone(&keep_running),
            max_rows,
        );

        // 3. Fallback trade feed (spot). Spawned lazily from the heartbeat
        //    once the futures feed proves silent, so a healthy network keeps
        //    historical futures parity and a broken one still gets trades.
        let mut spot_spawned = false;

        let started = Instant::now();
        let mut last_beat = Duration::ZERO;

        loop {
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(200));
            let total = rows_seen.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
            if max_rows.is_some_and(|limit| total >= limit) {
                break;
            }
            if !keep_running.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {
                break;
            }

            let (seeded, events_seen, trade_owner, last_owner_trade_ms) = {
                let guard = capture.lock().expect("capture mutex poisoned");
                (
                    guard.seeded,
                    guard.events_seen,
                    guard.trade_owner,
                    guard.last_owner_trade_ms,
                )
            };

            // Stall guards: distinguish "no network at all" from "REST seed
            // unreachable" -- both otherwise look like a hung capture.
            let elapsed = started.elapsed();
            if events_seen == 0 && elapsed > STALL_TIMEOUT {
                keep_running.store(false, Ordering::Relaxed);
                return Err(format!(
                    "no market data received within {:?}: cannot reach the websockets. \
                     Check the network, then retry.",
                    STALL_TIMEOUT
                ));
            }
            if !seeded && elapsed > SEED_TIMEOUT {
                keep_running.store(false, Ordering::Relaxed);
                return Err(format!(
                    "REST depth seed failed for {:?}: the book cannot be trusted. \
                     Check DNS/VPN for fapi.binance.com, then retry.",
                    SEED_TIMEOUT
                ));
            }

            // Lazy spot fallback: no trade venue has produced anything for
            // TRADE_FALLBACK while the capture is demonstrably alive, so the
            // documented futures-aggTrade-silent network loses no data.
            if seeded && !spot_spawned && elapsed >= TRADE_FALLBACK {
                let silent_for = now_ms().saturating_sub(last_owner_trade_ms);
                if silent_for >= TRADE_FALLBACK.as_millis() as u64 {
                    spot_spawned = true;
                    eprintln!(
                        "[capture] no aggTrade for {TRADE_FALLBACK:?} -- also subscribing \
                         the spot feed (stream.binance.com:9443)"
                    );
                    spawn_stream(
                        format!("wss://stream.binance.com:9443/ws/{symbol}@aggTrade"),
                        VENUE_SPOT,
                        live::parse_trade,
                        Arc::clone(&capture),
                        Arc::clone(&rows_seen),
                        Arc::clone(&keep_running),
                        max_rows,
                    );
                }
            }

            // Heartbeat: the evidence a capture is alive and the book is sound.
            if elapsed - last_beat >= HEARTBEAT {
                last_beat = elapsed;
                let (book_ok, gaps, seeds, staged_left, trades) = {
                    let guard = capture.lock().expect("capture mutex poisoned");
                    let c = guard.merger.counters();
                    (
                        guard.merger.state().book_valid(),
                        c.gaps,
                        c.seeds_applied,
                        guard.staged.len(),
                        c.trades,
                    )
                };
                eprintln!(
                    "[capture] rows={total} book={} trades={trades} venue={} \
                     staged={staged_left} gaps={gaps} seeds={seeds}",
                    if book_ok { "valid" } else { "UNSEEDED/STALE" },
                    venue_name(trade_owner),
                );
            }
        }
        keep_running.store(false, Ordering::Relaxed);

        let mut guard = capture.lock().expect("capture mutex poisoned");
        let trailing = guard.flush_pending()?;
        guard.csv.flush().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let counters = guard.merger.counters();
        eprintln!(
            "[capture] wrote {} rows ({trailing} trailing)",
            guard.csv.rows_written()
        );
        eprintln!(
            "[capture] applied={} diffs={} trades={} gaps={} seeds={} \
             covered_dropped={} foreign_dropped={} staged_dropped={} venue={}",
            counters.events_applied,
            counters.diff_frames,
            counters.trades,
            counters.gaps,
            counters.seeds_applied,
            guard.covered_dropped,
            guard.foreign_dropped,
            guard.staged_dropped,
            venue_name(guard.trade_owner),
        );
        Ok(())
    }

    #[cfg(test)]
    mod tests {
        use super::*;
        use event_merger_live::schema;

        fn temp(name: &str) -> std::path::PathBuf {
            let mut p = std::env::temp_dir();
            p.push(format!("uets_record_{name}.csv"));
            let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&p);
            p
        }

        fn seed(id: u64) -> BookSnapshot {
            BookSnapshot {
                last_update_id: id,
                bids: vec![(108_663.2, 0.095), (108_662.9, 0.553)],
                asks: vec![(108_666.0, 0.004), (108_668.8, 0.002)],
            }
        }

        fn diff(text: &str) -> MarketEvent {
            live::parse_diff(text).expect("diff frame")
        }

        fn trade(text: &str) -> MarketEvent {
            live::parse_trade(text).expect("trade frame")
        }

        const DIFF_OLD: &str =
            r#"{"e":"depthUpdate","E":1751245200083,"U":1,"u":900,"b":[],"a":[]}"#;
        const DIFF_SPANS: &str = r#"{"e":"depthUpdate","E":1751245200406,"U":901,"u":1100,"b":[["108663.2","9.141"]],"a":[["108665.6","12.556"]]}"#;
        const TRADE_WIRE: &str = r#"{"e":"aggTrade","E":1751245200354,"a":2773899591,"p":"108665.6","q":"0.002","f":6438488841,"l":6438488841,"T":1751245200354,"m":true}"#;

        #[test]
        fn events_stage_until_the_seed_then_flush_without_a_phantom_gap() {
            let path = temp("staged");
            let mut cap = Capture::new(&path, false, 20, 1_000).expect("open");

            // Before the seed nothing is written: an unseeded book would put
            // wrong book_state__* values on disk.
            assert_eq!(cap.offer(diff(DIFF_OLD), VENUE_NONE).unwrap(), 0);
            assert_eq!(cap.offer(trade(TRADE_WIRE), VENUE_FUTURES).unwrap(), 0);
            assert_eq!(cap.offer(diff(DIFF_SPANS), VENUE_NONE).unwrap(), 0);
            assert_eq!(cap.events_seen, 3);

            // Seed at id 1000: DIFF_OLD (u=900) is covered and must vanish;
            // DIFF_SPANS (u=1100 > 1000) joins without flagging a gap.
            // The reorder buffer still holds both survivors behind its
            // lateness window, so nothing reaches the file yet.
            let written = cap.apply_seed(&seed(1_000)).expect("seed");
            assert_eq!(written, 0, "rows wait in the reorder buffer");
            assert!(!cap.reseeds_pending, "no phantom gap from covered diffs");
            assert!(cap.merger.state().book_valid());

            // A covered diff arriving after the seed is also dropped.
            assert_eq!(cap.offer(diff(DIFF_OLD), VENUE_NONE).unwrap(), 0);

            let trailing = cap.flush_pending().expect("flush");
            assert_eq!(trailing, 2, "trade + spanning diff become rows");
            cap.csv.flush().expect("csv flush");

            let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).expect("read back");
            let rows: Vec<&str> = text.lines().collect();
            assert_eq!(rows.len(), 3, "header + two rows");
            let width = rows[0].split(',').count();
            assert_eq!(width, schema().len(), "header matches schema");
            for row in &rows {
                assert_eq!(row.split(',').count(), width, "no column shift");
            }
            assert!(
                rows[1].contains("TRADE") || rows[2].contains("TRADE"),
                "a TRADE row"
            );
            let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
        }

        #[test]
        fn trade_venue_ownership_is_exclusive_and_self_healing() {
            let path = temp("venue");
            let mut cap = Capture::new(&path, false, 20, 1_000).expect("open");
            cap.apply_seed(&seed(1_000)).expect("seed");

            // First claim wins; the other venue is refused while the owner is alive.
            assert_eq!(cap.offer(trade(TRADE_WIRE), VENUE_FUTURES).unwrap(), 0);
            assert_eq!(cap.trade_owner, VENUE_FUTURES);
            assert_eq!(cap.offer(trade(TRADE_WIRE), VENUE_SPOT).unwrap(), 0);
            assert_eq!(cap.trade_owner, VENUE_FUTURES, "no takeover while fresh");
            assert_eq!(cap.foreign_dropped, 1);

            // Owner silent past VENUE_TAKEOVER -> ownership moves.
            cap.last_owner_trade_ms =
                now_ms().saturating_sub(VENUE_TAKEOVER.as_millis() as u64 + 1_000);
            assert_eq!(cap.offer(trade(TRADE_WIRE), VENUE_SPOT).unwrap(), 0);
            assert_eq!(cap.trade_owner, VENUE_SPOT, "takeover after silence");
            assert_eq!(cap.offer(trade(TRADE_WIRE), VENUE_FUTURES).unwrap(), 0);
            assert_eq!(cap.foreign_dropped, 2, "old owner now refused");

            let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
        }

        #[test]
        fn a_fresh_seed_supersedes_older_diffs() {
            let path = temp("reseed");
            let mut cap = Capture::new(&path, false, 20, 1_000).expect("open");
            cap.apply_seed(&seed(1_000)).expect("first seed");

            // Gap -> re-seed at a much higher id; diffs below it are stale.
            cap.reseeds_pending = true;
            cap.apply_seed(&seed(5_000)).expect("second seed");
            assert!(!cap.reseeds_pending, "seed clears the re-seed flag");
            assert_eq!(cap.offer(diff(DIFF_SPANS), VENUE_NONE).unwrap(), 0);
            assert_eq!(cap.flush_pending().unwrap(), 0, "u=1100 <= 5000 dropped");

            let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod strategy_tests {
    //! Test 2: the executor knows strategy1 and strategy2 are present and can
    //! switch their state builders / feature builders on demand.

    use super::*;
    use uets::core::feature::FeatureRow;
    use uets::core::strategy::{Decision, MarketState, Position};

    fn row(price: f64) -> FeatureRow {
        let mut row = FeatureRow::new();
        row.insert("price", price);
        row
    }

    fn state(feature: &str, value: f64) -> MarketState {
        let mut features = FeatureRow::new();
        features.insert(feature, value);
        MarketState {
            features,
            position: Position::Flat,
            sequence: 1,
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_executor_knows_both_strategies_on_disk_and_in_the_binary() {
        let switch = StrategySwitch::new();
        let available = switch.available();
        assert!(available.contains(&"ofmr"));
        assert!(available.contains(&"strategy2"));
        let discovered = switch.discovered();
        assert!(discovered.contains(&"ofmr".to_string()));
        assert!(discovered.contains(&"strategy2".to_string()));
        for dir in &discovered {
            assert!(
                available.contains(&dir.as_str()),
                "folder {dir} has no compiled module"
            );
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn selecting_ofmr_returns_ofmrs_builders() {
        let mut switch = StrategySwitch::new();
        assert!(
            switch.state_builder().is_err(),
            "nothing selected yet is an error, not a default"
        );

        switch.select("ofmr").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(switch.active(), Some("ofmr"));

        // Its feature builder computes Column_0..Column_14...
        let outputs = {
            let features = switch.feature_builder_mut().unwrap();
            let computed = features.compute(&row(100.0)).unwrap();
            for i in 0..15 {
                assert!(
                    computed.contains(&format!("Column_{i}")),
                    "missing Column_{i}"
                );
            }
            features.output_names()
        };
        assert_eq!(outputs.len(), 15);
        assert_eq!(outputs[0], "Column_0");
        assert_eq!(outputs[14], "Column_14");

        // ...and its state builder holds when features are missing.
        let builder = switch.state_builder().unwrap();
        let empty = FeatureRow::new();
        let st = MarketState {
            features: empty,
            position: Position::Flat,
            sequence: 1,
        };
        assert_eq!(builder.decide(&st), Decision::Hold);
        // strategy2's rule would hold on this state: it reads `dz`.
        let other = live_strategy::load_strategy("strategy2").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(other.builder.decide(&state("dz", 2.0)), Decision::EnterLong);
    }

    #[test]
    fn selecting_strategy2_returns_strategy2s_builders() {
        let mut switch = StrategySwitch::new();
        switch.select("strategy2").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(switch.active(), Some("strategy2"));

        let outputs = {
            let features = switch.feature_builder_mut().unwrap();
            let computed = features.compute(&row(100.0)).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(computed.get("dz"), Some(0.0), "first row: price - mean");
            features.output_names()
        };
        assert_eq!(outputs, vec!["dz".to_string()]);

        let builder = switch.state_builder().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(builder.decide(&state("dz", 2.0)), Decision::EnterLong);
        // ofmr's rule would hold: it needs all 15 columns and reads nothing but them.
        let other = live_strategy::load_strategy("ofmr").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(other.builder.decide(&state("dz", 2.0)), Decision::Hold);
    }

    #[test]
    fn switching_back_and_forth_returns_the_right_builders_each_time() {
        let mut switch = StrategySwitch::new();
        for (name, feature) in [
            ("ofmr", "Column_0"),
            ("strategy2", "dz"),
            ("ofmr", "Column_0"),
        ] {
            switch.select(name).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(switch.active(), Some(name));
            let outputs = switch.feature_builder_mut().unwrap().output_names();
            assert_eq!(
                outputs.first(),
                Some(&feature.to_string()),
                "after selecting {name}"
            );
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_unknown_strategy_is_rejected_and_keeps_the_current_selection() {
        let mut switch = StrategySwitch::new();
        switch.select("ofmr").unwrap();
        let error = switch.select("no_such_strategy").unwrap_err();
        assert!(error.contains("no_such_strategy"), "got: {error}");
        assert_eq!(
            switch.active(),
            Some("ofmr"),
            "a failed switch changes nothing"
        );
        let outputs = switch.feature_builder_mut().unwrap().output_names();
        assert_eq!(
            outputs.first(),
            Some(&"Column_0".to_string()),
            "still ofmr's builder"
        );
    }
}

/// `record` argument parsing: `record SYM1 [SYM2 ...] [DIR] [--dir DIR] [--rows N] [--append]`.
///
/// Every positional that is not path-like is a symbol; a trailing path-like
/// positional (contains `/`, `\` or `.`, or is an existing directory) is the
/// output dir, which keeps `record btcusdt <dir>` working. `--dir` always
/// wins and removes all ambiguity when a bare directory name would otherwise
/// read as a symbol:
///
/// ```text
/// record btcusdt                     # one symbol, default dir
/// record btcusdt ethusdt bnbusdt     # three symbols, default dir
/// record btcusdt ethusdt ./captures  # two symbols, explicit dir
/// record btcusdt out --dir out       # bare dirname needs --dir
/// ```
fn parse_record_args(rest: &[String]) -> Result<(Vec<String>, String, Option<u64>, bool), String> {
    let mut dir_flag: Option<String> = None;
    let mut max_rows: Option<u64> = None;
    let mut append = false;
    let mut positionals: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
    let mut i = 0;
    while i < rest.len() {
        match rest[i].as_str() {
            "--rows" => {
                let value = rest.get(i + 1).ok_or("--rows expects a number")?;
                max_rows = Some(
                    value
                        .parse::<u64>()
                        .map_err(|_| format!("--rows expects a number, got: {value}"))?,
                );
                i += 2;
            }
            "--dir" => {
                dir_flag = Some(
                    rest.get(i + 1)
                        .cloned()
                        .ok_or("--dir expects a directory")?,
                );
                i += 2;
            }
            "--append" => {
                append = true;
                i += 1;
            }
            other if other.starts_with("--") => {
                i += 1; // unknown flag: skip rather than misread as a symbol
            }
            other => {
                positionals.push(other.to_string());
                i += 1;
            }
        }
    }
    let mut symbols = positionals;
    let dir = match dir_flag {
        Some(d) => d,
        None => {
            // Backward compatible: `record btcusdt <dir>` still resolves the
            // second positional as the dir -- but only when it is path-like,
            // so `record btcusdt ethusdt` reads as two symbols.
            if symbols.len() >= 2 && looks_like_dir(symbols.last().expect("len checked")) {
                symbols.pop().expect("len checked")
            } else {
                default_live_db_dir()
            }
        }
    };
    if symbols.is_empty() {
        symbols.push("btcusdt".to_string());
    }
    Ok((symbols, dir, max_rows, append))
}

/// Symbols are bare alphanumerics (`btcusdt`); anything with a separator, a
/// dot, or an existing directory on disk reads as an output path.
fn looks_like_dir(s: &str) -> bool {
    s.contains('/') || s.contains('\\') || s.contains('.') || std::path::Path::new(s).is_dir()
}

/// Run `record`: one capture thread per symbol, one CSV per symbol.
///
/// Each thread owns its websocket pair, seed thread, merger, and CSV writer,
/// so symbols never block each other -- nineteen quiet books and one frantic
/// book still write at their own cadences. `--rows N` applies per symbol.
/// Ctrl+C stops the process; per-row flushing (fix A) means at most one row
/// per symbol is lost on a hard kill.
fn run_record(rest: &[String]) -> ExitCode {
    let (symbols, dir, max_rows, append) = match parse_record_args(rest) {
        Ok(parsed) => parsed,
        Err(e) => {
            eprintln!("record: {e}");
            return ExitCode::FAILURE;
        }
    };
    if symbols.len() == 1 {
        let mut config = live::CaptureConfig::new(&symbols[0], dir);
        config.max_rows = max_rows;
        config.append = append;
        return match record::run(config) {
            Ok(()) => ExitCode::SUCCESS,
            Err(e) => {
                eprintln!("capture failed: {e}");
                ExitCode::FAILURE
            }
        };
    }
    eprintln!("[record] {} symbols -> {}", symbols.len(), dir);
    let handles: Vec<std::thread::JoinHandle<(String, Result<(), String>)>> = symbols
        .into_iter()
        .map(|symbol| {
            let dir = dir.clone();
            std::thread::spawn(move || {
                let mut config = live::CaptureConfig::new(&symbol, &dir);
                config.max_rows = max_rows;
                config.append = append;
                let outcome = record::run(config);
                (symbol, outcome)
            })
        })
        .collect();
    let mut failed = false;
    for handle in handles {
        match handle.join() {
            Ok((symbol, Ok(()))) => eprintln!("[record] {symbol}: done"),
            Ok((symbol, Err(e))) => {
                eprintln!("[record] {symbol}: failed: {e}");
                failed = true;
            }
            Err(_) => {
                eprintln!("[record] worker panicked");
                failed = true;
            }
        }
    }
    if failed {
        ExitCode::FAILURE
    } else {
        ExitCode::SUCCESS
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod record_args_tests {
    use super::*;

    fn args(v: &[&str]) -> Vec<String> {
        v.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect()
    }

    #[test]
    fn empty_defaults_to_btcusdt() {
        let (syms, _dir, rows, append) = parse_record_args(&[]).expect("parses");
        assert_eq!(syms, vec!["btcusdt"]);
        assert!(rows.is_none() && !append);
    }

    #[test]
    fn two_bare_words_are_two_symbols() {
        let (syms, _dir, _, _) = parse_record_args(&args(&["btcusdt", "ethusdt"])).expect("parses");
        assert_eq!(syms, vec!["btcusdt", "ethusdt"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn trailing_path_is_dir_not_symbol() {
        let (syms, dir, _, _) =
            parse_record_args(&args(&["btcusdt", "ethusdt", "./captures"])).expect("parses");
        assert_eq!(syms, vec!["btcusdt", "ethusdt"]);
        assert_eq!(dir, "./captures");
    }

    #[test]
    fn dir_flag_wins_over_heuristic() {
        let (syms, dir, _, _) =
            parse_record_args(&args(&["btcusdt", "out", "--dir", "out"])).expect("parses");
        assert_eq!(syms, vec!["btcusdt", "out"]);
        assert_eq!(dir, "out");
    }

    #[test]
    fn single_symbol_with_legacy_dir() {
        let (syms, dir, _, _) =
            parse_record_args(&args(&["btcusdt", "market_data/live_db"])).expect("parses");
        assert_eq!(syms, vec!["btcusdt"]);
        assert_eq!(dir, "market_data/live_db");
    }

    #[test]
    fn rows_and_append_parse() {
        let (_, _, rows, append) =
            parse_record_args(&args(&["btcusdt", "--rows", "50", "--append"])).expect("parses");
        assert_eq!(rows, Some(50));
        assert!(append);
    }
}

fn main() -> ExitCode {
    let args: Vec<String> = std::env::args().skip(1).collect();
    let cmd = args.first().map(String::as_str).unwrap_or("help");
    let rest: Vec<String> = args.iter().skip(1).cloned().collect();
    let first = || rest.first().cloned().unwrap_or_default();

    match cmd {
        "market" => {
            let symbol = if first().is_empty() {
                "btcusdt".into()
            } else {
                first()
            };
            let datatype = rest.get(1).cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| "aggTrade".into());
            reader::market_websocket(&symbol, &datatype);
        }
        "depth" => {
            let symbol = if first().is_empty() {
                "btcusdt".into()
            } else {
                first()
            };
            reader::market_websocket(&symbol, "depth@100ms");
        }
        "kline" => {
            let symbol = if first().is_empty() {
                "btcusdt".into()
            } else {
                first()
            };
            reader::kline_websocket(&symbol);
        }
        "ticker" => {
            let symbol = if first().is_empty() {
                "btcusdt".into()
            } else {
                first()
            };
            reader::ticker_websocket(&symbol);
        }
        "all-tickers" => reader::all_trades_websocket(),
        "multi" => {
            let symbols: Vec<&str> = if rest.is_empty() {
                vec!["ethbtc", "bnbusdt"]
            } else {
                rest.iter().map(String::as_str).collect()
            };
            reader::multiple_streams(&symbols);
        }
        "record" => {
            return run_record(&rest);
        }
        "run" => {
            return match run_live_system(&rest) {
                Ok(()) => ExitCode::SUCCESS,
                Err(error) => {
                    eprintln!("UETS runtime failed: {error}");
                    ExitCode::FAILURE
                }
            };
        }
        "feed" => {
            if rest.len() < 2 {
                eprintln!("usage: dev_test feed <symbol> <strategy>@<asset> [...]");
                return ExitCode::FAILURE;
            }
            let mut config = live::CaptureConfig::new(&rest[0], default_live_db_dir());
            config.fanout_instances = rest.iter().skip(1).cloned().collect();
            return match live::run_capture(config) {
                Ok(()) => ExitCode::SUCCESS,
                Err(error) => {
                    eprintln!("live feed failed: {error}");
                    ExitCode::FAILURE
                }
            };
        }
        "monitor" => {
            let symbol = if first().is_empty() {
                "btcusdt".into()
            } else {
                first()
            };
            monitor::monitor_websocket(&symbol);
        }
        "diag" => {
            // One row per event: timestamp, update_type (aggTrades|orderbook),
            // that stream's data — the other stream's columns stay NaN.
            let symbol = if first().is_empty() {
                "btcusdt".into()
            } else {
                first()
            };
            let dir = rest
                .iter()
                .skip(1)
                .find(|a| !a.starts_with("--"))
                .cloned()
                .unwrap_or_else(default_out_dir);
            if let Err(e) = diagnostic::run(&symbol, dir) {
                eprintln!("diag failed: {e}");
                return ExitCode::FAILURE;
            }
        }
        "strategies" => {
            // The executor's own view of what it can run: compiled-in
            // bundles vs folders on disk, and (with a name) the switch.
            let mut switch = StrategySwitch::new();
            println!("compiled:   {:?}", switch.available());
            println!("discovered: {:?}", switch.discovered());
            let name = first();
            if name.is_empty() {
                println!("selected:   (none); try: dev_test strategies ofmr");
            } else {
                if let Err(e) = switch.select(&name) {
                    eprintln!("{e}");
                    return ExitCode::FAILURE;
                }
                println!("selected:   {}", switch.active().unwrap_or("?"));
                println!("state_builder present:  {}", switch.state_builder().is_ok());
                println!(
                    "feature_builder present: {}",
                    switch.feature_builder_mut().is_ok()
                );
            }
        }
        "strategy" => {
            // Run a strategy's Unix-domain socket consumer. Aggregator
            // (aggregator.py) binds the exchange side at
            // sockets/exchanges/<venue>.sock and fans out to
            // sockets/strategies/<name>_<symbol>.sock so multiple instances
            // can run side by side (ofmr->btc, ofmr->eth, ofmr->sol,
            // ... 100 assets).
            let identity = first();
            if identity.is_empty() {
                eprintln!("usage: dev_test strategy <strategy>@<asset> (or <strategy> <asset>)");
                print_help();
                return ExitCode::FAILURE;
            }
            let (name, symbol) = if identity.contains('@') {
                match parse_strategy_instance(&identity) {
                    Ok(instance) => instance,
                    Err(error) => {
                        eprintln!("{error}");
                        return ExitCode::FAILURE;
                    }
                }
            } else {
                let symbol = rest.get(1).cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| "btcusdt".into());
                match parse_strategy_instance(&format!("{identity}@{symbol}")) {
                    Ok(instance) => instance,
                    Err(error) => {
                        eprintln!("{error}");
                        return ExitCode::FAILURE;
                    }
                }
            };
            if name != "ofmr" {
                eprintln!("strategy socket consumer is currently implemented only for 'ofmr'");
                return ExitCode::FAILURE;
            }
            let socket_path = format!("sockets/strategies/{name}_{symbol}.sock");
            println!("instance:   {name}@{symbol}");
            println!("socket:     {socket_path}");
            if let Err(e) = uets::live_strategy::ofmr::socket::run(&socket_path, &symbol) {
                eprintln!("strategy socket failed: {e}");
                return ExitCode::FAILURE;
            }
        }
        "user-stream" => {
            println!("note: requires a real API key (edit reader::user_stream)");
            reader::user_stream();
        }
        "user-stream-ws" => {
            println!("note: requires a real API key (edit reader::user_stream_websocket)");
            reader::user_stream_websocket();
        }
        "help" | "--help" | "-h" => print_help(),
        other => {
            eprintln!("unknown command: {other}");
            print_help();
            return ExitCode::FAILURE;
        }
    }

    ExitCode::SUCCESS
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod strategy_instance_tests {
    use super::parse_strategy_instance;

    #[test]
    fn parses_canonical_identity_case_insensitively() {
        assert_eq!(
            parse_strategy_instance("OFMR@BTCUSDT").unwrap(),
            ("ofmr".to_string(), "btcusdt".to_string())
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_incomplete_or_unsafe_identity() {
        assert!(parse_strategy_instance("ofmr").is_err());
        assert!(parse_strategy_instance("@btcusdt").is_err());
        assert!(parse_strategy_instance("ofmr@../../tmp").is_err());
    }
}

===== FILE: uets/live_strategy/mod.rs =====
//! `live_strategy` — one folder per strategy, picked by `executor.rs`.
//!
//! ```text
//! uets/live_strategy/
//!   mod.rs        # this file: module wiring + loader
//!   traits.rs     # ComputeFeatures + StateBuilder contracts
//!   runner.rs     # StrategyRunner: per-(strategy x symbol) state
//!   ofmr/         # OFMR strategy: LightGBM inference over Column_0..Column_14
//!   strategy2/    # YOUR strategy: same shape, independent state
//! ```
//!
//! Flow per row: `compute_feature` first, `state_builder` second, back to
//! `executor.rs`. Unknown name is a hard error, never a silent hold.

pub mod ofmr;
pub mod strategy2;
pub mod traits;

pub mod runner;

pub use runner::{StrategyRunner, discover_strategy_dirs};
pub use traits::{ComputeFeatures, LiveStrategy, StateBuilder};

/// Strategies compiled into this binary.
pub fn available_strategies() -> Vec<&'static str> {
    vec!["ofmr", "strategy2"]
}

/// Build a fresh bundle by name (independent state per call).
pub fn load_strategy(name: &str) -> Result<LiveStrategy, String> {
    match name {
        "ofmr" => ofmr::load(),
        "strategy2" => strategy2::load(),
        other => Err(format!(
            "unknown strategy '{other}' (available: {:?})",
            available_strategies()
        )),
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::core::feature::FeatureRow;

    fn row(price: f64) -> FeatureRow {
        let mut r = FeatureRow::new();
        r.insert("price", price);
        r
    }

    #[test]
    fn known_strategies_load() {
        for name in available_strategies() {
            assert!(load_strategy(name).is_ok(), "loads {name}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn unknown_strategy_is_an_error() {
        assert!(load_strategy("typo").is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn discovery_lists_strategy_folders() {
        let dirs = discover_strategy_dirs();
        assert!(dirs.contains(&"ofmr".to_string()), "got {dirs:?}");
        assert!(dirs.contains(&"strategy2".to_string()), "got {dirs:?}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn step_is_isolated_per_strategy_and_symbol() {
        let mut runner = StrategyRunner::new();
        runner.step("ofmr", "BTCUSDT", &row(100.0)).unwrap();
        runner.step("strategy2", "BTCUSDT", &row(100.0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(runner.slot_count(), 2);
        runner.step("ofmr", "BTCUSDT", &row(101.0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(runner.slot_count(), 2, "same slot reused");
        runner.step("ofmr", "ETHUSDT", &row(100.0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(runner.slot_count(), 3);
    }
}


===== FILE: uets/live_strategy/ofmr/features.rs =====
//! strategy1/compute_feature.rs — YOUR computation.
//!
//! Runs FIRST on every stream row: canonical `FeatureRow` in, computed
//! feature row out. Stateful: one instance per (strategy x symbol), so put
//! rolling windows / running sums here — they never leak to other strategies.
//!
//! Replace the body with your formula. Keep `Missing` semantics: emit
//! `insert_missing` while history is too short, never zero.

use std::collections::VecDeque;

use crate::core::feature::FeatureRow;
use crate::live_strategy::ofmr::settings::Settings;
use crate::live_strategy::ComputeFeatures;

pub const N_FEATURES: usize = 15;

pub fn output_column_names() -> Vec<String> {
    (0..N_FEATURES).map(|i| format!("Column_{i}")).collect()
}

fn rolling_zscore(window: &VecDeque<f64>, value: f64, min_size: usize) -> Option<f64> {
    if window.len() < min_size {
        return None;
    }
    let n = window.len() as f64;
    let mean: f64 = window.iter().sum::<f64>() / n;
    let var: f64 = window.iter().map(|v| (v - mean) * (v - mean)).sum::<f64>() / (n - 1.0).max(1.0);
    let std = var.sqrt();
    if std == 0.0 {
        return Some(0.0);
    }
    Some((value - mean) / std)
}

fn push_window<T: Clone>(w: &mut VecDeque<T>, cap: usize, value: T) {
    if w.len() >= cap {
        w.pop_front();
    }
    w.push_back(value);
}


#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
struct RsiState {
    window: usize,
    avg_gain: Option<f64>,
    avg_loss: Option<f64>,
    prev_price: Option<f64>,
    seeded: bool,
    seed_count: usize,
}

impl RsiState {
    fn new(window: usize) -> Self {
        Self {
            window,
            avg_gain: None,
            avg_loss: None,
            prev_price: None,
            seeded: false,
            seed_count: 0,
        }
    }

    fn next(&mut self, price: f64) -> Option<f64> {
        let Some(prev) = self.prev_price else {
            self.prev_price = Some(price);
            return None;
        };
        let delta = price - prev;
        self.prev_price = Some(price);
        let gain = delta.max(0.0);
        let loss = (-delta).max(0.0);
        if !self.seeded {
            self.seed_count += 1;
            let g = self.avg_gain.unwrap_or(0.0) + gain;
            let l = self.avg_loss.unwrap_or(0.0) + loss;
            self.avg_gain = Some(g);
            self.avg_loss = Some(l);
            if self.seed_count >= self.window {
                self.avg_gain = Some(g / self.window as f64);
                self.avg_loss = Some(l / self.window as f64);
                self.seeded = true;
            }
            return None;
        }
        let ag = (self.avg_gain.unwrap() * (self.window as f64 - 1.0) + gain) / self.window as f64;
        let al = (self.avg_loss.unwrap() * (self.window as f64 - 1.0) + loss) / self.window as f64;
        self.avg_gain = Some(ag);
        self.avg_loss = Some(al);
        if al == 0.0 {
            return Some(100.0);
        }
        let rs = ag / al;
        Some(100.0 - 100.0 / (1.0 + rs))
    }
}

/// Per-symbol streaming feature state. One instance per (strategy x symbol).
pub struct OfmrFeatures {
    config: Settings,

    mid_prices: VecDeque<f64>,
    rsi_2: RsiState,
    rsi_14: RsiState,
    rsi_14_window: VecDeque<f64>,
    spread_bps_window: VecDeque<f64>,
    ma5_prices: VecDeque<f64>,
    prev_ma5: Option<f64>,
    prev_ma_slope: Option<f64>,
    ofi_window: VecDeque<f64>,
    prev_ofi: Option<f64>,
    cvd: f64,
    cvd_delta_window: VecDeque<f64>,
    cvd_slope_window: VecDeque<f64>,
    prev_cvd: Option<f64>,
    tick_rule_window: VecDeque<i32>,
    prev_trade_price: Option<f64>,
    inst_buy_vol: f64,
    inst_sell_vol: f64,
    retail_buy_vol: f64,
    retail_sell_vol: f64,
    total_volume: f64,
    depth_imbalance_window: VecDeque<f64>,
    abs_ratio_window: VecDeque<f64>,
    delta_divergence_window: VecDeque<f64>,
    sequence: u64,
}

impl OfmrFeatures {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::with_settings(Settings::load())
    }

    pub fn with_settings(config: Settings) -> Self {
        Self {
            config,
            mid_prices: VecDeque::with_capacity(200),
            rsi_2: RsiState::new(2),
            rsi_14: RsiState::new(14),
            rsi_14_window: VecDeque::with_capacity(14),
            spread_bps_window: VecDeque::with_capacity(60),
            ma5_prices: VecDeque::with_capacity(10),
            prev_ma5: None,
            prev_ma_slope: None,
            ofi_window: VecDeque::with_capacity(60),
            prev_ofi: None,
            cvd: 0.0,
            cvd_delta_window: VecDeque::with_capacity(60),
            cvd_slope_window: VecDeque::with_capacity(60),
            prev_cvd: None,
            tick_rule_window: VecDeque::with_capacity(60),
            prev_trade_price: None,
            inst_buy_vol: 0.0,
            inst_sell_vol: 0.0,
            retail_buy_vol: 0.0,
            retail_sell_vol: 0.0,
            total_volume: 0.0,
            depth_imbalance_window: VecDeque::with_capacity(60),
            abs_ratio_window: VecDeque::with_capacity(60),
            delta_divergence_window: VecDeque::with_capacity(60),
            sequence: 0,
        }
    }
}

impl Default for OfmrFeatures {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl ComputeFeatures for OfmrFeatures {
    fn compute(&mut self, row: &FeatureRow) -> Result<FeatureRow, String> {
        self.sequence += 1;
        let bid = row.get("bid_price");
        let ask = row.get("ask_price");
        let trade_price = row.get("price");
        let trade_amount = row.get("amount").unwrap_or(0.0);
        let is_buyer_maker = row.get("is_buyer_maker").map(|v| v != 0.0).unwrap_or(false);
        let mid = match (bid, ask) {
            (Some(b), Some(a)) if b > 0.0 && a > 0.0 => (b + a) / 2.0,
            _ => trade_price.unwrap_or(0.0),
        };
        push_window(&mut self.mid_prices, 200, if mid > 0.0 { mid } else { 0.0 });
        let rsi_2_val = if mid > 0.0 { self.rsi_2.next(mid) } else { None };
        let rsi_14_val = if mid > 0.0 { self.rsi_14.next(mid) } else { None };
        push_window(&mut self.ma5_prices, 5, mid);
        let ma5 = if self.ma5_prices.len() == 5 {
            self.ma5_prices.iter().sum::<f64>() / 5.0
        } else { mid };
        let ma_slope = self.prev_ma5.map(|p| ma5 - p).unwrap_or(0.0);
        self.prev_ma5 = Some(ma5);
        let spread_bps = match (bid, ask) {
            (Some(b), Some(a)) if b > 0.0 && a > 0.0 && mid > 0.0 => (a - b) / mid * 10000.0,
            _ => 0.0,
        };
        push_window(&mut self.spread_bps_window, 60, spread_bps);
        let spread_bps_z = rolling_zscore(&self.spread_bps_window, spread_bps, 10);
        let price_roc_3 = if self.mid_prices.len() > 3 {
            let len = self.mid_prices.len();
            let prev_price = self.mid_prices[len - 4];
            if prev_price > 0.0 { (mid - prev_price) / prev_price } else { 0.0 }
        } else { 0.0 };
        push_window(&mut self.rsi_14_window, 14, rsi_14_val.unwrap_or(50.0));
        let sma_14 = if self.rsi_14_window.len() == 14 {
            self.rsi_14_window.iter().sum::<f64>() / 14.0
        } else { rsi_14_val.unwrap_or(50.0) };
        let ma_accel = self.prev_ma_slope.map(|p| ma_slope - p).unwrap_or(0.0);
        self.prev_ma_slope = Some(ma_slope);
        let rsi_z = rsi_2_val.map(|v| (v - 50.0) / 25.0).unwrap_or(0.0);
        let rsi_x_ma = rsi_z * ma_slope;
        let tick_rule = match trade_price {
            Some(p) => {
                let tr = match self.prev_trade_price {
                    Some(pp) if p > pp => 1,
                    Some(pp) if p < pp => -1,
                    _ => 0,
                };
                self.prev_trade_price = Some(p);
                tr
            }
            None => 0,
        };
        let ofi_raw = spread_bps;
        push_window(&mut self.ofi_window, 60, ofi_raw);
        let ofi_diff = self.prev_ofi.map(|p| ofi_raw - p).unwrap_or(0.0);
        self.prev_ofi = Some(ofi_raw);
        push_window(&mut self.cvd_delta_window, 60, ofi_diff);
        let ofi_z = rolling_zscore(&self.ofi_window, ofi_diff, 10);
        let cvd_delta = tick_rule as f64 * trade_amount;
        self.cvd += cvd_delta;
        push_window(&mut self.cvd_delta_window, 60, cvd_delta);
        let cvd_delta_z = rolling_zscore(&self.cvd_delta_window, cvd_delta, 10);
        let cvd_slope = if self.cvd_delta_window.len() >= 5 {
            let len = self.cvd_delta_window.len();
            let prev = self.cvd_delta_window[len - 5];
            self.cvd - prev
        } else { 0.0 };
        push_window(&mut self.cvd_slope_window, 60, cvd_slope);
        let cvd_slope_z = rolling_zscore(&self.cvd_slope_window, cvd_slope, 10);
        push_window(&mut self.tick_rule_window, 60, tick_rule);
        let tick_autocorr = if self.tick_rule_window.len() > 5 {
            let vals: Vec<f64> = self.tick_rule_window.iter().map(|&v| v as f64).collect();
            let n = vals.len();
            let a = &vals[..n - 1];
            let b = &vals[1..];
            let ma = a.iter().sum::<f64>() / n as f64;
            let mb = b.iter().sum::<f64>() / n as f64;
            let sa = a.iter().map(|v| (v - ma) * (v - ma)).sum::<f64>().sqrt();
            let sb = b.iter().map(|v| (v - mb) * (v - mb)).sum::<f64>().sqrt();
            if sa == 0.0 || sb == 0.0 { 0.0 } else {
                let cov: f64 = a.iter().zip(b.iter()).map(|(x, y)| (x - ma) * (y - mb)).sum();
                cov / (sa * sb)
            }
        } else { 0.0 };
        let retail_threshold = 0.05 * trade_amount.max(1.0);
        if trade_amount < retail_threshold {
            if is_buyer_maker { self.retail_sell_vol += trade_amount; } else { self.retail_buy_vol += trade_amount; }
        } else {
            if is_buyer_maker { self.inst_sell_vol += trade_amount; } else { self.inst_buy_vol += trade_amount; }
        }
        self.total_volume += trade_amount;
        let inst_ratio = if self.total_volume > 0.0 {
            (self.inst_buy_vol + self.inst_sell_vol) / (self.total_volume + 1e-9)
        } else { 0.0 };
        let depth_imbalance = match (bid, ask) {
            (Some(b), Some(a)) if b > 0.0 && a > 0.0 => {
                let ba = row.get("bid_amount").unwrap_or(0.0);
                let aa = row.get("ask_amount").unwrap_or(0.0);
                let denom = ba + aa + 1e-9;
                (ba - aa) / denom
            }
            _ => 0.0,
        };
        push_window(&mut self.depth_imbalance_window, 60, depth_imbalance);
        let depth_imbalance_z = rolling_zscore(&self.depth_imbalance_window, depth_imbalance, 10);
        let price_range = match (bid, ask) {
            (Some(b), Some(a)) if a > b => a - b,
            _ => 0.0,
        };
        let abs_ratio = if price_range > 0.0 {
            cvd_delta.abs() / (price_range + 1e-9)
        } else { 0.0 };
        push_window(&mut self.abs_ratio_window, 60, abs_ratio);
        let absorption_z = rolling_zscore(&self.abs_ratio_window, abs_ratio, 10);
        let delta_divergence = (self.inst_buy_vol - self.inst_sell_vol)
            - (self.retail_buy_vol - self.retail_sell_vol);
        push_window(&mut self.delta_divergence_window, 60, delta_divergence);
        let delta_divergence_z = rolling_zscore(&self.delta_divergence_window, delta_divergence, 10);
        let mut out = FeatureRow::new();
        out.insert("Column_0", spread_bps_z.unwrap_or(0.0));
        out.insert("Column_1", ma_slope);
        out.insert("Column_2", rsi_x_ma);
        out.insert("Column_3", ma_accel);
        out.insert("Column_4", price_roc_3);
        out.insert("Column_5", rsi_2_val.unwrap_or(50.0));
        out.insert("Column_6", sma_14);
        out.insert("Column_7", ofi_z.unwrap_or(0.0));
        out.insert("Column_8", cvd_delta_z.unwrap_or(0.0));
        out.insert("Column_9", cvd_slope_z.unwrap_or(0.0));
        out.insert("Column_10", absorption_z.unwrap_or(0.0));
        out.insert("Column_11", tick_autocorr);
        out.insert("Column_12", inst_ratio);
        out.insert("Column_13", delta_divergence_z.unwrap_or(0.0));
        out.insert("Column_14", depth_imbalance_z.unwrap_or(0.0));
        Ok(out)
    }

    fn output_names(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        output_column_names()
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn tick(price: f64, bid: f64, ask: f64, amount: f64, buyer_maker: bool) -> FeatureRow {
        let mut r = FeatureRow::new();
        r.insert("price", price);
        r.insert("amount", amount);
        r.insert("bid_price", bid);
        r.insert("ask_price", ask);
        r.insert("bid_amount", 1.0);
        r.insert("ask_amount", 1.0);
        r.insert("is_buyer_maker", if buyer_maker { 1.0 } else { 0.0 });
        r
    }

    #[test]
    fn output_names_are_column_0_to_14() {
        let names = output_column_names();
        assert_eq!(names.len(), 15);
        assert_eq!(names[0], "Column_0");
        assert_eq!(names[14], "Column_14");
    }

    #[test]
    fn first_tick_returns_all_columns() {
        let mut f = OfmrFeatures::new();
        let row = f.compute(&tick(100.0, 99.99, 100.01, 1.0, false)).unwrap();
        for i in 0..15 {
            assert!(row.contains(&format!("Column_{i}")), "missing Column_{i}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn rolling_zscore_returns_none_when_window_short() {
        let w: VecDeque<f64> = VecDeque::from([1.0, 2.0]);
        assert!(rolling_zscore(&w, 3.0, 10).is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rolling_zscore_returns_zero_when_std_zero() {
        let w: VecDeque<f64> = VecDeque::from([5.0; 10]);
        assert_eq!(rolling_zscore(&w, 5.0, 10).unwrap(), 0.0);
    }
}




===== FILE: uets/live_strategy/ofmr/mod.rs =====
//! OFMR — live strategy bundle.
//!
//! `features` runs first on every stream row: raw market tick -> 15-column
//! feature row (Column_0 .. Column_14, exact LightGBM training order).
//! `strategy` runs second: hand-rolled LightGBM txt inference over those 15
//! columns, mapping P(WIN) >= threshold to `EnterLong` (long-only gated).

pub mod features;
pub mod settings;
pub mod socket;
pub mod strategy;

use super::{LiveStrategy, traits};

/// Fresh bundle with independent state (called per runner slot).
pub fn load() -> Result<LiveStrategy, String> {
    Ok(LiveStrategy {
        name: "ofmr".to_string(),
        computer: Box::new(features::OfmrFeatures::new()),
        builder: Box::new(strategy::OfmrStrategy::new()),
    })
}

/// Re-export the contracts so strategy code reads `super::ComputeFeatures`.
pub use traits::{ComputeFeatures, StateBuilder};

//! OFMR live-strategy settings.
//!
//! No `serde_yaml` dependency: the live values mirror
//! `config/portfolio.json` `_strategy_defaults` and
//! `strategy_system/strategy/ofmr/config.py`. `load()` returns the hardcoded
//! defaults; a future pass can overlay a YAML file with a hand-rolled parser.

/// LightGBM model configuration.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ModelConfig {

    pub threshold: f64,
    pub trade_direction: String,
}

pub struct FeatureConfig {
    pub rsi_14_window: usize,
    pub rsi_2_window: usize,
    pub sma_14_window: usize,
    pub ma_slope_window: usize,
    pub price_roc_window_3: usize,
    pub price_roc_window_5: usize,
    pub rsi_z_window: usize,

    pub min_order_size: f64,
    pub stop_loss_bps: f64,
    pub take_profit_bps: f64,
    pub max_hold_seconds: u64,
    pub lockout_seconds: f64,
    pub max_trades_per_hour: u32,
}

/// All OFMR settings, hardcoded to match research defaults.

    pub model: ModelConfig,
    pub feature: FeatureConfig,
    pub trade: TradeConfig,
}

impl Settings {
    /// Hardcoded defaults matching `config/portfolio.json` and `config.py`.
    pub fn defaults() -> Self {
        Self {
            model: ModelConfig {
                path: "uets/models/ofmr/trader.txt".to_string(),
                threshold: 0.20,
                trade_direction: "long".to_string(),
            },
            feature: FeatureConfig {
                rsi_14_window: 14,
                rsi_2_window: 2,
                sma_14_window: 14,
                ma_slope_window: 5,
                price_roc_window_3: 3,
                price_roc_window_5: 5,
                rsi_z_window: 20,
                deeplob_z_window: 10,
                orderflow_z_window: 10,
                rsi_roc_window_3: 3,
                rsi_roc_window_5: 5,
                barrier_vol_bar_window: 20,
            },
            trade: TradeConfig {
                max_position_usd: 100.0,
                min_order_size: 0.001,
                stop_loss_bps: 5.0,
                take_profit_bps: 5.0,
                max_hold_seconds: 300,
                lockout_seconds: 1000.0,
                max_trades_per_hour: 300,
            },
        }
    }

    /// Load settings: defaults, optionally overlaid by a YAML file at
    /// `CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR/live_strategy/ofmr/settings.yaml` (parsed by a
    /// hand-rolled parser in a future pass). For now this returns defaults.
    pub fn load() -> Self {
        Self::defaults()
    }
}

impl TradeConfig {
    /// Return the configured TP/SL widths and maximum holding duration for
    /// constructing an execution barrier at the actual entry fill.
    pub fn barrier_parameters(&self) -> (f64, f64, u64) {
        (
            self.take_profit_bps,
            self.stop_loss_bps,
            self.max_hold_seconds,
        )
    }
}

impl Default for Settings {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::load()
    }
}


===== FILE: uets/live_strategy/ofmr/socket.rs =====
//! OFMR socket consumer — IPC Unix socket (`sockets/strategies/ofmr.sock`).
//!
//! Framing: `u32` LE length prefix + payload (max 1 MB). Each frame carries a
//! per-symbol tick row; we decode it into a `FeatureRow`, run `compute` +
//! `decide`, and emit trades through `execute()` (print-only here).
//!
//! On win32 (`cfg(not(unix))`) this is a compile-time stub so the crate
//! builds everywhere; the live loop only runs on Unix.

#[cfg(unix)]
use std::path::Path;

#[cfg(any(unix, test))]
use crate::core::feature::FeatureRow;
#[cfg(unix)]
use crate::core::strategy::{Decision, Position};

#[cfg(unix)]
const MAX_FRAME: usize = 1_048_576;

/// Outcome of claiming our socket path.
#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[cfg(unix)]
enum Claim {
    Free,
    OwnedByLive(u32),
}

/// Decode one IPC frame into a `FeatureRow`. Frame body is expected to be a
/// simple `name:value` line protocol, one feature per line.
#[cfg(any(unix, test))]
fn decode_frame(payload: &[u8]) -> Result<FeatureRow, String> {
    let text =
        std::str::from_utf8(payload).map_err(|e| format!("frame payload is not UTF-8: {e}"))?;
    let mut row = FeatureRow::new();
    for line in text.lines() {
        let line = line.trim();
        if line.is_empty() {
            continue;
        }
        let Some((name, val)) = line.split_once(':') else {
            continue;
        };
        let name = name.trim();
        let val = val.trim();
        if let Ok(v) = val.parse::<f64>() {
            row.insert(name, v);
        } else {
            row.insert_missing(name);
        }
    }
    Ok(row)
}

/// Emit a trade decision (print-only in this phase).
#[cfg(unix)]
fn execute(symbol: &str, decision: Decision, position: Position) {
    println!("OFMR execute symbol={symbol} decision={decision:?} position={position:?}");
}

/// Run the OFMR socket consumer loop (Unix only).
#[cfg(unix)]
pub fn run(socket_path: &str, symbol: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
    use std::io::Read;
    use std::os::unix::net::{UnixDatagram, UnixListener};
    use std::time::{Instant, SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

    if let Some(parent) = Path::new(socket_path).parent() {
        std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)
            .map_err(|e| format!("create socket directory {}: {e}", parent.display()))?;
    }

    // Claim the socket path (reuse aggregator-style stale-socket cleanup).
    let claim = crate::aggregator::cleanup::claim(Path::new(socket_path))
        .map_err(|e| format!("claim socket {socket_path}: {e}"))?;
    match claim {
        Claim::OwnedByLive(_pid) => {
            return Err(format!("socket {socket_path} is owned by a live process"));
        }
        Claim::Free => {}
    }

    let listener =
        UnixListener::bind(socket_path).map_err(|e| format!("bind {socket_path}: {e}"))?;

    let mut features = OfmrFeatures::new();
    let mut strategy = OfmrStrategy::new();
    let settings = Settings::load();
    let mut telemetry = StrategyTelemetry::new(symbol);

    let mut position = Position::Flat;

    for stream in listener.incoming() {
        let mut stream = stream.map_err(|e| format!("accept: {e}"))?;
        let mut len_buf = [0u8; 4];
        loop {
            // Read 4-byte LE length prefix.
            let mut got = 0usize;
            while got < 4 {
                let n = stream
                    .read(&mut len_buf[got..])
                    .map_err(|e| format!("read length: {e}"))?;
                if n == 0 {
                    break; // peer closed
                }
                got += n;
            }
            if got < 4 {
                break;
            }
            let frame_len = u32::from_le_bytes(len_buf) as usize;
            if frame_len > MAX_FRAME {
                return Err(format!("frame too large: {frame_len} > {MAX_FRAME}"));
            }

            // Read payload.
            let mut payload = vec![0u8; frame_len];
            let mut got = 0usize;
            while got < frame_len {
                let n = stream
                    .read(&mut payload[got..])
                    .map_err(|e| format!("read payload: {e}"))?;
                if n == 0 {
                    break;
                }
                got += n;
            }
            if got < frame_len {
                break;
            }

            // Decode -> compute -> decide -> execute.
            let started = Instant::now();
            let row = decode_frame(&payload)?;
            let computed = features
                .compute(&row)
                .map_err(|e| format!("compute: {e}"))?;
            let state = crate::core::strategy::MarketState {
                features: computed,
                position,
                sequence: 0,
            };
            let decision = strategy.decide(&state);
            position = state.apply(decision);
            execute(symbol, decision, position);
            let event_time_ms = row.get("event_time_ms").map(|value| value as u64);
            let processing_ns = started.elapsed().as_nanos().min(u64::MAX as u128) as u64;
            telemetry.publish(event_time_ms, format!("{decision:?}"), Some(processing_ns));
        }
    }

    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(unix)]
struct StrategyTelemetry {
    socket: Option<std::os::unix::net::UnixDatagram>,
    symbol: String,
    latency: crate::aggregator::latency::LatencyHist,
    clock_skew: crate::core::telemetry::ClockSkew,
}

#[cfg(unix)]
impl StrategyTelemetry {
    fn new(symbol: &str) -> Self {
        use std::os::unix::net::UnixDatagram;

        let socket_path =
            std::env::var("UETS_GUI_SOCKET").unwrap_or_else(|_| "sockets/gui.sock".to_string());
        let socket = UnixDatagram::unbound().and_then(|socket| {
            socket.set_nonblocking(true)?;
            socket.connect(&socket_path)?;
            Ok(socket)
        });
        let socket = match socket {
            Ok(socket) => Some(socket),
            Err(error) => {
                eprintln!("[telemetry] GUI unavailable at {socket_path}: {error}");
                None
            }
        };
        let mut publisher = Self {
            socket,
            symbol: symbol.to_ascii_lowercase(),
            latency: crate::aggregator::latency::LatencyHist::new(),
            clock_skew: crate::core::telemetry::ClockSkew::new(),
        };
        publisher.publish(None, "enabled".to_string(), None);
        publisher
    }

    fn publish(
        &mut self,
        event_time_ms: Option<u64>,
        decision: String,
        processing_ns: Option<u64>,
    ) {
        let now_ms = SystemTime::now()
            .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
            .map(|elapsed| elapsed.as_millis().min(u64::MAX as u128) as u64)
            .unwrap_or_default();
        if let Some(event_time_ms) = event_time_ms {
            self.clock_skew.record(event_time_ms, now_ms);
        }
        if let Some(processing_ns) = processing_ns {
            self.latency.observe_ns(processing_ns);
        }
        let skew = self.clock_skew.snapshot();
        let memory_mb = process_memory_mb();
        let statistics = serde_json::json!({
            "status": "enabled",
            "latest_market_data": event_time_ms.map(|time| time.to_string()),
            "latest_decision": decision,
            "processing_latency_ms": processing_ns.map(|value| value as f64 / 1_000_000.0),
            "exchange_latency_ms": event_time_ms.map(|event_time| (now_ms as i64 - event_time as i64) as f64),
            "latency_p05_ms": self.latency.percentile(0.05).map(|value| value as f64 / 1_000_000.0),
            "latency_p95_ms": self.latency.percentile(0.95).map(|value| value as f64 / 1_000_000.0),
            "latency_p99_ms": self.latency.percentile(0.99).map(|value| value as f64 / 1_000_000.0),
            "clock_skew_mean_ms": skew.mean_us.map(|value| value / 1_000.0),
            "clock_skew_max_ms": skew.max_abs_us.map(|value| value as f64 / 1_000.0),
            "memory_mb": memory_mb,
        });
        let snapshot = serde_json::json!({
            "strategy_id": "ofmr",
            "asset_id": self.symbol,
            "statistics": statistics,
            "equity_history": [],
        });
        if let (Some(socket), Ok(payload)) = (&self.socket, serde_json::to_vec(&snapshot)) {
            if let Err(error) = socket.send(&payload) {
                if error.kind() != std::io::ErrorKind::WouldBlock {
                    eprintln!("[telemetry] GUI update failed: {error}");
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(all(unix, target_os = "linux"))]
fn process_memory_mb() -> Option<f64> {
    let statm = std::fs::read_to_string("/proc/self/statm").ok()?;
    let resident_pages = statm.split_whitespace().nth(1)?.parse::<u64>().ok()?;
    Some(resident_pages as f64 * 4096.0 / 1_000_000.0)
}

#[cfg(all(unix, not(target_os = "linux")))]
fn process_memory_mb() -> Option<f64> {
    None
}

/// Stub on non-Unix platforms: nothing to run.
#[cfg(not(unix))]
pub fn run(_socket_path: &str, _symbol: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
    Err("OFMR socket consumer requires a Unix platform".to_string())
}

        let payload = b"price:100.5\namount:1.25\nbid_price:100.49\n";
        let row = decode_frame(payload).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(row.get("price"), Some(100.5));
        assert_eq!(row.get("amount"), Some(1.25));
        assert_eq!(row.get("bid_price"), Some(100.49));
    }
